         The beginning

Episode 1.


"Inna lillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un hazbunallahu wani imal wakil,wanan wace irin masiface rana tsaka saida lokaci ya kure,saida komi ya kusa kammala wannan yaron ya cucemu wannan yaro akwai dan iska,yanzu ya zanyi da wannan fitinanaiyar yarinyar,ya zanyi da bala,in jafar yauni wannan ni aka jefa cikin shiga tara ba uku ba,ya zanyi da wadancen mutanen dake jibge awaje?mezan cema su ga yan jarida agefe kowa yayi xuru ni akejira."
Xuface take karyo mashi kamar wanda yake hadiyar kunama,mutane uku ne kusa dashi sahun farko angama sallar juma a.
Yayinda sauran mutane dake cikin masallacin da basu kai ga watsewa duka ba,daya daga cikisu yakejiyo abunda yake wakana daga can gefe tsakanin liman da madaura auren.
Sake jiyo su yayi wani yana magana cikin fushi.
"Baba shin dady jafar haihuwarka yayi,shin dady jafar yayankane kaidashi waye sama da dan uwanshi,inajin kafin dady jafar akwai mutun biyu tsakaninku,don me kake son mayar da xuciyarka karama kamar ta yaro dan shekara goma.baba meyasa kadauki tsoron duniya ka daura mashi,saikace shine azara,ilu mala,ikan mutuwa.idan anfasa auren kai miye naka aibakai kace suce sun fasa ba,wlh wannan abun da kake shike kona mun xuciya.nida nake danka baji tsoron dady ba why kai da kake babban shi kake tsoronsa ba shiyasa yabi ya addabi rayuwarkw ba,baka da wani abu da zakai gaban kanka dole said kajira umurninsa.kafita kashaida wa al,ummar tashi cewar ango da yan gidansu sunce sun fasa,sungano yarinyar bata da tarbiya yes abunda zai jawo fasuwar auren kenan,waye duk garin nan baisan ta ba,baisan iskancin ta ba wane dan gidan mutunci ne daman zai aureta ai daman na xuba ido inga iyakar inda wasan zai tsaya wlh *NADIYAH* badai ta samu miji na gari ba muddin dadyne ubanta kuma muddin................tas tas tas kakeji baban ya daukeshi da kyawawan maruka har kala biyu,duka gefe da gefen fuskarshi.
Cikin fushi baban yace mashi "ibrahim ashe baka da kunya,ashe baka da mutunci agabana kake zagin dan uwana kake zagin yarsa,har kayi wannan isar........katseshi abokin baban yayi yace"ya isa haka don Allah Abbas kabar maganar haka,duk da gaskiya yake fada baiyi daidai ba kayi mashi hkr kaikuma ibrahim kakiyaye ka kuma bashi hkr."
Idonsa yayi jajawur yace"kayi hkr baba"
Banza yayi mashi liman yace"yallabai auren nan ana yinsa ko kuwa kuma kunfasa kun tsayar dani bacin nima inda abunyi da yawa."
Ajiyar xuciya yaja jikinsa yayi sanyi qalau cikin karyewar murya yace"kayi hkr liman kana iya tashi kayi tafiyarka aure kam babu shi tunda babu angon.............."yallabai ni zan aureta"
Kwatsam sukaji wata murya ta yi magana daga bayansu.
Su duka juya wa sukai suka xuba mashi ido,cike da madaukakin mamaki yasakar masu murmushi ya idasa shigowa yana zama kusada ibrahim yasake maimaita maganarsa yace"eh dagaske nake inhar zaka aura mun ita nikuma zan fitar dakai kunya"
Ibrahim ya kallai cike da mamaki yace"kai!karkayi wannan kuskuren kasan ko wacece ita kau........kallon da Baban yake auna mashi yasa yayi shiru.
Murmushi yayi mai kyau da fidda ainahin kwarjininsa yace"ba dan ita zanba abokina,zanyine don na fitar da baba kunya da kuma cecekuce na jama,a duk da cecekucen ma sai anyi amman za,a samu rangwame idan ba afasa auren ba."wani sanyin dadi baban yaji aransa nan take yaji yayi na,am da wannan yaro mai cike da haiba da kwarjini,duk da cewar ba zakace yana da kudi ba bakuma zakace dan gida mai kudi bane tana cikin shigarshi ta kamala,manyan kaya farare tas shadda ce mai rangwamen gata amman kuma kallo daya zakai mashi ka shaida natsuwa da kamalarsa.
"Dan samari hakika naji dadin wannan maganar amman kana ganin bakai wauta ba,auren yarinya irin wannan ba naku bane karka jefa kanka cikin matsala "
Shafa kansa yayi yana sadda kai yace"baba ai shi namiji babu macen dake gagararsa,kuma insha Allah zanbi da ita inhar kayi na,am dani kana iya daurawa dani."
Abokin baban yace"Abbas karfa garin neman mafita ka jefa kanka cikin wata matsalar,ya bakasan yaro ba kwatsam ya fado saika nemi amuncewa dashi"
Baban yace"Alh bala!jafar dai aure yake son yau adaura rashin daurama nadiya aure ayau shine zai jawo rigama da tashin hankalinsu shida ita,bama kamarshi domin ya turo manyan mutanensa daga kashshe daban daban,daga garuruwa daban daban ba kananun mutane ya kira ba na tabbata da bai samu kiran gaggawa ba babu abunda zai hanashi halatar daurin auren yar lelensa ta gaban goshinsa,yace mun karna yarda asamu wata matsala karna yarda na kunyata shi cikin mutanensa,yasa suntaso kafa da kafa sannan ace kuma babu aure yakake zaton zasuji kumashi jafar zaiji"
Shiru Alh bala yayi amma tabbas yaga kuskure anan,shi kallon yaron yake kamar ya sanshi amma bazai iya tabbatar wa ba.
Yana kallo baban ya biya mashi sadaki dubu dari Alh balan ne kuma yayi mashi walicci,yana jin angama daurawa ya lumshe idanunsa wani mashawurin murmushi maikyau ya kubce mashi,yana shafa kanshi kafin yafurta "Alhamdulillah"
Dan danan laud speaker ta dauka fatiha andaura auren *NADIYAH JAFAR MANI DAULA TARE DA ANGONTA ABBAKAR ASSADIQ MUSADDIQ*
kadan daga cikin dunbin mutanem dasuka shaida wannan daurin aure sunsha mamakin jin ba sunan wancen angon bane aka daura dashi,amman dayake basuda wanda zai warware masu lamarin said sukabi yarima asha kida.
Nanfa maroka suka ringa bin ango ga ango babu ko asi,saidai shi baban ya bashi yan wasu kudi yake raba masu.
Ya samu kudi ba yan kadan ba wajen abokan Alh jafar daula,don saida aljihunan sa suka kasa dauka.duk da kallo daya suke mashi su raina mashi domin ba haka suka so gani sirikin mani daula ba,duk zakin bakinsa ace wannan zai zaba wa yar matsayin miji gaskiya kimarsa ta fado karyar tashi ta banza ce.
Cewar wasu abokan Alh jafar din dasuka zo daga japan said wasu daketa maganganu kasa kasa sua duk akan *ABU KHALIPAH* Suna ganin wannan karantace ace yarasa wanda zaiba yarsa sai kaskantaccen talaka domin su ko matsayin mai gadinsu bazasu daukeshi ba.
Baban yana jin abunda suke tattauna wa yayi kunnen uwar shegu dasu yace anshirya resuption a *daula hotel* hotel din dake tashe agarin wanda said mayan kusoshi suke zuwansa,sabone amman yayi mutane kamar yayi shekara da shekaru.
Yawancinsu duk don suxo suga wannan hotel da mani daula ke zuzutawa sukazo,shiyasa dan danan aka gayya cugar mutoci akanufi wajen don cin abinci da kuma kashe kwar kwatar idonsu.
Wasu kuma tuni suka kama hanyat komawa inda suka fito don daurin auren yabata masu rai matuka.
Zasuje yadda akai mani daula yayi masu wannan karantar.
(Taufa always kura fah)

Baban ne yajawo Abu khalipa gefe yace mashi"samun no dinka bayan sallar isha,i zan kiraka saika zo ka sameni agida muyi magana kasan baka gaya munkomi akanka ba do haka dole inason sanin komi game dakai da iyayenka."
"Shikenan baba ngd Zan jiraka insha Allah"
Ahaka suka rabu.
Ya juya yana neman abokinsa dasuka zo salla tare tun dazun yake binsa bai samu damar kebewa dashi ba jiyayi an jawoshi.
Ibrahim ne yajashi suka shiga motarsa yana kallonshi har lokacin shi baijin yayarda da cewar mahaifinsa ya fidda kunya,domin duk wanda yasan Alh jafar mani daula yasan kuma tarihin yarsa nadiya babu tsotsan dazai saka yace zai aureta sai idan da wata akasa.
"Kace sunan ka Abbakar ko?"
Murmushi yayi mashi yace"eh sunan kenan"
Gyarazama ibrahim yayi yace"Amman kai ba dan nan garin bane ko?"
Still murmushin yake mashi yace"mekagani ni haifeffen yola ne amma zama ne ya kawo ni nan banfi wata hudu dana dawo nan ba sana diyyar wasu dalilai"
"Meye dalilan naka?"
"Uhmm wannan sirrina nane bai shafeka ba shin mema yasa duk kake mun wadanan tambayoyi kamar wani barawo"
Shafa kanshi yayi yace"shikenan yanzu nasan cewar bakasan komi akan mani daula da yarsa nadiya ba amman kayi wauta kayi kuskuren jefa kanka cikin wannan rudanin,domin kau dady jafar bazai taba amunta da siriki irin ka ba domin shi baya hudda da irinku,sannan nadiya ba haguwar yarinya ce bakowane namiji zai iya zama da ita matsayin mata ba,ina baka shawara aduk sadda kaji sakon dady ya zo gareka to ba makawa takardar nadiya zaka bashi,don haka kana isa gida ma ka rubutata ka ajiye don kowane lokaci zai iya dawowa kuma rashin yin hakan shine babban kalubalenka.ina kaunarka shiyasa nake gaya maka gaskiya,karka shigar da kanka ciki rigimar su do ba karewa take ba kafita cikinta kaje kaji da rayuwarka da ganinka baka da wasu abubuwa dake damunka to inkau ka dauki auren nan da gaske zaka daukowa kanka dala babu gammo,shawarace nabaka inkaji kayi aiki da ita inbakaji ba kana iya nacewa akai wamda baiji bari ba zaiji hoho"
Yabude mashi yace "kana iya fita"
Murmushi yayi yace"nagode sossi da shawara amman kasan me niba kaar kowane namiji nake ba wanda take jin tsoron tunkarar kowane iri kalubale,ni nan nine *namiji mai suna namiji mai dakakkar xuciya mai kafiya akan muradinsa mai juriya akan kowace irin dauwa inhar zan cinma burina da muradina,ibrahim kake ko zan koya maka wasu abubuwa arayuwa hafuwata dakai nayau naji inasonka kuma naji inason baka darussan rayuwa zaka dauki salo zaka koyi rashin tsoro da kuma juriya,zan sake nemanka karo na biyu ngd"
Yafito yabar mashi motar ya nufi hanyar da zata kaishi titi yahau abun hawa

Episode 2.

"Assalamu alaikum"
Yayi sallama cikin wani gida wanda kallo guda zakai mashi ka tabbatar da cewar gidan marasa arxikine,amman kuma idan ka sake kallonsa zaka tabbatar da cewar akwai tsafta,akwai kula, akwai wadatar xuci da rufin asiri.
Tsaftace yake ashare tsakar gidan yayi sumul,dama kuma gashi shafe da siminti.
Ginin bulone amman ya dade sosai dakuna biyu ne ciki da falo,sai daki buyu falle guda ua sai dan wajen sanwa da aka kange da langa langa saboda lokacin damuna.sai bayi daka shigo farko.
Mutun gudace tsakar gidan ta ansa mashi sallamarsa cikin murmushi da sakin fuska.
"Aaha SADIQU sai yanzu ka dawo daga masallacin,tun dazun inanan ina tambayar duk wanda yashigo cikin su salisu sai suce mun basu hadu dakai ba ina katsaya."
Isowa yayi kusada ita yana sauke numfashi da murmushin nan akan fuskarshi mai kara ainafi nuna tsantsar ruwa kyansa.
"Ya zaka zauna kasa kuma kayanka farare"
Ta jawo tabarma dake gefenta ta shimfida tana cewa ya zauna akai,ya koma sama yana saka hannu cikin duk aljhunansa yana fiddo duk kudaden dake ciki ya xube mata gabanta.
Yace"ina can masallaci kuma sai akai sanarwar daurin aure,dole na tsaya"
Duka idanunta ta fiddo tana kallonsa cike da mamaki marar iyaka.
Dariya ya danyi yace"rufe bakin innamu kar kuda yafada wai duk na ganin kudin ne haka"
Murmushi tayi tace"hada haka ma sannan kuma daga fita xuwa masalaci ina ka samo kudi haka,tunda dai nasan ba,a bakin aiki kake ba yanxu."
Kwashesu yayi yasaka a leda jin shigowar kanensa mata sunta dariya ko sallama ma basuyi ba.
"Meke damunku sumayya kamar wasu sakarkaru kuntaho kunta dariya ko ince kinata dariya kamar mahaukaciya,ko sallama ma bakuyi ba"
Ganin yayansu zaune yasa kowace ta natsu tana gaidashi yana hararsu don sunsan sunyi laifi.
Cikin shan mur kamar bashine yake murmushi ba yake kallonsu kafin ya tsayar da idonsa aka *Asmau* ita yake kallo amman maganar ba da ita yake ba. Sadda kanta tai kasa tana komawa gefen inna ta labe.
"Daryar me kike sumy?"
Zama tai gefensa tana satar kallo kanwar tata cike da murmushi.
Tace"yaya inna ta aikemu bayan layi gidan Anty kubra to bayan munfito muna tahowa gida shine wani yatsaida mu wai yana son asmau........ta soke kai tana dariya kasa kasa.
Sake d'aurewa yayi yace"sumy kakankine niko,meye na dariya aciki to. zan ballaki anan wajen  wlh,saime yafaru sai ita asma,un ta bashi number ta kome?"
Barin dariyar tai tana sadda kai tace "Kayi hkr yaya,bata bashi ba kawai dai cewa tai ya bata tausayi da ace babu aure kanta data saurareshi."
Wani irin duka kirjinsa yayi yanajin kunan maganarta ta karshe,har saida bacin ransa ya bayyana akan fuskarsa.
Uffan bai sake magana ba jin shiru nashi yasa tasan yagama da ita tatashi tashige dakinsu.
Inna tace"duk laifina ne sadiqu saboda dana aiki sumayya shine asmau tace zata bita nace aa bazata ba shine  tadage da rokona nace ta bari kadawo ta tambayeka ko kuma ta kiraka niba ruwana shine tace mun bata da kati,dole na barta suka tafi.ganin ta dade bata fita ba shiyasa ma na barta suka tai,kayi hkr"
Duk da ransa abace yake ya taushi ransa yace"bakida laifi innamu karki damu kanki ya jefawa Asmau wani kallo dataji kamar ta nitse yace mata"thank you"
Ya tashi yana mika ma inna kudin "ki ajiye anjima idan na dawo dadare zamuyi magana"
"To sadiqu bari akawo maka abincinka"
Ta dallama asma,un harara.
"Watau ke bakida hankali ko"
Idonta yayi narai narai zatai kuka "ba kuka na tambayeki ba akoke keda idonki baida wani aiki sai kuka,kece da laifi amman kece da kuka kije kiyi mashi bayani to,har yau dai babu wayau tir."
Tatashi ta shiga daki don adana kudin daya bata.
Kamar wadda kwai ya fashewa aciki haka tatashi ta nufi madafarsu,abincinsa dake cikin kula ta dauko sannan ta je ta dauko plet da cokali dakinsu ta dora bisa tire,ta dauko kankarar da aka siyo ta sirka mashi da ruwan randa ta zubaa mashi cikin jug ta dauki karamin cup ta daura.
Idan kaga yadda ta tsara tiren saika dauka daga wani gidan aka shigo da shi,yayi kyau sosai.
Gabanta yana faduwa ta nufi dakinsa dake can gefe,hanyar waje.
Salisu ne yashigo sukai kicibis yayi dariya yace"Asama za,akaiwa maigida abinci ne"
Hararar sa tai cikin yar muryarta kamar ta yan yara tace cikin sonyin kuka.
"Walahi yaya salis saina gayama inna batace kabar jana ba."
Gwalo ya mata yayi gaba tace da dan karfi don yaji.
"Kuma naje nace salisu hallakakwabo"ganin zai juyo yasa tai saurin afkawa cikin dakin yayan nasu babu ko sallama 
Yana kwance yana kallon sama idonsa rufe ya dora hannunsa ga kirji ya rungume.
Tsaye tayi tana kallonsa ahankali ta ajiye tiren saman table din da take ajiyewa idan ta kawo mashi,ganin ko takalmansa bai cira ba yasa ta isa kusada gadonsa tana sanda gabanta yana faduwa ta fara warware igiyar takalmin ahankali take komi kamar tana cirewa jariri karya tashi bacci.
Tagama cirewa ta zare mashi safar,ta xubama kafafunsa ido sun mata kyau sosai.
Tasan idonsa biyu kuma bazai tanka mata ba,yasa ta fara xuba abincin dambun shinkafa ne yaji zogala da gyada,ga albasa yasha sai kamshi yake.
Da miyane sukai kuma shiyace yana sonsa yau shiyasa ta zage tahada mashi yadda zaita mata santi.
Ta gama xuba komi ta sauko mashi kasa ta shimfida kafet saman ledar dakin taxuba mashi ido kurin ta rasa yadda zatai tabashi hkr.
Jingina tai da gadon tafara shashshekar kuka don haka kadai zai iya sashi ya kulata baya jure kukanta acikin kunnuwan sa.
Runtse ido yayi jin yadda ta dage take kuka kuma yasan dagaske kukan take don yasan bata kukan karya,oh shida akaima laifi baiyi kuka ba sai ita datai laifin.
Rasa yadda zaiyyida ita yayi don bazai huce don yau ba.
Cikin hasala yadda yasan dole tayi mashi shuru don bata son tsawa.
"Kimun shiru Asmau!"
Tsit tayi kamar ruwa ya cinyeta sai numfashi da takeja wasu hawayen suka silalo mata shiyasa bata son bata mashi rai,duk saita fita hayyacinta baya mata da sauki gigita ta yake shima ya gigita kanshi.
Ahankali tatashi ta nufi kofar fita don itama ranta ya baci,bata ga abun dogon fushi anan ba tunda bata kula mutumen ba bata saurareshi ba bata bashi no dinta ba balle address dinta,to miye zai mata wannan punishment din bacin yasan bazata jure ba.
cikin sauri ya tashi ganin tafiya zatai,idan ta barshi taya zai ci abincin duk fushinsa inhar bata gabansa abincin baya shiga.
Har yanzu muryarsa bata fita sosai saboda bacin tai.
Daker ya iya furta sunanta cikin wani yanayi da zai nuna maka cewar yana son ta dawo.
"Asmau"
Tsaya wa tai bakin kofar fita hawaye suna fita idonta tana son ta jiyo amman ta kasa kamar yabita bakin kofar yakeji amman yasan shima bazai iya ba,don baya zakewa a soyayyarta saboda yasan bashi take soba.
"Come here Asmau"
Girgiza kai kurin tayi ta kasa cewa komi kawai saita fice aguje tana sake rushewa da kuka tashige dakinsu tai uwar daka ta wuce sumy falo tana tilawar haddarsu ta islamiyya.
Binta tayi da kallo ta rufe qur,anin tatashi tashiga cikin dakin samunta tai kan gafo tana rusa kuka gefe ta zauna tana kallonta kafin ta jata ta dora kanta bisa cinyarta tana shafa kanta ahankali,batace komi ba haka taita kuka har xuwa sada tasamu salama.
Cikin kuka kuka take cema sumyn "yaya sumayya yaya fushi yake, korata yayi daga dakinsa yaya sumayya yaya tsawafa yayi mun,saboda Allah meye laifina kawai dai don baya sona saiya ringa mun haka yaya sumayya yasani bana son shotting,Amman yau harda mun tsawa bazan sake xuwa inda yake ba daman ai kece kike cewa yana sona yanxu. kuma kece kike cewa na daina nuna ban damu dashi ba Amman danayi laifi kadan saiya ringa mun haka,daman nasan wlh haka zai ringayimun. yanzu da *Abdul-basir* dina ne bazai mun haka ba.........."
Sumy tai saurin rufe mata baki tana cewa"karna sake jin sunan sa abakin ki Asmau,don yanzu ke ba mallakinsa bace ke matar aurece haramunne kina anbatarsa haramunne tunaninsa axuciyarki,komi kikaga yaya yayi akanki wallahi asmau soyayya ce,bazaki gane hakan ba sai nan gaba saboda har gobe Abdul-basir yana ranki kuma nasha gaya maki kiciresa ki raba xuciyarki dashi,shine zai saka kigane me yaya yake nufi akanki.idan har ada ya nuna baya yinki to ayanxu yana yinki 100%. Kuduka kun kasa kwantar da hankalin ku ku fahimci haka,bance karkija ajinki ba domin hakan yana da kyau,amman karkija da yawa kiyi hkr yaya yana sonki Asmau."
Hawayenta take gogewa kamar ba ita tasha kuka ba.tashi tayi ta ja filo ta kwanta kawai batace ma yayar tata komi ba.
Murmushi sumy tayi don dagani miskilancin ya motsa tashi tai ta koma ta cigaba da tilawarta don karfe shidda na yamma suke tafiya sai bayan isha,i suke dawowa.

Tun bayan fitar asmau yaji kamar duniyar ce duka ta ruguxo mashi bisa kanshi,dan danan yaji ya huce fushinta gareshi tashin hankaline don gigitashi yake,da bazai iya binta ya kirata ba amman jin da yake xuciyarsa kamar ta fito waje yasa ya yarda duk wani girman kai yafito,tsakar gida ba kowa ya nufi dakinsu yana saka kafa falon yajiyota tana kukan ta tana furta sunan mutumen da yatsana *ABDUL-BASIR* runtse idonsa yayi yana jin jiri yana daukarsa duk dokin son ganinta da sonrf lallashinta sai yakau,ashe haukan banza yake asma,u har yau yaron nan shine axuciyarta,ashe d'an zarginsa da dokinsa akan tafara damuwa da lamarinsa duk ba haka ba.
Juyawa yai kurin ya koma daki ya kulo kofar,gadon ya koma ya kwanta ya kurawa fankar dakin ido,tunaninsa ya kasu kashi kashi,tunaninsa ya dauka ya gama da case din asmau saidai ya shiga sabo yanxu ashe ba haka bane.sai yaji duk fargaban tasan ya sake wani auren tafita ransa .tunda har cewa bata yinsa rufe ido yayi yanajin duk yunwarsa ma tatafi,abincin da yake ma dokin yaci yaji yafita ransa,me yasa yafara son yarinyar nan har da zata watsa mashi kasa ga ido..............sumarsa yake ya mutsawa yana soka yatsunsa ciki.
Haka ya kasance cikin wannan halin har sadda akaita kiran sallar la,asar sannan ya iya tashi yayi alwallah yafita sallah,bai dawo gidan ba sai gaf da magruba lokacin su sumy sun shirya tafiya islamiyya dake nan kusa dasu.
Duk sun tsakar gida yashigo babu waddaya kalla cikinsu ya xuba ruwa abuta ya zauna yana alwallah.
Almajirine yayi sallama yana bara daidai ya gama alwallar yana daura agogo ga hannunsa ya kalli sumy dake saka safa. Asmau na zaune kusada  inna ta kasa ma kallonsa shima bai kali inda take ba.
Yace ma sumy"shiga dakina akwai abinci nanan ki juyewa wannan yaron shi."
Cikin sauri tatashi taje ta dauko abincin taga ko spoon guda ba,aiba ta kalli yayan nasu dake zaune yana jiran kiran sallah.
"Yaya bakaci ba kuma kace duka za,a juye mashi"
Wani kallo ya mata yace"ina ruwanki,kiyi abunda ke gabanki"
Hkr ta bashi ta zuba ma yaron ta kai kayan wajen wanke wanke.
Inna ta kasa yin shiru tace"sadiqu badai bakaci komi ba tun rana?to mezakaci saina girka maka kafin kadawo."
Mikewa yayi kwata kwata babu annuri fuskarsa yayi yake yace"innamu daman fa naci abinci wajen daurin auren nan karki damu kanki zanci tuwon dakika yi idan nadawo da wuri,inkuma ban dawo ba zan siyo abun da zan iya ci. sai nadawo yafice bai sake dubar kowa ba cikinsu."
Asmau tashare kwallah tace"kingani ko inna gabanki nasha wahalar girkin nan dazun amman baici ba,inna don Allah ina laifin nan ina dogon fushin da take dani"
Harara ta watsa mata tace"kizauna kalon ruwa kwado yai maki kafa,sakarya kurin idan baki fitar da wannan dan iskan yaron ba saina sabarmaki agida nan Allah ya kashe yabaki miji na nunama sa,a kina wasa kina sakarci inji sumayya take koyar dake don nasan dai ada ba haka kike ba."
Zaro ido sumy tayi tace"Allah sarki inna sumy sarkin laifi,wlh da kinsan kokarin danake akan Asmau saikin godemun,wlh inna babuni ciki ina bakin kokarina akanta,kuma itama shaidace"
"Uhmm kuyi sallah kukama kagaban ku inkuma baku xuwa sai inji"tashige daki tabarsu.


✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨

Kamar yadda sukai da baban ibrahim haka ta kasance ana gama sallar isha,i yakira shi tare da bashi address din gidanshi inda zai sameshi saida yakoma gida yayi wanka ya canza  kaya wannan karon nasha mamakin shigar da yayi manyan kayane shadda dakakka gixner blue color yasha sawu ciki bakake da bakar hularsa,ga babar riga sai wani masifeffen kamshi yake,fuskarshi tana fitar da wani annuri na daban saiya fito sak angon inna rike baki tai tana kallonsa.
"Sadiqu ina zaka! rabon dana ga kayi gayu irin wannan tun ranar daka dawo garin nan,nifa kasoma rudani"
Dari ya  yayi yana sake gyara zaman hularsa ya daura agogo yace"inna karkiyi bacci da wuri,amman idan kinga nakai goma ban dawo ba ki kwanta dasafe sai muyi magana"
Tana ta kallonshi tace"to yarona Allah yadawo dakai lfy,kaga kuwa yadda kayi kyau da asmau nanan zatace zance zaka"
Tafada cikin tsokana.
Murmushi yayi daya sake fidda ruwan kyansa yafita baice komi ba don akwai kunyar innar tare dashi.
Daidai isowar abokinsa Nuradden daidai su asmau sun taso suna ta fira da juna itada
 sumy,kwatsam idonta yafada kan mijin nata cikin wanan gayun dan danan gabanta ya fadi.har batasan ta riko hannun sumy ba bakinta na rawa tace"dubi yaya kalli kigani yaya sumayya "
Itama tazaro ido tace"yayi kyau sosai ko ina zasu da daddaren nan"
Har suka karaso gabansu bai kallesu ba yana magana da nura kurin,har saida nuran yace mashi"bakaga amarya ba wai ka wani basar"
Murmushin gefen baki yayi yace"tada mota don Allah"janshi yake da iskanci yana mashi dariya "kasan kayi laifi shiyasa kake basarwa ko,kaje ka kwaso ma kanka dala babu gammo"
"Inji ubanwa yace maka babu gammo aini koda gammon kobabu dauka nake kuma zakace na fada maka,kafi kowa sanin dalilin wannan auren so don haka kabar abun ahaka asmau bata sona nura,koda taji na kara aure hakan bazai dameta ba"
Tuki yake cikin kwarewa yace"kai kake ganin haka *khalipha* Amman asmau tana sonka kaki bata dama ne."
Hum kawai yace bai sake tankawa ba sai nuran daketa dauko mashi hirar abunda ya shafesu.har sadda motarsu taxo bakin get din gidan.maigadin bai jira tantance kosu waye ba ya wangale get don ganin motar dake shigowa tayi kama da abokan kasuwancin  yallabai.

Episode 3.

Basu wani jimaba azaune falon da ya gaji da haduwa,don gaskiya babu karya komi yaji ga wata tv kamar ka dauko mutanen dake ciki.
Agaban su tire ne guda biyu na kayan motsa baki,sai lemuka kala uku da cups masu shegen kyau.
Nura kau ya saki baki yana ta azawa kamar wanda daman yake jira.har khalipa ta kasa jurewa yayi masa magana.
"Kaga da wannan zalamar dakake kana wani hannu baka hannu kwarya kace su baka leda kajuye kawai."
Yafada yana kunshe dariyarsa.
Aikuwa yaji haushi yace mashi.
"Bama sai anbani leda ba ga aljihunana zan saka"
Sukai dariya gaba daya kafin suji motsin shigowa falon suka sake natsuwa,nura yana goge baki da tishu din dake kan table din.
Baban ibrahim ne shida ibrahim din suka fito xuwa cikin falon suna gaisawa da juna,nura yanata washe baki yana mikawa Ibrahim hannu.
Yayin da ibrahim ya cika da mamaki da al,ajabin shigar khalipan,domin idan ka hangoshi adaxun wajen daurin aure bazaka ce shine anan zaune ba kamar wani babban attajiri.jinjina kai kurin yayi yana ta kallonsa,don baida halin yin magana.
Bayan sun natsu baban yace ma khalipa
"Ka fada mana sunanka bacin shi baka sanar damu komi akanka ba,don haka zanso nasan waye kai kuma miye aikin ka don nasan irin taimakon da zan maka,duk da dai naga kamar ma kaidin mai haline sosai ba kamar yadda nayi zato ba."
Murmushinsa dayake kara mashi kyau da kwarjini yayi yana sadda kai kafin ya nisa yace cikin ladabi da nuna da,a.
"Baba kamar yadda kaji daxun nidai sunana Abbakar Abbakar,ni marayane ba uwa ba uba,saidai ina da kawuna wan mahaifiyata awjensa na tashi tun bayan rasuwar babana,ni dan yola ne babana fulanin yola ne na asali, haka ma mamana ni kadaine da garesu inada shekara sha biyar aduniya suka rasu arana guda sakamakon wani abu wanda yake sirrina ne.
na dawo ga kawuna ya cigaba da rikona acan yolan ahannunsa nai karatu na karanta computer nayi digree akan ta,ban Dade da kammala bautar kasa ba shima kawuna Allah ya anshi ranshi.mafarin dawowarmu nan garin kenan domin can din ya gagaremu zama saboda halin rayuwa.
Muna zaune rijiyar zaki nan cikin kano,da kannena hudu daya tai aure saura uku biyu mata daya namiji.
wannan abokinane tun da muka dawo nan shine abokina,kuma bana wata sana,a muna dai cikin nema kuma muna fatan dace,shine ma ya dinka mun wannan kayan da wannan sallar,don shidin abokin kwarai ne kuma sabanin ni shi yana aiki, banki yake aiki acan  Abuja.yau yazo nace ya rakoni don andaura mun aure,ya dauka wasa nake,don yasaba jina ina cewa bazan aure ba saina samu aikinyi. kaji dan tarihina baba,kuma insha Allah zan tsaya tsayin daka wajen ganin na rike  amanar da ka dankamun."
Jinjina kai kurin yake yana ajiyar xuciya,yayin da ibrahim kwata kwata baiji gamsuwa acikin wannan labarin ba,haka kawai yaji ya dora mashi alamar tambaya tun maganar da sukai dazun ya nuna mashi akwai wani abu,amman baida hujjar dazai ce baida gaskiya kawai zargine da hasashe.
Baban ya nisa yace"Ammm sadiq ni bazance maka Abubakar zallaba inajin nauyi don sunan sirikina ne inajin kunya"
Dariya sukai suduka yacigaba da cewa"nayarda da duk bayanan ka kuma insha Allah zan tallafa maka,daman muna da bukatar kwararre ta bangaren irin karatunka can company,don haka gobe karfe goma ka saman *MAI DAULA ION COMPANY"* yafada yana mika mashi ID card dinsa.
 "insha Allah zanbaka gurbi mai kyau, fatan zaka tsare doka kakuma dage don indan muka ga kwazonka yayi yawa zamu turaka sabuwar ma'aikatar matatar man oki dake kaduna mai suna *mai daula oil limited* shine babban company da *J daula* yake tutiya dashi,acikin companoninsa kaga idan kai sa,ar xuwa can na tabbata bazai takura cewar sai yarabaka da yarsa ba,don kuwa daga shi har yar tasa basa hudda da talaka kuma basa sonsa.yana daya daga cikin abunda yasa nake jin dari darin nadiya tasan da wanda ta aura,don tun daga sanda aka daura har xuwa yanxu bata san waye ba,tana dai can ta gigice da rashin samun wancen saurayin nata *jadeed*,zan hada ka da ibrahim ya warware maka wacece nadiya don kasan ta yadda zaka zauna da ita,inason kafin dawowar Jafar ka dauketa ta tare don muddin ya dawo tarewarta abune mai matukar wahala,ibrahim nabarka dasu salan kayi gaban kanka."
Ya mike suna sallama dasu khalipan ya shiga ciki.
Ibrahim yasan me gaban kanshi yake nufi,shima kuma bazai so fallasawar ba don kamar ya wulakanta babansa.

Episode 4.


"Ina ganin kamar wannan time din bazai ishemu muyi maganar da duk zamuyi ba,ina ganin idan babu damuwa zamu iya bari sai gobe"
Cewar ibrahim bayan tafiyar  baban.
Nura ne yace"haka ne gaskiya,don ni kam daga nan ma zance zan wuce"
Yafada cikin tsokana.
Ibrahim yace"to agaida ta inajin mu hadu goben komi kenan."ya fiddo wayarsa yana ma khalipa magana "samun phone no dinka saina nemeka idan lokacin yayi nima ba zaune nake ba"
Baidaice mashi komi ba ya anshi wayar ya saka mashi layinsa da yake anfani dashi tun baya cuwan sa garin.
Kafin suyi musabaha suka fito,hannun nura rike da apple yana ta gatsa.
"Banga ka debo sauran ba duk zarin naka iya Apple ya tsaya"
Suna dariya nuran yace"baka luraba sadda nake xubasu cikin aljihuna kalla kagani."
Yana nuna mashi aljihun wandonsa dana rigarsa,mamaki da dariya suka kama khalipan yace"Amman kau kaban mamaki wlh ban zaci zaka diba ba duk daukata wasa kake"
Nura na jan motar suna hira har suka bar unguwar.

"Amman kana ganin ansar aikinsu bazai bada wata matsala ba?"
Shigewa yayi cikin kujerar dayake yayi yana ajiyar xuciya yace"duk abunda zanyi nura saina gama tsarashi,saina tabbatar da cewar yin shin bazai bada wani implication ba,don haka karka sama ranka wata damuwa kawai dai lokacin dake gaba shine mai matukar muhimmanci,ka tayani addua wannan aikin ba nau bane kadai na mutane dayawa ne nasha wuya kafin na iya dagewa har nayi nasarar ansarsa,don haka bata lokaci ba nau bane"
Nura ya numfasa yace"hakane Allah ya shige mana gaba,amman gobe ya dace ka sanar da shi kana da aure,don kar yayi bincike yagano kayi mashi karya rashin yadda zai shigo tsakaninku."
"Insha Allah duk wani abu da nasan zai bada kofar zargi bazam barsa ba."
Wajen mai nama suka tsaya yafita yasiyo ya biya shagon drinks sannan ya dawo,ya ajema nura leda guda sannan ya bar biyu gabansa.
"Da alama dai na toshiyar baki akasiyar wa asmau"
Murmushi yayi yace"kyale Asmau nura kawai dai ina bukatar cine suma na siya masu,Asmau har gobe wannan yaron dai *Abdul-basir* shine cikin ranta,nama rasa ta inda zan biyowa matsalar.tana daukar cewa har yau bana sonta,sannan tagaza daukar cewa ita fa yanzu matar wanice kula wani ko tunanin wani duk bai dace ba,idan nadauki zafi bata gane cewar laifinta ne taxo ta bani hkr saidai ma ta dauki dogon fushin dayafi nawa,ban sani ba ko yarintace ko kiyayyatace ko wani abun na daban."
Yayi shiru yana jin ciwon Abunda yake faruwa.
Nura ya katseshi da cewa "cool down Aboki karka zama mai karyayyar xuciya mana,wannan duk ba wani abu bane mai wuya ko ince mai tsananin da zaka tada hankalinka akai,Asmau yarinyace idan nai daidai bata wuce 20 years ba,idan ma takai kenan sannan maganar Abdul basir dolene zai ki goguwa cikin ranta domin tana matukar kaunarsa.sannan anrabata dashi ta karfi da yaji anhada ta da kai wanda kai din kuma da zaka yi abunda zai mantar da mata da wancen din kakasa,kana nuna mata bata kai ba komi zakai baka janta jiki kana sakar mata fuska,idan laifi tai cikin ruwan sanyi zaka ladaftar da ita har tagane cewa tayi badaidai ba,karka bani kunya duk kasan wannan amman zafin kishi yana na hanaka dole fa ka ajiyesa gefe kanuna mata matsayinta,indai kana son goge komi daya faru baya don nasan xuwa yanzu kana matukar son matarka,so pls karka bata damar da zata rikice maka karasa gabanta balle bayanka,ga kuma wani ka sake rarumo ma kanka bansan ya zakai da wadannan chakwakiyar ba"
Idonsa lumshe yana sauraren Nuran har ya gama magana.
Ahankali ya iya cewa"zan gyara kusuren insha Allah,waccen maganar kuma zanbi da ita ne"
Basu sake magama ba har suka iso gida ya saukeshi ya wuce wajen budurwarsa da auren bai wuce sati uku ba nan gaba.

✨✨✨✨✨✨✨

Akwai wuta gidan dakin salisu bude yake da alama kallo yakeyi ya leka ya gansu shida abokinsa suna cin tuwo suna kallon kwallon.
"Yaya ina wuni duk yau bamu hadu ba tun kafin sallar juma,a"
"Wlh kuwa salis na zama busy yau,memakon na ganka kana karatu saina sameka gaban tv"
Sosa kai yake yana dariya abokin nashi yace"yaya ai yanzu damun gama shi zamuyi insha Allah"
Yace"to da dai yafi kuna gaf da ajin karshe karkuyi wasa"
Sukace insha Allah 
Ya wuce cikin gidan bayan ya cira babnar rigar jikinsa,karfe goma yanzu,aman tau babu wanda yayi baccin wuri sabanin da lokacin dasuka saba kwanciya.
Inna da sumayya suna nasu kallon suma,amman babu asmau dakin innar.
Sallama yayi yana shigowa sumy dake kwance tatashi tana mashi sannu da xuwa.
Ya ansa sannan yagaida innar.
"Banzaci samunku ido biyu ba yau kuma hirar ta motsa"
Inna tace"wlh kuwa kadai gani,wai wani film ne tun kwana uku ake tallarsa sabone shine muka tsaya kallo,wadda ke nacin kallon nashi ma kaga bata wajen."
Yasan Asmau take nufi ya mika mata ledar hanunsa guda yace"gashi nan nakune idan na gama zan shigo muyi magana inna,ke kuma ki turu mun Asmau"
Yafada yana nufar kofar fita.
"Yaya idan tayi bacci fah"
Wani kallo ya auna mata yafice.
Inna tace"idan nace kece mai daure mara gindin kinkulawa da Aurenta sai kice mun wlh tallahi ko"
"Aa innamu gani nai tun da muka dawo daga islamiya ko abinci bataci ba tashige daki kinga zuwa yanzu ya isa tayi bacci"
"Zaki wuce kiyi abunda ke gabanki ko surutun zaki cigaba"
"Kiyi hakuri na tafi"
Tafita ta wuce dakinsu wutar uwar dakar kashe ta kunna ta hangota can kuryar gado,tasaka rigar baccinta mai kyau pink mai sulbi tasa hula ta rufe gashinta,ayadda ta ganta rubda ciki tasan idonta biyu.
Ta isa wajenta ta bubbuga kafarta "Asmau kinyi baccine"
"Ya akaine yaya sumy?"
Cike da mamaki tace"wai  Asmau kina nufin tun dazun bakiyi bacci ba"?
Tashi tai zaune hasken dakin ya haske surarta da fuskarta saitayi kamar wata balarabiya,Asmau akwai ruwan kyau masha Allah.

"Ban ma jima da kwanciya ba nayi bitar hadda ne ya akai ne?"
Jinjina kai tayi tace"daman yayane ya dawo yace kije"
Kallon yayarta take kamar zata gano wani abu dake jikinta,kafin ta sauke idonta kasa tace"nariga na gama yanke shawara zanje ga baba mudi araba auren kowa ya huta"
Sumayya ta zabura tana kallonta cike da tsoro da mamaki,cikin fada tace"bakida hankali ashe?ubanwa ya baki wannan shawarar,to wallahi babu ruwna Asmau meyasa kulum tunaninki ragaggene?wannan ai haukane kuma wallahi kirufa ma kanki asiri tun kafin innamu taji wannan maganar,kuma idan kin rabu da yaya wlh babaki nai maki ba bazaki samu kamarsa ba,nagarta natsuwa kamala kyau wadannan kadai idan kika rasasu daga gareshi ba lallai kisamu daga waninsa ba,nidai Allah yasani inayin iyakar kokarina akanki zanje na sanarma da Anty kubra,idan ni kinraina ni ita ai kila da sauran kimarta idonki,kuma idan baza kije ba zanje na sanar dashi shi sai yazo ya tadda ki ai, kuma wlh kiris yarage innamu bataji wannan labarin ba,kema kinsan sauran."
Tatashi tafice tabarta hawaye ta share ta mike ta dauki hijab dinta doguwa data dawo islamiyya da ita ta saka,sannan ta nufi dakin nashi.

Wanka yayi yashirya three quarter ne jikinsa baki sai farar shet mai guntun hannu,banda kamshi bakajin komi dakin sai iskar fanka kayan kallonshi kashe suke,system dinsa ce kan cinyarsa yake aikin sarrafawa,ya lankwashe kafafunsa kan gadon kamar wanda zaici tuwo.
Daga kasa table din dayake dora abune idan zaici.
Naman ne cike da wani da bowl yasha kabeji da albasa ga tumatir,sai maltina da zeza milk sai cups guda biyu.
Tun dazun yake sauraren shigowarta harya sare sai yaji motsinta,kamar wadda za,a ce arrr ta ruga sallama tayi can ciki inbadan yana kallon bakinta ba bazai dauka tayi ba.
Saman Capet din dakin kusada table din ta zauna tana soke kanta kasa.
Duk saitake jinta yau wani iri tare dashi,wani sabon abune takeji yau kamar yaune tafara shigowa dakinsa irin wannan lokacin.
Bakinta yana rawa kamar xatai kuka tace"yaya ina wuni,yaya sumy tace kana kirana"
Tattara computer yayi ya ajiye gefe ya sauko kasan kamar yadda tayi,ya sauko da duk abunda yake kan table din yana jerawa kasa kusa dasu,ganin haka yasa tatay shi da cups din.
Baice mata komi ba ya fara xuba malt din cikin cup guda sannan ya zuba zeza milk din ga dayan ya daukeshi ya kai bakinta.
Cikin mamaki ta dago take kallonsa,da idanunta tubarkalla kamar madara,saida ya lumshe ido don sunyi tasiri akanshi.
Saida yace mata"oya open ur mouth"
Sannan cikin kunya da kasala ta bude bakin ta kurba,yasan babu lemun da takeso sama da mai madara shima kuma zeza,ajiye wa yayi ya dauko yankan nama guda yamika mata ga baki again.
Langabe kai tayi tana narai narai da fuska zatai magana yadora yatsansa kan lips dinsa alamun bayason maganar,dole ta bude baki tana ansa ko saudaya baiciba ita kurin yake cidawa,idan yabata sau uku ko hudu sai yabata xexa din.ahaka har taji zatai amai sannan ya kyaleta.
Ya fara ciyar da kanshi satar kallonsa take yau din duk saiya koma mata kamar wani sabo,jitai yace"karkisa na kware don Allah"
Soke kai tayi kasa tana jin tsananin kunya ta rasa meyasa taketa kallonsa duk ya canza na yini guda.
Har ya gama sannan yasha ruwa yarungume hannu yana kallonta,duk ta rasa yadda zatai.
Dagowa tai suka hada ido kallon dayake mata yayi bala,in kashe mata jiki kamar zatai kuka tace"yaya inajin bacci"
Kamar jira yake tai magana yana kallonta cikin sanyin murya mai laushi yace mata.
"Asma,u waye ni"
Ahankali take wasa da hannunta tai shiru na dan lokaci kafin ya sake maimata tambayarsa.
Muryarta yar firit tace"yaya ni ban fahimci tambayarka ba nidai nasan kai yayanmu ne"
Gyara zama yayi yana kusanta inda take,ya sake tsareta da kallo yace"awajen su sumy da salis da kubra ni yayankune mana amman kefa?"
Shiru tai yace"banson shiru magana zakiyi mun"
"Yaya nima ai yaya nane,kuma........kuma....mij......mijina"ta fada tana dukunkunewa waje guda.
Lumshe ido yayi yace "oky na fahimta meyasa baki daukan matsayin mijinki ba?"
Muryarta tayi raurau kadan ya rage tayi kuka tace"aa ba haka bane kayi hkr"
"Nace kinyi mun laifine?"
Girgiza kai tayi.
Jawota jikinsa yayi gefensa ya kwantar da kanta gefen kirjinsa,ya riko duka tafukan hannayenta ya cigaba da magana cikin sanyi da lallausan lafazi.
"Asmau!ni mijinki ne wanda igiyoyina uku suke bisa kanki,nasan kina da ilimi kinsan me ake nufi da miji,kinsan komi amman meyasa kike son sabama ubangiji meyasa kikeson yin abunda zai bata mun rai,har yau bakisona ne ko kuma har yau wancen yaron kike bukata arayuwarki"
Kukan datake rikewa ne ya kubce mata ta sake soke kanta kan kirjinsa tana kukan kasa kasa,hannunsa yasa bisa kanta yana shafawa.
Cikin kukan take cewa"kayi hakuri xuciyata ce ta rayamun komi,amman tunda muka rabu wlh bana maganarsa sai yau cikin tsotsai,kuma kaine ka tun xirani,ni babu wanda nake kulawa na haramtawa xuciyata tunanin wani."
Dora kanshi yayi bisa kanta cikin salo da jin wani sonta yace"har tunani bakiyi kenan,meyasa ni bazakiyi tunani ba saboda bakibani muhimmanci bane koko saboda baki sona"
Girgiza kanta take ahankali tana shashshekar kuka matseta yayi gaba dayanta jikinsa,kamar xai tsagata dago fuskarta yayi ta rufe ido hawaye share share,kuncinsa yasa yana goge kwalar yadda takejin yanyin da yasat dole tasa ta sake shigewa jikinsa ajiyar xuciya take saukewa.
Daker ya iyace mata "baki ban ansa ba"
Boye kanta tai tace"wallahi kana da muhimmanci wajena kuma ni bance bana sonka ba."
Jinjina kai yayi yace "kinson muringayin fada?"
Saurin girgiza kai tayi.
"Good,karna sakejin kin ambaci sunansa raina baya so,daga yau ana zaki ringa xuwa muna dinner kinaji ko"ta daga kai ya dagota sai sadda kai take yace "kindaina fushin dani ko?"
Daga kai tayi ta kasa hada ido dashi.
"To dago ki kalleni kimun wannan murmushin naki ingani"
Dukewa tai tana dariya kasa kasa "nidai zantafi bacci nakeji"
"Xoki hau gadona to,"
zaro ido tayi tai sauri mikewa.
Ya fixgota ta fado jikinsa ba zato taji ya cafke bakinta,saida numfashinta ya dauke na wucin gadi dagaske kissing dinta yake saida ya samu natsuwa sannan,babu bankwana ta ruga ta barshi nan yana mika.........yaushe rabonsa dajin wannan feelings din amman yayi alkawari bazai yi muamalar aure da ita gaban innaba sai sun tare gidansu.
Yajima yana daidaita kanshi kafin yaje ya samu inna yasan tana jiransa.

Wandon saiya fidda ainahin shef din kugunta,haka rigar kamar zata yage boobs din sufito,Allah yayi mata suffa mai matukar daukar hankali,ko mata insuka kalleta saudaya karawa suke balle da namiji,wani irin ruwan kyaune Allah yabata wanda baitsaya ga jikinta ba har fuskarta,wadda take doguwa mai dauke da sirirn hanci kamar biro,saika rantse bata shakar iska sosai saboda yadda hancin yake siriri kofofinsa kananu.
Idonta kuwa idan tana kallonka saika dauke ido saboda tsabar girmansu da haskensu, girarta sirirya itama bata da ciwo sosai bakinta kamar tea spoon kadai zai iya shiga cikinsa,yana dauke da fararen hakora masu azabar kyau.
Tana beauty point guda daya ga gefen kuncinta na dama,wanda yake masifar fidda ruwan kyanta.
Gashinta baida cikowa sosai kamar na yan babyn roba,Amma sai masifar tsawo zai kai tsakiyar bayanta.kasancewar

"Wow nayi kyau nasan jaded dina har janbakin nan saiya shanye idan yaga wannan kyan danayi,bari na kara kadan nasan tas zai shanyesa wajen kiss."
(😳wannan yarinya akwai yar iska)
Tubke gashin tayi tana sake line fuskar da soson powder dinta,rolling tayi da dark blue din siririn gyale saita kara yin kyau,jacket ta dauko wadda bata wuce guiwarta ba.

Saida ta kaiwa mai aikin dankwashi bisa kai.
"Shigiya may ya karki lasheni"
(🤣🤣🤣🤣)

Anty mami ke falon tana duba system sai deluwa na kasa zaune ana mata tausa Anty halima na gefe tana danna waya,jin saukowarta yasa suka maida hankali kanta.

 deeya ta iya tafiya mai daukar hankali,kuma tsabar gayune har hakan yani jikinta,idan tayi gayu tana tafiya kowa kallonta yake.

Cikin masifa Anty mamin tace"kiga shege jikin yarki ba dai deeyah ba,ke kullun bakinki bazai fadi alkairi akan yarinyar nan ba,wlh halima kincika bakin ciki kawai don kinga taki yar bata da gatan uba shikenan saiki tsangwami yar dan uwanki tirrr da halinki"

tatashi itama tabar falon.

Kwalla suka xubowa deluwa tana girgiza kanta saboda wani danasani daya shigeta,tanajin inama agogon baya yadawo ta goge duk abunda yafaru,kila daduk haka bata faru ba,Amma inaa bakin alkalami yariga ya bushe.

"Kaga bari na saukaka maka tafiya cikin taxi don wataran muna bukatar ka dawuri kuma kana iya makara,don haka anshi nan jeka da wannan motar ta waje na,na aiki driver yadauko wata gida kafin dawowar J asama maka accommodation da kuma motar xuwa wajen aiki,kamar yadda mukewa duk wani babban ma,aikaci."
"Banson gardama dakai baba zan ansa ngd sosai da kaunar da kakemun."

"Badamuwa,ka cancanta ai saidai akwai yar matsala kadan,dazun J yakirani hakika ya samu labarin fasawar auren deeyah da jadeed,yaci mun mutunci yayi mun kaskancin daya saba,yace injira dawowarsa sannan intabbatar kafin ya iso gobe na war ware auren dana daura mata dakoma waye baya so muddin yarsa bata so.hakika inajin tsoron abunda zai iya maka,Amman na rasa dalilin dayasa xuciyata bata son rabuwarka da deeyah."
Cikin damuwa yake fadar hakan.
"Baba nima hakan nakeji araina,bakuma don inasonta ba,don banma taba ganinta ba. tunda kaji haka akwai abunda Allah ya boye cikin wannan auren,don haka muddin kabani goyon bayan cigaba da rike igiyoyin aurenta tabbas ko sama da kasa zata hade bazan saketa ba,kuma zan jure duk wani kalubale daga shi J din."
"Khalipa hakika raina ya amunce dakai,kuma bana son rabuwarka da auren deeyah,inaji araina Allah ne ya kawo wanda zai gyara mata dabi,unta zai gyara tarbiyyarta,hakika deeyah bata da tarbiyya kuma inaganin kaine kadai zaka daidaitata,don haka mafita dayace karfe takwas na dare kazo kasameni zan baka address din gidan J,acan zaka taddani inson kadauki deeyah yau katai da ita gidanku.idan da hali ka kaita waje mai nisa inda bazai taba tunanin tana nan ba,amman idan ka hanata anfani da waya kowace irice balle kuma wata computer,da haka zakasha daga J sannan zaka cigaba da zuwa wajen aiki normal.kuma zan gabatar dakai matsayin sabon ma,aikaci.sannan zanbi duk hanyar da zanbi don ganin anturaka kaduna,don kayi nesa dashi insha Allah komi zayyi daidai."
Jinjina kai yayi yace"hakika baba shawara mai kyau kakawo,Amma ga gyara aciki baba.
Mai zai hana ka daga tarewarta har sadda J din zai saki jikinsa daga baya nida kaina zan dauketa batare da sanin kowa ba,kaikuma idan ya matsa maka akan neman wanda ka aura mata. kace tun jiya katura jami,an tsaro gidana sukamoni sunje nagudu annemeni anrasa,aman kabaza hotunana ko ina ana nemana,kaima saidaga baya kaga rashin dacewarmu da ita.kadai kwantar mashi da hankali ka raina mai wayau sai sun saki jiki zan sake gaya maka yadda zakai."
Kallon khalipan yayi cikin mamakin kaifin tunaninsa yace"tabbas shawararka tayi,kana da kaifin tunani shikenan naji dadin hakan kana iya tafiya komi kenan mun hadu gobe dare nayi."
Ya anshi key din motar yashiga yajata yabar company,fuskarsa cike da tsananin jin dadi,tuki yake cikin nishadi saboda samun nasara duk da abun bai tafi yadda suka tsaraba amma suna fata.
Yana can tunanin yadda abubuwan zasu kasance yaji anbankai ta baya har kanshi yana bugun sikiyarin motar tashi.
Don ma babu cinkoso titi sosai amman kadan ya hana shima bai bugi wanda ke gabansa ba,dagashi har wanda ya bankai din burki suka taka.

Cikin tsananin fusata yafito daga motar bayan yayi parking bakin titi,can ya hango motar itama tayi parking din,atare suka tunkari juna cikin fushi kowa da bala,in dake cinsa,Amman saiya hango wata budurwa tana nufoshi...............

Yashiga motarsa shima cikin fushi yabar wajen,lokacin har ten ta taho da ace sadda ya taho gida ya sauka da ya manta da yahuta,amman gashi wannan yar iskar yarinyar mai kama da tabarya ta bata masa lokaci.

"Asmau,anyi aurenmu lokacin da muduka bamaso,kuma hakan ta kasance bisa ga dalilan da suka faru.nasan kinji tsanata da haushina,saboda datse maki makaranta danayi.inaganin hakan shine daidai saboda matsalarki da wannan yaron,saigashi kuma lokaci guda Allah ya dasamun kaunarki marar iyaka,kisani duk abunda zai saki farin ciki zanyis bakin kokarina,haa duk abunda kikaga yafaru na akasarin farinciki daga gareni bada gangan hakan ta kasance ba,sannan kinsan  inada matsalolin danake son wararewa tsawon shekaru tun kafin ahaifekukeda sumy,don haka tun kafin auren mu akwai wata niyya cikin raina,wanda dagani sai inna muka san da wannan kudirin,mun boyene saboda lokacin kusani baiba,Amma yanzu dole zaki sani asmau saboda abun da nake da niyyar yariga ya afku,ina baki hkr banyi hakan da niyyar wulakanta ki ba,kema kuma idan kikaji dalilaina anan gaba zaki goya mun baya nasani,saboda asmau na mai fahimtace da bawa kowa uxiri."

Dagowa tayi daga kwanciyar datake cikin kirjinsa,don xuwa yanzu xuciyarta tafara duka akan abunda yakeson sanar da ita.

wata irin tasowa tayi daga jikinsa har tana bugewa loka,ba karamin rudewa maganar tasa ta ba,tuni idonta ya cika da kwallah bakinta na rawa.

"Inna wlh Asmau ta rikitani jiya,duk wasu kalamaina na juye mata na lallashi da kwantar da hankali amman yarinyar nan ta inda take shiga bata nan take fita ba,har mamakin yadda bakinta yabude yake xuba mani masifa,kuka share share haka tabar dakina.innanmu don Allah kishawo man kanta wlh bana son fushinta kuma kinsani banyi auren nan don na cuta mata ba,wlh jiya bacci na ragaggene."

"Kai kuma muddin baka bincika wadanda keda saka hannu cikin aikin nan ba wlh sahabi ba ruwana da dadewarmu da hannuna zan kasheka"
"Kayi hkr yallabai nafara aikin zan idasa kayi hkr nayi kuskure"
Tsaki yaja yana nuna masa kofar fita,yana tun tube yafice.
Khalipa ya saka Ear pies dinsa don yasan dole akwai magana bakin J wadda zaiyyi bayan fitar kowa,shiyasa yasaka Abunda zai jiyo masa komi,connecting yayi phone dinsa yana saurare,ya dora daya kan daya cikin motarsa.

Bayan ficewar sahabi yajiyo kan Alh Abbas cikin yanayin firgici da tsoratarwa yake nunasa da hannunsa,murya kasa kasa.
"Abbas nasan kasan komi,ka gaya mun idan da saka hannunka acikin bacewar yarinyar nan,karkaja nayi maka abunda zanyi nadama,tabbas idan nayi wani abu akanka dole zanyi nadama,Amma hakan bazai hana nadauki mataki akan kaba,muddin baka sanar dani mekake shirin yiba."
Numfashi yaja yana gyara kwalar rigarsa,kafin ya dago yace masa.

"Jafar kenan!kana tsarguwa aduk sadda wani abu ya tunkaro nadeeyah,why zargin ka baya wuee ni."

"Saboda bayan kai babu wanda yasan komi"

Murmushi yayi yace"kayi kuskure kau,dahar kake ganin haka karka manta nariga nayi rantsuwar bazan tona maka asiri ba,saidai inhar ni ban tona maka ba ba shakka Allah zaitona maka asiri,don hausawa sunce rana dubu ta barawo rana guda ta mai kaya.zan baka shawara ka dauke zarginka akaina kafara binciken mutanen ka wadanda suka san sirrinka bayan ni,don komi zai iya faruwa inuwarka ma saita cuceka ba abun mamaki bane."

"Abbas,waye ka ba auren y'ata?"

"Wani yarone ya ceceka yaceceni daga jin kunya,alokacin da Amininka yazo yake sanar mun da sun fasa auren nadeeya,yace zai aureta batare da sanin komi akanta ba,daga baya kuma naga rashin dacewar hakan natura akirashi akanemesa yagudu,bansan ma,anar gudun nashi ba Amma nasamu labarin garin dayaje,ashe bashi akebinsa za,a kaishi station yasa yagudu.don haka inakan aikin sa zaizo yabata sakinta don shidin badan kowa bane bakusan irinsa,saidai kasani ba kowane zai cika umurninka ba saidai idan kar nadeeyah tayi aure."

"Ka sa marwa kanka mafita,don akan auren nan nata zanbaka mamaki,kafin na dawo gareka ka tabbatar kasa moshi,inba haka ba zakabar aiki karkashin dukiyata."

"Dukiyarka jafar!"
Baban yafada yana kallon fuskarsa.

"Inbatau ba ta ubankace?Abbas kafita idona wlh kanacin darajar deluwa"

Jinjina kai kurin yayi bai sake magana ba.
J din ya juya yafita yabarsa nan,masu tsaronsa suka take mar baya,saida motocinsa kaf suka gama ficewa sannan yafito ya koma dakin taron.

"Khalipa da nayarda ka maidata gida,Amman yanzu bana son ka maidata,Akwai abubuwan da nakeson ka ansa hanunta wasu zaka samesu cikin jikkarta,Allah yasa baka barota wajen da akai musayar wutar nan ba,don inhar can kabarota akwai damuwa."

"Aa tana motata"

"Yawwa Zan hado maka sauran gobe nabaka,karkayi sakakin da har yan sanda xasu gane inda take,duk wata hanya dazasu gane karka barta."

"Amman baba Kai fa,kana iya shiga matsala"

"Karka damu dani,kaidai kayita kanka,kafin sadda zakubar garin.J yana da naci inhar yasaka ido kan abu saiyaga bayansa,kaine wanda naketa rokon Allah ya sanyo cikin rayuwarmu,Alhamdulillah kozan rasa komi ciki harda raina bazan bari J yacika burinsa na karshe ba.khalipa wannan auren naku yana cikin gagarumar hadari,akwai abubuwa dayawa acikin wannan lamarin,fatana wannan yarinyar tatsira kafin komi yabiyo baya. Don bayan J akwai wasu masu harin rayuwarta."

"Amma baba inace yarsa ce?"
Murmushi yayi yace"J akan kudi za,iya kashe uwarsa ma bama yarsa ba,shi kashe rai bakomi bane wajensa.ko bata mashi rai kayi zai saka a harbeka ko mota tabi ta kanka anwuce wajen,babu mai iya cewa dan me siyasar hannunsa take.kuma yana da kudi"

"Baba kaina ya kulle kabani haske"

"Akwai lokaci khalipa kaidai kufita cikin 
wannan track din,nasan kanajin yunwa kaje gida mun hadu gobe"

Yafita yabarshi jinjina kai kurin yake.
Yacire yar na urarsa daya makala ya sa aljihu,shima ya fice wajen motarsa.

Episode  .10

Muryarta da kuka tace"Anty nibansan yadda zan iya zama da kishiya ba,idan yar bala i cefa niban iya bala in ba,idan muguwace fa tazo ta nakastani"

Rungumeta tayi tana lallashi,
"Karkisa ma ranki  haka,kidauka babu mugun nufi akanta kuma idan ita ta kudurci cutarki ke kisama ranki zama da ita da xuciya daya,sannan kiringa adduar Allah ya kareki daga sharrinta,kuma nasan yaya bazai bari taringa cutarki ba.nasan da haka tunda mayucine idan bata girmeki ba,don haka kirike girmanki na uwargida,kibata nata girman daya kamata duk hakkinta ki sauke baruwanki da shiga abunda basu saki ba,idan kinjisu suna abunsu kija bakinki kiyi shiru,sai idan kinga zaiyyi ba daidai ba saiki ankarar dashi gudun karya yayi abunda zai jefa kanshi cikin sabon Allah,ko fushin sa."

"Maza tashi kije karya tai jira gyare fuskarki karkiyi masa kuka kinji ko,"

Haka ta lallasheta ta yafa gyalen rigar jikinta tafito falon,Anty mariya na janta goggo na rama mata,haka ta dauki abincin ta fita xuwa dakinsa.

Kasancewar magriba ta gabato shigar kananun kaya yayi marasa nauyi,yayi feshin turare yaja system tana tura bayanai xuwa office dinsu.
Wani mashahurin kamshi yaji tun kafin sallamarta,dole yasa ya dakata da aikin dayake ya xuba ma kofarsa ido,ahankali ta shigo cikin sanyinta tare da siririyar sallama.
Daker ya iya ansawa, yana ture system din ya iso yasa hannunsa yataryo tire din tare da riki hannunta,suka ajiye tare bisa table.
Yahado duka hannunta yajata suka zauna kusa da kusa,yadago habarta yana son kallon cikin idonta.

"Asmau na"

Dago idonta tayi da suka danyi ja kadan,tana kallon fuskarsa matsowa yayi sosai kamar zai rungumeta,yana saka nashi idon cikin nata cikin shaukin soyayya yaja numfashi.

"Kayi hkr raina ne yabaci"

Daga masa kai tayi.
"Yawwa amaryata,sauko ki xuba mun abincin yunwa nakeji tun safe fa bansa cin komi ba."

Saurin kallonsa tayi.
Yace"kinji tausayina ko?shiyasa naketa kokarin naga na daukeki mun tare gidanmu,yadda bazaki barni da yunwa da ko"
Kasa ansawa tayi ta fara xuba masa abincin yana tabinta dakallo yadda yau daya ta canza mai.
Data gifta kamshine yake nusar masa  hanci,har saiya ja jiyar xuciya.

"Naga ma"
Numfashi yaja yana kalonta.

"Zo ma,una"
Turo baki tayi don yasani tun tana yar yarinya bata son ma,u.

Murmushi yayi yana kai lomar abincin bakinsa,jawota yayi ya zaunar kan cinyarsa guda,ya debo yakai bakinta kallon shagwaba tayi masa ya lumshe ido yace"i like it cigaba damun don Allah"

"In anacin abinci shiru akeyi"
Sunba ya bata ga kuncinta yana shinshinar wuyanta cikin shauki yace"kibarni inyi santin canzawarki wlh kin kara kyau kice nima zanzo tayi mun"
Dariya yasata ba shiri bata shirya ba,ajiye spoon din yayi yana kallon yadda dariyar tayi matukar yi mat kyau,ruwa y kurba ya fesa mata ga fuska har ya sauka bisa wuyanta.

Kasancewar ruwan da sanyi saida ta ja ajiyar xuciya,lumshe ido yayi yasaka hannunsa yana shafo ruwan dake jikin fuskarta da wuyanta,a slow dauke numfashi tayi,don jitai kamar yana tafiya da numfashinta.

Jikinta banda kerma babu abunda yake,sakonsa yana shigarta haka tsoronsa yana kamata,muruarta bata fita sosai kamar zatai kuka.

"Please....ya....y..a"

"Shiiiit,kimun shiru zaki sake fushi da mijinki?"

Kanta na sauri ta girgiza masa shi tana kokarin ture hannunsa dake kokarin shiga cikin rigarta,muryarsa ta sauya saboda wani feeling daya taso masa.

"Oya tell me zaki sake bata fuska,zaki kara juya mun baya?idan nazo da magana zaki sake kin fahimtata."
Jin ya jura hannunsa inda taketa kokowar hanawa, yasata bashi ansa da karfi karfi........."wlh bazan sakeba na rantse kuwa plss kad..a.....i.....n...a"
Abun ne yana neman gigita ta yasa maganar daker ta fita.............daker ta kwace tana tashi zata ruga yayi saurin rikota idonsa kamar na dan maye saboda jaraba.

"Karki fita haka xo ga gyalenki kin bari."

Jikinta na kerma ta dan matso kadan tana mika hannu,kamar zatai kuka tace"yaya don Allah karka rikeni kabari na tafi,wlh na rantse bazan sake bata maka rai ba kayi hkr"

Dalalo ido tayi harda rufe baki,ta kalli kofa kowani yaji.
"Karufa man asiri wlh yaya ban iyawa"

"Okay give me a kiss"

Rarraba ido take ganin dagaske bazai kyaleta ba,tace"to karufe idonka"

Lumshe ido yayi yana jiran yaji labbanta masu saurin dauke masa wuta,saidai yaji tafece aguje.

Yabude idonsa da sauri dariya yayi yanajin wani annakirin sanyi ganin ya shawo kanta cikin sauki,da alamun zamansu zayyi dadi bata da dogon fushi,kanshi yake shafawa yana tuno abunda yayi yanaji kamar ta dawo ya cigaba............karar wayarsa ya maidoshi cikin natsuwarsa,sunan ANTY  yagani yayi saurin dauka.

"Zan gaya Mata inna insha Allah"

Yayi Mata sallama yatafi driver sa da yaba gyaran motarsa tuni yana waje yana jiransa.

"Nagode kasim,kana iya tafiya da waccen motar sai sadda nasake nemanka"

Sukai sallama yashiga motar ya nufi *AYSHER SPECIALIST HOSPITAL*
sunan asibitin kenan,wanda yake mallakine ga doctor AYSHER ASSADIQ MUSADDIQ yaya kwallin kwal ga *ABU KHALIPA*

Episode  .11

Fuskarsa yadda kasan bai taba dariya ba,babu annuri ko kadan akanta.
Yana gama parking yafito yadoshi cikin asbitin,word din da aka kaita ya nufa.

Kasancewar duk petien guda daki guda ake bashi,don kar ka takurawa wani marar lfiyar.
Yana shiga ya iske nurse na ta kowa da ita za,ai mata allura su biyu sun kasa riketa,kusan tare suka shigo da Anty dr din.

"Ku sakeni bana so nace banaso,ubanwa yace ana mun allura ni nace bana so,wlh saina gaya wa daddy na duk wanda ya sakaku wannan aikin shima saiya gane kurensa,kubarni natafi gida banaso Allah ya isana inkuna sake riken............wata mazga ya kaiwa bakinta iyakar karfinsa saida gefen lips dinta yafashe tsabar dukan dayayi mata.
Hawaye share share fuskarta take kallonsa,kafin ta fashe da kuka.

"Ka gani ko,tun bayan da muka samu ta dawo natsuwarta muke wahala da ita,duba kaga drip din da aka saka mata bata bari ya kare ba,tacire tana ta ihu mu kyaketa tatafi gida.Allurai biyu za,ai mata ansamu daxun anyi guda saura daya munfi awa da rabi ana kokowa da ita kan haka,duba nan kaga tea ne mai zafi na hado mata office dina tasha saboda ga magani nan yana jiranta,amman jifa tayi da cup din kaga yadda tea din ya bata tsakar dakin."

Kukanta kurin take tana shahshsheka.
"Ni nace maki inashan tea dinki ehy?nace bana so ni gida nakeson kubarni natai,kai kuma wlh saika dan dana kudarka karo na biyu yauma ka saka hannunka mai kama da kato ka dakeni,Allah ya is.............baiji ko dar ba yasake faska mata mari cikin fushi yace.

"Ubanwaye Sa,anki anan?ke fitsararra ko?zan koya maki hankali zanga karshen rashin kunyarki,sakara ballagaza.kafin na kirga uku wlh kitsaya ayi allurar nan inba haka ba"........ya cire belt dinsa yarike "na rantse da Allah mai kwatarki anan sai Allah,dukanki zan kamar Allah ya aikoni."

Idonta ya raina fata kuka ya tsaya saidai ruwan hawaye dake fitowa suna gangarowa har kan rigarta..........numfashinta yana wani ciccijewa tana kallonsa jitake duk duniya babu abunda takejin ta tsana sama dashi,jitake kamar ta saka bindiga ta harbeshi.wai miye ma ya hadota dashi,daga haduwar hanya shine zai nacewa rayuwarta haka.tana so tayi magana amman tsoro ya hanata,saidai hawaye.

"Bar kallona da idonki irin na mage"
ya Kalli Antyn yace "yi mata sonake mutafi."

Ansar sirinjin tayi hannun dayar nurse din tana kokarin bude inda zata tsira...........wata irin zabura tayi tana karkarwa bilhakki dagaskiya bata tsaya wa.

"Musaddiq inajin saika riketa,wlh bazata tsaya don kanta ba"

"Wai Saina riketa Anty?"
Yafada yana ya mutsa fuska kamar yaga kashi.

"Inba haka ba zata iyaja ayi mata rauni,kokuma ruwan Allura yaki shiga"

Kokadan baya son tabata kyankyaminta yake har ransa.

Kallonta yake kallon wulakanci ya matsa kusada ita cikin haushi ya tattarota ya mata wani jan riko,kamar wadda za,a yanka.
Saida dr tace ya sassauta mata,kuka take kasa rika don bata da abunda zatai taji saukin bakin cikin dake cinta illa kukan,magana kam bata yuwuwa.

Allurar na ratsata ta fasa kara tana kankameshi numfashinta kamar zai fita,idan kaga yadda take kukan saika dauka yankar namanta ake.
Yafincike jikinsa harda wani kakkabewa yana harararta.

"Ji yadda tayi mun da riga banza,rufe mana baki anan sai wari yake"

Dariya nurses din sukai suna ficewa,Antyn tayi dariya tace"banda sharri musaddiq"

Dunkulewa tayi waje guda tana shashsheka maryarta har ta dishe tana cewa.
"Dadd kaceci rayuwata zanmutu hannun wannan mutumen,mug.......kasa idasa sunan mugun tayi tace"baida imani kasheni zai,nashiga uku don Allah karabu dani kamaidani inda kasatoni konawa kakeso daddy zai baka nidai karabu dani."

"Oh satoki ma nayi ko?bacin ni da yanxu kina barzahu shashasha kawai,kuma babu inda zaki daga yau nine zan koya maki hankali"

"Wlh ni da kabarni na mutun yafiye mun wannan azabar da wulakancin naka,."

Yi yayi kamar zai kai mata mazga yace"da me zakije wa Allah,rashin kunyar koko rashin tarbiyyar sakarai"


"Musaddiq ban sanka da zafi haka ba,kabita asannu ba laifinta bane haka aka koya mata,bata san girman babba ba saika sa hkr aranka ka taushi duk wani zafi dake ranka kafin ka iya daidaita ta."

"Anty raina kuna yake idan na tuna yadda Ammaa tabamu tarbiyya ta iya bawa yaro kulawa,take iya nuna masa daidai da ba daidai ba,Amman yau anwayi gari............"

"Ya isa kodan wannan saka sama xuciyarka ruwan sanyi,ba ita taimaka ba don haka kar zafin wasu yasa kana hucewa kanta,wannan zafin baisa ta canza zaka koya mata tsanarka."

Baison maganganun sucigaba agaban deeyah shiyasa yace "bari muwuce dare nayi"

"Allah yabata lfy akula da lafiyarta kana gani numfashin bai ida daidai ta ba,banda ruwan sanyi kasan dai sharuddan"

"Sauko mije"

"Don Allah dr kice ya kyaleni bazan bisa ba narantse bazan bisa ba,gara naita zama anan har inmutu."

Dr tace mata cikin lallashi "kinga kanwata babu abunda zai maki inhar kika daina gardama,gidan daddynki zai maidaki nina gaya maki inkuma kinfison yayita mazgar bakinki toni ba ruwana"

Ido ta kwalala mashi fuska duk hawaye ya bushe sai shashsheka take,ta yamutsa fuska tana dafe kirjinta.

"Dr kirjina ciwo yake inajin numfashina kamar zai tafi"

"Ga magani nan,kuma kiringa ajiye inhaler ki akusa ki kiyaye abunda zai jefaki cikin irin wannan yanayin."

Ahankali take ziro da kafafunta farare kal kamar na sabon jinjiri,bin kafafun yake da kallo xuwa fuskarta,yaja tsaki yana kauda kai canciki-ciki yace"kyan dan maciji"

Ganin tayi gaba karta yi wani wajen yasa yayi saurin binta yana ma Antynsa sallama.

"Kee ina zaki ga motar nan,bude kishiga"

Jitake kamar ta fallamai mari.
Amman babu dama dole ta bude kofar gaba ta zauna,tana yamutsa fuska ko ina jacket dinta da jikkarta,halan yazaci akwai kudi masu yawa yasa ya sace ta.
Barawo kawai duka one hundred thousand ne yaje yayita kaya,inhar zai maidani ga daddy nasan ya dawo yana ta nemana Allah sarki inada labaran da zan bashi masu yawa.........tunaninta ya kare sadda ya shigo yana jifarta sa jikkarta ga fuska,runtse ido tayi tana luliya goshinta inda yajefa,kasa shiru tayi cikin muryarta kamar zatai kuka tace.

"Meyasa komi naka irin na mugayene?koko kaina kawai kake gwada muguntar,wai kasan babana agarin nan kuwa?"

"Kin fadi gaskiya akanki kawai nake gwadawa,kuma bazan fasa ba har sai kin shiga taitayinki,babaki kuma bashine wannan kasurgumin barawon ba,wannan dan ta addan da ake nema ruwa jallo"

Zaro idonta tayi cikin mamakin yadda yake zagin daddy babu ko tsoro da shakkan hakan.
"Wlh karyane daddy ba barawo bane,daddy ba dan ta adda bane,kaine dai kake kama dasu."

Ta fada tana lafewa jikin kofa din tasan zai ita maketa.

"Ai banda tsantsagwaron rashin kunya da rashin tarbiyya baki iya komi ba,shiyasa naga yadace na koya maki hankali,yadda ko motsina kikaji jikinki zai dauki masassara wata kila har fitsari saikinyi."

Dariya tahauyi babu kakkautawa harda rike ciki,don maganarsa ta bata dariya jinjina kai kurin yake ganin yadda ta maida abun tatsuniya,murmushin mugunta yayi ya fizgi motar da karfi,kanta yabada gamm jikin taga.

Daga dariya takoma kuka.
"Banda kuka baki iya komi ba shashasha"

"Kadaina zagina wai mena maka,ban sanka ba bana fatan na sanka daga haduwar hanya,kayiwa Allah da iyayenka ka saukeni wai ina kake tafiya garin zaka bari dani,wayyo Allah nashiga uku..........."shiru tayi tana gunjin kuka ganin kwalbar lemu ya rarumo zai maka mata.

Har suka iso janbulo bata sake magana ba sai hawaye,shikenan tayi bakwana da rayuwar jin dadi,tayi bakwana da duk wani gata datake samu,hawayen dai taketa xubarwa don ta gaji da fiddo kukan numfashinta,yana neman daukewa...........saida suka shigo cikin get din maigadin gidan ya rufe sannan yazo yana bude masu kofa,tare dayi masu sannu daxuwa.

Togewa tai bakin kofar shiga da zata sadaka da waiting falo kafin ka isa main falon.
"Idan na iso nan bazakiji dadi ba,wlh karkija duk da baki lfy na jibgeki son raina"

Hawaye take ta hada hannuwanta waje guda alamun roko,tana kukan tace"na rokeka kabarni natafi,mekakeso ne tare dani,idan kudi kake son abaka wlh insunkai miliyan dari zan baka,Amman ka rabu da rayuwata............ta karashe tana kuka duk rashin imanin ka dole ka tausaya mata,idan kaga halin data shiga.
Kauda kai yayi don kar tausayinta yasa takasa yin abunda ya kamata........... "Bana bukatar komi tattare dake sai kedin,ke kadanki nakeso don haka koda yardarki ko babu dole zaki bi umurnina"

Tana kukan ta xube kasa bisa guiyawunta tace"idan abunda kakeso ajikina yake kazo ka dauka kayi abunda zakai inhar zaka rabu dani,wlh bana sonka wlh jinake kamar na kashe ka da zan samu dama,menayi maka zaka shigo rayuwata ina cikin gatana da mulkina da isata zaka raboni dasu,kakawoni duniyarka kai wane irin azzalumine"

Idonsa yarine da bacin rai cikin takunsa na namiji ya isa inda take ya finciketa yatsayar a tsaye cikin muryarsa mai amon fushi yace.

"Bakida abunda zan so ajikinki,baki cikin jerin matan da zanji ina bukatar jikinsu,inbaki sani ba kisani ni kyamarki nake,duk sadda hannuna yakai jikinki jinake kamar na gutilesa na jefar,nima bana sonki bana kaunarki saidai hakan bazai hana ni na cikasa burina ba,nan dakike gani shine kurkukinki daga yau xuwa randa zan canza maki waje,kidauka wani gatanki,wani mulkinki wani isarki duk cikin bacci suka faru,domin kinyi ban kwana dasu anan kece zaki zamewa kanki gatan da isar dakomi ma."
Ya nuna mata hanya cikin fushi don idan tasake masa gardama Allah kadai yasan me zai mata.
Tana layi da kuka kamar ranta zaifita,shikenan mafarkinta ya tabbata tafi shekara biyar tana mafarkin irin wannan sabuwar rayuwar,mutumen da take kasa ganin fuskarsa gashi gabanta yana tabbatar mata da abunda take gani cikin baccinta,tabbas tun daga randa ya kafta mata mari mafarkinta yafara dawo mata,gashi abubuwan suna sake tsananta..............binsa take kamar zata kifa ta na girgiza kai shikenan shikenan rayuwarra takare,domin wannan sabuwar dazata fara bata cikin rayuwar datake buri........gata karkashin wani wanda yake niyyar zama dodo agareta.

Babban falone da komi aciki yasha kayan alatu daidai misali,akwai hanya biyu dazata kaika bedrooms suna kallon juna.
Sai hanyar kitchen da store ahade,sai dining room daga hannun hagu,wata mata suka tadda da yarta suna ta kallo,gaidashi sukai cikin mutuntawa wanda da alamun sun jima da sanin junan su,dirshen deeyah ta xube kasa tana cigaba da shashshekarta.

"Babah ga yarinyar dazaku zauna tare nan na dan wani lokaci,kisamun ido kanta kinsan saka idon danake nufi,koda kofar fita backyard ne ban yarda ba.haka zalika iyakarki dafa mata abinci a ruwanki da gyaran dakinta kowani toilet dinta,haka wanki koda hankchif ban lamunce kiyi mata ba.sannan koda wasa karki bari wayarki taje hannunta,mistakly karki bari tayi anfani da waya koda babu saninki,kisaka ido sosai.komi kikaga yadace nasani ki kirani zanzo cikin lokaci."

"To yaro zankula insha Allah,Amma don Allah kabata baki kafin katafi kabarta damu ahaka."

Baice komi ba ya mike yana mata alamun tataso,bata wani zabi daya wuce binsa amman iyakar kunci yau tana ciki yau rayuwar ta juya mata baya,data san da haka mezai kaita fitowa tunda daman tayi sati bataje aikin ba,sai yau da tsotsai ya ritsa da ita.

Wani dakine maikyau yasha gyara ga gado nan da kujeru guda biyu,two seater da one seater akwai toilet da kuma wata yar bagala da zaka hangi backyard ta cikin glass din tagar wajen,akwai malelen kafet da miroww da wadrop,acikin kewayen kuma akwai komin wanka da masan fulotion akwai shawer,da heater ga kuma washing machine.
Dakin yayi sosai amman ahaka bai kamo kafar dakinta na gidan daddy ba,ya zatai da fadan dayafi karfinta.

"Nasan kin raina wajen,Amma ahaka zaki rayu ciki har sanda zan sake canza maki wani kila ma baikai wannan ba,idan kika cigaba da kukan ciwonki ya tashi wannan kekika sani,don ba zama zanyi nayita gadinki ba,idan kinyiwa kanki kiyamullaili kin hkr to,kin zauna lfy inkuma kinganin kuka da ihu da haukanki zai fiddaki ga fili gamai doki."

Ya juya zaifita bin bayansa tayida kallo wanda takejin inama da wuka saidai yaji ta soka mashi ta baya,Amma bata samu wannan damar ba kota samu bata iya kisankai.............."kai ko ajikinka zalincin da kakemun,dagaske bakajin komi kuntata rayuwata da kayi.zaka tai kayi baccin ka cikin kwanciyar hankali,yayin da ni kajefani cikin halin da bazan iya baccin ba.kayi mun adalci kenan,menatare maka"

Taxube kasa tana saka kuka mai tsuma xuciya,kasa magana yayi do hakika yaji wani iri Amma ya zaiyyi dole hakan shine daidai...............ficewa yayi yana jan kofar yana jiyo kukanta,amman ya runtse ido ya bargidan.

✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨

Gari ya rude da neman shakele,yan sanda an hana su saket tunda rana suke abu guda har duhun dare ya kama,duk inda hankalin J yake yayi masifar tashi,ya gigice yafita natsuwarsa.
Tun yana daukar abun ba komi ba har zama yazo yana niyyar gagararsa,kaf gidan babuwanda bai tadama hankali ba.

Deluwa kuka riris takeyi don ita kadai tasan me takewa kukan,tabbas bacewar yarinyar akwai rikitarwa kuma ta shiga damuwa sosai,saidai akwai abunda ya fi wannan damuwa agareta...............nadama take tafi cikon buhu,tasan kuwa wannan nadamar taringayi kenan har illa masha Allah.

"Yallabai nayi iyakar kokarina wajen aikin nan,kayiwa Allah kajikaina karka koreni,tanan hanyar naked samu nake ci dasha da iyalina,nake daukar dawainiyar iyayena,kannena duk yan matane ga hidimar karatunsu,don Allah yalabai ka daga mun kafa wlh kuskurene,lokacin munje taroka hakan yafaru tuba nake yallabai,zan dauki duk hukuncinka Amman kayi mun adalci karka rabani da abincina"

Wasu irin muxurai J keyi yana huci kamar kasa,wani mugun halbi ya kaimashi da kafarsa ga ruwan cikinsa,bacin yasa hannu babu makawa sahabi saiya tafi asibiti,idan ma baiyi masa wata illar ba.

"Nizaka tonawa asiri,nizaka fallasa taya kake ganin zan kyaleka bayan babu yata,babu wadanda sukai silar bacewarta sanan kagaya mun na kyaleka!lallai sahabi kana dauka na da wasa."

Alh Abbas ne yayi sallama yashigo,J idonsa kamar zai fado kasa saboda kallon yayan nashi,kamar jira yake ya iso ya cakumi wuyan rigarsa............."wlh kunci karya kun kwana da yunwa,daga kai har wannan sahabin mekuka maidani kuna ganin kamar bazan iya komi akanku ba ko?uhmmmmm kun mance *JAFAR MANI DAULA* ko?na rantse da Allah bazan kyaleku ba muddin babu shalele kai kuma baka kaddamar mani da dan iskan daka ba wa auren ta ba."

Deluwa na kuka Anty halima na taya ta tace"jafaru!jafaru!jafaru!ko mutuwa tana kunyar idon mahaifi,meyasa kullum dabiunka basu canzawa kullun sake lalacewa kake,dan uwanka ne fa yayan kane yariga xuwa duniya shekara biyar ya baka,kodan darajata bazaka sassautawa Abbas ba.wahala yake dakai tun shekara da shekaru,tunda kazo duniya yake wahala dakai ace har yanzu shekararsa hamsin da biyar bai huta ba,kaikuma baka raga masa ba."

Ingiza baba abas yayi saida yatafi kamar zai fadi kafin yasamu nasarar tsayawa.

Cikin hauka hauka da masifa ya doshi inda uwar tashi take.
"Deluwa me kike cewa?dabi,una suna sake lalacewa?kin manta kekika lalatani,shin kin manta kekika koya mun rashin ganin girmansa,wai ko tsufa yasa kin manta?toni ban manta ba.mutuwa kikace tana kunyar idon mahaifi ni ai ba mutuwar bace,don me zanji kunyarki wai ko kin manta sadda kike tayani wulakanta shi,saboda baida ko sisi shine yanzu akan dukiyata kike fadar na raga masa!uhmmmm deluwa!deluwa!deluwa!bari kiji tokoke bazan ragama wa ba balle shi,do haka kozan  yi yaga yaga da namansa baki isa kidagatar dani ba."

Ya juya yabar wajen kukan suka cigaba dayi ita da halima yana xuwa gaban baba Abbas din shikuma ya fiddo masa takadda daga aljihunsa.

"Wannan ta ajiye aikina ce,wannan kuma takaddar sakin yarka ce yaron mota ta kadesa ban saniba ko kaine kasaka akai masa haka,daman kuma ya rubuto da niyyar xuwa ya kawo man don haka kana iya kamani da wani laifin banda wannan"

"Abbas!kayi abunda ya dace Amman kasan ai ba companyna kadai kake zaune akai ba."

Murmushi yayi yace"nasani ai shiyasa nazo don na zayyana maka har gidan da motoci dakomi naka na ajiye maka naba maigafi mukullin gidan,saduwar alkairi nagama nawa aikin"

Ganin dagaske Abbas din tafiya zaiyyi yasa yafara kiransa.
"Kaga Abbas dawo don Allah kadawo mudaidaita,karka tafi ke deluwa kiyi masa magana idan yatafi wlh company nina yashiga uku,Abas!!!"

Amma ko waige bai waigo ba,deluwa tatashi tana kuka tabar wajen halima ta kira ikey tace ta dauko masu jikkunan su zasubi yaya Abbas suma basuga wajen zama ba....................

Episode  .12

Khalipa yaba baba Abas wannan shawarar na ya rubuta ta kaddar ajiye aikinsa,ya kuma bargidan sa dayake ciki da duk wasu abubuwa da suke karkashinsa.
Domin yin hakan zai karya xuciyar J din ya daina yimasa barazanar dayake masa.

Kuma hakan yayi rana don kuwa J haukacewa yayi yafita daga hayyacinsa,aguje yabi bayan yayansa yana kwala masa kira lokacin har yashiga taxi zasu fita yasa aka rufe get din.har wata shashsheka yake yaje ya bude inda baba Abas din yake zaune yasha mur.
"Fito dan uwana fito muyi mgana wallahi duk abunda nace xan wasa nake,kurin dai don na baka tsoro idan kasan inda nadeeya ta take kayi saurin gaya mun,Amman badan ina nufin zan aikata wani abu akanka ba.idan kabarni ai nashiga uku kayiwa Allah kadawo gareni,yo inkabarni ai nariga na gama lalacewa."

Abunda yaketa fada kenan yana jawo hannun baba Abas din xuwa waje.

"Jafar duok wannan wasan kwaikwaiyon naka bazai canza mun ra ayi na ba,tsawon lokaci yaci ace kamar yanzu na dogara da kaina,kuma ina tsaye da kafafuna ba kafafunka ba,tunda gashi har gobe kana nan kan ra,ayinka na rikau duk sanda nai abu kayi mun gori,kaci mutunci na kace na ajiye maka duk abunda na mallaka karkashinka,lokaci yayi dazan barka dakomin ka."

Arikice yace"aa......kaga Dan uwana na rantse maka bazan sake yunkuri aikata maka haka ba,wlh kankalina ne yayi masifar tashi akan bacewar nadeeya,tunanina tunda kai kadai kasan komi kasan kudirina kaine zakasan masaniyar yadda akai,saina ga idan banyi maka barazanar ba bazaka bani bayanin inda deeyah take ba"

Tabe baki baba Abas yayi yace"inso kake kasan wanda ya farauci yarka to kaje kabin ciki wadanda suka san sirrinka,na gaya maka wannan ba tun yanzu ba,kuma ni bazan tona maka asiri ba Amma ajuri xuwa rahi watarana tulu zai fashe."

"Naji naji tulun zai fashe Abas,Amma kafin yafashe saina kaddamar da burina,alkawarina ne wannan kuma duk inda shalele ta shiga bazanyi kasa aguiwa ba saina nemota,kuma zanyi binciken masuyi mun zagon kasa tabbas minister Abdulkadir shaila na daya daga cikinsu,Amman bana tunanin zai iya cin amanata."

Tsaki baba Abas yayi yace"bayan sa ma akwai wasu,har wanda baka tunani don haka ni nayi nan"

"Don Allah Abas ka koma gidanka,wlh ni nariga na mallaka maka shi halak malak,kaho da takaddun gidan zan saka maka hannu shedar mallaka,sannan suma motocin na baka kadawo company ya na bukatarka,kataimaka."

Murmushi yayi cikin ransa yana kara jinjina kaifin basira irin kalipa,daga kafada yayi yace"zanyi tunani xuwa safe."

"Yawwa nagode dan uwana ngd insha Allah bazan sake maka abunda nai ba,kukuma sai ku koma inda kuka fito ko"

Yacewa halima da yarta.
"Aa zamubisa can muzauna tare dashi"

Tsawa ya daka mata "nace babu inda zaku kukoma kafin raina yabaci"

Aguje suka koma ba haka suka so ba,saboda wulakancin yayi yawa dole zata san hanyar barin gidan,koba yanxu ba.

Kallon sahabi yayi da gardawansa sukai masa wani jan riko kamar barawo.

"Ku sake shi,inhar kana son na kyaleka sahabi to na baka kwana uku ka binciko mun duk mai hannu acikin wannan shirin,inba haka ba zaka fuskanci hukunci."

Godiya yayi kamar zai shide kafin yasaka takalminsa ya ranta cikin nakare.


💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕

Lefen Auta duk wanda yagani sai yaba,don kuwa kayane na kece raini akaxuba cikin akwatunan,masu masifar kyau da daukar hankali.........mutane duk wanda yazo ya gani saiya yaba ita kanta ta yarda da irin kaunar da yayansu yake mata,don kuwa ba kananan kudi ya narkar akan hidimar lefen ba,ga kudaden dayake kashewa duk rana wajen xuwa gyaran jikinta,hakanan zai basu kudi yace ko zasu bukaci wani abu.
Kasancewar yaa cikin hidimar auren Nura abokinsa bai samu zaman daya leka wajen deeyah ba,duk da yana waya da babah babu wata matsala.

Acan company kuma Baba abas ya koma kamar yadda sukai da khalipan,ankuma saka sunan kalipan na farko cikin wadanda zasu je babbar masana anatarsa ta mai dake kaduna. Nan da kwana biyar zasu tattara sukoma can,hakan yayi masa matukar dadi don lokacin yayi daidai da sadda za,a kai mashi amaryar sa...........duk wanda yasan sa yasan yana cikin farin ciki domin kuwa abubuwa suna xuwar masa yadda yadace.
Tsakaninsa da Asmau kuwa abun ba,acewa komi wata irin kauna da soyayya suke gudanarwa,har take mamakin yadda ta saki jiki dashi ta bude masa xuciyarta,har mantawa take da wani batun aurensa,saboda ko kadan bata ga yana waya da wata ba,kuma bata ga alamar yana xuwa ba.hakan yakan bata natsuwa duk da tana ganin kamar ba adalci bane,kasancewar bai mata maganar ba yasa itama takasa mashi maganar.
Hidima ake sosai don inna har yinin bikinta tayi da yan uwanta na an kanon dana yola,har yan wasu dag cikin yan uwan baba sunxo suma,akai shagalin dasu.

Ana gobe tafiyarsu kadunan baba abas yakirasa awaya,lokacin suna zaune da asmau suna cin abincin dare,don yanzu kusan kullum suna tare adaidai irin wannan lokacin,saitayi dagaske take guduwa cikin gida,don idan tana biye masa anan dakinsa zasuci amarcin.

"Yaya alfarma nake nema wajenka don Allah"
Kanshi yana jikin wuyanta yana shakar masifeffen kamshinta yana lumshe ido yace.
"Kifadi duk abunda kikeso zan maki inhar ina da damar hakan"

Lamo tayi jikinsa tana shafa sumar sa tare da yimasa susa cikin gashin nashi.
Take magana cikin sanyi da natsuwa.
"Yaya nasan nabata rawata da tsalle awancen lokacin danake makaranta,kuma bakomi yajawo hakan ba illa makauniyar soyayyar Abdulbasir,wlh yaya duk abunda akafada awancen lokacin karyane,abunda kurin nasan Abdul ya taba yimun shine kiss,shima din ga goshina kuma kuskurene saida na nuna masa bacin raina,duk rade raden da ake akansa na kasa yarda saboda sonsa danake,karshe ta dalilin anganni amotarsa yana samun zobe akaso yimun kazafi,kaida baba kuka hau sama kuka kasa yarda dani"
Ta fada tana kuka ahankali saboda tuno irin ciwon da taji acikin ranta sadda abun yafaru.
"Sorry my love,hakika nasan nabiyewa baba munsaki cikin kunci,wanda duk nine nabada goyon bayan hakan saboda natsani yaron inason arabaki dashi,har maganar makarantarki nine nace ya dakatar dake,bakuma don na cuta maki ba saidon kikoyi hankali nan gaba kigyara kuskurenki.ashe tun lokacin ma kishin ki nake ban sani ba naketa haukan banza,sorry kinji yar asama ta"
Share Mata kwallar yayi yace"komi ya wuce atarigaba,wace alfarma kike nema wajena"?

Ta sake lafewa cikin kirjinsa tana jin sabon sonsa tace"yaya ka maidani makaranta don Allah katai maka mun wlh inson karatuna"

Ajiyar xuciya ya sauke yana gyara mata zama bisa cinyarsa ya tallabo habarta yana kallon cikin idonta.
"Asmau kin yarda da mijinki?"

Daga kanta tayi tana shagwabe fuskarta.

"Da kyau matar kirki,abunda nakeso dake shine ki tattara batun karatunki zuwa wani lokaci,nima zanso ace uwar y'ay'ana tayi karatu,amma yanzu akwai abubuwa da yawa kiban lokaci har sadda zan samu natsuwa da kaina zanzo maki da admission."

Cikin farin ciki tace"dagaske kake yaya?"
Lumshe mata ido yayi alamun tabbatarwa.

Rungumesa tayi tana murna shima ya matseta yana mata cakulkuli,tana dariya da rokon yadaina yanacewa bazai bari ba,cikin wannan nishadin wayarsa tashigo.

Yana rungume da ita ya sunbaci lips dinta yace"shiiit zanyi waya da ogana"

Tayi lamo kamar yar mage tana saurarensa.

"Baba ina wuni"

"Lafiya lau khalipa ya kokarinka,ya hidima kuma"

"Komi Alhamdulillah,fatan lfy daga wajenka"

"Ehy komi daidai,J yaje abuja kasan har yanzu cikin binciken inda nadeeya take yake,don haka akwai wasu takaddu danace zan dauko maka kahada da wadanda ka dauka cikin jikkarta,sune nace kazo muhadu saina baka,sannan inason nayi magana da nadeeyah sonake tasan matsayinka ayau"

"Shijenan baba ganinan xuwa ina zan sameka"

"Dake nake don ubanki,waye dan iskan."
