"Na tuba wlh badakai nake ba,don Allah karka dakeni kar kayi mun tsawar bazan sake ba"

Idonsa akanta yana mata kallon wulakanci yace"stand up"
Ahanxar ce tatashi ta manta daga ita sai bra da pant,wani mugun kallo yabi jikinta dashi yaja tsaki,.
"Dauki kayanki kisa kizo kiyi salla nasan baki santa ba,don ba koya maki akeba"

"Laaa sallar ce banayi,wlh inayi"

Prayer mate ya shimfida can gefe ya tada sallarsa yabarta nan.
Shafa,i da wutri yayi xuwa lokacin ta gama shirya wa sai takejinta cikin natsuwa,iska tana ratsata lumshe ido tayi tana jin wani bacci bacci.

"Taso kiyi sallar duk da ake binki yau,ga hijab nan kigama magunguna na jiranki."

Har sauri take wajen daga mashi kai.
Sallar tafara yana xuba mata ido yaga yadda zatayi ta.mamaki kasheshi yayi ganin babu kuskure cikin sallarta,kamar tv haka ya maidata iyakar kirgarsa sallah hudu ta rama banda asubar yau.
Jinjina kai yayi yanajin dole yayi mata tambayoyi amma ba yanzu ba.

Tajima tana azkar din gama salla kafin ta ajiye hijab din kan kujera.
Zare mata ido yayi yace ta linke ta.

"Kayi hkr wlh ban iya linkewar ba"

Ansa yayi ya linke yabata yace ta sake irinsa,ahankali ta warware yadda yayi sai tabi ta maida.

"Good,muje"
Tabisa bakin gado ta zauna ya ballo magungunan ya miko mata ragowar lemunsa yabata.
"Kixuba saiki bude bakin nasaka maki maganin,idan kika sake kikai mun amai wlh allura zan kaiki asibiti aimaki."
Saurin girgiza kai tayi"Aa bazan aman ba kayi hkr."

Saida taxuba lemun yace ta bude bakin tadaga kan sama tabude yazuba mata duka ukun,zaro ido tayi yace "maza hadesa ko indade maki hanci"

Kamar zatayi amai haka ta daure ta hadiye,Amma jitake kamar ga makoshinta suka tsaya,itafa agida jikawa ake abata idanma yazama dole saitasha din.

Yajawo hannunta mai carnula yace"waye yace kisa mata ruwa"

"Kayi hkr hannun guda ne ban iyawa dashi shikadai."

Yajona drip din yana jan yar tayar yadda zai ringa digowa ahankali.

"Oya sleep"

Tasilale takwanta taja bargo yarufeta har bakin wuyanta,yaje yayi switching din wutar dakin,ya kunna dumlight.
Rigarsa yacire ya rataye ya dauko blenked cikin wadrop yazo yahau kujera doguwa ya kwanta,yana duba wayarsa yaga karfe daya na dare,text yayi wa Nura da drivern sa cewar karfe bakwai su hadu agidan Inna,yana son sutai gaba daya.

Yayi adduar sa ya juya ya kwanta daman baccin yakeji sosai.

Episode  .14

Karfe uku ya farka ya cire mata drip din ganin kanta ya langabe ya gyara mata shi,motsin dataji anyi da ita yasa ta cafke hannunsa ta rike kam,kafin yaji tana magana ahankali.

"Ke wacece?kullin kina xuwa cikin baccina meyasa kwana nan kika bari,kinsan halin dana shiga kuwa ina cikin kuncin rayuwa,duniyar ta juya mun baya gareki nake samun wasu abubuwa danake jin sassauci cikin raina,meyasa kika daina koya mun karatu"

Idanu ya xuba mata cike da madaukakin mamaki,kafin ya fita cikinsa yaji ta cigaba da maganar tata ahankali.

"Ai nace maki nai alkawarin bazan fadawa kowa ba,Amman ki gaya mun yadda zan yi na rabu da wannan mutumen,baya kaunata ya takurawa rayuwata cutata yakeyi"
Tayi shiru irin dai saureren abunda ake sanar da ita take,kafin ta yamutsa fuska tace"ban taba kin yarda da abunda kika fada mun ba,Amma tayaya wannan mutumen zai kawo haske arayuwata,bana cikin duhu komi nake nema nasamu ilimin addini kece kika tsaya mun na ke kan iyawa,amman bazan gardama dake ba zanyi hkr kamar yadda kikace,inga iyakar gudun ruwansa.don Allah kicigaba da biya mun karatun,yaushe zakixo to"

Gaza tsayuwa yayi ya don arikice yake ainun,ya zauna gefenta wajen kanta,har sannan hannunsa damke cikin nata.
Sake yamutsa fuska tayi kamar zata rushe da kuka,tace

"Wannan din zai koya mun karatu,don Allah karki tafi kibarni cikin tunani,kece abokiyar firata kekike warware mun duk abunda ban sani ba,yanzu idan kika bar xuwa yazanyi shifa banda cin zalina bai iya komi ba,kuma ya za,ai zaman mu yadore bacin banda alaka dashi,kinji don Allah karki tafi........meeeeee ki fahimtar dani karki tafi naji zan daina nai maki alkawarin haka,amman kidawo gareni............kuka tasa wanda yayi daidai da tashinta zaune cikin sauri,tana karanta.ayatul kursiyyu tare da la,ilaha ilallahu wahdahu lasharika lahu lahul mulku walahul hadu wahula ala kulli shai,in kadir.
Kuka ta saka tana makalkale hannunsa "karki tai bazan ji dadi ba inkika barni inasonki duk da bansan ko wacece ke ba............

"Pretty..........ohhh yabugi kanshi alamun dai sunan yana xuwa batare da ya shirya ba.

Jin sunan daya kirata yasa tai xunbur ta dago da idonta jawur tana kallonsa,bakinta har rawa yake tace"mekace?don Allah mekace kamar wani suna ka fada yanxun nan"

"Aa magana nake maki bakiji ba,mafarki kikeyi ne naji kamar kina kuka yasa nazo nan na tsaya"

"Kaji menake cewa?"

Girgiza kai yayi yace"aa naji dai kamar kina magana Amman banji komi ba,wacece kikai mafarkin da ita harkike kukan karta tafi tabarki"

Girgiza kai tayi tana share hawayenta "ban santa ba nima,kuma bata gaya mun sunan ta ba tace karna gaya ma kowa abunda yake faruwa,Amman kai tace ingaya ma meyasa kai zan gaya maka banda hadin komi dakai,Amma tana sake jaddadamun kaidin jigon rayuwata ne,me hakan yake nufi?tana gaya mun lokacin daina xuwa wajena yayi tunda rayuwata ta shigo hannunka,don Allah kai waye meyasa ka saceni daga wajen babana?meyasa take alakantani dakai tun kafin na hadu dakai take gaya mun akwai lokacin da zaizo rayuwata zata canza,akwai wanda zan hadu dashi da cainxa komi nawa?dama kaine?"
Tafada tana kuka ahankali,shi kanshi kwallar ta cika idonsa domin hakika wanan lamarin daga ubangijine,ba shirinsa bane shirin Allah ne.yanayin dayakeji kamar yajawota ya rungume saboda wani abu dayake tunawa.........Amma  lokacin baiba da saura.

Hannunta yariko duka cikin kwantar da hankali yace"Tun tsawon yaushe kuke tare da ita?"

Tana jan majina gashinta duk ya hargitse ya rufe mata fuska,yasa hannu ahankali yana gyara mata.

"Bazan manta ba tun bayan dana gama secondary school dina?"

Jinjina kai yayi yace "metake gaya maki"

"Bata Fara gaya mun wasu abubuwa ba sai cikin dan lokacin nan,baifi shekara biyu dasuka wuce ba.amman sauran lokutan karatune take koya mun,Ahlari tauhid, tajwid,huruf,da qur,ani tunda muka hadu dakai bata sake xuwa ba sai yau."

Jinjina kai kurin yake yace"hizif dinki nawa yanxu?"

"Talatin da biyar,mun gama sauran littatafan shine take cewa ta gama nata kaine zaka dorani akai,ni kuma bana son naka nata nakeso,"

Murmushi yayi ya kalli agogo uku da rabi da minty goma.
"Tashi muyi nafila kafin akira sallah"

"Yanzu kaima bazaka budemun hasken dake cikin wannan lamarin ba,akwai fa abubuwa dayawa take gaya mun ba zaka so kaji ba?"

Har ya mike ya dawo kusa da ita yace"inaso mana"

"To inkana so duk tambayar da nai kabani ansa?"

Wani murmushin yayi yace"ba yanzu ba,akwai time tashi kije kiyo alwallah."

Ya mike yabarta ciki da tarin tunani,wai meke faruwa da ita ne ada bata san wani tunani ba ko wata damuwa,Amman cikin kwana biyun nan abubuwa suna neman fin karfin kwalwarta.
Tana nan zaune har yafito yadan sha mur "taso nace kiyi Alwallah"

Jiki amace tataso ta dauki ribon tana tattare gashinta,kallon gashin yake kamar yau yafara ganin irinsa,Asmau na da gashi sosai amman ko kusa baikama kafar wannan ba,kyansu ma ba daya ba.
Nata asmau na fulani ne,ita kuma nadeeyah na asalin india ne.

Hula ta saka ta nufi bayin,yana kallo saida tayi addua sannan tashiga.
Wata gwauron ajiyar xuciya ya sauke yana shimfida abun sallarsa,ya shimfida mata nata itama.

Abayarta ta warware ta tada sallah,saida yayi raka,a hudu kwarara sannan ya sallame.ita ko gabansa saida tayi shidda masu dogon surori duk da aciki ciki take karatun.
Wata drower dake side bed ya bude ya dauko qu,ani ya bata "yimun karatun inji yau safiyar juma,a karanta mana suratul kahfi"

Murmushi tayi maidan karen kyau,wanda tunda suka hadu baiganshi ba.bugawa kirjinsa yayi yana ambaton sunan Allah,wannan yarinyar kamar aljana saboda kyau.

"Tirkeni zakai,uhmm badan inaso karatunba dabazayi kaji ba."
Tana ansar qur,anin tayi Auxiyya tare da bismillah tun anan ya tabbatar akwai tajwid acikin bakinta,bai sake tabbatar da cewar ta iyan ba saida yaji sautin kira arta hakika sakin baki yayi yana kallonta,bata damu ba don jitake wani dadi yana ratsata duk sanda take karantawa.
Jingina yayi da gadon ya rungume hannu idonsa ko keftawa baiyyi saboda kallon bakinta.

Itace bata gama ba saida akafara kiran sallah asuba,tayi sadakallahul axim ta rufe ta tashi tayi raka,atanil fijir sannan ta dora asubar..........ta gama ta zauna tayi azkar dinta na safiya taruga tahaye gado har idonta yana rufewa.

Kamar status haka yakoma don Al,ajabinta.
Bargo taja zata rufa yarikeshi yana kallonta.yamutsa fuska tayi kamar tai kuka tace"kayiwa Allah kabarni nayi bacci,wlh bai isan ba kaina zaiyi ciwo inbanyi ba."

"Idan kika nabarki kika koma makara zamuyi."

"Makara?ina zamu kuma daga nan kuma wani wajen zakayi dani!nashiga ukuna ni diyar daddy"

Yasha mur yace"karna sakejin kalmar shiga uku bakinki,ki kwanta baccin awa guda gareki zanfita yanzu idan nagama sallah"

"Don Allah ka karamun minty talatin ne"

"Na kara maki Amma karki sake na dawo baki tashi ba."

Tashige bargo tana magana can kasa kasa.
"Mtsss sai shegiyar takura,ni nabani rayuwata tana cikin kwale kwale"

💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕💕

Company yaje don tattaro duk wasu documents dinsa daya harhada zai isar can office dinsu,sunyi waya da baba Abas yace zaije can janbulo yajirasa don yana son yayi magana da nadeeyah tunda jiya ciwonta bai bari sunyi ba.
Alokacin shikuma har yashiga company,magadi ne kurin suka gaisa ya wuce ciki yana adduar har ya fita bai hadu da kowa ba, don baison barin kowace iri kofa da zata kawo zargi akansa..........saida kashhh yana xuwa bakin kofar office dinsu ya tadda kofar bude,ya tura yashiga sahabi ya tadda aiki kurin yake kasa rik'a dagowa yayi yana kallonsa cikin kallo tuhuma.
"Yallabai Kaine?"

Murmushi yayi yana bashi hannu sukai musabaha.
"Wlh nine ashe kananan kanata aiki,ban zaci samun kowa ba ganin garin bai idasa wayewa ba."

"Aini nan na kwana oga J ya matsa lamba idan banyi wasa ba wlh kujerar nan tawa zan rasata,abubuwa sunata cabewa duk bansan yadda akai ba da alama dai anyi mun kutse cikin aikina,abunda tunda nafara aikina dashi hakan bata faru ba"

"Tadijam,na tausaya maka sahabi Allah yafidda ka nazone na dauki tarkacena kasan mun samu kanmu cikin wadanda zasuje kd *MAI DAUKA OIL COMPANY* "

Zaro ido sahabin yayi yace"na taya ka murna sosai don zaka samu kudi ba kadan ba,amman inabaka shawara duk abunda kagani ka kauda idonka inba haka ba zaka shiga matsala,ba ayiwa J shishshigi cikin harkokinsa ina maka fatan sa,a."

Wannan maganganun suka sake sa masa kwarin guiwar aiki tukuru acan din har saiya cika aikinsa,murmushin ya sake yi masa yace"nagoe da shawara sahabi kaima Allah ya raba ka da J lafiya"

Office dinsa yashiga ya dauko jikkar sa ta zuwa aiki duk wasu abubuwan daya samu axuwansa na dan lokaci ya tattarasu ya saka ciki,dole saiya dauki computer guda cikin wadanda ya tadda saboda yana da ajiyarsa aciki,amman gudu sahabi ya darsa wani abun yasashi ya budeta ya kunna babbar tablet dinshi,yafara tura duk wasu abubuwa daya samu yanayi yana kallon agogo shidda tayi harda minty goma.
Dan danan duk suka shige ya goge na cikin tasu computer,ya tattara ya rufe komi ya zage jikkarsa yafito.

"Saduwar Alkairi sahabi"
Sukai musabaha............yana masa Allah yakiyaye.

Gudu yayi sosai don Baba yace masa yana cikin falon yazo.
Su nura yakira yaji idan yataho daga kadunar,yake sanar dashi ai shi asuba cikin kano tayi masa gashian yaa break zaikarasa gidan innar.
Drivern ma yace yanxu haka yana gidan innar anbashi kalaci yanayi.
 Baidade ba ya isa gidan shaf shaf yashigo suna gaisawa da baban.

"Ka natsu kashirya da kyau,don J bazai samu shiga company ba sai jibi,don na hadashi chakwakiya da minister Abdulkadir,kasan sune  suka kaimata wanan farmakin don haka yasau mu masaniya shine yayi takakkiya shekaran jiya yabisa har Abuja,rigima sukeyi ba yar karama.don yace shi yasan yatura asatota badan komi ba saidan takaddun nan dake cikin jikkarta dana baka su ,yace Amma tabbas bayan shi akwai wani don sune sukazo da bindugu suna harbi,rayuwarra suke nema bilhakki da gaskiya,yanzu dai J yaki yarda baida hannu wajen daukarta suna can suna rigima har gaban shugaban kasa."

"Kayi kokarin dauke hankalinsa daga nan baba,don haka dole zan sake kulawa sosai akanta,bari naga ita tashi na tado makata."

Ya wuce dakin nata,tana zaune saman stool tana drying gashinta,kallo daya tayi masa ta dauke kai uffan batace masa ba.
Jingina kafadarsa yayi da kofar dakin yace"kitashi kisaka kaya ga babanki Abas nan falo yazo ganinki"
Saurin jiyowa tayi tana mikewa tsaye.dan danan fuskarta ta kawatu da annashuwa "nagode Allah, zuwa yayi ya maidani gida ko?nagode maka daka kirashi Allah yabiya maka bukatunka kamar kasan wlh na gaji da zama dakai,jina nake kamar cikin prison"

Dauke kai yayi yana murmushi daya kubce masa don yaga dramer da zasuyi yanzu ita da baban.
"Nabaki minty goma bazaki bata mun lokaci ba,karki bari na sake dawowa dakin nan "
Harara ta danna ma bayansa tana jan dogon tsaki,ta nufi kayanta data fiddo cikin jikkar daya shirya mata jiya.
"Shegen mutun sai isar banza da wofi,wlh dabadan alkawarin da nayi ba dasaika gane kurenka,kuma kaci sa,ar ina tsoron tsawa Allah wuyata zakasha"tana saka wasu riga da wando  t-shet din pink and blue ce tasha kwalliyar duwatsu,wandon ma blue ne Amman ba jeans bane cotoon ne sai takoma kamar fulawa don kyau,jacket ta dora sama baka tana da dan tsawo zata kawo guiwarta,sai tayi rolling da pink din gyalenta.gaskiya duk hassadarka idan kaganta saikaji kamar kasaceta karuga,tasha kyau kuma bata sa komi fuskarba daya wuce hoda da lipstick,ko kwalli babu idonta.
Falon ta nufo da jikkar ga hannu ta dauko daker,tana tafiya cikin gayunta dole hankalin namiji yagushe idan ya tsaya yana kallon tafiyarta..........hakance ta faru da khalioa don bakinsa kasa rufuwa yayi,Amman ganin baba sai ya maje yayi kamar baiga komi ba,nan ko idonsa sai kokowar sake kallonta yake,yana mamakin yadda komi tasa kyau take.

"Baba nagama shiryawa muje,na kagara kona ga daddy wlh nayi bala,in kewarsa"

Hannu ya mika mata cikin tattausan lafaxi yace"xo yaki nan yar albarka"
Cikin azama ta isa inda yake tana zama kusada shi tana makale masa jikin hannunsa.
"Nadeeyah ba wajen daddy zan kaiki ba,kidauka keda daddy kuma sai bayan wani lokaci wandani kaina ban sanshi ba,abunda nakeso dake shine king girma nadeeyah.kin kai matakin daya kamata ace kinsan mekike ciki,yadace ace kwalwarki da xuciyarki suna aiki akwai abubuwan da yadace ace xuwa yanxu kingane su kin kuma nemi sani,Amman kullin sake komawa yarinya kike yanzu ba lokacin dogon bayani bane abunda nakeso dake nadeeyahni dai bazanyi abunda zai cutar dake ba,zaki gane hakan nan gaba.wannan dakike gani yanzu yafi kowa iko dake yafi kowa kusanci dake,umurninsa shine abunda nakeson kibi kikuma kiyaye. Ayau  *sati guda kenan dana daura maki aure shine mijinki naddeyah*"

"Baba!!!..............."

Ta fada cikin kara tana rufe bakinta tare da mikewa tana ja baya dashi,hannunta har rawa yake take nuna ABU KHALIPA............tama kasa cigaba da maganar tuyi idonta yaciko da kwallah har sun fara xuba...........ahankali take silalewa kasa dirshen tadafe kanta hirarta da matar ta yau take dawo mata kamar lokacin take gaya mata.

_"Kiyi min alkawarin idan kika san matsayinsa gareki bazakiyi abunda zai bata mun rai ba,kinsan miye banso?pretty bana son kina daukar komi da zafi,ki kasance mai dannar xuciyarki kin mance nasihar danayi maki nace maki Allah subhanahu wata ala yana so bayinsa masu shanye fushi.aduk sadda kikaji waniabu daya daga maki hankali ki kasance mai furta hazbunallahu wani imal wakil,Auzubillahi mishshaidanir rahim, innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un. Kiyita maimaitawa ina mai tabbatar maki zakiji xuciyarki tayi sanyi."_

Bakinta yana rawa take karanto duk adduoin datake jinsu cikin kunnenta,don jitake kamar sannan take furta mata su,tana kuka tana ambaton su tana shashshekar kuka.........

_"Inhar kin yarda da Allah  to ki mika masa dukkan lamuranki ba shakka zai jibanci lamuranki,kuma inhar kika dauki abunda nace maki to rayuwarki da wannan mutumen rayuwace mai kyau da ingaci,Amma sai kinyi hkr sai kin zama jaruma wajen jure dadi da wuya,hakika rayuwarki tana buktar mataimaki to ki dauka wannan shine mataimakin ki kiyi mun alkawarin jure wuya da dadi ki yi masa biyayya"_

Ajiyar xuciya takeyi tana jan numfashi tana goge kwallarta kafin ta dago idonta dasuka dan canza saboda kukan datayi.
"Baba kana iya tafiya zanyi duk abunda kace karka damu indai akaina ne"

Mamaki,alajabi da tsabar daure kai duk suka kamasu shida khalipan,ko kusa bai kawo haka abun zaizo ba,don duk tunaninsa tana nan tana murje murje tana bala,i da rashin kunyar data saba.

"Yunwa nakeji bazan iya tafiya banci komi ba."

Ta fad a kamar ba itace tagama rusa kuka yanxu ba,ajiyar xuciya kurin takeja kasan ranta kuwa Allah kadai yasan wutar dakeci,kuna takeji ainun cikin xuciyarta  wannan shine mijinta dashi aka daura aurenta innalillahi Allah menayi haka...........

_"duk sadda kika ga Abu yafaru dake wanda bakiso ba to kiyi hkr ki daukesa matsayin kinaso koda baki so din domin haka Allah yatsara kaddararki zatazo,kuma karki manta yarda da kaddara mai kyau da marar kyau imanine pretty,duk sadda kikaji shaidan yana ayyana maki wani abu kiyi saurin furta auxubillahi minashshaidanir rajim astagfirullah wa atubi ilaik"_

Bakinta taji yanata furta "astagfirullah A,uxubillahi mishshaidanir rajim."

Kasa wata doguwar magana baba yayi yace"shikenan nadeeyah zan ringa xuwa ganinki kinjiko ki kwantar da hankalin ki Sadiq mutumen kirkine zai kula dake kinjiko"
Daga mashi kanta kurin tayi,don idan tace zatai magana kuka zatai,bakinta zai iya kubcewa har maganar banza tafito tsabar yadda ranta yake tafasa.

Khalipa ne ya miko mata tea mai zafi mai kauri tare da bread."kirage yunwar da wannan kafin mutafi na siya maki abunda kikeson ci"

Sallama sukai da baban ya rakashi saida yatafi ya dawo,tusata yayi yana kallo kamar tv,lamuranta kara rikita masa kai suke,ko inda yake bata kalla ba don idan ta kallai ba shakka wata harara zata auna mashi,bata son tayi rashin biyayya ga wannan matar don komi ta umurceta saitaji bazata iya takewa ba,baya ga haka tana tsoron tayi abunda Allah zai kamata da laifi.

Tana gamawa ya dauki jikkarta yayi wa babah sallama yace sukula da gidan da kansu zai ringa yi masu aike,idan ya samu time kumsa zaizo.haka suka fito gidan
Ta na biye dashi kamar tayi bindiga haka takejin xuciyarta, Allah kadai yasan kunan da takeji,gaba ya bude mata ta shiga yasa jikkarta baya,sannan shima ya shiga ya tashi motar suka bar gidan........

Episode  .15

Har suka isa gidan inna uffan ba tace ba,fuskarta murtik babu alamun rahama,shiyasa ya kama kanshi don idan yace zai kulata rashin kunya xatai mashi.

Saida suka shiga unguwar taga wani mai sayar da glass kala kala tace"kasiya mun glass"

"Mezaki yi dashi?"

Tsaki taja kadan tana kauda kai,baisan maganar ma kamar zata mutu idan tayi haka takeji ba,.

Episode  .16

Sun iso lafiya Asmau tatashi tana kalle kallen garin,don wannan ne xuwanta na farko jiyowa tayi tana kallonsa murmushi mai kyau yayi mata yace"barka da rana mrs khalipa"

Bin bayansa sukai har cikin gidan wanda yake plat ne guda biyu,Amma daya yafi daya girma,babban yafi tsari dakyau  wanda anan ne khalipa da matansa zasu zauna,karamin kuma su inna da sumy da salisu wanda suna isowa  Nura ya sauki su inna acikinsa,babban falone maicin kujeru manya set guda,akwai matakalar da zakabi ta kaika bedrooms guda biyu masu dauke da komi na bukata,sai kuma wata kofar idan kadawo cikin falon,inkabita zata kaika wani bedroom din wanda nan na salisu ne akwai komi na bukata har toilet aciki.

Inji inna nan ta hau yi masu nasihar zaman takewar rayuwa,akaikaice Asmau da nadeeyah takewa Amman saika natsu zaka fahimta,sosai take masu nasiha da wa,azin hkr da kuma kyautata mu,amalarka da abokin zamanka.
"Duk wanda Allah ya hadaka xama dashi kayi kokarin ganin ka kyautata masa,karkayi la,akari da cewar aishi baima ka aa kai kayi kasauke nauyin da Allah ya dora maka,koba komi Allah bazai tambayeka ya kazauna da mutun ba,kuma gaba daya rayuwar babu abunda yake isa idan ba hkr ba,don haka ke Asmau kece matsayin babba bakuma girma na shekaru ba aa,girma na aure Allah ne yabaki karkiga kece tafarko kiringa tsula mata rashin kunya,Aa bazan malunta ba don agirme ta girmeki saiki bata girmanta na shekaru daidai gwargwado,sannan duk abunda yabiyo ta wajenki kiyi kokarin ganin kin kyautata mata,kinyi adalci ta haka ne zaki gujewa fushin Allah da kuma fushina,idan ma kinga Abu tayi maki ba daidai ba cikin salama da lalama kisameta har daki,kice mata nikau Nadeeya abu kazan daya faru daxun ko jiya ko rannan,banji dadin sa don Allah karki sake,ahaka idan ma kuskurene zatace maki batasan hakan zai bata maki rai ba,cikin ruwan sanyi zaku daidaita kanku babu maijin tsakaninku,mijinku hakan zaiyi matukar faranta masa.kunajina"

Muryar nadeeyah na rawa kwalla sun cika idonta,saboda wani feeling takeji na kewar mahaifiya.

"Haka nakeson ji,Nadeeyah karki ji komi kidaukan tamkar nina haifeki,yadda kika gansu wadana haka kema nakejinki ajikina,don haka inhar kina da damuwa karkiji ko dar kixo ki sameni,insha Allah zanyi maki maganinta kisaki jikinki da kowa banson dari dari nan,inkinayin haka sai inga kamar baki daukeni uwa ba sukuma baki daukesu yan uwanki ba,kiware gidan mijinki ne mu cin arxiki muka xo"

Dariya sumy da Asmau keyi salisu na tayasu,itama innar tayi dariya tace"ehy mana idan tace bata son zama damu aikaga akwai damuwa"
Tafada donjin ta bakin nadeeya.
Dan murmushi tayi tace"Allah ya kiyaye nakoreku nice dai yar cin arxikin inna baku ba"

Kowa yaji dadin maganarta don jikinta yayi sanyi kalau,tana nema wacce zata zame mata uwa ashe zata samu,duk haukanta inta kebe takanyi kukan rashin uwa mahaifiya,tundaga yarintarta har xuwa girmanta babu wanda ta tabaji labarin mahaifiyarta bakinsa,saudaya ta taba tambayar Daddy sai yace karta sake masa maganarta idan ba so take suyi hannun riga ba,tundaga sannan ta daina neman sanin ta,saidai tayi kuka tagode Allah babu mai sani balle ya lallasheta...........shigowar khalipa ya katse mata tunani.

Shiru tayi donjin alamun fitowarta,sumy tai saurin dauko mata abun salla tana shimfida mata da hijab,kasancewar suna isowa  tashirya kayanta tsaf cikin wadrop.

deeyah ta sake fada

Nadeeyah bata damu ba don daman bata saka ran zai kulata ba,shiyasa ko kujerar dayake bata kalla ba ta saki jiki taci abincin sosai don yunwa takeji kamar zata hada da cokalin ta cinye,har saida ta kware bakuma karamar kwarewa ba,sosai take tari saboda tsabar kidima baro kujerarsa yayi yazo yana bubbuga bayanta,daker take jawo numfashi donhar saida shinkafar data sarketa tafito ta hanci,ruwa ya mika mata takafa kai tana sha...........inna sannu kurin take jeramata da sumayya,ma'u danne kishinta tayi tana mata sannu.

"Kina cin abinci kamar wadda akace zaikare baki koshi ba,sai hannu baka hannu kwarya kike"

Haka suka bisa yana kusada Asmau sai tsokanarta yake,deeyah na baya tana karewa gidan kallo,wajen furanni take kallo sunyi kyau,ga lambu acan baya kusada plat dinsu.

"Ammm inaganin muyi addua ko."

"Asmau kibude mana taron da Addua"

Episode  .17


Ba saina ce komi ba akan soyayyar data gudana tsakanin Amaryar da angonta watau Asmau,hakika tayarda takuma amunce cewar yayansu yana matukar sonta,don taga tattali kulawa da kauna duk da adaren tasha wuya,Aman lallabawar datasha bai bari zafin dataji yayi tasiri ba,ta bashi hadin kai sosai da sosai wanda shima yakejin baida kamarta,washe gari jikinta bai wani nuna sosai ba saboda kulawar data samu.Amma duk da haka ya hanata tunkarar kitchen,yaje ya tado deeyah wai ta dora break............batare data ba sharia wuya ba tace bata iya ba.
Abunda ya bata masa rai kenan,,,,,,

"Mekike nufi da baki iya ba,karki ce mun kishi kike da Asmau?karkice mun kinji haushin ba dakinki na kwana ba?..............don Allah karka tadamun hankali da sanyin safiya,miye ne aciki don nace dakai ban iya ba,karya xan maka?ince kaike cewa babu abunda aka koyar dani sai rashin kunya da rashin tarbiyya,abunda kake fada kenan akaina all d time,to haushi name zakaji don nace ban iyaba.kake maganar naji haushi baka kwana dakina ba inbasani ba kasani *MUSADDIQ* bana mararin bana fata bana muradin kwanan ka,ajiya ban fada ba saboda agaban matarka ne,amman yanzu kasani na tattara kwanakina kaf dazanyi acikin gidan nan nabaku kaida ita,bana bukata ita dakeso nabar mata kucinye juna karkuyi ragi,ban son neman fitina amman duk abunda zai ja kasani dogon surutu kasani nayi maka rashin kunyar da kake fada kasani,don haka please and please stay away from me."

Ta na gama fadar abunda ke ranta ta wuce kan gado tahaye taja bargo,don daman daga toilet take tayo brush bata iya cigaba da bacci duk sanda tatashi koba abunda zataci saitayi brush.

Rasa abunyi yayi don tariga ta gama gasa masa magana,idan yace zai sake wata maganar abun zayyi tsayi juyawa yayi yafita yana jinjina kai,yasan matakin dauk akanta.

Bayan fitarsa deeyah tashige bargo bakin cikin duniya ya isheta,wai dawane zataji da rabota da gatanta dayayi ya kawota nan zataji koko da bakar masifarsa,koko da abubuwan dake faruwa da rayuwarta.ita babu mai sonta bata da wa bata da kane,bata uwa mahaifin ma tayi nisa dashi,babu mai lallashinta babu mai fahimtar halin da take ciki yajata yabata baki,bata san sadda kuka ya kubce mata ba kuka mai sunan kuka,wanda duk wanda yaji zaisan maiyinsa yana cikin kuna marar iyaka.
Tayi kuka wanda tunda suka iso daga jiya xuwa yau batayi ba,saida taji numfashinta yana niyyar daukewa ta samar wa kanta maslaha tayi shiru ta lallashi kanta,magana take cikin rauni da kewa.

"Inakike mama na,ban sanki ba koga hoto babu wanda ya taba nuna mun ke,balle danginki meyasa muka rabu?meyasa da zakitafi baki tafi dani ba?idan ma mutuwa kikai meyasa mutuwar bata hada dani ba?ina ciki kishirwar ganinki babu wanda zan iya tambaya yabani labarinki,mahaifina kadai nakeda hurumin yiwa wannan tambayar shikuma yanuna baya son,shin ya zanyi da raina.............kukan yana neman sake dawowa tari take da shashsheka tana son kukan ya tsaya,Amma bata sanin yana dawowa.

"Tabbas kamar yadda kika gaya mun nadawo cikin hayyacina na fuskanci abubuwan dake faruwa,nafara dawowar don ada jinake kamar an kulle mun xuciyata daga tunanin duk wani abu mai muhimmanci,shigowarki rayuwata yayi matukar tasiri ya haska mun abunda da bana gani,ya hasmun duniyata nasan Allah daya halliceni nasan tayadda zanyi na bauta masa,sabanin da danake cikin duhun jahilci,mahaifina da uwar rikona basu koya mun komi ba sai tsabar girman kai da rashin ganin babba da kima,basu bani tarbiyyar da za,a iya zama dani ba ban san komi ba saidai nayi wanka naci abinci azagi wance na wulakanta wance,isa adaure wancen insa akori wancen daga wajen cin abincinsa............why why why.........

Ta gumi na buga ina xurfafa tunanina tsawon lokaci kafin naji anbude kofar falona anshigo,ban kawo kowa ba tunani na shine yashigo,don dagani saishi gidan waccen yar sarkin yariga ya mata ciwo bazata iya fitowa ba,munafuki ahaka dashi sum sum har yasan ya nemi mace.

Tanufi waje tana cewa saina xo.

Ganin goma tayi kuma nayi brush dina ga yunwa nacina yasa na gyara kujera na zauna ina bude  kulolin kamshin ferfesun naman ragon ya dakeni,na lumshe ido inajin yunwar tana sake tasowa,mikewa nai naje kitchen na dauko plets da saving spoon da cups duk guda uku uku,na dawo nafara xuba nawa na hada tea dina mai kaurin gaske,na xuba dankalin turawa soyayye harda wainar kwai manya manya,sai mazari nake inson nafara lodawa......na gutsiri bread din dake sama nayi bismilla nafara xubawa uban ba,u.............ci nake atsanake don gudun kwarewa don jiya nasha wuya,shiyasa ko naman daya shigo dashi da dare banci ba da lemun suna frig.
Ina tsakarci najiyo dariyarsu da shagwabar asmaun,lumshe ido nai nabude ban kalli inda suke ba na cigaba da cin kalacina,hankali kwance ashe abun nasu ma babbane don daukota yayi kamar jinjira yaja kujera ya zaunar da ita.

"Yau ina zaki nizan kaiki,ban lamunci kiyi aikin komi ba,kofita nai Allah inkikai aiki ko idan nadawo Nadeeya ki gaya mun"

Yafada yana xura idonsa cikin nawa,kallon yan seconds na masa na saki murmushina nace"baka da matsala kam,nima bazan bari ta wahala ba ai Amarya kishare waje kizauna hutu ake bukatar kiyi"
Tabbas taki nauyi da kunyar Abunda yayi mata agabanta,don kamar cin fuskane amma ganin kamar bata nuna komi na yasa tayi murmushi itama tace"hardake ko,uhmm nima xan rama"

Wani kallo ta sakar mata shiba harara ba shiba normal kallo ba cikin wani murmushi da nina san ma,anar abuna nace mata
"Ashe zaki rame,karkiga laifina anbani aikine dole zan kuka dake"
Mikewa nayi ina zarar tissue na goge bakina da hannuna.
"Aci break lafiya nashiga daga ciki"

Daga Asmaun harshi bin bayanta sukai da kallo kowa da abunda yake ayyana wa cikin ransa.........."kinga open ur mouth",ya debo dankaki da fork yana kaiwa bakinta tabiye masa sukaita cida juna,Amma maganganun nadeeya suna mata yawo akai,tadai sharene kurin.
Anan suka lalace da soyayarsu,wasa da dariya tsokana haka sukai tayi,inajiyosu daga falona time din ma ina tilawar karatuna don banda mai dorani yanzu,tunda tace bazata sake dawowa ba.

Bansan time din dasuka dauka ba saidai na daina jiyo muryoyinsu.

Ina gama karatuna daman nayi wanka na saka kayana marasa nauyi, nasamudoguwar kujera na kwanta sai tunanina ya dawo sabo,ayanxu bana kusada daddy balle har nafara bincikar Asalina ta wajensa,haka bana kusada deluwa haka baba Abas yayi nisa shima,shin ta ina zanfara binciko nidin taya aka samar dani.

_"Musaddiq shine jigon duk farin cikinki pretty,kuma duk abunda ya shige maki duhu shine zai zama mai haskan maki har kigani,ayanxu ba zaki gane ba domin xuciyarki anmata daurin da bazata iya fahimtar hakan ba,sai ranar da xuciyarki ta bude daga rufewar da aka mata,itace ranar da kika san asalin wacece ke alokacin duk sauran kofofin fahimta da lura da tunani zasu bude.karkiyi nadamar kasancewa dashi don shidin xinarine,amma kiyi komiki cikin lura da kuma hankali."_

Kalaman matar kenan aranar da zasu rabu,tabbas daga haduwarta dashi Abubuwa da dama suna neman bude mata kwalwa,xuciyarta tana haskomata wasu abubuwa dasuka faru,kuma sai yanzu take neman sanin hujja da dalili shakka babu ta kanka zan fara *Musadiq* to Amma ta inata wace hanya still ina bukatar lokaci da dogon nazari,tare da bincike binciken ne saina saka ido don gudun ya kamani.

Tashi nayi nafito nasaka hijab din sallata don shiga wajensu ina na gaida ta,falon Asmau da yar hayaniyar kallo alamun suna ciki ita dashi,ban damu ba nawuce su kurin na tafi.

"Ah aha Nadeya shigo mana kin tsaya daga bakin kofa"
Inji innar data fito daga kitchen.
Falon suka zauna na gaidata cikin biyayya don jinake kamar ina gaban mahaifiyata,na saki jiki muna taba hira nace"ina sumayyar takene inna"

"Yanzu nan taleka nan makotanmu,wai ta gaidasu asan juna kinsanta da shegen son jama,a ga shege shige"

"Hakan nada kyau inna ina akasan juna yafi,babu abunda za,a tayaku"

"Babu komi nadeya gyaran gidane har mun gama,saura sanwa da sumayya ta dawo zata dora."

"Aa inna muje sai kina nuna mani yadda zanyi ba saina koya ba,tunda ban iyaba"

Har cikin ran inna taji dadin yadda bata dauki girman kai ba,bata iya ba kuma tana son koya.

"Shikenan nadeeya muje kitchen din to"

Mikewa tayi tabi bayanta suka shiga kitchen din,babu jimawa suka fara aikin cikin nishadi dan danan taji ta saba da innar,kuma tana yin komi yadda tace duk abunda ta bata zatayi yada tace taxuba,hira suke cike da jin dadin aikin.

"Inna daga yau tun daga kalacin safe har xuwa abincin dare zanzo nake tayaki ina koya"

"Aa ban amince ba saikin gayawa mijinki,kuma banda break abincin rana dana dare kurin na lamunce kifito."

Cike da shagwaba wadda na mance rabon danayi wa wani ita.
Saboda ban samu damar hakan ba kokuma banga fuskar da zanyi wani ba,nace mata"inna don Allah kiyarda bana son yasan ban iyaba saidai yaga inayi kurin"

"Shikenan na yarda Allah yabamu sa,a bani salad din nan karki yanke hannu tunda ba sabawa da wukar kikai ba"

"Nidai kibarni nayi da haka zan koya kinuna mun yadda zan yanka to"

Ahankali inna take yankawa har tayi da yawa kanan ta bani na cigaba amman bani sauri saboda tsoron wukar nake karta tafi da yatsana.
Hirar mu muke tayi da ita najiyo muryarsa falo yana magana da sumayya data shigo yanzu,kusan tare ma zance sun shigo........"idan na sake ganin kafafunki waje saina saba maki,har kinsan wurin da har zaki fara shige shigen dakika saba."

"Yaya inna tasani kuma fa banjima ba kayi hkr bazan sake ba"

"Inbanda abunka sadiqu ahaka zamuyi ta zama mukadai kamar mayu,ai u da saba da makota ko,kogaisawar kirki ayi"
Inna data fito daga kitchen take fadar haka

"Inna ba ita kadai ba gaskiya,don nan ba kamar can bae,kua akwai dalilin dayasa nace karta fita ita daya inso samune ma duk inda wani zashi aimun magana akwai driver yakai mutun ya dawo dashi,inason komi ya zama cikin tsaro,mutun ake kiwo ba dabba ba"

"Kana da gaskiya anan kuma,za,a kiyaye insha Allah..........wayyo hannuna inna hannuna nayanke wlh na yanke jini kefita innalillahi na bani hannuna inna"
Suka jiyo karar deeyah a kitchen,aguje innar ta ruga wajenta ta rike hannun sai kallonsa take tana kuka sosai,ga jinin kuma yana diga.

"Subhanallahi,sadiqu sadiqu zomana yosauri"

Ahanxarce ya nufo kitchen din har yana tun tube da kafar sumayya.
"Garin yaya haka,ke kiyiwa mutane shiru waya aikeki daukar wuka dallah kirage wannan karar,"
Ya anshe hannun daga wajen inna yatara wajen sink ya kunna famfo yana wanke jinin,wani na itowa,Asmau da shigo warta kenan yace mata cikin sauri kamar zai kifa.
"Ruga part din mu kije dakina bed side drower ki bude akwai fast aid box na nan kikawo mun yanzu da sauri."
Aikuwa rugawar tayi kamar yadda yace matan,yajawo ni xuwa falon zaman dishen nayi gabansa shikuma yana kan jukera,kuka nake kamar yarinya don babu abunda nake masifar tsiro irin jini yana fita ajikina na ciwo,sannu inna da sumayya kurin  sukeyi man,ban masan sunayi ba sai kokarin kwace hannuna nake.

"Wai baka ganin jinina zai kare ehy,ka sakemun hannu inshanye kayana"
Gaba daya me zasuyi mata ba dariya ba har inna saida ta dara shi kanshi baisan murmushi ya kubce masa ba,daidai dawowar Asma,u ta mika mashi tana ja baya.

"Sannu kinji nadeeya saida na hanaki yankan salad din nan kika nace,kinga abunda ai nake jiye maki."

"Inna kice karya samun aidin,wlh azaba gareshi inna kiyi masa magana wlh bana so....wayyo Allahna kayi mun rai banaso......"

Tattarota yayi yasa gabansa ya matse da kafafu,ya matse gudan hannun sannan ya dauki audugar daya dan gwalo aidin din yasama ciwon..........wata uwar kara na fasa duk saida suka dode kunnuwansu duk na cukuykuyesa fuskata kaca kaca da hawaye,.....

"Sadiqu kabita sannu don Allah"
Inji inna

Daura bandage yake ga yatsan yace"kibar ma biyeta inna ko dan yaro shekara biyar baya wannan raki sai ita babbar kawai."


"Wlh ko kai kaji wannan ciwon aka sama ka aidin saikayi wanda yafi nawa,kasani ai yana da zafi ko"na fada ina gunjin kuka na gefe
Harara ta yayi yana gama mani dressing din,ya dungure mun kai.
"Sai shegen kuka kamar jaririya."
Su inna nata dariya Asmau kishi yazo mata wuya,yak'e kurin takeyi Amman wannan duk salone aganinta,kokarin saita xuciyarta don bata son nuna kishinta a fili amma Abun fin karfinta yake duk yadda take kokarin  ganin ba taji komi akan abunda yake wakana tsakanin su ba sai xuciyarta ta gaza,duk da azahiri tagani ba sonta yake ba amma xuciyarta ta kasa natsuwa.

"Inna ni zanfita saina dawo"

"Adawo lafiya"tacw dashi tana kama hannuna uka auna kan kujera tace ma sumayya takawo mun ruwa maidan sanyi kada insha,Asmau kuwa sum sum tabi bayansa bama wanda yaga fitarta.

✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨

Nice banbar part din inna ba sai da aka gama sanwar dare wajen karfe shidda,sannan namasu sai anjima na wuce part dina,kayana nacire nashiga wanka don sai warin sanwa nake,ina fitowa ana kiran sallah magruba dama nayo Alwallah ta kawai kaya na xura nashafa cream sannan nafara sallata,ina gamawa nayi addu'oina natashi,turare na fesa spray mai suna 2hot u dan danan yarikide jikina da kamshi,dakin na dan tattara duk da gadonne ya yamutse,ban dai iyaba haka na gyara shi.
Banajin yunwa don saida naciwo tuwon semo miyar agushi,kafin na taho.
Rigar jikina sawai take kamar ban saba amman kuma ba,a ganin jikina ahankalia fito daga dakina na jawo kofar.

Falon babu kowa da alamun Asmau tana cikin dakinta hanyar dakinsa na nufa ina sauri tare da Adduar Allah yasa bai rufe kofar ba,aikuwa naci sa,a bude take na tura ahankali don kartayi kara.............wani sanyin dadi hade da kamshi mai saka baccin dole ya bugi fuskata,yayin da kafata take lumewa cikin lallausan kafet din dake malale atsakar dakin,ban san ma bakina yace Wow ba.
Rufe kofar nayi na barta bude yadda na ganta dakin nake bi da kallo yayi masifar burgeni,komi yellow and white daga kujerun har kadon da sauran kayan dakin,wani wajene an dan yimasa cover sai aka saka labue mai shara shara,kayan motsa jikina awajen machine din da ake tafiya ne sai karafunan dagawa,manya da kanana akwai wanda da hannu guda zaka daga wani kuma saika saka hannu biyu,gasunan dai sai wanda kakeso.
Tsalle na buga ina jin dadi don na samu wajen fakewa.
Baro wajen nayi na dawotsakar dakin ina sake kare masa kallo,na fara da bed side drower dinsa ina budewa duk abunda naci karo dashi saina bude duk kankantar rubutu saina karata shi bana tsallakewa, ban samu komi ba na baro wajen na dawo dayar itama,samanta akwai tarin littatafai English novels gasunan jere kala da kala,na jinjina kai bana son karance karance amma zan fara wata kila haka ya bani wan  experience na daban,na cigaba da duba lokar itama dai babu wasu abunda zan iya karuwa dashi,saidai kamar naga photo album tabbas shine kuma babbane can kasan takaddu na ganoshi,saidai ina cikin kokowar fiddoshi naji alamun tahowa za,a shigo.........hankalina tashe nabar wajen drower ina gyara ta yadda ba,a gane ba wurin kaya motsa jikinsa naje  na hau machine dinsa ina kure malejin tafiyar yadda zai sani jogging...........kafin naji anbude kofar anshigo.

Episode  .18


Yi nayi kamar bata san da shigowarsa ba,jogging kurin nakeyi bihakki da gaskiya,kamar daman tundaxu abunda nake kenan,sai shashsheka nake ka rasowa yayi cikin dakin yana ajiye jikkarsa alamun daga wajen aiki yake,sai lokacin na dago kaina muka hada ido,rage malejin gudun nayi ina zare ido na sauko ahankali,nafara bin gefe da gefen bangon dakin xan fece,har nakusan isa ga kofa yasa kafa yarufe ta yana jingina bayansa  da ita,ya tsareni  da mayun idanunsa wadan da sai yanzune na lura da wani ruwa ruwa aciki,mai sanya su shaiki kamar anxuba mangyda aciki,wanda hakan ke hanani tsurawa masa ido,.nufoni yafara dan danan na shagwabe fuska ina koma wa baya,ganin zai cin  mani yasa na durkushe ina boye kaina cikin kafafuna.
Jinsa nayi yazo ya durkusa kudani sosai har kamshinsa yana cika mun hanci,duk da yadda na duk'e.
Kunnena naji ya kama yadan murda azabure na dago kaina ina hawayen karya.

"Please *ya musaddiq* kayi hkr wlh ni babu abuda nayi maka acikin daki,nazo ina motsa jikine kawai,kayi hkr bazan sake ba ka sakarmun kunnena cirewa zai"
Na fada ina dora hannuna bisa nashi daya rike kunnen dashi,inata matso kwallar karya.
Inajin yanata kallona kamar ya rasa abun cemun,dagowa nai ina kallonsa na sake marairaicewa.............."bincike kike mun ko?mekike nema adakina?"

"Na rantse maka babu abunda na taba in banda wadanen books din,dama sonake in tambaye ka zan ringa aro don Allah ya musaddiq"
Ni kaina bansan dalilin dayasa nake duk wannan acting din ba,aman ga duk alamu yana hawa.

Mikewa dani yayi atsaye rike da kunne,mukaje bakin bed dinsa ya zauna ni kuma na zauna kasa wajen kafafunsa.

"Keba namiji ba meya hadaki da kayan motsa jiki?"

Idona duk na fiddo na xuba mashi su nace"laaa wlh agidan daddy inayi,ina wancen machine din dakina da wancen karfen kananun cen"
Na riko kafarsa na zare takalmin ina marairaice muryata,nace"don Allah yaya naringa xuwa inayi?"

Shi gaba ki daya ma yarasa abunyi,ba da haka ya nufeta ba,meyasa yaji kamar tana zare masa duk wani kuzarinsa,lumshe ido yayi sadda yatsunta sirara masu tsananin taushi suka sauka ga fatar kafarsa,ta zare masa safar kafar.
Maganarta ta dawo dashi duniyar daya tafi........"don Allah yaya karkace aa"
Ajiyar xuciya yayi yana shafa kanshi yace"its oky,salan ki maida mun dakina ma daddalarki,kinjini"
Muryarsa kamar a frig ta fito don sanyinta.........cikin jin dadi na daka tsale "nagode yaya bazan maka banna ba kuma bazan lalata maka daki ba.............shigowar Asmau yasa nayi shiru ina idasa zare masa safar dayar kafar na saka cikin takalmi,na mike na dauki book daya dake saman drower mai suna *SECRET LOVE WITH EMOTIONS*.

Yi nayi kamar ban ganta ba nace"yaya natafi saida safe"
Na wuce hanyar datake tsaye bakin kofar tana bina da wani kallo,kamar bazata bani hanya ba naja natsaya bance mata komi ba na juya garesa cikin yar muryata nace masa.
"Yaya kace mata ba abunda nayi maka,safa kurin da takalmi na cire ma bayan haka babu komi"
Ido kurin ya zuba mani da alamun dai yau na kashemasa bakin magana,kanta na jiyo nace"I'm sorry nashiga aikin da banau ba inaso zan wuce"
Sai time din ta matsa daga bakin kofar na rabasu na wuce dakina.

Idasa shigowa tayi fuskarta babu walwala wasu kayane masu kyau riga da siket,cotton ne kayan sunyi mata kyau.
Riko hanuwanta yayi yan jawota jikinsa,wanda duk yakejin ya mas nauyi sadda yashigo bayjin wannan gajiyar tome ya kawota yanxu???

Muryarsa cike da sanyi yace mata"kinji haushi ko ma,una?I'm sorry nan na same ta."

Kwantawa tayi jikinsa lamo tana jin xuciyarta tana zafi,bata san meyasa ba duk abunda zai hada shida nadeeya ranta kuna yake,yanzu ma kururuwa ne kurin batayi ba,duk da tana son ta koyi kauda kai amman kishin akan nadeya mai zafine...........kwalla ta zubo mata tace tana masa shagwaba "Amma yaya kasan dai kai nakejira,tundazun nayi maka kwalliya har ta huce baka zo ba,kuma mezai kawota nan ehy,nidai bana son haka"

Lumshe idonsa yayi yana dago fuskarta jikinsa ya sunbaci lips dinta dasuka sha lipstick mai kamshi yace"Sorry dear,bari nayi nazo nayi maki taki kwalliyar kema kafin na isoki,bana son kiji warin rana jiki."
Harararsa tayi ya lakuce mata hanci.
"Ehy mana matar *I S S ABBAKAR ASSADIQ*,kidaina damuwa akanta kikoyi danne kishinki nasani kinji babu dadi kinsamemu tare,ki dauka hakan is nothing,because itama matatace hakan ba haramun bane,kuma kema kinada right din shigowa nan duk sadda kikeso bazan hanaki ba,kamar yadda itama bazan hanata ba.keda ita duk mata nane bakuda banbanci da juna awajena plss kina ringa danne irin wadannan abubuwan,don irin hakan zasu na hanaki sukuni oky?"
Ya idasa maganar yana leken fuskarta,wadannan maganganun sunyi mata tsaye,kenan aiki ya sameta muddin hakan tacigaba da faruwa,nashiga ukuna ya zanyi da wanan kishin taya zan ringa kallonsu tare.........kokarin nuna masa take bataji haushi ba,don kar ta bata masa rai.
Ahaka yashagaktar da ita sukaita kaunarsu,har wanka saida yasata ta sake,koma ince suka sake tare,yajasu sukai sallar isha,i karshe adakin ranar suka kwana.

✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨✨

Banyi bacci ba sai around two na dare,Ina gama abunda duk zanyi na shirin kwanciya naman na jiya da dare da sumy ta sake gasoshi cikin oven,naci nayi ket nabi da lemu nayi shafa,i da wutri na tare da tilawar karatuna sannan naja filo na jingina,book din na bude na fara karantawa,tun ina niyyar ajiyewa har naji na kasa labarin ya laloni over............saida bacci yaci karfina sanan nayi alamar da zantashi awajen gobe na ajiye,nayi adduoina na kwanta.

💕💕💕💕💕💕💕

Zaman takewar mu acikin gidan nidashi da asmau zamane na mutunta juna,bana shiga sabgarsu tsakanita dashi haka duk abunda zai janyo mun raini wajenta bana yi,koda tayi abunda ya bata mun rai zan shanye na nuna mata nidin ina sama da ita,saboda ganin yadda yaya musaddiq ya banzatar dani yasa take mani kallon wadda bata da wani daraja ko isa acikin gidan,tana mani kallon wata abun tausayi don ganin hanyar dakina ma bata ishesa kallo ba,koda kuwa ranar kwana nane saidai ya tafi dakinsa ya kwana,baya gaba dani amman baya shiga sabgata,koda ya sameni dakinsa bazai cemu komi ba duk da abuda naso nayin dafari na ajiyesa tukunna,binciken da nake so nayi karatun nan ya dauke mun hankali sosai badan na hkr da binciken ba,akwai dalilin yin hakan,karatu yana budemun wani waje acikin raina don nafara gane wasu abubuwa wadanda ada ban sansu ba.
Idan na gaji da zaman saina shiga wajen inna muyi fira ga kuma girkin danake koya wajenta,sau dayawa ma can nake yini sai magaruba nake dawowa,Asmau kam tabaje hajar soyayyarta da khalipa don tun waniabun batayi idan ta ganni yanzu toharta murje idonta,haka shima din bajin nauyin yayi mata abu gabana yake ba, basu cika zama gida suci abinci ba saidai sufita waje suci.duk da dan lokacin baicika zama gida ba wataran ma harnayi bacci bai dawo ba,koda inna take masa maganar yace Abubuwanne suke bukatar lokacinsa,amma ana kan samun nasara cikin karamin lokaci tatayasa da addua.

Ahalin dana tsinci kaina ada bansan wani feelings a soyayya ba bansan wani feelings na kaji kana son wani ba,saboda koda nayi soyayya da dan abokin daddy hadamu akai,munyi sabo dashi sosai because school daya mukai UK inbanda romance dayake yawan kokarin yi dani bai iya komi ba,nikuma anawa haukan duk daukata shine son!sai yanzu danake karanta irin wadanan books din nake iya gane wa,ta hakan har nakejin xuciyata tana sauyawa,abubuwa suka fara juyata...............kamar yadda wani abu yafaru ranan.

Week end ne yana gida ranar da safe yana dakin Asmau na je nadan motsa jikina na awa guda nabaro dakin,nadawo nawa nashiga toilet ina hada ruwan wanka na gama na since jikina nayi wankan na dan jima kafin na fito,so banji kiran da yake kwalamunba daga main falo.
Towel din dana daura iyakarsa cinyata daker ya rude mun bom bom dina,nafito ina tsane kaina da wani towel din,kicibis mukai bakin kofar bayin............wanda banyi aune ba najini na afka cikin jikinsa gaba dayana,yai saurin dafa  mirrow da hannunsa shi ya hana ba mu xube kasa ba nidashi,tuni na runtse idona nai zaton sai mun fadi kasa saboda silbin tayils,shiru banji munkai kasan ba yasa na bude idona kuri yayi wa fuskata kamar yaga sabon halitta,ayau na kasa janye nawa idon akansa,idanun nan nasa nakejin bazan iya daina kallo ba,don gani nai sunyi mun kyau kuma wani abu yana shiga cikin idona daga nasa idon..............dagoni yayi yana rike da towel dina don da bai rikesa ba kun cewa zaiyi,hakan yayi daidai da shigowar Asmau still yau ma.sororo tayi tana kallon mu na kauda kai na shikuma ya cikamun inda yarike din na daure sannan na rabashi nabar wajen bayin.

Muryarta naji cike da bacin rai take cewa "yaya miye wannan?karo na uku kenan kaida ita duk ranakun da ba nata ba,meyasa kuke munafuntata?tabika dakin ka ko ita kabiyota!idan nayi magana kace mun aiba haramun bane,amman ai cikin kwana nane,idan ma wani munafurcin kike yi wlh kinyi ki gama yadda kikazo haka zaki kom................"Asma,u!!!" Cikin wata irin anger ya daka mata tsawar da saida naji xuciyata ta buga,naji ina neman sumewa saboda tsoro da firgici,Amman kasancewar maganganunta sunfi tsawar zafida kuna acikin kirjina yasa na dake,rigar danake sawa doguwa na ida sawa,kafin naji yana magana cikin bacin rai.

"Ban jamaki kunne ba last time?Asmau harni zaki kalla kice ina munafurtarki?..............katseshi nayi cikin muryarta dake rawa saboda bacin rai.
"Aa babu abunda zakace anan,kabarta tafafi son ranra ta ganine  taga kabata dama,gara ta wulakantami yadda kakeyi,kun hade kai kun maidani bola,mekike dashi Asmau da zanyi munafurcin ki,ashe duk abunda kuke yi agabana idan kece ni xuciyarki bugawa zatai,to kinci sa,ar mijin naki baya gabana bana masa kallon da kike zato,wlh bacin haka dasai kin raina kanki,"na kama hannunsa hawaye na zubo mun na danka mata ganata hannun,ina danne kukana nace"gashinan don Allah kimaida shi cikin ciki ki haifo,karewar bakison ya rabeni,kinga hakanzaifi kuje,kutafi bana son ganinku kuma wlh inhar akwai cuta tsakania dakai Allah says saka un,tua har ka iya xubda mun kima wajenta,.......na turasu waje na rufe kofata.......ina silalewa bakin kofar ina kukana mai isata,hakika duk abubuwan dasuke faruwa banji ciwo irin wannan ba,meyasa zanji ciwo meyasa donta nuna mun iyakata awajen mijinta nakejin kamar xuciyata zata fito waje,hakika feeling din nada banne don jinake kamar ana turamun garwashi cikin kirjina,haka nasha kukana na godewa Allah wankan danayi ko mai banshafa ba.

"Daga rana mai kamar tayau kika sake gaya mata magana duk inda ranki yakai saiya baci,Asmau har ki kalli tsabar idona kice ina maki munafurci,ni Asmau ashe zafin kishinki har zaisa ki nemi zagina imagine!..........."yaya don Allah.......karkice dani komi Asmau,kin nuna mun iyakata ashe har kina da zarrar yiwa nadeeya wulakanci irin waa ki gaigaya mata maganganu kawai don kinga na baki dama?to ya maki kyau."
Kuka ta fashe dashi tana binsa aguje ta rungume bayansa cikin rudewa don bata san abunzai kai har haka ba,xuciyarta ce ta fusata tagaza control dinta zafi takeji ainun idan tana ganinsu tare.
"Kayi hkr yaya wlh kuskurene sharrin xuciyata ne,banyi da niyyar bata maba nakasa jurewa ne."
Ransa bace ya banbare jikinsa yana cewa"kullin haka kike gaya mun kin kasa jurewa,Amman miye bamayi gabanta?ehy ince har goyaki kike cewa nayi gabanta inkuma goyakin,ita batayi abunda kikayi ba saike Asmau,don kina ganin ina fifitaki sama da ita,don kina ganin ina wofintar da ita saboda ke?"
Kuka take sosai ta rike hannunsa hankalinta ya tashi ganin yadda yanuna bacin rai,Allah wadaran xuciyarta......."narokeka karkayi fushi bazan iya jure fushinka ba wlh bazan sake maimaitawa ba, kuma ganinai ba sonka take ba bata damu da duk abunda zamuyi gabanta ba shiyasa,kuma kaima.......kaima naga ....naga basont........ciki tsawa yace *"na gaya maki?nayi dake?ehy nake Asmau?ko bana ma nadeeya son soyayya,ina jinta cikin jinina,kuma ita din ba abar wulakantawar ki bace don bakisan ko wacece ita ba awajena!,ehy zakice kingani inayi wannan ma hujjace wajenki,to daga yau zan gyara kuma inhar bakison fushina bakison bacin rain kije kibata hkr"*

Yajuya ya fice cikin zafin rai wanda yakejin kamar yafashe don bakin ciki,baitajin irin hakaba aduk sadda yake mata wani abun na wulakanci,Amma yau da wani yayi bashi ba  sai yakejin kamar zai haukace,duk soyayyar da yakewa Asmau yaji ya nemeta ya rasa don ya nuna mata kuskurenta,wanda shine babban mai kuskure ba ita ba,ko karyawa baiba yafice office gashi yau sunada tattaunawa da ogansu kan case din dasuke cikin yi dare da rana,anya yanada natsuwar da har zai zauna meeting din nan kuwa,amma zaman gidan bazai masa dadi ba.bangare guda na zuciyarsa yaa gaya masa nadeeya nacan na kuka,dafe kai yayi jikin mota.
"Ya Allah! Kar ciwonta ya tashi nasan ba karamin kuka zata sha ba."

Baida yadda ya iya yashiga motar yabar gida shida driver dake tukashi..........

Episode  .19


Yini xungur  ban saka komi acikina ba,kuma tunda na rude kofar ban budeba har Asmau ta gama zaryarta inajinta,saidai tazo ta tsaya tana son tayi knocking amma girman kan tsiyarta ya hana,taje ta dawo taje ta dawo haka ta ringayi,naja tsaki na tashi sadda na gama kukana na lallashi kaina,don idan nabiyewa kukan nice masha wuya,bakin gado na koma ina ayyana yadda zan rama wannan kalar cin kashin da sukai mun,ba ma dai kamar shi xucyata tafi daukar zafi dashi fiye da Asma,u don ita nasan zafin kishine zai kaita ga gaya mun magana,wai yadda nazo haka zan koma!duk ciki wanan kalmar rafi mun zafi,nayi k'wata ina jingina bayana da gadon,littafina na dauka ina duba inda natsaya wani tunani yake xuwarmun,wanda nake ganin ta haka zan rama abunda sukai mun daga ita harshi.
Labarin da nake karantawa salon da star din tayi anfani dashi yayi matukar kayatar dani,littafin akwai love maishiga jiki ban sanin ina murmushi,injawo filo in makale inajin nishadin yadda lamura suke faruwa,to amma ke ai duk baki iya wannan ba,soyayyar ma bakisan ta yadda zaki gane kina son wanin ba balle har ki iya nuna masa irin naki salon!,taya zaki koyi da abunda wata tayi cikin book?.
Duk xuciyarna ce take gaya mani haka,ajiye book din nayi inajan tsaki,cikin haushi na mike ina dage labulen tagar dakin ina iskar waje na shigowa mai dadi,.

"Ban iya komi ba sai tsabar rashin kunya da rashin tarbiyya!"

Kalamansa kenan all d time,uhmmmm "ban iya komi ba kam,amman ina kan koya kuma narantse saika gane shayi ruwane,nidai ko?nikuke rainawa?uhmmmm muje xuwa lokaci yana nan ai,wallahi inhar nice Nadeeya jafar saika dan dana kudarka kema haka Asmau."
Agogona ya buga karfe shadaya sai lokacin yunwa tataso mun,cikina har wani sauti yake ba dawa.
Duk yadda ban sonfita dole nafito din,ina complain din ajiye kayan tea main falo,in adalcu zaiyyi ya siya kowace nata yabata ta ajiye falonta mana,fuskata tayi fayau babu mai babu kwalli,idona daka ganshi kasan nasha kuka don yayi ja kadan,tana zaune main falon tayi ta gumi da waya ahannunta,ko inda take ban sake duba  na wuce kitchen.
Duk da naga kayan kari saman dining table kin xuwa nai inyi,nafara kokarin kunna gas don xuwa yanxu har nakan dafa indomi,na soya kwai harda dankali har farar shinkafa ina dafawa abubuwan dana koya wajen inna suna da yawa gaskiya,pan na dora ina xuba mai kadan......alamun tahowarta naji na sake shan mur,na juya ga frig din dake kitchen din na dauko tarugu biyu albasa daya,na rufe.ganin man yayi zafi kuma banyi greeting din kayan miyar ba saina kashe gas din,na dauko greater nafara aikina.
Tsaye tayi bakin kofar kitchen din ko nauyi bakinta yayi mata data kasa fadar abunda ya kawota oho,ta dai tsaya kamar icce tana kare mun kallo,har nagaji na juya ina tsareta da nawa idon tubarkallah.

"Malama lafiya?ya zakixo ki tsareni da kallo ina aiki,pls bana so"

Kallona take idonta da kwallah nai kamar van gani ba na cigaba da aikina na gama greeting din na xuba cikin pan din,na sake kunna wa ya soyu na tsaida ruwa daidai bukatata.

"Amm...m........daman hkr zan baki."

Jinjina kai nayi ina bude drower na dauko noodles guda biyu kananu na ajiye gabana,ban bata ansa ba na dauko kwai hudu na ajiye,na fasa kwan na sa masa magi ina kadashi naji ta sake magana.

"Kuskurene hakan bazata sake faruwa ba,kuma ga break can nayi ki kashe basai kin dafa komi ba"

Idan gas dina yayi magana nima nayi,na cigaba da dahuwata na dafa indomie dina sai kamshi take,na soya kwaina na hado tea dina mai kauri sannan na wanke kwanonin nakoma daki.saida naci nai k'at na maida kwanonin kitchen suma na wanke,na dawo dakina na cigaba da karatuna ina tsara dramer da za,ai anjima.
(Wohoho yar rigima abun ya motsa kenan😄)


Karfe hudu suke tashi daman ranar weekend,hankalinsa duk yayo gida duk yadda yakejin dadin kasancewar case din sa yana gaf da shigowa kotu,idan ya tuna rigimar dake gida har gabansa faduwa yake,tsoronsa guda wannan yarinyar ciwonta yatashi aita rigima da ita.
"Yadai Angon Asmau da Nadeeya?"
Nura yake tsokanarsa lokacin ya sa mesa yana hada hadar tafiya gida,cikin damuwa yace "kyaleni da maganar angon Asmau da wata nadeeya,wallahi yaran nan nema suke su caza mun kai"

Dariya nura yayi yace"Au haba,ai nadauka nasarar da muka samu bazata barka kayi wani kunci ba abokina,meyake faruwa"

Shafa kanshi yayi yana girgiza kai yace"haka ne inacikin farin cikin hakan,amman har banson tunkarar gida wlh,"
Yadan bashi labarin Abunda ya faru safiyar yau din,......"Bantaba zaton Asmau zata dauki hakan matsayin ban dauki deeya da muhimmanci ba,sai yau datake goranta mata."

Dafa kafadarsa Nura yayi yace"come down Mana Musaddiq,karka bani kunya wannan baika abunda zai saka jin tsoron xuwa gidan ba,kayi kuskure bai dace kana nuna wa gaban Asmaun ba tunda kanta zayyi girma ganinbaka shiga sabgar nadeeya,its mean baka sonta ita kake so,so don haka daman dole zata ringa mata kallon shakatafi,kuma xubin nadeeyah shizai sata yin kishi da ita mai zafi,ganin babu abunda ta dara deeya dashi.abunda kurin zakai shine gyarawa,barakar daka bude ka gyarata kaku ma ba deeya hkr Asmau kuma kaja mata kunne,kaifa ke bani hanyoyin da zan bi da mata amman yau abun yazo kanka duk ka gigice."
Ajiyar xuciya ya sauke yace"bazaka gane zama da mata biyu ba abokina,shiyasa dana kammala aikina doke zan sawwakewa deeyah,don daman da manufa na aureta kuma bata sona bana sonta zaman mu bazai dadi ba, gara idan komi successful saina sawake mata taje ta auri wanda takeso"
Murmushi nura yayi yace"Alright we shall see,katashi kaje gidanka cikin compidence ni nayi nan"

Wayarsa keta ruri tunaninsa Asmau ce shiyasa tun dazun bai dauka ba,don tunda yataho take kiransa so yake tayi hankali yadda bazata sake kokarin bata masa gida ba,zaro wayar yayi yaga Anty Aysher..,..........."na tuba Anty,wlh shaf mancewa nake duba da ayyuka dasuka tasoni gaba,ga rigingimu anata famanyi shiyasa nake mancewa,Amman yau kafin dare zakijimu."

"Wlh inabazo mun da pretty yau ba zaka gamu a fushina,tun monday nake garin nan inata faman binka kana kiyawa"

"Insha Allah yau kam kafata kafar pretty"

"Saina jika" ta kashe wayar.
Jikkarsa ya dauka ya fito secretary dinsa yana rufe  office din.

"Assalamu alaikum"
Yayi sallama part din inna don yau ko gaisuwar safe basuyi ba yafita cikin bacin rai.

"Was,alaika salam,sadiqu kaine antaso kenan"

"Wlh kuwa ina wuni inna"

"Lafiya lau ya aikin naku"

"Da godiya inna daman xuwa nayi nasanar dake abun farincikin daya faru yau wajen aikinmu"
Cikin jin dadi da farin ciki tace"kai masha Allah meya faru gaya mun dan nan"
Murmushi yayi ya fiddo ta kadda daga aljihunsa yace"wannan dai wani aikine aka turamu nida nura syprus,zamu tafi karin kwarewa ta fannin aikinmu,zamuyo wata shidda.amman tafiyar sai nan da one week,sannan abu mai muhimmanci da muka jima muna fatan tabbatuwarsa shine *JAFAR DAULA YASHIGO HANNU INNA*"

Daga kan kujera ta sauko kasa gabansa tana riko hannunsa cikin farin ciki da jin dadi tace"dagaske sadiqu!,dagaske ankamashi? wannan labari da dadi yake,kai Alhamdulillahi Allah Abun godiya sannu, sannu da kokari sadiqu,ubangiji ya cigaba da taimakonka akoda yaushe,ya kareka da ga sharrin makiyanka,Allah yajikan mahaifanka ya kai rahma kabarinsu dole agodewa Allah."

Jiyayi kwalla tana silalo masa daga kwarin idonsa bai shirya ma hakan ba,wasu na bin wasu hanky ya ciro yana tsanewa,tausayinsa ya kama inna itama taji kamar ta fashe da kukan.

"Kayi kuka kam sadiqu,wannan azzalumi Allah ne zai daukar maku mataki,kayi hkr ka cigaba da hkr dauriyar nan dana sanka da ita kacigaba dayi,haka Allah yaso daman."

"Nagode inna banda bakin yi maki godiya,keda kawu kune jigon komi nawa,bazan taba mantawa daku ba."

"Kabar gode mana Sadiqu,Allah shine abun godiya,shine yake taimakonka akoda yaushe.yanzu shi ina Abas din to"

"Hmm inna shima wannan ai munafuki ne,anbiyo ta wajensa don neman sace deeyah,ranar da muka taho masaniyar hakan nasamu shiyasa na dauki mataki da wuri,ya hada kai da minister Abdulkadir,akan yasa masa makudan kudi inhar kawo masa deeyah.......wallahi daman tun da fari ban dora yardata akanshi ba,tun farko daman baison aurenta da wannan jaded din,yaron minister,yaje yashiga ya fita yaron yace ko sama da kasa zata hade bazai auri yarinyar ba.
So yake yasamu wanda zai dauketa daga hannunsu J dashi Minister din,don idan antashi ansota yasamu makudan kudi don yabude nashi hanun jarin ya kafa kanshi,tunda komi karkashin j yake samu,ya gaji da gori,shiyasa suka hada baki da minister akan yabashi miliyan talatin shikuma zai kawo mashi deeyah da duk takaddun kadarorinta,baisan kar nake kallonsa ba.shima tuni ankamo dan iskan yanxu minister ya rage akai ma sammaci"

"Oh ni saude wannan duniya ina zaki damu,kowa inya samu dama sai ya yi abunda ransa keso,akan kudi babu abunda mutane yanzu bazasu iyaba.ubangiji ya cigaba da tona masu asiri har muga bayansu"

Kanshi yashafa yanajin yana ciwo yace"Ameen inna bari naje nadan watsa ruwa ga yunwa ga gajiya"
Sukai sallama ya wuce can part dinsu,inda hali soyake ya huta amma yasan yarinyar can nacan rigimarta na jiransa.

Afalo yaci karo da asmau tunda ya mata kallo guda bai sake waiwayar inda take ba,xunbur tatashi zata biyo sa..........."I need to rest pls don't disturb me"
Ya wuce yana jin babu dadi sosai yaji yayi missing dinta yau,Amma yaso ya koya mata tattalin bacin ransa ne kurin badan yana jin dadin abunda yakeyin ba.
Daidai dakin deeyah ya tsaya,bazai wice baisan halin da take ciki ba.
Asmau na gani yabude kofar falonta yashiga,jitai wani kundu memen kullutu ya tokare wuyanta,fashewa tayi da kuka tanajin kamar tayi bindiga "ya Allah ka sassauta mun wannan kishin mai zafi,idan haka tacigaba da faruwa dani xuciyata bugawa zatai"ta fada cikin kuka mai taba rai.

Tun kafin ya karasa yake jiyo karan kukan ta.  dagaske kamar zata sike,afalon ya saki jikkarsa yana kara sawa da gudu cikin dakin nata yabanka kofar,daman ina sane nabarta bude tunda naji shigowarsa daxun naketa tsara plan dina yadda zai kasance,shigowarsa yasani sakin murmushin mugunta ina soke da kaina cikin cinya,gashina ya watse ko ina najikina,nayi kaca kaca da dakin,kayan jikina kuma legens ne sai top banjima da fitowa wanka ba,na tsara wannan plan din na karanta cikin book dina danake karantawa inga ko zanci nasara inba a dace ba incanxa wata.............durkushewa yayi gabana duk ya rude yana dago kaina cikin son sanin halin da naked ciki,kuka nake sosai nasa hannuna duka na dafe cikina inacewa.................

Episode  .20


"Wallahi idan na mutu akan cin zalina da kuke bazan yafe ba,wayyo Allah cikina nashiga uku,cikina ciwo yake mutuwa zanyi"
Fixge fixge nake ina durkushe na rike cikin mata kau,ga hawaye kamar an bude famfo,sai halba kafa nake ina gaza wanda ina tunanin shiya janyo Asmau shigowa.
Shikuwa duk ya rude yama rasa ta ina zaifara,tallabo fuskata yayi da duka hannayesa da najisu sunyi sanyi kalau,cikin son na maido hankalina kanshi yake cewa.
"Shiiiit nadeeya kallani,bude idonki ki kalleni gaya mun meyafaru bayan fitata,mekikaci cikin yake ciwo."
Ture hannunsa nayi ina fasa kara ina durkushewa.
"Babu ruwanka malam,ko mutuwa nai bakada asara"
Zaman dirshen yayi gabana ya jawoni dukana donba wani nauyine dani ba,ya dora kan cinyarsa yasake riko fuskata still.
"To yi hkr gaya mun menene"
Kwantar da kaina nayi kan kirjinsa duk da yadda nakejin gabana yana faduwa,tsoro yana shigata na daure na shagwabe murya ina kuka ahankali na nuna Asmau da take kallon mu cike da kuna.
"Kawai don kuna son na bar maku duniyar sai ko horani da yunwa,tun safe ban ci komi ba gashinan cikina ciwo yakeyi."
Ajiyar xuciya ya sauke yana kallo Amaun,raurau tayi da idonta tace cikin raunin murya, "wallahi yaya saida..........karya take yi kurin don kunga ni banda kowa banida mai sona,duk kun tsaneni ko."
Relex yayi yana kallon fuskarta da tayi fayau sai ta kara kyau sosai,dan bakinta sai turosa take murmushi keson kubce mai ya dan dake yafara ma Asmaun magana cikin fada fada,.
"Abun da kikai kenan Asmau,bance duk sanda kikai sanwa kigaya mata tazo taci ba?shine don baninan kuma kinajin haushinta zaki mata horon yunwa,to da ban dawo da wuri ba ya kenan?kisan kai kikeson koya ko"
Hawaye suka silalo mata tana zama kasa don jiri na neman kadata tarike hannunsa,zatai magana nasa nawa na kabe hannun ina harararta.ni kaina dariya nake wa kaina cikin xuciya.
Cikin kukan tace"wallahi yaya nazo na gaya mata,kuma har hkr na bata bata ce mu komi ba"

"Dalla can karya banza,yaushe kika gaya mun inajinta fa yaya tana ta zarya bakin kofata,tana jiyo yadda nake nishi Amma bata shigo taga halin dana ke ciki ba,ni baxan cigaba da zama daku ba nagaji wlh ni duniya zan shiga."
Ina rushewa da sabon kuka na gashina duk ya hargitse.
Ihunsa ne kurin bai fitoba amman yarinyar nan na niyar zautar dashi,sake sa idonsa yayi cikin nawa yana goge mun hawayena yace"sorry Asmau bata kyauta ba,bazata sake ba kuma yanzu zan sata ta dafa maki abunda kikeso..........bana son nata banci nata abinci a waje nakeson naje naci"

"Shikenan to,kukan ya kare ko Asmau bata hkr zata hkr daga yau haka bazata sake faruwa ba,kuma Asmau bazata sake maki maganar da bata dace ba,kinan damu babu inda zakije"

Har lokacin ina kan cinyarsa asmau na kuka kasa kasa tace"kiyi hkr nadiya bazan sake ba,insha Allah"

Yaleko fuskata "kinyi hkr ko?"
Harararsa nayi na daga kai kurin.

"Shikenan kije Asmau tace ta hkr,kema kiyi hkr."
Kamar zata kifa tatashi ta fita xuciyarta kamar garwashi.

"To dagani naje nayi wanka sainaxo na kaiki inda zakici abincin,"ya fada yana yaye gashina daya baje ajikina da fuskata.
"Ya kamata kije saloon wannan gashin ban tunanin kina wankeshi"
Tashi nayi ina juya baya ribon dina dana cire na wurgar daxun na dauko ina daure kan,bance masa kala ba.
Bayana ya tsaya idonsa kamar maijin bacci saboda gajiyar dayake dauke da ita,yajiyo dani ina fuskantarsa ya langabe kai yana kallona,dauke idona nayi na maida kasa inajin duk nakasa sukuni,tunda plan A ya tafi daidai burina yatafi kar xuciyata tafito don dukan da take.
Hanunsa yasa yana gore silin gashin dayaje reto,wanda na saki agaan fuskata.
"Yau kwanan kine hajiya,kin shirya tarbata ko?"
Dagowa nai ina zaro manyan idanuna masha Allah, "rage girmansu pls karki cinyeni"
Na saukesu kasa har time din labbana nauyi suke mun,ni ban saba da wannan yanayin ba.
"Oya muje kitaya ni shiryawa agajiye nake sosai,rigimarki tasa na mance ma kaina na ciwo sai yanxu."
Makale kafada nayi nace cikin siririyar muryata data canza amo "nidai katafi don Allah,nace ai ana son kwanakin da kuke rabawar,wlh har raina nabar maku"
Murmushin da bai shirya ba yayi yana koma wa bakin bed dina ya kwanta yana dafe da kanshi.
"Idan da har xuciyarki kin bar mata ni why kike ji haushin abun da yaru yau da safe"
Jiya masa baya nai ina kallon fuskata jikin madubi,banda wannan ansar bansan meyasa ba "amman ai hakan baya nufin inason kwanan ba"
Na samu kaina da fadan hakan,na bude robar cream zan shafa.

"Karki shafa shi don ko kinshafa sai kin sake wani wankan,ba dai kwanan ki kikewa fushi ba. daga yau kinshirya ansar su kenan har mahadi ya bayyana."

Tsoro ya kamani ina zaro idona zanyi wani kukan "ni wallahi ba haka ba,bana fushi dashi don Allah kayi hkr bana son kwanan,mezanamaka to badai gani cikin gidan ba kuma kaima kana nan,hakan ya wadatar"
Dariya yayi yace"wasa kenan,abunda Asmau take mun kike ma kishi kema shi zan maki,ai ban dauka har kiyayyar tawa ta kare ba,keda ke cewa duk duniya bakida makiyi sama dani,to har na zama masoyi kenan duka bamu cika 3week ba."
Duk na tsorata hakika nasha fadar hakan. Muryata da kuka nace"Amman kuma ni banji ai inason ka ba,kuma bazansoka ba har abada, kawai don na nuna bacin raina kan wulakancin da matarka tayi mun shine kishin?"

"Zakiyi bayani ne yarinya,duk randa kika fada sona zakisha wuya"
Ya fada yana bude kofata yafita.
Zaman dirshen nayi tsakar dakin ina hawaye,xuciyata nake tambaya.
"Wai dagaske na fara sonsa?aidai karya yake yiko?wlh niba zan soka ba kingin watan zan so?"na fashe da kuka wanda bansan dalilin yinsa ba,meyasa ma nayi wannan abun,in hakane bazan yi plan A din ba don shima cewa zaiyyi sonsa nake.kuma ni bana sonsa bazan taba sonka ba,sai na sake rushewa da kuka.

Dakin Asmau ya nufa yasan tana can tana kuka sallama yayi tayi saurin tashi tana goge kwallah.
"Wallahi yaya ban barta da yunwa ba,bata fito ba sai shadaya, tashiga kitchen ita da kanta tafara dora sanwa,nace mata ga break nan nayi tazo tayi banza tayi ta kyaleni,ta dafa indominta da kwai tahada tea tashige taje taci,don ta wanke containers din dana nuna maka,sharri tayi mun don Allah kayi hkr"
Tana fashewa da kuka rungumota yayi yana lallashinta, "ya isa haka ma,una nasani karya takeyi ai bani zata yiwa wannan game din na yarda ba,don karta gane nasan plan ta shirya shiyasa nayi maki fada gabanta,sorry komi ya wuce fatan bazaki sake janta da fada ba tsakaninku mutunta juna bana so waan zama kinajia,keface babba uwargidana kuma uwar baby's dina karki xubar da kimarki wajenta kinjiko"
Daga kai tayi ya lallasheta sosai saida yaga tayi dariya sannan yajuya yaje dakinsa.

Kayan dake jikina na cire na dauko wata riga arebiyan gown,bakace amman kwalliyar da akai mata akwai red color ajiki da stones suma akwai red aciki,sai pink color.
Tayi mata kyau ainun,kamar don ita akai gown din,turarenta ta saka mai shiga rai.kunsan bakin abu yana ma farare kyau haka ta koma kamar balarabiyar gaske.
Don ma fuskar ta babu walwala daka ganta kasan tana cikin damuwa,ganin lokacin la,asar ya wuce banyi salla ba yasa nai saurin shimfida pray mate na kabbatar sallar,najima kadan kafin na gama nayi azkar dina na yammasannan na fito daga uwar dakar.
zama nai falo na kunna kallo wanda ban cika kunnawav ba,mostly nafi kunna speaker's dina insa karatu.
Zee wold ce suke mahik inabinsa sadda ina gidan daddy film din yana masifar yimun kyau,amman yau bana dokinsa don har cikin raina atsorace nake,if maganarsa ta zama gaskiya ya zanyi ni nadeeya?girgiza kai nayi ia fada afili idona cike da kwallah.
"Aa har Abadan abidina haka bazata faru ba."

Hanky na sake hannuna nasa ina tsane kwallar saboda jin anbude kofar falona.
Ban taba samun kaina cikin fargaban hafuwa dashi ba sai yau,haka duk sanda zaishigo ko mu hafu banajin gabaa kaar zai fito waje,,haka duk zare idon da zaimun wani lokavi idan muna fada bana fasa kallon idonsa ko fuskarsa.Amma yau why?why? Duk nake kasawa,shigowa yake cikin takunsa na halitta,hanunsa guda cikin aljihun wandonsa jeans blue.shikadai zan iya fadar color dinsa Saboda nan kurin idona yake iya kallo.............sai mayen turarensa wanda tun fil,azal nasansa dashi koban kallai ba tabas yayi kyau,don bai taba saka abunda bai mashi kyau ba,...........

"Muna iya tafiya rankiya dade"
Tashi nai kurin ban bari ma na dago fuskata ba balle har muyi ido hudu.

Hanya ya bani na shige gaba yana kashe tv sannan ya rufa mun baya,Asmau na zaune main falo muka fito,itama kallon take yace"baby love zamu fita sai mun dawo kina da tsaraba,"murmushi tayi wanda na karfin haline tace"ehy yaya inason shawarma ita kadai zaka siyo mun."
Fuskarsa ta fadada murmushi yadawo kusada ita ya zauna hannun kujera yana dukowa saitin fuskarta,cikin tsokana yace "ma,una kodai mun samu dan shawarma ne"dariya tayi shima yana taya ta tace"aa kurin dai ita nakeso"

"Kina boye mun ko?zan dawo saikin gaya mun gaskiya by by"

Itama tana masa by by din ni kam dai gaba nai inajin tahowarsa,duk sai naji bana son fitar,don daman ba wata yunwa nakeji ba.
Tsaye nai har yazo ya bude motar na bude gaban nashiga don bankai gatan da zai bude mun ba,kuma ni ba sunana Asmau ba.
Sai naji wani zafi araina amman ban bari hakan yayi tasiri kaina ba,.
Inata tunanina har muka fito daga get,.

Tunda yashiga falonta kwakliyarta tayimasa kyau,duk da baiga zahirin fuskarta ba,don ta kasa kallonsa,yajima baiga macen da jallabiyar larabawa take maidata balabiya ba irin deeyah,sau tari sai yake ganin taso tafi *Ammerh* kyau kodan ta hado jinin fulani,shiyasa kyan ya so yayi mata ma yawa,tukinsa yake amman shirunta yana tabashi ya lura kamar bata cikin walwala.
Hannunta da take wasa dashi yake kallo,kafin ya mika hannunsa ya rikosa wanda sai sannan hankalina ya dawo jikina.
Sai sanan mukai ido hudu dashi,wanda ji kamar yana sukata da wani abu.

Girarsa ya daga mani yace"what's wrong with u"
Girgiza kai nayi kawai don bakina ma nauyi yayi mun.

"Duk yunwarce ko menene,?ko plan din nakine bai tafi yadda kike so ba,"
Dagowa nai ina kallonsa yadda ya gane plan nashirya.

Murmushi yayi yace"mekika daukan ne deeyah,uhm na ganoki ai Amman daga yau kisan kalar wanda zakiyi da bazan gane ba."

Ban san meya saka kwalla fito mun ba har suna bin kuncina,bakuma don yagane shiri nayi ba kunyace da danasanin yin hakan,gashinan zaimun wata daukar daban.

"Kukan me kike to,wai duk ina masifar na taki badai har ansamu abunda ya ladaftar dake ba."

Cikin kuka nace masa"pls ka kyaleni naji da abunda yake damuna,inba sokake ka kure hkr na ba,ni nama fasa cin abincin ka maidani gida."

Dariya yayi yace"sai kinci sa wlh bazan yi fitowar banza ba,gobe ma saiki dora inda kika tsaya mr plan"
Ya fada yana dariya ban sake kulasa ba har muka isa wani wajen cin abinci mai kyau.
Ferfesun zallar ya ciki yace kawo hade da fride rice mai kaza,sai maltina da hollandia.
Tunda kuma zauna wani guy yake kallona daga bayan musaddiq din,yi nayi kamar ban ganshi ba.

Dora kafa daya kan daya yayi yana kallona duk saina kasa cin komi,yadda yake bina da ido duk sai xuciyata ta kama rawa,kamar zan kuka nace "pls zan kware inkana kallona"
"Keda baki fara ci ba kwarewar mi zakiyi,naga ai ba yau nafara tsareki kikaci abinci ba."

"To yau nidai bazan iya ciba inkana kallona,ka koma mota in nagama saina sameka"

Wani kallo ya watsa mun yace"ashe zamu kwana nan " dole ba yadda na iya haka nafara cin abincin dana dago idonsa akaina saidai ya daga mun gira kurin,ga kua wancen mutumen dake bayasa shima kamar zai hadiye ni.

Ferfesun yan cikin ya mun dadi sosai duk nafi cinsa shinkafar ma naci ba yawa kazarce dai batayi mun dadi ba,na tura mashi ina ya mutsa fuska.
"Kaci kaji babu dadi wlh."
Yaja gabasa yasa fork yana ci yana satar kallona,haka kamar kurame mukaita zama saidai satar kallon juna har sadda na gama ya biya kudin muka fito.
Sannan shidda tayi ana kan gabda kiran sallah............"excuse me"
Duk muka juya muna kallonsa wannan dai wanda yaketa kallona ne daxun.
Idonsa akaina yace"don Allah yayan mu magana nakeson nayi da kanwarka minty biyar yayi yawa pls"
Kauda kai nayi dariya na son kubce mun saboda kallon da naga yana auna mashi duk anurinsa ya dauke.
Nai saurin cewa "yaya bari naji dame yazo just one minute...........muryarsa da bacin rai yana zaro mun idonsa kamar zasu fito yace"xoki shiga mota"kai kuma ka kama gabanka kafin katafi babu kafafu,yan iskan banza haka kuke agana da matan jamaa"
Ai ban sake ganin wulgin mutumen ba.
Afusace ya shigo motar yana fada kamar zai kaimun duka.
"Idan ke bakisan darajar aure ba ni nasani mahaukaciyar banza,kenan da nabarki binsa zakiyi saboda kanki yana rawa?kibari randa na sawwake maki saiki tsaya da duk dan iskan da kika ga dama,amman banda igiyar aurena akanki."
Ban san ina kuka ba saboda fadan yayi yawa ban zaci abun zai kai har haka ba ni nayi don na tsokanesa amman ji yadda yake mun tijara,ashe akwai lokacin daya kayyade zai bani saki..........dago jajajen idon nayi nace masa"mai zai hana ka sawwake mun yanzu,mai zai saka sai wani lokaci ?"
Muryata da rauni wanda nakejin xuciyata kamar an yanketa.
Fixgar motar yayi kaina yabugu da karfi har saida na fasa kara,ina dafe goshin banga alamun ya damu ba,fixgar motaryake kamar Allah ya aikoshi ba abunda nake  sai kuka don abun yafi karfina,........bai kulani ba tafiya kawai yake cikin fushi aikuwa na fusata na hau balbalin bala,i nima.
"Dalla malam karka  kasheni ka saukeni inhar ba zakai tuki hankali ba,kawai daga wani yamun magana ehy,yadda kake wulakantani kowa ma haka kakeson yayi akaina ko,to walahi da nasan zakaji haushi har haka saina kukashin karshe ka bi takaina da mota karewar kana buga mun kai ga glass,wlh duk salon cuta ka iya miye laifina aciki iye..............wani uban burki ya taka gaban wani babban gida mai shegen kyau idonsa yayi jajawur yace cikin tsawa "miye laifinki kike tambaya?nace miye laifin ki kike tambaya ko?dayake bakida cikekken hankali ai shiyasa ba zaki gane ba stupid."
Nima tsawar nayi masa ina kuka har muryata tana dishewa"karka sake kirana mahaukaciya kuma niba stupid bace,wai komi kai saka nuna baka kaunat,m tare maka aduniyar nan ne,wanda kake azabtar dani,nagaji haka wlh nagaji..........na durkushe ina rasgar kukana get din gidan naji anbue,kafin naji tsayuwar mota kusada mu,ban damu da ganin ko waye ba kukana kurin nake.kafin naji muryar mace tana fitowa daga motar.......... *"MUSADDIQ MEKAKEYI HAKA?MEKAYIWA YARINYAR NAN,ASHE RASHIN HKRINKA HAR YAKAI HAKA"*
