Episode  .21


Ban motsa daga inda nake ba naji tadurkusa gabana ta dagoni,inata kukan har muryar ta dishe ba ajinsa sosai.muryarta da fada take cemasa,"kai baka iya sarrafa xuciyarke ne wai?duba kaga yada take kuka har muryarta ta dishe,ko tausayinta bakaji ne wai?ka kyauta gara dana gani azahiri."
Ina jikinta ta rike hannuna muka nufi get din gidan,driver ta yana shiga da motar tata muka barsa nan tsaye,kafin daga bisani yashigo da tashi motar.sanan har munbi ta wata hanya ta sadamu da cikin falonta,ya hadu shima sosai tsayawa fadar hakan bata bakine,yan yaranta mace da namiji ne afalon suna kallo gakuma home work sunayi.
Oyoyo suka taso suna yiwa ummin tasu ganin bakuwa yasa sukai burki suna kallonsu,wucewa mukai da ita ta wata kofa xuwa wani corridor kafin mu karasa wani bed room.
Zaunar dani tayi lokacin sai ajiyar xuciya kurin nake saukewa,saboda tsabar kukan dana sha sai lokacin na gane ashe likitar nan ce data dubani sadda asthma ta tatashi wancen lokacin.
"Kiyi hkr kinji nadeya,yayanku bashida hkr ko kadan."
Girgiza mata kai nayi wasu hawayen na xubowa nace"Aa anty,akaina kurin yake gwada rashin hakrinsa,"
Inajin wani sabon kukan yana tahowa.
"Kibarni dashi ina zuwa."
Mikewa tayi zata fita sai gashi ya shigo fuskar nan babu walwala ko kadan.
Harararsa tayi tace"matsa bani waje,macuci kawai ashe izayar da kake gana mata kenan."
Cikin shakakkar murya yace"Anty kibar yimun fada don baki san abunda tayi ba kema sai kinyi abunda nayi."
Turesa tayi tana fita tabarmu nidashi cikin dakin,gyalen jallabiyata na cire na ajiye gefe saboda zafi ma ya isan ga xufa nayi nasha kuka,ko kadan ban dago ba balle na kalleshi.amma shi idonsa kamar zai fado saboda kallon,bansan kowane irin kallo yake mun ba ama jikina yaban kallona yaketa yi.
Shigowa tayi da tire ga hannunta jug ne da cup sai plet biyu arufe su daya saman daya.,tajawo table ta dora sannan ta wuceshi tabude kofar toilet ta dauko mun towel......."kina ta xufa ki watsa ruwa sannan kixo kidan sha wani abu,Allah zai saka maki kinjiko."
Ansa nayi na mike na nufi wata yar coner da aka zagaye da wani cobet,na cire rigar na dauro towel din na dauki hijab dinta nasa sanna na fito.
Kwata naji yayi yana bina da kallo kafin yace"Anty sai faman goyon bayanta kike wallahi niba zalintarta nai ba,itace ta zalinc..........shiru kuma yayi yana jan karamin tsaki kafin yace"nan da kike ganin ta bata da hankali."
Afusace ta jiyo tace"kaine babban marar hankali,wallahi idan ka kara kiranta marar hankaki ranka in yayi dubu saina bata maka shi."
Shigewa nai kewayen nayi wanka na nakuma ji dadinsa sosai,sai jan ajiyar xuciya nake inajinsu tana masa fada kamar zata dakesa.
Banjima ba nafito don ba dauda gareni ba,kaya na gani ta ajiye mun riga da wando,silic ne kayan masu sulbi gaba dayansu red ne da zanen fulawoyi ajiki.
Dryer ta dauko tana busar mun da kaina ni kuma ina shafa cream,take cewa "wanan gashin nadeya baki kula dashi duk yayi firji firji,babu mai ajiki da alamun yana da dauda."
Muryata data kara yin sanyi saboda kuka nace mata"bana saloon shiyasa,agida duk bayan sati uku nake xuwa tunda bana kitso"
Kallonsa tayi ta tabe baki cikin fada tace"kaji haushi wlh,sanan kace mun kana kula da ita yada ya kamata."
Gajiya yayi da tsayuwar yasilale kasan kafet din dakin ya zauna,kamar zai kuka yace "tunda ni kin kasa tambayata ita gata nan ki tambayata Abunda tayi."
Banza tayi dashi ta cigaba da gyara mun kaina,mayuka kala kala take shafamun wanda yasa naji kan nawa yana dawo cikin hayacinsa,iska mai dadi tana kwararawa ciki.
Na gama naje nasa kayan nadawo na zauna kasa daga gefe,ta sauko mun tiren ta ajiye gabana. Taceccen inibi ne ta xuba mun ga cup,ta miko mun.
Tana cewa sha ki gaya mun duk abunda yake maki yau zan daukar maki mataki,wannan ya nuna tunda ya daukoki yake gana maki masifa."
Ansa nayi inajin kwalla na cika idona,kurba guda nai naji mugun dadinsa yana kaimun har cikin kwalwata,ban san sadda na shanye ba.
Kafin nafara magana muryata na rawa "Anty wallahi tunda na baro gidan daddy ban samu kwanciyar hankali ba,kullin da kalar masifar dazai xomun duk abunda nayi cikin ihu da fada yake mun magana,yaringa xagina yana kirana mahaukaciya marar hankali,sannan yasa matarsa kanta ta samu damar maidamun nata martanin,saboda ganin irin yadda ya maidani bolarsa............kuka nasa ina cewa "Anty kimun rai bazan koma gidansa ba,don Allah nidai kibarni wajenki wlh wataran xuciyata kama wa da wuta zatai"
Rungumo kaina tayi tana lallashina tare da share mun hawaye, "ya isa haka,naji wannan me ya haddasa fadanku na mota.?"
"Ki gaya mata sharrin dakikai wa Asmau,nasan kunya zataji tayi karya alhalin kuma ba haka bane,"
Ya gaya Mata abunda ya faru da safe,da fadan da yayiwa Asmaun sai kuma matakin da ita nadeyar ta dauka matsayin ramuwa............"ni banyi da nufin nayi mata sharri ba,Allah yaga xuciyata"
Nafada batare dana kallai ba,kallonta yake baisan meyakeji aransa ba,yace "to da niyyar mekikai?uhmm kinji dai bata gaskiya duk wannan bai isheta ba saida na daukota na kawota taci abinci,shine wani ya biyo mu wai na bashi ita minty biyar zai magana da ita!Anty wai yarinyar nan don bata da hankali wai zata bisa,bisa fa tajiya zatai wlh da ban dakatar da ita ba saita bishi,nikuma raina yabaci na mata fada kuma shine take cewa ita bataga laifinta ba anan,abunda ya sake harxuka ni kenan kuma don sharri ni banyi kuka ba sai ita,wlh kin kware wajen sharri"
Yafada yana harararta wadda ban san yanayi ba kaina kasa yake.

"Akan dan wannan abun zaka nemi hauda mata jini khalipa?uhmmm ai bata haka zaka nuna kana kishinta ba."
Dago kai yayi nima idona akanta cikin ya mutsa fuska yace"miye kishi kuma,Anty kishin me zanyi akan wannan yarinyar?ni kurin abunda yaban haushi aurena da take niyyar maidawa abun banza"
Murmushi tayi tace mun"kyaleshi zanyi masa fada kinji ko ga dambun kifi nan,da yamboll nasan ba yunwa sosai tare dake kici bari naje nadawo."
Tabude kofar ta fita tabarmu dashi,a kasalance nake cin dambun kifin wanda yaji magi da tarugu daidai misali,duk yadda yake kallona yinayi ban san dashi a wajen ba,wanda hakan baisa ya daina bina da mayen idonsa ba............wani abu mai taba xuciya ya tsaya mun ga makoshi,har na kasa hadiye abunda ke bakina.
Saidai naji saukar ruwan kwalla kawai,.bazan iya fassara abunda nakeji ba,amma koma minene yana soya mun raina.

Hannu nasa ina sharewa na cigada cusa yamboll din,guda uku naci na rufe sauran na xuba ruwa na kurba na ture tire din gefe.
Na tashi don kiran sallar magruba da aketayi,
Baisan abunda zaisake cemata ba bayan ido dayake ta binta dashi,yana jin xuciyarsa na halbawa ahankali,yana jin wani tausayinta yana narkemasa ga duk jikinsa,tunda suka shigo gidan daidai da mistake bata kallesa ba,bata saka idonta cikin nashi ba.wannan shariyar tana tabashi yasan kuma fushi take...........Antyn ce ta shigo tayi wanka ta shirya ta kallai.
"To gadin me kake mata,katashi kaje kayi sallah kaxo katafi gida kar matar taka tagaji tabiyoka."

"Anty kenan,bari naje nadawo muyi magana."
Ya fice masallacin dake kusada gidan kadan.

Saida na gama sallata tana kan gado ina kasa bisa sallaya tace"kin tabbata zaki zauna dani,karfa daga baya kice ke mijinki kikeson gani"
Kaina na kasa na girgiza kai kurin
"Magana zakiyi mun kince bazaki bisa bako?"
"Eh Anty inanan wajenki don Allah kice yakyaleni"

"Shikenan zamuyi magana dashi,yi zamanki anan bari na jirasa falo"

Kwanciya nayi ina fadawa kogin tunani,lokacin dana diba yayi daya dace nasan asalina,nasan ta inda na hado da wadanan mutanen.don jikina ya gama bani inada hadin jini dasu,saidai Amma kuma ta ina?tambayar da nakewa kaina kenan don iyakar sanina nidai ta wajen daddy bamuda wasu yan uwa da suka wuce Anty halima da Baba Abas,to kota wajen mahaifita?Amma ai babu wanda yakeda labarinta kodai sudin suna da masaniya akan wani abu daya shafeta?.
Tashi nayi jingina bayana da bangon gadon nace"photo album!tabbas akwai wani abunda zan fahimta aciki wata kila ma nasamu wani bayanin,amman taya zan dauko bayan nace mata bazan koma ba."

Bayan dawowar khalipa daga masallaci ya zauna dasu ashraf sharifa suna hira,yana tambayarsu karatunsu Antyn ta kawo masa nashi yamboll din da lemun inbin yana ci,yana mata bayanin cigaban da aka samu daga wajen aikinsa harda kama su J da kai,da kuma irin aikin da aka turashi shida nura xuwa kasar syprus,.
"Alhamdulillah,mungode Allah saura zaman kotu sai mun dage da addua sharrin mutumen nan yawane dashi,Allah ya cigaba da taimakonku."

"Game da tafiyar taku yaushene kenan?"

Jingina yayi yace "nan da kwana bakwai,koma ince biyar don sai mun biya abuja akwai wani shugabanmu da zamu tafi dashi,zamu kwana abuja washe gari mu bi jirgi."

"Kai kadai zaka tafi ko anbaku zabin tafiya da iyalinku?"

"Ehy sunce inaka iyawa katai da matarka,da wani abu naji kina tambaya?"

"Dawa zakatai?"

"Anty inada wata matar ne bayan Asmau?,ohhhh wai waccen yarinyar kike magana akai,kema kinsan ai tana nan mezanje nayi da ita"

Jinjina kai tayi tace"oky yayi kyau Allah ya bada sa,a kana iya tafiya."

Kalonta yake cikin son karin bayani "mekike nufi da ina iya tafiya,kituro ta mutafi to"
Mikewa tayi tace"babu inda zata idan katai da ita me zatai maka?kaga gara katafi wajen wadda zatai maka wani abu,sannan kasani duk randa kazo mun nan da kukan ka fada soyayyar pretty aranar zanka gane kurenka"

Dariya yake yana shafa kanshi cikin nishadi yace"wlh kina da abun dariya,wai sonta Anty kin mance ba dan haka na aureta ba?da angama zaman kotu zan sawake mata ta samu wanda take so yake sonta ta aura"

"To kaga tunda hakane baka da bukatar ajiyeta agidanka,saika bari duk sada aka gama zaman kotun saika xo ka bani takaddarta shikenan,amma yanzu zamanka da ita aibada anfani ko?"

Shiru yayi don haka maganarta take amman kuma bazai iya tafiya yabarta ba,koba zata mashi komi ba ganinta kusada shi zai samasa natsuwa.

"Kinga Anty,don Allah kibar duk wani batu,kibarni natafi da ita ranar da zan tafi saina maido maki kiyita riko"

"Khalipa ko kaki ko kaso son yarinyar nan kake kaima bakasan haka ba,don haka inhar ka yarda da haka kuna iya tafiya inkuma har yanzu kana kan cewar bata kai kasota ba right kanarta nan,don bata da abunda zata maka"

"Wallahi ni bana sonta,kuma bana jin ko nan gaba zan sota inadai son ta zauna har lokacin da zan tafi saita dawo."

"Na gama magana inhar ta yarda zata bika kutafi inkuma bata yarda ba babu inda zata karka takura ma kanka ma"
 Mikewa yayi yabi hanyar dakin da take don jin ta bakinta,yasan ma binsa zatai don maytarsa take yaga alama,kuma bata fushi dogon dashi.
(Ni kam nace maji ma gani ai)

Ina kwance nayi rubda ciki wani littafi na adduoi nake dubawa na ba kofar baya naji anshigo,waiga wa nayi ina ganinsa na juya na cigaba da duba littafin,wanda dan dananan naji na rikice nadaina gane meke cikin littafin.
Jingina yayi da kofar yana karewa bayanta kallo,kayan sunyi bala,in ansar jikinta lumshe ido yayi yama rasa ta cewa,gyaran murya yayi ya karoso inda nake ya zauna kan bed side yana kallona,kadan ya hana ban kallai ba na dake inata danbe da xuciyata akan wannan karon bazan sauko ba,saiya gane abunda yayi ba daidai bane.

"Amm deeyah kitashi muje gida dare nayi."

Juya masa baya nayi kurin don ma kar sanyin muryarsa ya yaudareni.
Hannunsa tasa ya zare littafin,ya rufe ya ajiye "magana nake dake kina banza dani,nace kitashi muje gida ko?"
Runtse idona nai don yana gaf da kureni na maida magana,nikuma maganarce bazanyi ba...........tashi nai na sauko daga gadon na nufi toilet,ganin shigewa zan cikin zafin nama ya rikoni har muna faduwa kan gadon nidashi,ya zama nice nake samansa dare dare,saurin tashi nayi yana sake rikemun hannu gam,ya riko fuskata ya dora goshinsa akan nawa samun idona nai da lumshewa,ina jan numfashi bana son ko kadan na nuna na huce,don ganin ina hucewa da wuri yasa yake mun duk cin kashin daya ga dama.
Idonsa rufe shima ya sassauta murya da lallami "I'm sorry,nayi hurting dinki bazan sake ba,kixo muje gida kinjiko"
Kwalata dake arha yanxu suka xubo mun,na kwace fuskata na juya mai baya nace"bazan bika ba,na gama zama dakai kaje kawai tunda kona bika banga abunda zan maka ba,illa kuringa cusawa xuciyata bakin ciki........ni....nidai ba...bazan je ba."
Na fashe dakuka ina matsawa daga inda yake,cikin muryar duk ya rikice yace"to kibar kukan naji baki bina,inturo driver da safe ya daukeki ko?"
Girgiza kai nayi ina kuka na kadan kadan "aa bana so,ka aiko mun da kayana kurin ni anan zan zauna."

Kamar ya kaima bango duka don haushi,yarsa meysa xuciyarsa da ransa basa son barinta gidan,har cikin kokon ransa baya son yayi nisa da ita.
(Bacin zakai tafiyar almost 6 months batare da ita ba😉)
Matsawa yayi kusa dani ya riko kuguna wanda jinai kamar ya soka mun allura,yadda naji hannunsa jikina haka.
Idan fa yana son abu wajena yayita kalmashe murya da rausaya kai,amma sadda zai shuka mun rashin mutuncinsa baisani ba ma.
"Meyasa zaki gujemu,mu muna son zama dake fah"

"Aa Baku so wlh bakuso na hkr da zama daku tunda har nazo nan bazan koma ba,ni ka kyaleni taya zaka zauna da mahaukaciya marar hankali,kaje ai ita duk bata rasa su ba."
Nashige toilet na kulle kofar na cigaba da kukana,hargitse gashin kanshi yayi gaba daya yana cije baki,jiyake gidan zai masa wani iri inbata ciki.
"Wai meke damuna ne?dole saida ita zan tafi"yaja tsaki yatashi yafice yana buga kofar........durkushewa nai ina kuka sosai,don na  gama yardar ma kaina ina cikin ruwa har wuya,don ba shakka soyayyar sa na niyar zautar dani,niban san yadda akai ba amman koma miye dana bari ya gane gara ta kasheni zaifiye mun kwanciyar hankali.


"Saida safe Anty"

Binsa tayi da kallo tana dariya kasa kasa tace"ya haka taki biyoka ne?to Allah yabamu alkairi agaida uwargida sarautar mata."
Ta fada cikin tsokana bai bata ansa ba ya fice yabarta,dariya take sosai tace"dani kake bayani,inhar kere na yawo zabo na yawo wataran zasu gamu,inan saina nuna maka baka da wayau,yarinyar da kake raina wa saita hanaka bacci,saita zautar dakai kuje duk gidan ubanda zaku kaida ma,un ai dole zaku dawo."

Kwata kwata ya manta da wata shawarma da ma,u keso ransa ajagule ya isa gidan kwata kwata baijin sukuni,koda tataso ta tareshi cikin fara,a karfin hali kurin yayi yace"sorry ma u na mance da sakonki amma naba salisu zai kawo maki idan ya dawo,zan shiga ciki kaina naciwo"

"Ayya sannu yaya,ina nadeya kabarota to"

"Tana gidan Anty" ansar daya bata  kenan ya shige dakinsa ya banko kofar.

Cikin kwana kin da suka gaba ta zan iya cewa na samu canjin yanayi mai dadi,don ina yin duk abunda nake so agidan Anty,koda zata fita zata tambayeni idan ina bukatar wani abu,kuma ta hadani da mai masu girki kala kala na zamani tace na ringa xuwa munayi tare,koda kwana zatai bama wani damuwa zankuka da su ashraf da sharif don shareef din yana boarding school shine babba sai Ashraf sharifa auta,kyakkyawar yarinya mai wayau da iya kalami,da ita muke fira lokaci daya wa idan suna gidan.Abban su kuwa shashensa da ban sai idan yafito muke gaisawa,shima mutun mai kirki da faran faran,tun washe gari da xuwana nasa Anty ta tura driviya dauko mun kayana dake gidansa,sauran abunda nake bukata kuma na bari zanje da kaina in na tabbatar baya gida.don tun ranar bamu sake haduwa ba,ko yazo bana yarda na fito yayita knocking inaji duk yadda xuciya keson ganinsa da jinsa nakan daure,illa iyaka idan ya tafi nayi kuka son raina na lallashi kaina.
Har ana gobe tafiyarsu ban san da wata tafiya ba sai ana goben zasu tafi,ina corridor zan fito naji muryarsa suna magana da Anty.............

"Gobe zamu wuce abujar mu kwana can da safe zamu bi jirgin karfe goma,awanni biyar ne xuwa shida zasu sauke mu Cyprus.in Allah yasa mun gama training cikin wata shidan zamu dawo,inkuma bamu gama ba kila mucike wasu shiddan"

Wani irin bugawa xuciyata takeyi da karfi wanda saida na dafe bangon dake bayana,wanu irin rawa jikina yake idona suna ciccikowa da kwallah.............babu abunda yake kuwwa cikin kunnena illa watannin daya lissafa,wata shida ko shekara.

"Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un Allah kar kasa xyciyata ta buga akan wannan lamarin,ashe har yana da karfin guiwar tafiya yayi wadannan watannin,ya zanyi wai ya zanyi ne ni nadeya?Allah kai ka samun wannan yanayin ka bani hkr da juriyar abunda nakeji,hakika feeling din mai dacine wanda yake ciki kadai zai iya ganewa."
Kiran da Antyn ta kwala mun yasa nagoge fuskata,ina kokarin ganin ba,a gane halin da nake ciki ba,ahankali na fito ina tafiya xuwa falon xuciyata kamar ta fado kasa haka nakejinta.
Bangaren da take na zaga na zauna gefenta ina cewa"Anty na gama shiryawa kudin saloon din"

Tunda ta fito idonsa kamar zai fado kasa. cikin kwana biyar din da sukai basu hadu ba gani yayi kamar an sata cikin inji an wankota, saboda tsabar yadda ta kara fidda kyanta,sai kokowa yake su hada ido Amma abun yaci tura,ya kasa hkr har saida yace "ke baki iya gaisuwa ba"

Ina rike da hannun sharifa don ita zata rakani tare da driver ban kallai ba nace cikin cool voice dina, "ina wuni"

"Bana ansawa tunda saida na roka."

Cikin fada Anty tace "ai kaji tsiyarka mtss Allah ya gyaraka wallahi,kinga pretty driver bai dawo ba har yanzu kibi yayanku saiya ajiyeki idan driver ya dawo saiya xo ya daukeki."
Idona yayi rau rau cikin sanyin da jikina yayi kamar zanyi kuka nace "kiyi hkr Anti Na hakura da xuwa na bari gobe sai inje basai na bata masa lokaci ba."
Na idasa fada ina maida kaina kasa kurin,don ko kadan bana fatan ma idona ta shiga cikin nasa............"kinci sa,a bana komi kina iya tashi na kaiki."

"Kingani ko,sai shegiyar gardama kekika sani to"
Ya fada yanajin ciwon rejecting din sa datayi antyn tace"kiyi hkr pretty kije kinjiko,kingani goben akwai abunda zamuyi ba lallai ne kina da time din xuwa ba,kuma kanki yana bukatar saloon inke baki damu dashi ba ni na damu da gyaransa."
Rausayar da kai nayi ba zan iya cigaba da yi mata garda ma ba banda yadda na iya nace mata "shikenan Anty zanje,Amma don Allah ki gaya masa karya mun tsawa,bana son fitina yana iya jana ni bana son hayaniya plss"

"Au nikeda hayaniyar kenan,inbacin kin raina mun wayau sai kiyi abu naki tankawa?wallahi zance bana kaiwa"

Uffan ban ce masa ba don naga soya ke kawai na kulasa,ni kuma nai alkawarin tattara lamarinsa nasa cikin kwandon shara.
Anty tace "kayi kadan kuma wlh,bacin kai kanema kuma kake nuna kamar baka damuba,kafita idona khalipa wlh akwai ranar kin dillaci kuma karka ja abunda zai bata mata rai,na rokeka tunda tace bata son fitina kuje salin alin kudawo."
Kafada ya daga yana wulga mata wani kallo wanda ni bansan yanayi ba,ya mike yana yin gaba kurin.
Sharifa tace"ummi zan bisu"
"Aa sharifa kibari gobe sai kiraka ta duk inda zata banda yau"
Bata fuska tayi zatai kuka nace"kibarta muje mana Anty zatayi mun fira."
"Shikenan kuje saikun dawo."

Naja hannunta muka nufo compound din gidan,atamfa ce jikina riga da siket kayan antyne ta bani wajen kala biyar atamfa biyu less biyu material daya,kuma duk sun ansheni hijab nasa daidai guiwa tunda nasa su yau Antyn da sharifa ke faman cewa nayi kyau,ni kaina nasan haka sautari idan nakalli kaina ina godema Allah,don yariga ya gama yimun suffa irin wadda ba kowa yayiwa ba.

Tun daga nesa yake kallonta bai ma taba ganin zahirin kyanta ba irin yau,kullin idan ya ganta tayi mai kyau to gobe saiya ga ta linka jiya,bama kamar kwana kin da yayi bai sata idonsa ba sai duk ta koma masa wata sabuwa,baki su zauna waje guda ba su dauki dogon lokaci koda bazata  ce komi ba.
Haka nan yake samun kanshi da rashin son nisa da ita,bazai iya gane dalili ba.
Haka zalika daxun da tana magana jiyayi ita kanta muryar tata kamar ancanza mata ita,baison tayi shiru don kamar tana busa masa sarewa cikin kunne haka yakeji.........."uncle kabude mana kanata kallon mu"
Muryar sharifa ta katse masa tunaninsa da lula baisani ba,lumshe ido yayi yana jan numfashi kafin ya latsa malatsi kofar motar ta bude,badan kar ta ja magana tsakanin su ba baya zata zauna.amma tasan rigima ta jawowa kanta,ita kuma magana doguwa bata son tayi dashi,balle harta iya kallonsa.
Tana kokarin hora xuciyarta da wannan ne saboda ta saba da rashinsa akoda yaushe,kuma tanajin insha Allah zatayi nasara muddin ta cigaba da jure wannan radadin da yanayin da takeji,duk akanshi.

Ahankali yake jan motar kamar wanda aka zarewa lakka ajikinsa,kwata kwata babu kuzari tare dashi saidai ya kalleta ya cigaba da tukinsa,sai surutun sharifa ka wai akeji.

"Uncle ummi tace ka kaita wajen hajiya fatima,inda mukexuwa tayi mata waya ma tun jiya tace zamuxo,kasan ta iya sosai kuma bata sharce wa da zafi nasan Anty deeyah bazatai kuka ba kamar yadda nakeyi idan naje wani wajen ba wajen hajiya fatima ba."

Bansan murmushi mai hade da dariya ya kubce mun ba,saboda  shirmen sharifa...........wanan ya haddasa masa ya kusa bankar wani mai napep daker ya samu ya kauce,yana sake waigowa yana kallon fuskarta kozai sake cin karo da abunda ya halbar masa xuciya at d same time.
Amman sai yaga na daure kamar bani nayi ba,sharifa surutun ta ta cigaba dayi batasan ma meyake faruwa ba.

Haka tafiyar ta cigaba da kasancewa ta kurame,baice mun ba nima banda niyyar cema sa komi illa ciki xuciyata dambe kurin nakeyi da ita.
Ina jinjina iyakar tsayin lokacin da zasuyi basu dawo ba,bakin cikina shine nafarko yadda yake fifita Asmau akaina duk sanda ya tashin yin abu,sannan bai daukeni bakin komi ba.baya mun kallon inada wani muhimmanci atare dashi,ga kuma igiyarsa bisa kaina wadda nafi jin tsanarta aduk cikin abunda yake damuna,tunda banda daraja da muhimmanci wajensa mezaisa bazai sauwake mun ba,na huta duk da yadda xuciyata take mun radadin halin da na tsinci kaina ciki tsindum batare dana ankare ba,hakan bana jin zai hanani neman rabuwa dashi,saboda auren bashida anfani............tsayuwar motar wajen shagon yasa na dawo cikin duniyar tunanin dana tafi,inajin kirjina yana mun nauyi.
Aguje sharifa ta bude kofa taruga cikin shagon alamun dai sun saba sosai da matar wajen,nima naja mabudin motar zanfita yaki buduwa kuma nasan da gangan ya rude sa bansan miye dalili ba,ban jiyo na kallesa ba cikin sanyin muryata da yanzu duk sanda zanyi magana haka nakejinta,kila hakan yana da nasaba da halin dana ke ciki nida xuciyata......."don Allah kabude mun bana son nakai dare ne"
Tsikar jikinsa yaji tatashi babu shiri,ya soka hannunsa cikin gashinsa yanajin kamar yayita fasa kara,daker ya samun bakin da yayi magana don hakika yanzu muryarta tana tasiri sosai tattare dashi.

"Inason muyi magana ne kuma nasan idan na gaya maki kitsaya tafiya zakiyi."

Cigaba da juya xoben azurfata nayi da Anty ta bani shekaran jiya.
Idona akan hanun nawa ina kallonsa badan kuma hankalina yana kanshi ba,aa gaba daya tunanina yatafi wani wajen kafin na samu ansar bashi.

"Kayi hkr idan na gama in munje gida saika fada mun maganar."

Bai musaba yace"oky I'm waiting for u here."
Bance komi ba naja mabudin tabude na fita nabarsa,injin idonsa yana rakani har sadda na shige cikin babban shagon da keda bangarori da yawa,akwai wajen wankin kafa akwai na gyaran jiki,akwai wajen gyaran amare na musanman.wurin ya hadu sosai muka gaisa take jana da hira,tace"Ai Aysher kawarta ce tun suna jamia inda sukai karatun medicine,ita aka koreta sakamakon sharrun da wani lectura dinsu yayi mata,kawai don taki bashi kanta.
Yaje duk yasa aka lalata mata Caria dinta,hakan yasa ma ta rabu da karatun tafara sana,a.............munsha hira sosai tana ta yabon gashina da surar jikina,araina inacewa tubarkalla masha Allah.
Ita da kanta tayi mun duk da tana da yara masu mata aiki,tace ai kaina baidace da kowa ba sai ita yadda zata gyara man shi babu k'yuya.
Aikuwa dan danan naji kan nawa yana mun dadi,aka wanke shi tas da mayukan wankin kai,kafin tasani cikin standard dryer sharifa sai dariya take mun wai na koma abun dariya,haka muka zauna tsawon lokaci ina duba wani littafi,ita kuma tana yiwa sharifa kitso suna hira jefi jefi suna sanyoni.
Cikin awa biyu duk muka gama abunda zamuyi,muka biyata tace Allah ya kyauta ta anshi kudinmu.
Na tuna da maganar Anty kafin mu taho "kona baku kudin baxata ansa ba,kullin haka nake fama  da ita idan ma nace bazan je ba hargida take biyoni dole na hkr,saidai na samu wani abun arxikin nayi mata."

Har wajen mota ta rakomu tana cewa agaida mata qawarta.
Nai mamakin da har ya zauna tsawon wannan lokacin bai tafi ba,na jin jina kai kurin muka shiga yaja muka fara tafiya sharifa ta bude kanta tace "ka gani uncle kitsona yayi kyau ba"

"Eh uncle ta bude maka ka gani wlh inkana shafawa kamar karka daina,sai kamshi yake Anty deeyah gashi gareta kamar na indiyawa wlh"

Ya fada kamar wani yaro kamarta ban kulasu ba tace mani "Anty don Allah ki nuna masa ya gani,zanso ka gani uncle wlh"

Daure fuska nai nace mata" baby inbaso kike mu bata ba oya keep quiet"
Tabe baki tayi zatai kuka takoma cikin kujera ta kwanta,bata sake magananba.
Jiyowa yayi kaina yace"meyasa zaki hanata magana?don kurin karki nuna man gyaran,duk zaman sana sha anan"
Ina kallon window nace.
"Meye anfanin hakan?"

"Koba anfani ina da hakkin ganin ai"

"Tunda babu anfani barin shiyafi"

Bai sake cemun ba nima haka har mukaje gida.
Kafin mukarasa muka tsaya ya siya ma sharifa chocolate kala kala yabata yaceladar ganin kitso,kitsonta yamai kyau
Aikuwa sai murna takeyi tace mun "kingani kema dakin nuna masa daya siya maki,kuma bazan yan maki ba."
Murmushi nayi kurin ta falla aguje cikin gida ta barni dashi cikin motar,.jina nake duk adaure nagaji sonake naje na yi wanka na kwanta,gashi duk nakasa sakewa jinake xuciyata tana xillo gabana yana duka,karfin hali kurin nayi.

Rufe glass din yayi duka ya kunna Ac ga kuma freshener tana tashi sai hakan yakara mani wani yanayin,har inajin idona yamun nauyi yasa na kulleshi.

"Inajin bacci don Allah"
Na fada can kasan makoshi don ni kaina bansan meyasa mun kasala ba.

Jiyosa nayi shima kamar an canza shi yakoma so cool.
Yace"kiyi anan mana."

"Nidai ka fada mun nagaji so nake naje nayi wanka na huta."

"Nasan zakiji ba dadi."

Inason jiyowa na kallai amman na kasa nace "ba dadi akan me kenan?"

"Akan zanyi tafiya baki sani ba,kuma badake zan tafiba da Asmau.sai nake ganin zakiyi mun kallon marar adalci."

Yasosa mun inda yake mun kaikai idona har yafara tara ruwa amman na dake na maidasu,illa iyaka muryata zata nuna ina jin wani ciwo cikin raina.

"Akanme zanji babu dadi?saboda me zanji bakayo adalci ba?ai wajena adalcin kenan,karka damu kuna iya tafiya ko bangon duniya ne tare,ai matarkace"

"Kamar yadda kike matata kema,nasan zakiso ace dake natafi."

"Eh ni matarka ce amman muna da banbanci sosai da Asmau,wanda ban bancin yasa ka zabeta baka zabeni ba,kuma kayi kuskure ni koda kamun tayi bazan bika ba,Allah ya kiyaye hanya adawo lfya banajin komi"
Na fada ina danbe da idona dake son kwafsa mani,don kadan hawayena ke jira su xubo.

"Inhar bakijin komi kuma baki damu ba,jiyo inga idonki shine zai tabbatar mani ba bakinki ba,tunda bana iya ganin xuciyarki."

Gabana yake faduwa kirjina daga wa yake saboda kukan da ke ciki ina dannesa,tabbas in yaga idona zaigane inajin fain sosai Amman idan nakiya,zaiga kamar banda gaskiya ahankali nake dago kaina tare da kwayar idona na xuba masa su,kamar yadda ya zuba mun nashi.............innalillahi ai nayi nadamar kallonsa don wata suka naji har cikin kahon xuciyata,bashiri na dauke idona kwallata tana silalowa,nai saurin bude kofar ya rike mun hannu.
Xuwa lokacin hawayena sun samu hanya,xuba suke sosai kan fuskata suna sauka kan hijab dina,kokowar kwace hannuna nake tayi daker nasamu nasara nafice cikin sassarfa,ko kofar ban rufe ba burina naje dakina kurin,don bazan so Anty taga wannan kukan ba.

Hannunsa guda yasa saitin xuciyarsa ya dafe,yana dora kanshi bisa sikiyarin motar wani irin numfashi yake ja dakarfi,don jiyake mahadar iskarsa bata aiki.
Meyakeji haka wani abune da baitaba jinsa ba,koda makamancinsa ne idonsa dan danan yayi ja,makoshinsa yaji yabushe.jarkar swan faro dake gefen sa ya dauko yaringa tuttulawa amman bayajin sanyin yana kaimasa inda ya keso.
Jefar da jarkar yayi yana sake dafe kirjinsa,wajen xuciyarsa baisan iyakar lokacin daya dauka haka ba.
Kafin ya iya samun karfin fixgar motar yabar compound din

Episode  .23


Sadda nazo na wuce dakina aguje ina kuka Anty tana kitchen,ta hango wuce wata ahaxarce ta fito ta baro mai aikin tabiyo bayan,ina shiga na maida kofar na kulle toilet na shige na kunna ruwa don ma kar ajiyo karar kukan nawa,zaman dirshen nayi cikin toilet din na ringa kukana yadda zanji fain din dake cina ya ragu,ina cikin wannan halin naji knocking din kofara tare da kirana.
Kuma muryar Anty ce tashi nai na wanke fuskar sosai,wanda wanke war baya hana agane kuka nasha. Nafito ina tsane fuskar na bude mata kofat tare da bata hanya.
Cikin damuwa tace"lafiya pretty?kukan me kike ina kitchen na hango shigowarki kina kuka,wani abun halan yayi maki gaya mun komeye wallahi zan daukar maki mataki."
Zama nayi don wani jiriri nake gani nace "aa anty ba komi babu abunda yayi mun"

"Ban yarda dake ba,idan da babu komi bazaki kuka ba,gaya mun pretty bakida kamata zan share maki hawayenki."
Jinai wasu hawayen sun xubo na girgiza kai kurin.

Riko hannuna tayi tace cikin kwatar da murya "pretty kodai kinfara son yayanki"?
Saurin dago idona nai ina kallonta,tace "eh abunda nake zargi kenan kina son sa ko?"

Bakina yana rawa muryata ta shake nace"Aa Anty ni bana wani sonsa,kawai dai nace yabani wayoyina haka nan shine yace bazai bada ba,yamun fada shikenan fa Abunda yafaru"
Na samu kaina da sheko karya don bansan mexan ce ba inhar tagane abunda yake raina,kuma zanji kunya ba yar karama ba.
Murmushi kurin tayi tace"shikenan kibarni dashi zan masa magana kafin sutai yabaki wayarki,ya gaya maki zaiyyi tafiya."

Daga mata kai nayi tace"karki damu duk gidan ubanda zasu ma shida ma,un sutai munan zai zo yasamemu so nake kafin ya dawo kin sake wayewa duk wanda ya ganki sai ya kara,kinfi karfin wulakancin wani namiji don uban shi."

"Anty yace akwai lokacin daya fidda zai sawake mun,indai hakane Anty kice kafin yatai ya sawake mun kurin,aurensa banda wuya baya banu komi wlh"
Na fada ina sharar kwallah.

"Shi aure dakika ganshi pretty raine dashi,kuma duk wanda ta kasheshi kamar ya kashe raine,idan ma da gaske yake lokaci zai nuna hakan ban son kina saka damuwa cikin ranki,ki kwantar da hankalinki makarantar koyon dishes kala kala zan saki,sannan zan hadaki da customers da zaki ringa yiwa suna siya,da snacks suma akwai wadanda na sani da sana,arsu kenan saidawa kinga inkina wani abun bazaki samu damar zama ki kebe har wani tunani yazo ya sa maki bacin rai ba,don haka gobe zamuje ki cike form kafin kiyi register kifara xuwa."
Rungumeta nayi cikin tsananin jin dadi,hakika kamar ta shiga xuciyata,ina son kalar wadannan sanao'in more especially ma snacks din da dishes din duk inasonsu.sai naji kaso arba,in nadamuta ya kare.
"Nagode Anty ubangiji ya biyaki da aljanna"

"Ameen pretty na,ai duk abunda zai saki farin ciki shinake nema,kuma insha Allah zanyi nasara ki tattara khalipa da damuwarsa kisa kwandon shara kinjiko,karki sake ya gane kin damu dashi"
Daga kaina nayi ina murmushi ta shafi kaina sanan ta fita.
Kwanciya nai nai relex din kaina ina ajiyar xuciya,at least ina da wajen da zan kawo kukana ashare mun hawaye,sannan ana kokarin ganin farin ciki tattare dani,don haka bazan barka kaida xuciyata ku nakastani ba,insha Allah kuka yakare akanka yaya musaddiq.
Da wannan tunanin natashi nakoma toilet din na yi wanka tare alwallah nafito nazo na fara gabatar da sallarta.

         ✨✨✨

"Yaya kana lafiya kuwa?meke damunka kalli fa kaga da takalmi ka hau gado ka kwanta."

Asmau ke masa magana saboda yana samu ya ajiye motar direct cikin gidan ya nufa,ki sashen inna bai kalla ba.
Saboda tsabar kurin yana son yasamu natsuwa don abunda yakeji yafi karfin yasa.
Fuskarsa rufe da filo yayi kwanciyar kallon sama,numfashi kurin yakeja ahankali.

"Yaya kodai na kira Inna"

Sai lokacin yasaka hannunsa ya jawota jikinsa yana ture filon dake kan fuskarsa,idonsa sun rine yake kallonta itama take kallonsa.
Yanayin da taganshi ya bata tsoro,yaja hannunta ya dora bisa kirjinsa cikin sarkewar murya yace "Asmau zafi yake mun,ciwo yake mezansha ya daina"
Ya fada yana kallon cikin idonta,dan danan kwallah suka shatato mata cikin muryar son fashewa da Kuka tace"nashiga ukuna yaya meyasameka to,ciwon kirji kaiba mai ulcer ba kaiba mai wata asthma ba meke damunka"

Dora kanta yayi bisa kirjin nashi yana sauke numfashi,yace"I don't know,Kawai inajin kamar karna tafi"
Tashi tayi zaune don dan danan taji wani abu ya fado mara arai,tana kallonsa cikin tuhuma tace"daga ina kake yanxu?"
Kallon juna suke yakasa bata ansa tace"da alama duk daga inda kake acan wani abu yafaru wanda har kakejin Bazaka iya tafiya ba,miye shi yaya?kana boye mun ko?"
Ta fada xuciyarta tana karkarwa tsoro takeji tsoronta bai wucw ace daga wajen nadeya yake ba,taoronta bai wuce ace itace zata hanashi tafiya ba,kokuma ta hanashi ya tai da ita don yadda ta sama ranta tafiyar inhar yafasa tafiya da ita. bata san bakin cikin da zata shiga ba.

"Yaya karfa kamanta wannan tafiyar taka tana da muhimanci,ashe har akwai abunda zai zo yayi maka cikas acikin  wannan nasarar taka?kar bari hakan ya yi tasiri agareka."
Ta fada tare da saka ran cewar zaiji maganarta yabar duk wani kokwanton tafiyar.

Ta fada tana jan tsaki

"Yaya wai duk miye hakan nan ne,kamar dai wani dan goye da baya son rabuwa da mamansa.don Allah ka saki ranka konaji dadi"
Ta lakuto man tana bin jikinsa dashi,shidai ya kasa bata ansa kwata kwata daren jiya bai samu bacci mai dadi ba.ko kadan baida sukuni a xuciyarsa,jin ta yake duk a kuntace idan yaso yarda da abunda xuciyarsa ke gaya masa saiya karyata,don baiga dalilin da zaisa saboda nadeya yaji tafiyar ta fitar masa arai.

Yayi kyau cikin suit dinsa farin wando da blue din coat sai yar cikin da singlet dinsa duk farare,cover dinsa ma farace sal ba karamin kyau yayi ba.

Makale kafada tayi tana janyewa taje ta dauki hand bag dinta,on ready kayansu ansasu acikin mota.

"Sadiqu wannan tafiya badan ta zama dole ba wlh dana ce kayi hkr,gidan girma zai mana ga salisu ya koma makaranta dagani sai sumayya,ga itama nadeya bata nan kewa zatai mana yawa"

"Akan matanka,inhar ta kama zakuyi shekarar to Asmau na cika wata shidda kasanyota jirgi ta dawo yar uwarta nadiya tabiyoka,don gaskiya akwai kwara inhar kace da daya zaka tafi har kadawo."

Asmau wani makoko taji yazo ya tsaya mata ga wuya,meyasa inna zatai mata haka saikace baitace uwarta ba,ai yasan da nadiyar ya tsallaketa yace da ita zai tai don me kuma zatace wani indawo ita tabisa............

"Zan gaya Mata"
Yafito yabaro Asmaun suna sake sallama da innar da sumayya,sumy harda yar kwallarta yar uwarta zata tafi.


           ✨✨✨

Tun safe ina kitchen nida samha mai aiki muka hada break na dawo na shirya su ashraf da sharifa,suka wuce makaranta Anty nacan shashen Abban su nataya samha gyaran gidan,idan ina aikace aikace jina nake daban don kuwa inajin dadin yadda nake ganin kaina ina aikin gida,wanda ada bansan sa ba.
Haka yana dauke mun hankali sosai,don zaman ni kadai bana komi shike jawomun yawan tunani har nake jin xuciyata tana nauyi.
Saida muka gama ne naje nayi wanka nashirya cikin kananun kaya wadanda na jima ban saka ba,rigar iyakar guiwa take sai jeans din pencil ya kama jikina daidai daidai,na daura gyalen bisa kaina fuskata tayi wasai kamar nayi yar rama.
Saboda aduk sadda nazo bacci hotonsa  nake gani cikin idona da xuciyata,duk yadda nake yaki da ita idan nazo bacci abun wuya yake bani,ina kwashe awa biyu jir batare da ko gyangyadi ya daukan ba,banda tunaninsa da yadda lamuran suka juye kurin nake.wai nice tsundum cikin kewar sa bacin nasha sanar dashi irin tarin tsanar da nake masa.
Meyasa xuciya bata shawara idan zata afka cikin soyayya,hakika nikam batayi mun adalci ba.
Kwalla naji sun xubo mun tunawa da rabuwar mu dashi ta jiya,kilama suntafi kokuma ma sun isa.waya sani tashin asuba sukai,goge kwallar nai ina saka kwalli kadan ga idon.
Nasa turaruka na sannan na fito don karyawa,Anty nata shirin fita wajen aiki muka hadu falo tace"na hanaki break din ten bakiji ko?wai kokin mance kina da ulcer"

"Hum wataran ma ko brush bakiyi zaci kama buruntu a kitchen,tun kafin kowa ya tashi"

Dariya nayi ina rufe bakina alamun kyankyami nace"yan ya,ai sainayi amai Anty. kaci abinci baka wanke baki ba"

"Zakice na gaya Maki randa kika yi hakan"

Dariya nayi ina jan kujera nafara karya wa tana bani wasu labaran,haka muke da ita bata taba barina nayi jugum don zance itace take koya mun yadda zanyi hira da mutane,wadanda baka sansu ba koda ka hadu da mutin wani waje. Muna ta break din aka bude kofar falo,idona nai saurin kaiwa xuciyata tabani a tension yayin da bugun xuciyata ya sauya,sanda idona ya sauka kanshi saida numfashina ya sauya wani abu naji da baki bazai iya kwatan tasa ba.
Idona baya son daina kallonsa amman ina dannewa ina kokowa da shi,har nayi nasara.
Asmau ce biye dashi cikin shigar alfarma tasha gayu kamar zata zaben sarauniyar kyau,tabbas naji wani abu ya tsaya mun ga mak'ogwarona inajin shine kishin da ake kira,lumshe ido nai na kauda kai kamar ban gansu ba na cigaba da tura doya da kwan da nakeci,wadda dazun kamar zan hada da hannuna don jin dadin ta da nake,yanxu kau jinai tayi mun sake sake ga bakina.
Anty naji tana masu magana.

"Ga Asmau gidana,yau watanku biyu amman bakita ba xuwa ba,shima bai kawoki ba."

Cikin murmushi tace"afuwa Anty yau nazo ai kuma za cigaba da xuwa."

"Yau dinma don da inda kuka nufa shiyasa,to ya inna batai fushi dani ba na dauke mara sarakuwa"

Dan tabe baki tayi tace"Aa babu wani fushi tana gaidaku ma"

"Bismilla kuxo mu karya"

Tunda suka hada ido tashoshinsa suka kulle,ko kadan hirar da Anti keyi da Asmau bayajinta,inda nake kurin yake kallo amman idan ka kalleni zaka rantse basan sa zaman su ba.nan kuwa banda danbe babu abunda muke nida xuciyata da kwayar idona.

"Ai na dauka saidai kurin kamun waya kun tafi"

Sai loacin yaji maganarta ta ratsa kunensa.

"Uhm kai din ai nasan halinka sarai,ni zan wuce na makara zanje asbiti sai ince Allah ya kiyaye hanya yasa asamu abunda akaje nema,akuma rike addua da azkar duk shaidanin mutun da aljan basu isa suyi komi akanka ba,Allah ya kiyaye hanya Asmau asha amarci lfy"

Soke kai tayi kasa tana murmushi.

Nace "Anty saikin dawo"

"Okay pretty na a kula da gida yau ai samha ta gaya maki fankasu za,a ko"

"Eh anty yanzu zan fara hada miyar ma kije saikin dawo"

Haka tafice ta barni dasu nai saurin tashi nayi hanyar dakina nabarsu shida ita,.
"Yaya baka kallon agogo ko"

Yace"ina kallo mana saurin me kike,"ya mike itama ta mike "koma ki zauna zanga nadeya yanzu."

"Nima zan mata sallama ai ko"

Ransa yafara baci da wadannan dabi,un na asmau yace ciki bacin rai "ok muje duk abunda kika gani daya bata maki rai keki ka jiyo,tunda sana arki bin kwakwkwafi."

Kayan guga ta nake saka wa cikin wadrop wanda nayi hakane don na dauke hankalina daga kanshi,jikina har karkarwa yake don rashin sanin abunda zanyi.
Naji shigowarsu amma ban jiyo ba,inata aikina kamar ban damuba.

"Zamu tafi nadeyah"

"Allah ya tsare hanya"
Na bashi ansa batare dana kallesu ba, "ko kallo bamu isheki ba at least munyi bankwana murabu lafiya,bakida tabbacin zamu dawo mu sameki,kokuma zaki sake ganinmu"

Cikin hasala najiyo nace masa"idan kun mutu Allah yajikanku banda hakki kanku,inma nina mutu nima Allah yajikaina ba shikenan ba,kunga ga hanya kutafi jirgi yana jira umma ta gaida Assha"
Na jiya kurin zan bar wajen naji Asmau na cewa "ba saikin koremu ba,tafiya kam ai yi xamuyi ba bamu muka kar xomun ba"

Jikkarta ta figa ta fice kamar kububuwa.
Na kalleshi duk da nasan zan iya jin rauni tare dani  don kallon cikin idonsa yana matukar karya mun lagona.
"Zaman me kake katashi kabita mana..........jinai kurin yajawoni jikinsa banyi aune ba,runtse idona nai ina jin yadda jikina yake rawa.
Fuskarsa da tawa ya hade waje guda,muryarsa tayi sanyi sosai yana tallabe da fuskar tawa ya dora goshinsa bisa nau,numfashin mu yana haduwa dan danan na sake rikicewa kokowar kwacewa nake yace cikin wata irin murya kamar ba tashi ba, "shiiiiittt,bana son muyi rabuwar da bata da dadi deeyah,plsss ki bari mu rabu lfy tafiya ba kaike da ita ba Allah keda ita,bamuda masaniyar zuwa ko dawowa.bakijin wani abu ke aranki"
Shiru nai masa don tuni maka maina sun xube,karfin halin ya narke xuciyata banda azalzala babu abunda take,inajin kamar na rungumesa tsam ajikina,inajin kamar na kwanta kirjinsa naita kuka har saina gaji,amman duk bazan iya ba.
Saidai kwallata da bana iya hanata fita itace take silalowa daga idona duk da kuwa idon arufe yake..........jin ruwa ruwa yana biyo hannunsa yasashi bude idonsa,ya dago fuskarsa yana kallon tawa,yatsunsa yasa yana goge hawayen yayinda wasu suke sake fitowa.........jinai yace "oh may god,nadeya why are u crying"
Numfashina kurin nakeja nayi kokarin kwacewa amma ya hanani,kukan yake son fin karfina nikuma bana son nayi saiya tafi.
Tashi nai cikin kwacewa nayi hanyar toilet,still rikoni ya sakeyi ya dawo dani gabansa mamakin yadda yake magana nakeji,kamar zai fashe da kuka yace"plsss talk to me say something pls bazan tafi ba saikin mun magana"
Muryata da kuka nace"mezance?bacin nayi maku addua"

Matsoni yayi kamar zai maidani cikinsa yana riko hannuna duka yana matsewa,wanda daga nawa hamnun har nasa sanyi kalau suke........yace cikin sassanyar murya "can I hug u"
Saurin kallonsa nai idonsa yayi jaa kamar yayi kuka,fuskarsa tayi wani iri lumshe mun idonsa yayi ya sake cewa "pls can I"?
Hawaye na kamar anaturosu na girgiza kaina ina ja baya,ina son anshe hannuna rikeni yayi sosai yana sake matsoni,batare da yarda ta ba yasani cikin jikinsa ya matse,dan danan yaja ajiyar xuciya wadda ta sani jin tsikar jikina tana tashi.
Hannuna yana rawa suna son taya shi rungumar amman na hana,kukana nake kasa kasa.
Kunnena naji ya lalubo bansan sadda naji hannun nau ya zagaya ta bayansa ya kankamesa ba,saboda numfashinsa daya sakarmun a kunnena.
Magana yayi yace "Deeyah plss Can I kiss u"
Azabure na turesa na juya baya muryata adishe nace"no, no I said no pls ka wuce katafi bana so ka kyaleni........don.......don Allah"

Budewa yayi kurin ya shiga driver yaja suka bar gida,jiyayi shima kamar zaiyi kukan kwantar da bayan sa jikin kujerar ya rufe fuskarsa da hanky,don baya ma son aimasa magana.

Dawuri na dawo makaranta yau don zamuje gidan inna,wajen shabiyu ina gida na fara hada mana juice mai dadi nabarwa samha girkin ganin ba wani mai wahala bane.
Shinkaface da miya da naman kaza anyi masa Pepe chicken,sannan ansaka anta acikin miyar.
Juice din kuma dabino ne mai shegen taushi irin na saudiyya,sai aya da kwakwa da cocumber.
Kasancewar yana da taushi dabinon,saina fara blanding dinsa saida y markadu sosai yayi lumuy,sannan na tace na xuba ruwan cikin dan bowl din dake gabana.
Ayar kuma saida na dakata sosai,ita kuma kwakwar na gurxata da greeter.
Sannan na hadasu na markade sosai,saida suka markadu na tace su na hade da ruwan dabinon dana tace dafarko.
Sannan itama cucumber na yayyankata na xuba ga blander na markade ta natace na xuba ruwan cikin sauran ruwan aya da dabinon da kwakwa.
Suger nasa da liquid milk peak kenan ta ruwa,na juye gwangwani biyu sanan nasa frig.

Hakika kam inna taji dadin ganin mu tarba muka samu daga wajenta da wajen sumy yadda ya kamata.
Tuni sharifa da ashraf sun cikata da surutu,dakin sumayya na wuce nida ita muna hirar yaushe gamo.

Hirar sumy ta sani nishadi kuma nayardar ma kaina cewar na canza din,duk da kuma nima naga haka wai ahaka ma ba,ai komi ba gaskiya anty ta musanmance.

Nan muka bude sabon babin hira har tana cewa intatashi aure itama wajen anty zata agyarata,angon yakasa ganeta.
Sumy badai iya hira ba,dauke hankalina tai kaf harna mance da abunda ya kawoni gidan,sai gaf da zamu tai nace mata bari naje part din mu akwai abunda zan dauka,ta kawo mun keys din na fice ni kadai.............haka nan naji gabana yafadi bansan dalilin hakan ba,kodan najima ban xo ba oho.
Haka na bude na shiga duk yadanyi kura ba laifi,dakinsa kurin naima tsinke inajin bugawar kirjina yana karuwa................


Ina bawa masoyana hkr akan rashin jina dasukai kwana biyu,hakan ya faru ta dalilin wayata data fashe amman alhamdulillah komi yana gaf da zama  normal.
Ina mika godiya ta marar adadi ga masoyana yan grp dina *ABU KHALIPA FANS* hakika kun nuna mun kauna banda bakin dazan gode maku,fatana Allah ya bani ikon cigaba da faranta maku kamar yadda kuke mun.ina kaunarku sosai haka ina mika jinjinata ga yar uwata masoyiyata,abun alfaharina FAUXIYYA S MADAKIZ abisa kokarinta gareni,sister Allah yabar xumunci da kaunar juna I proud of you😍.

LOV U FISABILILLAH💝😊FISABILILLAH💝😊

Episode  . 25

Jikina asanyaye haka nake ta kokowar saka key din don bude kofar,wani bangare na xuciyata inajin kamar kawai na juya basaina yi abunda ya kawoni ba.
Amman muhimmancinsa gareni bazai barni na juya ba,ahaka dai nayi nasarar bude kofar nabi dakin da kallo,komi need kamar akwai mutun aciki.
Kunna kwan dakin nai haske ya gwauraye dakin,still har yanxu gabana baibar faduwa ba.
Kamar wadda aka zarewa lakkar jiki haka nake jan kafafuna xuwa cikin tsakiyar dakin,wannan dai bed sid drower din dana taba budewa nanufa wadda nai karo da photo album din dana so tun wancen karon dauka ban samu dama ba.
Ita na bude ahankali hannuna yana karkarwa cikin azama don kar anty ta gaji da jirana ta iskoni.
Daddaga takaddun wajen naitayi,ina binciken inda yake amman banga maikama dashi ba,karshe saida na fiddo komi amman banganshi ba.
Dafe goshina nai ina cixon lips dina,ina mamakin ina zai kaishi badai daukeshi yayi ya boye ba ni nabani.
Maida komi nai duk da ba yadda suke na maidasu ba,burina na ganshi ko nayi nabar dakin magaruba sake yi take.
Wadrop dinsa na bude cikin azama ina dubawa kayansa jere jikin hanger banda azabebben kamshinsa babu abunda suke,banga komi ba nagaji da dube dubena na rufe na bude gudar itama na dudduba amman banga wani abuba dazai nuna man akwai abunda nake nema wajen.
Gajiya nai na rike kuguna ina jan numfashi,ina bin dakin da kallo.
Wajen window naje nadage labulen ina ganin yadda garin ya fara hada duhu,amman akwai sauran hasken rana kadan bai idasa shigewa ba.
Kamar ance na juya bayana,idona ya sauka ga bangon dake fuskantata tsura masa ido nai ina sake nazartar wajen.
Kafin kwalwata ta tabbatar mani da abunda nake zargi awajen,cikin hanzari na karasa wajen ina shafa bangon tabbas drower ce awajen amman saika yi namjin gaske zaka iya ganewa.............mamaki ya kamani me yaya yake boyo haka irin wannan tsaro dayake bawa abu?na tambayi kaina.
Dubawa nake inga ta yadda zan iya budeta,plat take baka taba cewa wlh akwai wani abu wajen,duk daukar ka bangone kurin.
Wajen switch din kwai na duba aikuwa naga wani malatsi wanda banga abun da ake dashi ba adakin,ba mamaki na drower ne.
Nai saurin latsawa kamar almara saigashi bangon wajen yana budewa,drower ta fito zahirinta amman saida makulli ake budeta.
Nan take key din dana gani kasan takaddun bed side din can,ba mamaki na tane aguje naje na zalukoshi ina haki.
Nashiga gwadawa dan danan yabude ajiyar xuciya naja,ina bude ta ba wasu abu bane aciki dayawa.
Wannan dai photo album saigashi awajen,bayanshi akwai wasu takaddu jere amman batasu nake ba,na dauko photo album din.
Kusa dashi naga wani babban littafi irin masu cover din nan,babbane ba laifi kamar zan kyale sai naji sha,awar daukarsa.
Na dauko shi ina kare masa kallo.yayi kyau book din dayake da jikka nataho nazuba su aciki ina gyara komi sannan na rufe na maida komi yadda yake,sannan na kashe hasken wutar na rufe kofar da key,sai ajiyar xuciya nake ina ayyana yau babu bacci saina ga abunda duk yake cikin wadannan abubuwan.
Agaggauce na koma na samesu kau ni suke jira har sunci tuwon semo da miyar danyar kubewa da inna tace xatai da dare,kasancewar ni gaskiya ko da ina gidan daddy tuwo baya cikin cimata.
Zan iya cewa cin danai masa da wayau na baida wani yawa,amman sainaji yau ina sha,awar inci.
Nace ma sumy "kanwata ni ina nawa ko ba akasa dani ba"
Dariya tayi tace "wace ni ni na isa"
Innada anty na jana wai keda baki damu dashi ba.

"Ai yau tunda dai inna tace tayi aradu zanci kilama har kunnena saiya motsa."
Suka sake yimun dariya ashraf yace "Anty deeyah kamar kinsan nima kau inaci nawa yana motsawa"
Duk muka saka dariya sharifa tace "gaskiya ummi kixo inna ta koya maki irin wannan miyar mai dadi,ban taba jin kinyi irin taba."

Inna me za tai ba dariya ba ina taya ta.
Anty ta rike baki tace "kinji min yarinya,wlh inna konayi tuwon haka suke barina dashi,daga su har Abbansu shiyasa na daina wahalar da kaina.amman tunda abun naku wulakancine duk randa nayisa sai kuncisa,inkuka kiya mutun yayi ta zama da yunwa."

"Eh munji mun yarda,ko yah ashraf?indai irin miyar inna zakina yi zamuci sosai"

Sumy ta fito kula tana cewa "gashi nan matar yaya aci akara,indai tuwone kar ahadani da yaya asashi baro Cyprus babu shiri."
Harararta nai tana dariya inna tace"aikuwa kamar kinsani inhar kikai mata badaidai ba."

Ahaka dai muka tashi tafiya suna jin kamar kar mutai,duk sai sukejin kewa ta dawo masu daman sai su kadai suke shige da ficensu.

Koda mu kaje gida angama magruba,mun samu Abbansu ya dawo anty dasu Sharifa sukai sama wajensa,ni kuma na je kitchen na xuba tuwon na taho dashi daki.
Saida  nai sallata sannan na zauna cinsa,aikuwa sai naji dadin da su sharifa suke fada,na lashe miyar tas ina gamawa aka kira isha,i naje nai brush nazo na kabbara sallar.samun kaina nai da yin adua tsakanina da musaddiq.

"Yah Allah kafini sanin abunda yake cikin xuciyata akan wannan bawa naka,sannan kaika kawoshi cikin rayuwata har igiyar aure ta ratsa tsakaninmu.yah ubangijina ina rokon inhar akwai alkairi acikin alakarmu ka kawo mani sauyi mai kyau acikin rayuwarmu.
Inkuma babu alkairi Allah ka rabani dashi kabani wanda zai rikeni da amana,sannan ya Allah ban san mezan gani acikin wancen littafi ba,ina fatan koma miye ya zama silar warwarewar kokwantona,ya zama silar sanin nidin wacece Allah ka shiga lamarina ka haskaka mun abunda yake cikin duhu."
Najima sosai ina addua sannan na tashi nadauke sallayar naje na watsa ruwa,nashirya don kwanciya.
Gabana ya cigaba da duka sadda na dauki jikkata na rasa wane Zan fara dashi,littafin dai tabbas diary ne ga dukkan alamu,don haka zan fara dashi don sadda zan duba hotunan zanfi fahimtar mutanen dake cikinsa...................


💝💝💝💝💝💝💝

          *CYPRUS*

Bayan ya gama shirya wa ya fito ya sameta anan inda yabarta,ido yasha kuka har ya gode Allah.
Bihakki da gaskiya ita gani take yafifita deeyah akanta,burin ta karya so deeyah sai ita kadai wannan son kai na asmau yana daure masa kai.
"Kuka Kikai akan wannan maganar?inkuwa hakane ashe hawayenki karewa zasuyi ma,u wai ma in tambayeki mekike tsoro kar nafara sonta ke na gujeki?kokuwa dai nidinne bakison kowace mace ta rabeni?kin mance addini yabamu ikon yin har guda hudu,ina sauran iliminki yaje nasan dai ke ba jahila bace,kinsan komi amman kina biyewa son xuciya kina neman dorama kanki abunda zai dameki,wanda kuma kinsa ba zai yuwuba."

Kukan ta sake sawa "wallahi kana sonta,duk boye boyenka sonta kake nizaka munafur............."
Shiru tayi tana rufe bakinra don jin katobarar da take shirin yi,sadda kai kasa tai tana hawaye.

Ransa yasake baci yace"daman nace dake bana sonta?daman nagaya maki bazan sota ba?balle harki turkeni wallahi wallahi kinji rantsuwar dan musulmi kina gaf da fita raina akan wannan abun,kewai haka ake kishin ma tukun kwata kwata naki kishin babu aji babu kangado cikinsa,kota nan wajen kadai deeyah zata danneki,domin ita tana anfani da iliminta bandake kuma kicigaba da ga inda kika tsaya wlh kina gani zan maidake ita in daukota,marar kangado kawai kuma wannan haukar dakike zaije kunnen Inna."

Yasa kafa yafice yabarta tana kuka 
Wayarta ta dauko tana cigaba da kukan tana neman layin anty kubra,don sunyi waya bayan saukarsu kusan sau uku.
Dole tana neman shawara,tana neman yadda zatai da waccen matar dake neman zame mata masifa,duka yaushe suka tare da har irin haka zata faru,har idonsa yabude da wata ita zai sata kwandon shara,ina bazata ita ba dole tanemi mafita tana sonsa tana matukar kaunarsa bata buktar shigowar kowa acikin soyayyarsu,tun ba,aje ko ina ba har zata rasa matsayinta duk yadda yake nuna kulawarsa akanta wannan matar tana neman zame mata katanga.

✨✨

"Ranka yadade halan boyon asama,u kake tunda muka sauka garin nan bata zo ba nima banzo ba,yau kuma naso muxo kace aa"

Murmushi yayi yana zama kan kujera yace "tuba muke amarya kuma uwargida,bata jin dadine yau shiyasa.nima koroni tayi bata son hayaniya"
Rufe baki tai tana dariya kadan tace tana riko hanun mijinta.
"Qalbi anya yar asama ba,a yi gamoba,tambayar mana ogan muji"
Murmushi yayi yace "to ai sun sha miya hakanan princess,kinga inma andace yazo adaidai kenan."
"Amman in hakane naji maku dadi."

Khalipa yace"saura ku saiku dage"
Mikewa tai tana dariya tatafi kawo masa wani abun.

"Wai kaga yadda kayi dan wuya khalipa!koma miye yake damunka to kau babban al,amarine"

Lunshe idonsa yayi yana shafa kyakkyawar sumarsa da tasha gyara,yaja ajiyar xuciya kafin yace cikin sanyin murya.
"Ni kaina jinjina abunda yaramar dani din nake,don ni ko ciwo nake bana ramewa irin haka."

"To wai duk me yayi zafi."

*"Nadeeyah ce"*

Cike da mamaki da son karin bayani nuran yace "nadeyah kuma?meya sameta?ko ince meya hadoku inace tunda muka taho ma babu wanda kakira har yau."

Walida ta tawo masa snacks da fruit sai lemu ta ajiye tana cewa aci dadi lafiya.
Inibi ya ciro yasa bakinsa yana taunawa nura ya xuba ma ido yana jiransa yaji bayani.

"Nura bansan taya zan maka bayanin lamarin ba,amman tabbas abune mai girma acikin xuciyata.ban taba tsintar kaina acikin irin wanna condition din ba,kafin mutaho na alakanta hakan da cewar ina tausayinta ne kurin."
Ya idasa fada yana jin wani iri aransa,kwata kwata baya samun natsuwar xuciya haka nan dai yake falauniya don bashida wata mafita.

"Nifa ban fahimceka ba kayi mun bayani dalla dalla yadda zan san mekedamun ka,insan irin shawarar dazan dora akai."

Numfashi yaja yadafe kirjinsa yana jin yadda xuciyarsa take amsawa,aduk sadda abun yamotsa masa kamar wani ciwo.

"Nura nikaina fa bansan taya zan ganar dakai ba,abunda nasani dai tun bayan barinta gida ta koma wajen anty komi ya fara sauyawa,ban san miye dalili ba.
Bana son dai tana nesa dani ina shiga cikin fargaba da rashin natsuwa,na bar haka matsayin yadda rayuwarta take cikin gararine,shiyasa nakejin haka.daga baya kuma sainayi da gaske bacci yake daukata,ban saba rashin bacci ba aman yanxu walh nura sai dare ya raba tsakiya,har gab da asuba idona kamas,saboda xuciyata ta rike ranar rabuwar mu randa zamu je abuja,yanayin mai tsayawa ga raine sainake ganin kurin ban mata adalci ba,har yanxu idan na runtse idona kukanta nakeji cikin kunnuwana.wallahi nura abubuwan suna neman zautani"

Nura jingina yayi da bayansa ga kujerar dayake zaune,dariya yake boyewa amma ta kasa boyuwa har dai dole ya saketa yaringa kyalkyaka abunsa,yana rike ciki harda kwarewa ruwa ya dauka yasha yana dan tari kadan kadan,ga dariyar yanayi itama.

Tsura masa ido yayi kurin yana kallonsa,miye abun dariya anan maimakon ya jajanta masa ya nuna yaji tausayinsa shine yake dariya.

"Sorry abokina,wallahi dariyar ce tazo babu shiri kayi hkr"

"Miye abun dariyar to?asanina ba abun dariya nabaka ba,abune da zaka tausaya mun halin dana tsinci kaina."

Gyaran murya yayi yana dan murmushi yana son daina dariyar yace"I'm sorry pls,abunne najisa kamar almara abune da ban tunaninsa tun yanzu ba,amman ba abun mamaki bane"

"Wai mekake fada ne"

"Eh ina magana ne akan kariga kafada cikin soyayya,soyayyarma kuma matsananciya kana yiwa nadeya son da ba kayiwa asmau irinsa,duk halin da kake ciki kaunar tace take haddasa maka haka,akwai abubuwan da baka fada ba kila mancewa kai."

"Kana tsananin son jin Muryarta,idan tana magana bakason ta daina,kukanta gana daga maka hankali,baka son tayi nesa dakai,domin ganinta yana saita maka natsuwarka.inkana tare da ita baka son ku rabu.kewarta tana hanaka bacci da tunaninta,wadannan abubuwan sune suka hadu suke baka gwalgwale,yasa kake kasa walwala kake kasa sukuni xuciyarka bata hutu,baka isshen bacci inajin har abinci ba sosai kake ci ba."

Kamar tv haka khalipa ya tusa Nura gaba yana kallo,kaf abunda yake lissafa masa babu wanda bayaji,haka babu wanda baya azabtar dashi amman bai yarda da cewar yana son wata nadeya ba,haba har yaushe ma yasanta da wani abu waishi so zai shiga tsakaninsa da ita.

"Kana ta kallona kana mamaki ko,kana jin kamar karya duk nake"

Idonsa yadan canza muryarsa ta karye yace"aa ba ina nufin kana karya ba,duk inajin haka harma fiye da yadda kake zato.amman.......amma.......badai so ba,badai ina sonta ba saidai ina tausayinta,baka fahimci halin danake ciki ba wannan ba soyayya bace."
Ya fada yana soka hannunsa cikin kansa,yana runtse ido yana fatan abunda yafada yaje har cikin xuciyarsa,kuma ya zama hakanne ba gaskiya bane gana son nadeyah.

Murmushi nura yayi yana gyara zama yana kallonsa cikin murmushi,ganin duk ya rikice lemunsa mai sanyi ya xuba ya mika masa,ganin yana shafa kirjinsa kamar dai yana masa zafi.
Bai musaba ya ansa ya sha rabin cup din sannan ya ajiye,yana jan iska.

Nuran yace"saidai idan kaki gaskiya khalipa,amma duk inda kaje kuma kowa ka gayama halin da kake ciki tabbas anasar gudace zakaji, u are in love with her"

Kallon nura kurin yake kwalwarsa tana sake tariyo maganar nuran,xuciyarsa ta yarda dari bisa dari da abunda nuran ya fada,amman shi karan kanshi bayajin yayarda,kuma baida wasu hujjoji da zasusa yaji cewar ba hakan bane,meye mafita domin kuwa wannan abun mai sunan so dake bashi gwalgwale yana barazana da duk wani walwalarsa da natsuwarsa kai harma da jin dadinsa baki daya.
Tagefe guda bai sashi cikin abubuwan da zasu wakana ba,hakan zai bashi damuwa.
(Nikan made ai bawa baya tsarawa kanshi abunda zaifaru wannan hukuncin ubangijine kurin,don haka komi katsara ba kada tabbacin faruwarsa sai abunda Allah ya tsara maka)

Kamar zai fashe da kuka yake kallon nuran cikin rashin sukuni yace "yazanyi?Nura yazanyi da wannan abun to,wlh ban shirya ma hakan ba kwata kwata ban auri nadeya don nasota ba ko don nadawwama tare da ita saboda ban aureta ina sonta ba,meyasa yanzu wannan son zaimun haka,don Allah ka gaya mun yadda zanyi"

Nura cikin tausayawa don ganin abokin nasa abun dagaske yake damunsa,bai taba ganinsa cikin wannan halin ba jarumin mutun mai jure duk wani wuya da dadi,amman yau ansamu abunda ya karya masa duk wani taurin ransa da jarumtarsa,duk karfin hali irin na khalipa yau gashi gabansa ido jajir kamar zai kuka akan *soyayya*,shiyasa akace karka zuzuta abu haka karka wulakanta abu,dan hakin daka raina shi zai tsole maka ido.

"Yanzu yakake son ayi,ko ince mekakeson nace maka?wannan shawarar da xyciyarka zaka yanke vazan takura maka akan ka zauna da ita ba inhar bakada ra,ayin hakan,amman kashirya yaki da dunbin kaunarta da zazzafar soyayar da kake mata,don ina mai tabbatar maka bazaka iya rayuwa mai dadi baka tare da nadeya ba.domin ayanzu ma dubi yadda duk ka fita hayyacinka,to inaga ace kun rabu na har abada kayi tunani,sannan katuna diyar waye wannan yarinyar,kakuma san bacin kudin bata kowa kuma inka saketa still tana nan cikinku,gaban idinka wani zaizo ya nuna ayana sonta asannan zaka gane menake nufi,ni banga abun daga ahankali anan ba,kasauke girman kai kasanar da ita.ka nuna kayi kuskuren abunda kai mata abaya,hakan shine zaman lfyrka."

Shiru kurin yayi yanata ya mutsa gashinsa kamar zai haukace,tsawon lokaci banda numfashi babu abunda yake saukewa.

bazai iya ba dawane idon to,taya zai kalleta yace yana sonta bacin duk alwashin da suka daukarwa juna nacewar baza su taba son juna ba,duk kurarinsa akan bata da abunda yake so na mace,duo zare idon da cewa bata cikin sahun matan dake burgeshi!wane irin abun kunya xuciyarsa taja masa,ina zaisa kanshi shi dolene yayi yaki da wannan masifar dake neman haddasa masa ciwon xuciya.
Mikewa kurin yayi yace cikin sarkewar murya.
"Zan koma gida sai mun hado wajen training."

"Allah ya kaimu lfya,amman karka sama ranka damuwa akan abunda ba komi bane,wlh ni ban daukesa abun tada jiniyar wuya ba,inka biyewa son ranka wlh zarewa zakai I'm telling u"

"Zanyi tunani akai."
Yajuya yafice daga falon jiri na jansa,haka dai ya iya tarar taxi yawuce gida.


💝💝💝💝💝💝💝

        *NADEEYA*

hannuna har kerma yake wajen bude shafin farko na littafin da akayi rubutu kadan da wani kyakkyawan hand writing,wanda saboda kyansa saida na shafa rubutun don yayi kyau sosai.
Kafin na karanta abunda aka rubuta din *my diary* jinjina kai nayi don daman abunda na zata kenan,ko shakka babu anan zan samu duk wasu tambayoyi dake cinkushe cikin kaina.
Sake bude wani shafin nayi wanda yake cike da wannan kyakkyawan rubutun.
Nace"yadda yakeda kyau afuska rubutunsa ma kyaune dashi kamar fuskartasa."
Tsaki naja ina sake cewa "amman xuciya baki kirin babu komi cikinta sai tsabar sonkai da zalinci"nayi kwata ina dora idon abisa rubutun ina sake gyara zamana don karantawa da kyau.................

_"Sunana Abbakar Assadiq musaddiq,mahaifina Abu Assadiq musaddiq haifeffen garin yolane anan aka haifesu shida kanensa Abu kaseem,su kadai ne a wajen iyayensu,haka kuma anbasu gata yadda ya kamata.domin iyayensu sun jidasu dadin da duk wani da zaiji aduniya idan yana tare da iyayensa,saboda Allah yaba mahaifinsu kudi maisu tarin yawa,babban dan kasuwane awannan lokacin.shiyasa suka zama yan gata kuma na gaban goshin awajen iyayensu,sunyi karatu fiye dakima na addini dana zamani,iyayensu basu rasu ba saida Babana Abu Assadiq yayi aure,ya auri mahaifiyata mai suna *hauwa,u maijidda* ba yadda kakana baiyi da Abba kaseem ba akan yafidda mata ayi masu aure tare da maifina,amman ya murje idonsa yayita shagwaba shi bai isa abure ba,"daman dai shi yaya Abu Assadiq shine yadace da aure yanxu" inji cewar Abba kaseem kenan,dole suka kyalesa ashe babu rabon suga aurensa,don ana auren mahaifina da mahaifiyata da shekara guda gabaki dayansu sukace ga garinku nan,mutuwar data gigita duk wani masoyinsu karma ace yaransu biyu rak aduniya,babu wanda baiji radadin wannan mutuwa ba,kuma yadda mutuwar taxo ta gigita kowa da kowa,domin kuwa yankan rago akai masu cikin dare akuma cikin dakunan baccinsu.lamarin daya jefa iyayena uku cikin tsananin mawuyacin hali,wanda daga Abbana har Abba kaseema haukane kurin basuyi ba,saida suka kwashe tsawon wata biyu kwance gadon asibiti.mahaifiyata ce tsaye akansu ba dare babu rana,da taimakon yar aikin su kakana dake gidan mai suna *baba deluwa*  duk cikin yan aikin gidan itace 
amintacciya kuma wadda suka dauki shekara da shekaru tare dasu,itama taji mutuwar ba kadan ba.da mijinta *baba dauda* tare sukazo garin basu san kowa ba,sunyi gudun hijira daga kyauyensu suka fado cikin garin yola,har Allah yasa suka dace da iyayen gida masu sanin kima da daraja dan adam,kakana da kakata suka anshesu hannu biyu babu abunda basa samu daga garesu,har sadda mutuwa tazo ta gifta atsakaninsu.................Wannan shine mafarin duk wata matsala da matsaltsalu dasuka afkawa rayuwarmu"_😳

Anan page din farko yakare ma nadeyah.........

Nasan masu karatu zasuso jin yadda duk lamuran suka kasance,muje xuwa WASA FARIN GIRKI.

MOM MUHSEEN CE.

Episode  .27

Cikin kuka mai ban tausayi da tsuma xuciya nake cewa "na shiga ukuna ni nadeya,me na keji haka? Mena binciko wa kaina daddy ne yake aikata irin wannan mummunan laifin,innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un naje na jiyowa kaina abunda zai addabeni.........shiyasa daman nakejin gabana yana yankewa yana faduwa."
Dagowa nai na bude shafin ina kallonsa,kamar akan idona komi yake faruwa hawaye share share,sai kuma na duba akwai fa sauran pages da yawa,hakan nanufin ba wannan kadai ne abun da zanji da zai sake jefa ni cikin rudani ba,gabana yacigaba da faduwa anya kuwa!anya zan iya cigaba da karatun nan kuwa???hakika akwai kura akasa.
 don kuwa da alama iyayen yah musaddiq suma wani mummunan al,amari yana faruwa dasu,tunda gashi azahiri yanzu ban tabajin inda anty koshi sun anbacesu ba,kokuma suce zasuje garesu alamun dai basa duniyar.!
"Wayyo Allah na,aa bazan iyaba gaskiya zan ajiye in samu natsuwa wannan gigitaccen tashin hankalin karya hanani sukuni.amman kuma zan iya din kuwa,?saboda nasan koda yaushe tunanin ya ake karewa zanyi."
Motsin kofata naji yasa nai saurin soka book din kasan filona,na kwanta pretending as bacci nake.

"Aaaha pretty ashe bacci take naga glup duk akunne na zaci idonki biyu har karfe biyu." Kashe kwayayen tayi sannan,
Ta iso bakin kaina ta dago kaina tana maida hulata data zame gashina yabaje,kissing din for head dina tayi tace"komin ki irin na *Ammerh* Ubangiji Allah ya baiyyana ki *Ammerh* in kuma kin mutu Allah yakai rahma kabarinki"
Naji digar kwallah saman goshina alamun dai Antyn hawaye take,rudani na sake shiga wacece kuma wata Ammerh?meye alakarta da ita da komina yake irin nata,taya suka santa har suke kewarta.don ga duk alamu ya nuna ba karamin sabo ne yake tsakaninsu ba.
Gyara mun kaina tayi tamike tafita tana jamun kofar,bude idona nai batare dana tashi ba tunani barkatai cike cikin kaina,idan na ayyano wani abun saina ga abazai yuwu ba.kokuma naga ba haka bane,da haka har barawo bacci ya lallabo ya saceni ba tare dana shirya hakan ba,bacci mai cike da mafarkai kala kala na abunda na karanta naji acikin wannan diary..............

Ban sake bibiyar book din ba saboda muna ta shirye shiryen jarabawa a school dinmu,inason najajirce naxo  overall.
Anty ma tana taimaka mun ta wannan bangaren,haka duk week end zamuje gyaran jiki nida ita wajen abokiyarta.ina samun kulawa sosai tanan bangaren,don Anty kamar yarta haka ta daukan jitake dani sosai,ko kadan bata mun firar yaya musaddiq haka zalika nima ko saudaya bantaba tambayarta yayake ba,bana kuma ji ajikina cewar yakirani a waya.bana tunanin ma iyanzu bai mance da wata mai kama dani ba,shiyasa nima nake iyakar kokarina wajen sarrafa xuciyata inhar naga zata kawo mun cikas.
Gefe guda tarin tunanin cigaban labarin nan yana nan cikin raina,dana dauko sai gabana ya fadi haka zan maidashi,don idan na cigaba to hankalina kasuwa gida biyu zayyi,ga kuma jarabawata da muka fara cikin wannan satin.kuma alhamdulillah ina samun marks sosai da sosai.nabari damun karasa exams babu sauran wasu abubuwa akaina saina cigaba da karatun diary din.

Su inna sunxo mana yini ita da sumy,ranar banje ko ina ba mun samu interval na kwana biyu,mukasha hirarmu da wasa da dariya,nida sumy muka shige daki muna hira don sumayya badai iya labari ba.

(Bari muleka wajen su oga khalipa)
💝💝💝💝💝💝💝

          *Cyprus*

Tun daga lokacin da suka samu sabani da asmau,komi ya sake lalacewa tsakaninsu saboda kwata kwata asmaun daya sani da mai sanyin hali da kunya da biyayya,yanzu kwata kwata ta juye ta canza halaye,yana iyakar kokarinsa don ganin bai tayeta ba ta ko ina,kodan cikin dake jikinta.
Sau daya wa ma saboda cikin yake daga mata kafa baya biyewa haukarta,don babu ranar da bazata jashi suyi rigima ba,duk akan NADEEYAH.

khalipa duk ya rame ya lalace saboda wuta biyu ke cinsa,gata masifar asmau ga wadda take killace cikin kirjinsa da ruhinsa babu rana ko lokacin da yake samun sassauci ta nan bangaren,kamar zaiyyi karamin hauka haka yakeji.
Kwana biyu yakira anty suka gaisa ya bata hkr rashin jinsa datayi,tace masa tai masa uxiri ai amarci yakeyi,maganar ma saita bashi haushi wane amarci inbanda masifa da asmau take gana masa baiga wani jin dadi ga xuwansa wannan couse din ba,wata sa,in har nadamar tahowa da Asmaun yake,da ace shikadai yazo kila bala,in baikai haka ba. 
Gawajen training kullin tsaurarawa suke,jiyake kamar yayi tsuntsuwa yabaro kasar,inya dawo gida babu natsuwa, asmau kwata kwata ta canza,bata kulawa dashi tunda yaki yarda da kudirinta.
Kuma yaki sanar da ita gaskiyar lamari idan son nadeya yake ya gaya mata,yakiya yana mata munafurci inji cewarta.
Wannan dalilin yasa baima tambayi deeya ba yadai tambayi yara,har suka gaisa da su sharifa.

Suna cikin wata na hudu da xuwa yaji abun yana neman gagararsa,don aboye ciwo yake dagaske don har baya isashen bacci,ga ciwon kai matsananci dayake matsa masa.idonsa yayi xuru xuru babu natsuwa,nura ya gaji yace masa 
"Aboki na maizai gana ka dauki leave kurin kaje ka ganta,don wallahi nan da lokaci kadan ana iya baka gado inhar ba xuwa kai kaganta ba,don ga dukkan alamu dai ciwon nan natane"
Yana kwance ne saman three seater yana kallon sama,amman idonsa arufe yake ruf ba kuma baccine yake ba,tunani ne kurin yake yi.

"Bakace komi ba,Allah ba batun wasa va kana bukatar hutu mai kyau kodan kadawo hayyacinka,nifa tunda nake dakai bantaba ganinka cikin irin wannan mawuyacin halin ba."

Numfashi yaja muryarsa da tasake kankancewa ya koma abun tausayi yace"konaje ba hutun bane nura,saboda ga asmau bazata barni na zauna lafiya ba.saboda masifarta kadai yarinyar nan ta jefani cikin wani hali,balle kuma ga halin dana ke ciki"

"Asma u bata kai ta jefaka cikin wannan halin ba,ina mamakin ka ma dahar ka samata ido tana garaka kamar wani abun wasan yara,kadauki mataki kurin shine zai dawo da ita cikin hayyacinta."

"Ba ita kadai bace inajin tsoron wani abu ya sameta ita da babyn"

"Da wannan,aman gaskiya kasan matakin dauka bazai yuwu kayita zama cikin wannan halin ba,karka manta akwai case gabanka babba,yana bukatar natsuwarka da kuma hankalinka."

"Nasani wallahi, kullin yana raina,sonake na magance matsalar dake damuna,inason yakice yarinyar nan ne axuciyata amman abun yaci tura."

"Inhar kanason maganin wannan matsakar to kuwa kabada kai kurin,kaje ka kwantar da kai kabada baki ka gabatar da soyayarka shine zai fiddaka cikin wannan halin."

Runtse idonsa yayi yana yamutsa fuska yace "nura na gaya maka I can't abun ba mai sauki bane agareni,zandai nemi wata hanyar aman ba wannan ba."

"Aikuwa xaka kashe kanka ne abanza don wlh inhar taurin kai zaka nuna kaine a wahale,inkaji zaka je hutun to inkuma baka xuwa kai kasani ni nagaji da treating dinka haka kamar wani dan yaro."
Ya fada cikin jin haushi don ganin yakasa yarda da abunda yake ganin shine mafira kurin agaresa,miye aciki don yaje yabata hkr ya nuna kuma yana kaunarta,inbacin taurin kai.

"Karka ga laifina nura,don bakasan takun sakar da mukasha yi da yarinyar nan ba,babu irin bakaken maganganun da bamu gayama juna ba nida ita ba,babu irin kurari da cika bakin da banyi ba,sai kawai rana tsaka naje nadurkusa mata ina rokon soyayyarta,wane irin xubewar kima da mutuncine wannan zaijawo mun,mubar ma wannan maganar Allah daya dora mani shizai yayemun.maganar xuwa hutu kuma insha Allah zan dauka koda na sati ukune ko wata guda,anjima saika rakani na shigar da bayani."
Kafa da kurin nura ya daga don haushi ya kulesa baya iya cewa komi akai,yasan dai dole akwai ranar da girman kan zaisauka kilama har kuka sai yayi mata.

Bai samu matsala da oganinsu ba,saboda ganin da sukai masa ya nuna dagasken baya lafiya,mai makon su bashi hutun one month din daya bukata,sai kurin suka ce yaje sai lokacin dawowarsu yayi nan da wata biyu,daman zasu tai hutun wata biyu idan sun cike watanni shidan,
Sannan sudawo sucike wata shiddan daya rage masu.
Ya dawo gida agajiye ya sameta falo ta baje tana shan iska,da wani dan miny siket jikinta sai shimi tayi kyau sosai,jikinta yasake mulmulewa kamar wata yar turawa,dan cikinta ya tasa ba laifi don zai shiga wata na biyar da wani abu.
Kallonta yayi yaji ransa ya biya amman babu batun yace zai rabeta sa sunan biyan bukatarsa,sai sadda taga dama ko ita takeso.
Babu KO sannu da xuwa tamasa kallo daya ta kauda kai,tana cigaba da cin apple dinta da ayaba data yayyanka ga kallo tanayi.
Gajiya yayi da kallonta yace"Asmau mekika maidani acikin gidan nan?wane irin matsayine dani wajenki?shin kin daukan sakarai kome kike nufi,wane irin zama kikeson nayi dake kigaya mun ina saurarenki "
Juyar dakai tayi gefe guda tana turo baki tace"mefe!ni kurin inda zaka fito ka gaya mun inda kadosa duk da ba haka ba,na gayamaka ina matukar kishinka ni kaina bansan yawansa ba,don me bazaka tayanu kiyaye abunda zai sakani naji haushi akai ba,bafa don ka dore da aurenta ka aurota ba nasani,to akanme zaka fara sonta bayan kagaya mun cewar ni kadai na isheka.to inhar hakane meyasa ka canza bakada aiki sai tunaninta,dubeka yaya duk ka lalace kakoma kamar wanda yayi ciwon shekara,duk saboda ita taya kake zaton xuciyata data dauki wannan."
Ta fada tana xura masa idonta da suka cika da kwallah.

Kansa ya sara saboda bacin rai,idonsa ya karayin jajawur cikin bacin rai yace"saboda don ke kadai aka halicceni shiyasa bazan so kowa ba saike kadai?to bari na gaya maki abunda baki sani ba yau saiki sani,wata kila zaisaka kishiga taitayinki.bakomi yasa na fara sonta ba sai wannan mahaukacin kishin naki,daya kenan nabiyu soyayyar dakike kirarin kina yimun,rashin hkrinki da kauda kanki yasa duk bana ganinta,haka zalika son danake maki saboda rashin kunyarki da rashin biyayyar dakike mun wlh asmau duk sun xube,keda kanki kika daskarar mun da kaunar danake maki,wanda nayi imanin wadannan abubuwan suka jani ga kaunar nadeya,wanda ban shiryama hakan ba.bata anbata mun sunanki da nuna jin haushi,bata kirana munafuki kamar yadda kikeyi,wanda ina mamakin kaina da har nake kyaleki aduk sanda kika kirani haka.sannan tana bin abunda nace koda bai mata dadi ba,haka zalika hakkokinta da muka danne nidake bata taba daga kai ta kalleni ba balle har tazageni kamar yadda kikeyi,to hkrina yazo karshe tunda na kasa daidaitaki idan munkoma gobe saiyi zamanki wajen inna,wata kila ita ta daidaitaki tunda ni nakasa,alla bashshi sadda kikai hankali kikasan matsayina,kika iya sarrafa harshenki,kika iya yarda da duk abunda nace.lokacin sai na iya sake duban ki da wata soyayyar,amman ba yanxu ba da kika daukan sakarai,wanda bai isadake ba.na gama magana karfe bakwai jurginmu zaitashi,inkin iya tashi dawuri muntafi,sannan kisani karki sama ranki dawowa nan,kinriga kin gama zamanki ngd da wanda kikai."
Yasa kai yabarta tana kuka kamar ranta zai fita,banda Allah wadarai da take da kanta da xuciyarda data dorata akan turbar,babu abunda take.
Wallahi duk bayin kanta bane,ta gaza jurewa ne wai ya mata suke yi da kishine?anya nata datakeji ya danne na saura ba?don kuwa da ace saura haka sukeji akan mazajensu,wata kila saisunyi abunda tafi nata.
(Nikam nace Uhmmm Asmaun inna kenan,)

💝💝💝💝💝💝💝

         *NIGERIA*

Yaune zamu kammala exams dinmu,kuma yaudin za,a bayar da kyauta ga duk wanda sukai kokari,harda certificate za,a bada shiyasa tun da asuba ban koma ba,nafito na hau aikace aikacena dana keyi.
Yaudin ina cikin farin ciki marar iyaka,haka zalika gefe guda inajin bugun xuciyata yana sauyawa,bansan dalili ba wata sa in har ajiyat xuciya nake saukewa.duk jikina yana yin sanyi kuma,a haka muka gama hada break nida samha,wanda yana daya daga cikin girkin dana koyo,tun kafin yaje baki kamshinsa ya addabi gidan,samha cewa take "gaskiya Anty kinkai makura aradu wannan inakaci kunnenka saiya cire ya dawo" nikau mexanyi ba dariya ba.
Anty ma data fito santinsa kurin take,tace "wanan dai abincin kasashen turai kika hada mana ko pretty,nayi dariya kuri naje ina tayasu sharifa shiryawa,har suka gama muka fito dasu on ready daman saida nayi wanka nafito na fara aikin,break muka zauna munayi muna hira Abban su baya gari yaje abuja shiyasa yau tare da anty mukeyi,saida su sharifa suka wuce sch sannan anty take sanar dani abu da ya saka naji tun daga yatsuna har sauran sassan jikina sun daskare saboda yadda sashen dayake kulle yabude.
Cewa taiyi "pretty karfe nawa kuke dawowa"

"Anty bama wuce 12:00 insha Allah wani abunne?"

"Eh,inaso kidawo ne dakanki ki girkawa mijinki abunda zaici,suna hanya yakirani da asuba sun taho"

Gabaki daya jinai kamar ta sani cikin freezer saboda yadda na daskare,kalonta kurin nake with out say anything.
Murmushi tayi tace "wannan kallon fa,kika wani kame kamar soja lafiya"
Sauke idona nai nace"aa Anty miye saina yi masa dakaina,kinga kila saimun kai 2:00 saboda yaufa muke fanal,"
Na fada ina juya fork dina danake cin abincin dashi.

Murmushin ta sakeyi tace "kedin dai kexakiyi don haka ki tsara lokaci har kinje kidawo,kafin nan ga wannan dashi zakiyi wanka amman saikin dawo,inkin gama komi.sannan kishafa wannan matsayin man shafawa ajikinki,kin gane ai"
Kamar zanyi kuka na daga mata kai kurin,tatashi ta fara tattara wajen.

(Taufa GA ABU KHALIPA GA NADEYAH🤭 akafta)
