LOV U FISABILILLAH💝😊FISABILILLAH💝😊

Episode  .26

Jikina har rawa yake wajen budo shafi na biyun,don page din farko yafara kidimani da sonjin meya ke faruwa,meya kawo sunan deluwa aciki,harda tsohon marigayin mijinta.me hakan ke nufi ni nadiya.idona kuri akan takadar na sake gyara kwanciyata ina cigaba da karatun..............

_"Abbana da Abba kaseem saida aka hada da rokon Allah da addua kafin Allah yasa suka samu dangana,amman kallo guda zakai masu kasan suna cikin tsananin maraicin iyayen nasu.haka suka rungumo suka dawo gida duk jarumta da karfin hali irin na iyayen nawa saida suka fashe da kuka lokacin da suka ga gidan wayam,babu iyayen nasu aciki.karshe dai dole Abbana shine ya koma lallashin Abba kaseem,kafin bayan kwana bakwai da rasuwar ya tattara duk masu aikin gidan,tun daga kan maigadi har mai kula da fulawowi."_

_kallonsu yaringayi daya bayan daya,don ko zai samu idonsa yaga marar gaskiya cikinsu,amman ko daya idonsa bai ga haka ba,daga maigadi yafara yace masa "baba gaddafi inason sanin wanda yashigo last agidan nan,"baba gaddafi yana ta alwallar kuda yana muxurai yace "rankayadade *jafar ne* na wajen su dauda" Abbana bai sauke idonsa ko ina ba sai akan su deluwa da baba dauda,baice komi ba yamaida idonsa akan baba gadafi yace"bayan shi waye ya sake shigowa?" Yana rausayar da kai yace "wlh yallabai bayanshi banji ko yashigo ba,get rufe yake kuma banji motsin kowa ba"jinjina kai kurin ya juyo yana kallon baba dauda yace masa "ina jafar" "rankayadade jafar yana daki kwance da zazzabi jikinsa,don zan iya cewa tun da kai mutuwarnan baya lafiya,kuma bairasa nasaba sa rashin da mukaine ya kwantar dashi"ya fada da ainahin gaskiyarsa._

_Abbana baice komi ba yafara magana cikin rauni da kunan xuciya,don inka kalli idonsa yayi jajawur yake cewa "vana zargin kowa acikin masu aikin gidan nan,domin kuwa dagamu harsu su baban sunyi maku yardar da kokadan xuciya bazata kawo zarginku ba,amma dolene ayi bincike badan hakan nanufin wanine acikinku ba,rayuwa ta canza makashinka zai iya kasancewa ako ina,inanufin zai iya zama makusancinka ba abun mamaki bane,don haka kowa bincike yazo ta kanshi karya damu inhar yasan bayada saka hannu aciki,amman kuma inhar aka kama wani acikinku hakika ba karamin maci amana bane wannan kowaye,don haka bana bukatar bacewar wani acikinku har watan tanan ya kare,don haka kuzama cikin shiri kuna iya tashi na gama daku."_

_duk tashi sukai daya bayan daya,kafin dan sanda dake tsaye kan case din yashigo shida maitaimaka masa, "yallabai gaskiyar magana munyi duk wani bincike acikin kwanakin nan amman bamu samu wani abu dazai doramu bisa zargin wani ba,haka likitan daya duba gawarsu ya bamu shaidar cewar wanda yayi wannan aikin bai bar wata shaida da za,a iya ganeshi ba.abun gaskiya da daure kai amman yanzu zamu bi duk ma aikatan mucigaba da binciken mu akansu,insha Allah yallabai koma waye zamu ganoshi." Bai iya magana ba don kanshi ciwo yake,da hannu kurin yayi masu nuni yatashi yabar falon._

_haka aka cigaba da bincike babuji babu gani na tsawon lokaci acikin gidan,amman ko kadan basu samu wata hujja ba dazasu zargi wani,lalli hakan ya nuna mikisan yashirya kuma ba lokaci guda ya shirya hakan ba,hkr suka ba Abbana domin kuwa duk hanyar da zasu bincika sunyi amman babu wani cigaba.haka su Abbana sunaji suna gani suka hkr da neman wanda ya aikata masu wannan aikin,amman kuma sun tsaurara tsaro sosai agidan don ba lallai andaina bibiyarsu bakenan. Wannan kenan_

_"Deluwa meyasa lokaci guda zamutashi,bacin ba korarmu akai ba mutanen gidanan kullin banda kyautatawa babu abunda suke mana,rana tsaka kuma kuce zamu tafi." Cewar abbas dan dan deluwa wanda yake wane ga jafar,saidai ba baba dauda ne ya haifesa ba,mijinta nafari auren saurayi da budurwa suka haifesa.kallon wulakanci kurin take masa,jafar yana gefe yana cin naman kaza harda madara hollandia,cikin son ci abbas yace masa "kanena babutayi,inakasamo kaza haka naga yau ba juma,a bace balle ince ranar cin naman muce."cikin mugunta da zalinci ya hankadashi gefe yana zare masa ido yace"to maye bazaka ci ba,kuma sai akace maka dolene sai ranar juma,a zanci nama,angaya maka irinkane ni banza da baisan hanyar samun kudi ba,matsa daga nan makolon banza." Duk deluwa najinsu saima dariya da tayi tace"Allah ya kara banza kaxubda girmanka wajen kanenka saboda kwadai,to inkanaso karinga cin irin wannan katashi kanemi na kanka mana." Gefe yaja yana hawaye kurin daidai shigowar baba dauda adakin dasuke yaga shirgin kayan da suka hada,ya kallesu yace "duk na miye wadannan?" Harara ta maka masa tace"tashi zamuyi mana,dana danlelena ya samo mana gidan zama."cikin mamaki ya idasa shigowa yacewa jafar din "kai gidan ubanwa ka samu kudin da har kasamu halin kama gida,kuma nan din me akai mana da zamu tashi,deluwa daga mutuwar su Alhaji shine zamuyi wa yaransu tawaye,amana batace haka ba.gaskiya babu inda zamu nan din dai shine rufin asirin mu,."jafar yayi jifa da robar lemu yana hararar baban nashi cikin guna guni yake cewa "wallahi ni bazan cigaba da zama karkashin wasu ba,tunda dai nasamu galihuna dole mutashi,muma muje mu kafa tamu rayuwar kamar kowa,aiba don wahala aka haliccemu ba ko" cike da mamaki baban yake kallonsa bakinsa sake,bai samu damar magana ba yaji deluwar tana cewa."kwantar da hankalinka dan lelena tashi kam ai gobe babu fashi,wadanda muke jima kunyar babu su to zaman me zamuyi" Abbas dake gefe yayi tagumi yace"wai deluwa mekuke takama dashi har dakuka matsa sai muntashi,dagani har jafar bawani aiki muke ba balle musa ran kudi zasu shigo mana,dan kafintar da damuke xuwa nidashi yadaina nikadai naketa xuwa,ba wani abun kirki ake badawa ba balle ince zasu isa mu kafa kanmu." Baban yace"dadina dakai Abbas fadar gaskiya,inkuma aiki ka samu har kasayi gida saika gaya mana muji."harare harare yake tayi yace"ehy din wani ubangida na samu nake wa aiki,natara kudi shine nasiya." Kaitsaye baban yace"karya kake jafaru,kajidai nace karya ko,to itakake saidai idan sata kafara.............aa ya ishekafa dauda karka sake aibata mun dana,tashidai kamar munyi kenan,inkunji zaku bimu falillahil hamdu,inkuma kunanan zamu dawwama cikin bauta gafili ga mai doki"tana gama fadar haka tatashi tafito daga dakin._

_bangaren su Abbana kuwa lamura sunata tafiya yadda ya kamata,dukiyarsu da Alhajinsu ya bari yakira mairabon gado yace araba aba kowa nashi,saboda halin rayuwa Allah kadai yasan gawar fari.haka kuwa akai lokacin ummina tana da cikin Anty Aysher,baifi wata uku ba lokacin.babu yadda Abba kaseem baiyi akan yabar komi wajensa ba Amman abban yace aa,gara dai kowa yarike nashin zaifi,sauran kadarori kuma suka cigaba da juya su,kudi suna linkuwa linkin ba linkin.Abbana yana kulada gidan kaji daya fado cikin gadonsa,sannan akwai masana,antar dake sarrafa roba sune nasa kuma ba kananun kudine ake kawo masa ba,Abba kaseem company vesilen ne yake kula dashi sai wani kafcecen gidan gona.banda aikin gwamnati dayake,babban engineer ne yana daukar albashi mai tsoka,dukiyarsu bunkasa take haka talakawa anfana suke da ita,sadakatujjariya suketa yiwa iyayensu da dukiyar,hannunsu baya yankewa da kyauta.tadalilin hakan har karin albashi sukaiwa ma aikatan gidan,don su karu suma.amman wannan baisa deluwa da jafar sun yarda zasu cigaba da zama ba.Abba kaseem ya samu takaddar karo wani cause indiya,zaije yayo wata takwas sannan ya dawo ko kadan Abbana baiji dadin haka ba don baya son suna nesa da junansu,amman kasancewar cigaba ne yataso dole yayi hkr,ansaka ranar tafiyar kenan saiga deluwa da maigidanta akan zasu tashi sun ajiye aiki._

_"subhanallahi deluwa,baba dauda me akai maku?wani yayi maku wani abune?don Allah kuyi hkr duk zaman taren da akai baidace rana daya arabu ba.inma aikin kukeson ajiyewa nayarda don kuwa tsufa ya kamaku amman banzanji dadi kuyinesa damu ba."duk abbana yake wannan jawabin.deluwa tadauke kai tace "yallabai kayi hkr tashin nan dolene,badan kuma anyi mana komi ba kawai dai muna son murayu cikin yanci,munyi gidan kanmu don haka zamutashi Allah ya sadamu da alkairi" kallon mamaki yake mata,bata taba masa magana irin haka ba,meyafaru wai.yajuya kan baba dauda yace"baba kagaya mun inwani abune yafaru"kalon deluwar yayi yana yan dabur dabur yace"aa rankayadade,babukomi wallahi,yadda dai kajidin hakane"ya fada don baisan mezaice ba,deluwa ta riga tarantse saisuntashi,shikuma bazai iya zama inda babu iyalinsa ba._

_Abba kaseem ransa yabaci don yafi Abbana saurin hawa yace"madalah to tunda babu abunda akai maku,Allah yasadamu da alkairi nawane kudin aikinku sai asallameku na wannan watan."deluwa tai zakal tace"nidashi muna da dubu talatin talatin."jinjina kai yayi yana mamakin canzawarta,bai wani jinkiri ba yace"za,a baku har dubu hamsin hamsin ma kutai duk inda kuke ganin ya fiye maku nan,ai mungode Allah tunda bamuda hakkinku,inkuma kuna da namu kuda Allah."yafada cikin bacin rai jikkar dake gabansa ya zage cike take da kudi,ya kirga dubu dari yaba baba amma deluwa tai caraf ta anshe tana washe baki.Abbana yaxaro bandir din yan dari biyu yace "ku kara da wannan Allah yada saduwarmu"tasa hannu ta anshe tana cewa amen,tama riga baba dauda fita.ya durkusa cikin jimami yace"yaran nan kuyi hkr babu yadda na iya da ita,nima banso ba amman banida yadda zanyi,idan nataba maku wani abu wanda ba daidai ba kuyafe mun,nikam bakuyi mani komi ba iyayenku sunyi mana komi na rayuwa,ubangiji yakai rahma kabarinsu,kukuma Allah yayi maku albarka yabaku xuria tagari.nabarku lfy" ya mike jiki ba kwari yafita shima.ajiyar xuciya suka sauke Abba kaseem yace "matarcen abun tambayace yaya,akwai abunda yasa zasu tashi tunda tsawon shekarun dasukai damu,basu taba yunkurin haka ba anya baka ganin abincikesu." Girgiza kai yayi yace "aa kaseem karka sawa xuciyarka zargi,ka yarda da abunda sukace din kurin,kuma bana jin wadancen mutanen zasu cutar damu da zasuyi tunin duniya dasunyi,don haka kabar maganar kurin yaushe jirginka zaitashi" shiru yayi badan xuciyarsa ta yarda da abunda yayan yace masa ba cikin sanyin jiki yace "karfe goma na safen gobe" "oky Allah ya kaimu amma don Allah kaseem kayi mun alkawarin kulawa da kanka,kaga baka da kowa acan sannan magana ta gaba itace aure,kayi mun alkawarin daka dawo zakayi aure,tunda dai akwai yan mata dayawa dasuke sonka." Murmushi yayi yace "insha Allah,kasani ma ko indauko maka ba indiya ma" dariya sukai sannan suka wuce cikin gidan don cin abincin dare._

_sadda baba dauda ya isa bakin kofar da zata shigar dashi dakunan su,yaci karo da Abbas ya labe yakasa kunnensa yana jin wata magana daga cikin dakin,ganin baban yasa yayi saurin jawosa yana masa alamar yayi shiru ya saurari abunda ake fada acikin dakin,hakan kuwa yayi don yana jiyo karadin jafar yana cewa"wallahi deluwa kin iya tsara dubaru,kinsan duk hanyoyin dakika tsara mani lokacin su nabi,amman kuma nasha wahala,nasha fargaba don wallahi kirin yarage wannan jamilun mai kawo wa Alhaji abincin kaji bai kamani ba,kai gaskiya banida imani deluwa yadda na cire imani na yanka makogwaronsu,kinga yadda suke shurr shure kamar dabbar da aka yanka."dariya suka kwashe da ita tace"aini nasan ban haifi raggoba shiyasa nake mamakin wancen lusarin bai iya komi ba sai lankwashe kai kamar marayan jakki,na tabbata da yasan da wannan labarin tuni saiya tona maka asiri,kodan akamaka tunda bai kaunarka.amman kudin nan zasu ishemu shekara da shekaru ko dana" dariya yayi yace "uhmm deluwa kenan yo Allah na tuba deluwa wadannan yan kudin tuni zan karar dasu,kafin kifara hada mana plan b din da wasu zasu shiho wadanda zamu gudu mubar gari,koya kikace."shiru tayi kafin tace"anya jafaru bamun bari haka nan ba,inajin tsoron Aikata masu wani abun,sunyi mana komi fa kuma naga kudin daka dauko cikefa suke da akwati,gaba jafaru"tsaki yaja zai magana kenan tai saurin rufe masa baki,tana masa nuni da wajen taga.kallon wajen yayi inuwar mutum yagani,ido yazaro murya kasa kasa yace "mun shiga uku deluwa suwaye" itama cikin tashin hankali tace "ban sani ba leka kagani ahankali." Sando yafara har yasamu yaje yadaga labulen wajen,ido biyu yayida babansa da yayansa,sune suka kasa kunne suna jin duk abunda suke cewa,don babansa kuka yake sosai shikanshi Abbas din kukan yake,deluwa ta biyo bayansa tana rike baki cikin masifa tace "ashe labe kuke mana,Abbas dauda kenan duk kunji abunda muka tattauna."cikin kuka baban yace "ashe abunda kuka aikata kenan,deluwa ba shakka kincika maciya amana bakida sunan daya dace dake face annamimiya,maha,inciya wlh sai Allah yatoni asirinku,kuma yanzu basai anjima ba zanje na sanar da yallabai abunda kuka aikata." Yatashi daker saboda ciwon kafafu garesa,kafin yayunkura jafar yasaka wata igiya ya shake masa wuya,yajashi cikin dakin ya rufe kofa............dan danan hankulansu yatashi,don babu tantama jafar bazai kyale babansa ba,tunda har yaji cewar zaije yatona.abbas magiya yake roko yake akan ya bude kofar karya aikata masa wani abun,amma inaaa bayaji baya gani idonsa yarufe saida yaga baban nashi yabar shure shure da kafafu,sannan yajefar dashi yana nishi................_
Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un🙆🏼‍♀️

Abunda nadeyah take fada kenan tana ajiye littafin,basan sadda wani kuka yataho mata ba,tasoka kanta cikin filo tana rerawa..............

TAUFAH KUNJI ABUNDA JAFAR YAYI MASU KARATU,UBANKA DAYA HAIFEKA!😭

Mom muhseen ce.

LOV U FISABILILLAH💝😊

*ASSALAMU AKAIKUM,kafin mu cigaba zan danyi magana da duk wani ma,abocin karanta littafin abu khalipa,watau ina fuskantar kananun maganganu daga wasu daga cikin masu bin book din nan,abisa halin da asmau take ciki ana mun complain baidace ace yarinyar data fito gidan tarbiyya mai ilimi tana nuna irin wannan halin ba,sannan nanuna afarko nadeeya bata da tarbiyya daga baya kuma na nuna itace ta kirki sama da asmau,abunda nake son kusani shine da yawan mata kishi yana daya daga cikin abunda yake canza masu halayya,kun sani saidai kuki fada dayawa mata baza su iya control din xukatansu alokacin da suke ganin wasu matan zasu dauke hankulan mazansu ba,don haka kishi babu ruwansa da gidan dakika fito,haka ba ruwansa da iliminki,ya danganta da tadda kika sarrafa xuciyarki,inkin iya saita xuciya kin tsira!inbaki iya sarrafa xuciyarki ba kina ruwa aradu can kusada breaker* 🤷🏼‍♀️😂.
*sannan bayan haka saunawa yarinya za,a bata tarbiyya da ilimi daidai misali amman idan taje gidan aure ko kuma tabar gidansu zata aikata abunda zaka rantse bata san me ake nufi da tarbiyya ba,darasin danake son isarwa banxo wajen ba har yanxu,amman munfara samun matsala daku.sannan akan deeyah duk wanda yake bin book dinannan yasan cewar anreneta ne saboda wani dalili,bata tashi hannun iyayenta ba balle ta samu tarbiyya maikyau,kun sani duk dan da baya tare da iyayensa sai abunda aka dorasa akai,halaye na banza da basu dace ba sune zasu zama hakayensa saidai in Allah ne ya tsare,amman dayake Allah Alhakim ne daga gefe daya akasamu wadda take ankarar da ita,saboda Allah yaga cewar ga halin da take ciki,haka zalika babu wanda bai canzawa akoda yaushe,kafiri ya koma musulmi haka muslumi yayi ridda yakima kafurci,to don nadeya ta gyaru ta koma ta kirki ba abun mamaki bane,domin kuwa ni ban dora asmau matsayin star din book dina ba,labarin ba akanta yake ba kawai dai na shigo da itane don isar da wasu sakonni,don haka ina mai sanar da masu mun tsogunguma akan book dina,inzasu bini ahaka bismilla muje xuwa inkuma basa iyawa kofa abude take babu lallai babu tilas,don haka babu wacce zanbi ra,ayinta abun da natsara haka zanyi ehy saidai kuyi hkr haka ABU KHALIPA ZAI CIGABA DA TAFIYA INSHA Allah.*

Episode  .28

Jiki asabule nake shirin tafiya ko kadan ban kawo ma raina shi nan kusa ba,kwata kwata ban zaci har sun isa dawowa ba.tabe baki nai ina saka lifayata na yane jikina da ita,babu kwalliyar komi afuskata inbanda farar powder da lipstick,kwallinma yau ban saba saboda duk jina nake wani iri.
Tsaki naja nace "har sun gaji da amarcin kenan za,a dawo aida sunyi zamansu ni hakan ma yafiye mun kwanciyar hankali."
Yau anty bata xuwa ko ina shiyasa naga bari na barmata wani dan aiki kafin na dawo.

"Anty tunda kin matsa dole saina masa girki,naga akwai naman rago a freg don Allah ki dibi tsokar kamar guda ashirin ki dafa man ita tayi luguf,saiki ki sauke kibarmun shi saina dawo."

Dariya tayi tace "kice yau dadi zamuci,Allah yasa dai za,a yan mana ladar ganin ido."

"Uhm bacin kurin kin dage ne wlh da ko ruwa  nibazan bashi da hannuna ba"

"Lalalala mijin naki pretty."
Sunburar baki nayi nace "niba mijina bane,don yau zai bani takaddata"
Me anty zatai ba dariya ba,ganin idan nabiye ta zan makara yasa nafice kurin,nabarta tana cigaba da dariyar.

Cikin yardar ubangiji muka zana wannan jarabawa ta final,kuma inada tabbacin cewa babu inda nai kuskure,ba adauki dogon lokaci ba aka taramu ababban hall din da muke taro,don bayar da kyaututtukan da za,ayi.
Kamar yadda na sama raina hakance ta faru don kuwa nice na ciyo overalls,zokiga farin ciki wajena mutane da dama suna tayani murna,da farin ciki yayin da wasu bakin ciki zakaka suke fiddowa,hotuna mukayi kala kala kafin aka sallamemu.
Karfe daya daidai na dawo gida,na baje gift din gaban anty ina washe baki,tana tayani washewa muka hau bude kyaututtukan dana samu wajen malamai,da shugabar makarantar.
Babbar laptop ce mai suna lanovo sai madaidaiciyar oven da toaster.sai faket din seasoning cubes kala kala irin wadanda muke anfani dasu acan in zamuyi pratical,anty sai yabawa take ganin ina cikin farin ciki,nace mata cikin sanyin murya.

"Anty kin min komi,kin kara gyara rayuwata kin sani farin ciki alokacin dana yanke kauna cewar zan sake wata abu wai dariya,saigashi kin maida rayuwata tamakar ta yarki ko kanwarki,banda abunda zan saka maki dashi anty daya wuce na dawwama ina maki addua."
Na karashe ina jin hawaye suna zarya akan fuskata,mikin da *ABU KHALIPA* yabarni dashi yana taso mun,inajin abun har kan halshena.
Rungumoni tayi ta kwantar da kaina bisa kafadarta,tace"kici gaba da dauka cewar nidin yayarkice kuma matsayin uwa gareki Nadeya,domin kuwa abunda yadace nayi kenan nida khalipa,don akanki ne xamu iya rama alkairi da mahaifiyarki tayi mana"
Dago kaina nai ina kallonta da tsantsar mamaki,da sonjin karin bayani.
Murmushi tayi mani tace"karki matsa ma kanki da sonjin komi,lokaci ya kusa bazan so bata maki mood dinki yanzu ba,don haka karki damu komi zai warware,kije kifara hada abunda zaki hada inajin saukar magruba zauyi ko karfe biyar."

Goge kwallata nai ina tashi na wuce daki don canza kaya,amman maganar nanan makale cikin xuciyata,kenan sun san mahaifiyata!to tana ina?meyasa ta rabu dani?dole zan zo na idasa karata wancen diary din tun kafin maishi ya nemesa.
Watsa ruwa nai nayi salla sannan na canza kaya,nafito nashiga kitchen din ko samha ban bari ta tayani ba.

Miyar dambun nama ce nayi wadda za,a iya cinta da taliya,makaroni,cus cus harma da shinkafa.
Tuni miyar ta karade gidan da dan banzan kamshi,banjima ba na kammalata.
Sannan na fara kokarin gyra kifina sukunbiya,na budeshi na cire dattin na barshi bude,aman na cire kan bandashi zanyi anfani.
Lemun tsami na wankeshi dashi sosai sannan na badeshi da garin citta,da tafarnuwa sako da lungu sannan nasaka magungunan da zanyi anfani dasu,suma na shafesu dashi na saka barkono kadan yadda bazai hana aciba,sannan na yanka albasata asama komi ya zama daida sannan na sashi cikin oven.

Kankanata na gyara na cire kwallayen,na xuba cikin blender sannan na dauko gyararrar gyadata danya,itama na zuba aciki na saka ruwa na markadesu saida sukai lumuy sannan na tace,na dauko peak dinta ta gwangwani na juye,nabi da suger,saida ta yanarke na dan dana naji komi normal sannan nasaka cikin freg.

Nakoma wajen cocomber na yayyankata na matse lemun tsamina aciki sannan na markade itama,na tace ruwan sobona na gefe daya huce bayan samha ta dafashi da citta dq kananfari.
Na juye ruwan cocomber da lemun tsami acikin sobon,nasaka flavor dina mai kamshin strawberry nasa suger shima nasashi freg,na kammala da bangaren juices.
Juyawar da zanyi naga anty tsaye tana ta kallona,murmushi nai nace "anty yar labe kike mun ko?"

Dariya tayi tace "wlh kin hana hancina sakat har na kasa zama,miyauna har wani tsartuwa yake don Allah ga kofina ki dan sammani juicy din ko yayane"

Yadda tayi maganar sakace sharifa dariya na kamayi,ina dafe ciki nace"wlh Anty kina saka kaina yana girma fah"

Dariyar tayi tace "alqur an yanzu kince kau ban waje indai a kitchen ne"

Dariya nake nace "uhmmm anty kenan,gashi nan kiga inyayi sanyi kidiba dama donku nai ni"

"Nidai banson cika baki,sai yazo kiringa kalmashe ido"
Murmushi kurin nai najuya nafara kokarin dora dambun cus cus,ta juya ga freg din ta diba ta shatana lumshe ido.
"Aradu yau cikina sai inda ba waje"

Dariya n ringayi mata tana gefe tana jana da fira nikuma inata aikina,dambun yayi kyau ainun har anta nasaka aciki,ga coslow agefe na hada shima.
Shinkaface karshe nadafata hade a dankalin turawa,wadda za,aci da miyar dambun naman danai...............xuwa karfe shidda komi ya zama ready na kintsa table kamar zamuyi wasu gaggarumin baki,don ni ban daukesu wasu manyan baki ba,duk sadda na tuna da zuwan nashi nakan ji xuciyata ta tsinke,har saina dafeta na lumhe ido.
Babu abunda yake dawomun irin ranar rabuwarmu,har yau na gaza mance moment din.


💝💝💝💝💝💝💝

Jirginsu karfe biyar da rabi ya sauka bai niyyar xuwa da asmau gidan Anty ba,don yariga ya gama yanke hukunci duk halin da yake ciki da asmau saiya gaya ma inna,wata kila idan ita taimata fada ta dawo cikin hayyacinta,don wannan ba kishi bane haukane.
Abunda yake ayyana wa kenan cikin ransa,data kallesa zaisha mur ya dauke kai kurin,amman gefe guda jin xuciyarsa yake kamar kankara yau dai zaiga *SANYIN IDANIYArsa* (sunan wani book dina)tunda ya shaki iskar garin yakejin wani sabon yanayi yana saukar masa,kadan kadan ya lumshe idonsa har ya sauke ajiyar xuciya.
Driver yakira yace ya samesu air port gefe yaja ya zaro glass ya rufe idonsa,don karma yaji tausayinta don yaga xuciyarsa na da niyyar fasa abunda yayi niyya,har ga Allah baya kin asmau haka zalika bai shirya rabuwa da ita ba,halinta ne yake sakashi jin ko kallonta baya so.
Kwata kwata bai zaci haka daga gareta ba,baice kartayi kishi ba amman ba irin wannan ba,wanda zaisa taringa gallazamasa tana fada masa maganganu marasa dadi saikace ba mijinta ba,asmaun da ada bata iya tsayar da idonta cikin nashi bagaya masa magana mai tsawo.amman kishi yanxu yasa har bakar magana take gaya mai harda munafuki.kalmar data fi komi tsaya masa ga rai.
Matsowa tai inda yake zaune kamar zata fashe da kuka,ga cikinta daya fito yayi mata kyau tayi wani fresh da ita,ta durkusa kasa tana dafa guiwarsa tace.
"Yaya don Allah kayi hkr,wlh inka gaya ma inna nabani"
Kalonta kurin yake au bama yayi hkr akan abubuwan datayi ba,aa karya gayama inna!!!
Jinjina kai kurin yayi baice komi ba,driver na xuwa yatashi yashige yabarta nan,ta gaji da durkushinta tamike ta samesa.driver ya daukar masu kaya yasa but din motar suka nufi gida...........
Text ya turama Anty cewar nan da minty shabiyar zai iso,don daya sauke asmau bazai zauna ba zai wuce can,yadda yakejin xuciyarsa tana azalzalarsa shi har tsorata yake,anya xai iya abunda xuciyarsa take gaya masa,har baisan ayyano yadda kwayar idonta take tasiri atare dashi,don har tsikar jikinsa yakejin tana tashitashi.
Suna shiga get din gidan ta rarumo hanunsa cikin marairaita tace"kayiwa girman Allah yaya karka gaya wa inna,don Allah kayi hkr bazan sake ba Allah kuwa,inhar na kara kayi mun duk abunda ka ga dama"
Banzan kallo ta watsa mata cikin daurewar murya yace"cikani,kibude mota ki fice akwai inda zani,inkin shiga kin sanar da ita sai dare ko da safe zan shigo."
Turbune fuska tayi "wallahi ni bazan yarda ba,daga dawo wa kuma ina zaka ehy?"

"Inda kika aikeni can zani,comon get out inkinji inda zanin kin isa hanani xuwane marar kangado kurin"
Kuka tafara kokarin yimasa yaja tsaki ya bude kofar dayake ya zagayo ya bude inda take,ya finciko hannunta ya fiddota ya jata har bakin step din part din inna sannan ya juya yakoma motarsa cikin bacin rai.
Tana kallo motar ta fice daga get din ihune kurin bata kurma ba,saboda tsabar yadda takejin wani makoko daya tsaya mata ga makoshi,wai meye tarasa ne har da baya kallonta ya rude haka,me xatayi ta kauda idonsa da hankalinsa akanta.haka zan xuba ido ina kallo inkoma saidai kallo kurin,wani bangare na xuciyarta yana tunatar da ita abubuwan da tagaza,yana ankarar da ita sakacin da tayi tare da wauta.
_kigyara halayenki da dabiunki sune zasu gyara alakar aurenki_ 
Cewar xuciyarta dogon tsaki taja ta sungumi hand bag dinta ta wuce ciki...............

Tunda motar ta doso gidan bugun xuciyarsa ya sauya sosai,har yakejin jikinsa yana rikicewa koda driver yayi parking samun kansa yayi da kasa fitowa,ya jima yana anbaton sunan Allah koda zai samu sassaucin abunda yakeji,don har wani jiri yake gani wata kila harda yunwa da gajiya,aman gajiyar bata saukowa sai yayi tunanin ya take ahalin yanxu,sai yayi imagining kallonta da yanayin kwayar idonta,dan danan jikinsa zai saki wata kasala da gajiya zata lillibesa.
Yayi minty biyar amotar sannan ya iya budewa ya fito,kallon compound din gidan yake yana tuna ranar daya xo tana gaya mata tafiyarsa da Asmau cyprus,lumshe ido yayi yana yamutsa gashinsa ahankali yafara taka kafafunsa xuwa kofar da zata sadashi da main falon gidan.

Tun bayan dana kammala komi na naje nayi wanka nayi sallar magruba,jikina duk yayi wani sanyi inajin wani sabon feeling na daban,pencil din wandone jikina orange. amman na roba yabi fatar jikina ya lafe,sai wata yar riga silik ce tsawonta xai kawo guiwata anfidda mata shaf mai kyau.
Hannun shimi gareta ba karamin kyau kayan sukai mun ba,normally irin wannan lokacin daman kayan dazaka sameni dasu kenan,don har bacci inayi da abuna baruwana.
Daka rabeni zakaji tashin kamshi mai dan banzan shiga rai da kidima mai shaka,ni kaina nasan ta ko ina jikina sako da lungu tashi kurin nake,inka shaka saika lumshe ido haka saika bini da kallo.gyalena na daura bisa kaina nayi dauri mai kyau kasancewar Anty fatima kawar Anty har daurin kallabi takoya mun,da yadda zan ringa yin dressing dina.
Fuskata fes farar powder ce kurin sai kwalli da man baki,amman kamar zanje gasar kyau.
Zama nai saman stool din mirrow dina ina kallon kaina,ba komi nake ayyana wa ba illa wace irin tarba zansamu,kokuma shi wace irin tarba zan masa.bazai yuwu na washe masa bakina ba,kamar yadda bazai yuwu na daure fuskata ba,hakan zai nuna kamamr na damu dashi nikuma bazan so yasan haka ba,don haka dole nasan da wace fuskar zan tarbesa............karadin sharifa najiyo ita da ashraf,suna oyoyo ga uncle oyoyo.dammm xuciyata ta ansa duk naji na rikice,tun kafin idona ya gansa kenan nashiga uku karfa inbada kaina,Allah kabani kwarin guiwar tunkarar wannan bawan naka.
Kamar wadda aka dasa wajen haka na zauna kasa motsin kirki,kallon kofata nai don nasan sharifa nan zatayo ta kawomun rahoto,ban gama tunanin ba naji burum ta shigo.................

"Anty!Anty deeyah zokiga wani abu,taso kigani wlh uncle ne yadawo daga kasar waje,ingaya maki anty uncle ya zama Bature saidai anty kinsan wlh ya rame sosai,anya ba yan hanjinsa suke kwakwalewa ba."
Me zanyi ba dariya ba nariko hannunta ina ta dariya,yaro dai yarone nace "yan hanji kuma sharifa,ai in yan hanjinsa suke kwakwalewa mutuwa zai,kila dai wuya yasha."

"Uhmmm anty bakisan mutanen bane shiyasa,wlh suna iya kwakwalemasa kila sun rage wasu shiyasa mai mutu ba"
Dariyar nake nace"to naji jeki ina xuwa ganinan"
Makale kafada tayi tana cewa "wlh cewa yayi infiddoki mutaho tare,ummi ma nacen tana hira dashi kixo kema kika sani ko yazo da chocolate"

"Sarki uwar shan chocolate,muje to."
Ina rike da hannunta gabana tana ansawa,karfin haline kurin xuciyata kamar xata fito saboda suka,kwayar idona ta kasa zama waje guda anya zan iya control din ta kuwa,sharifa sai xunbuda sauri take tana jaye da hannuna kamar wadda zata ruga.
Haka muka shiga cikin karamin falon kafata naji tana neman hardewa,innalillahi kurin nake anbata don samun kwarin guiwa...............ashraf najiyo yace "yeee uncle ga Anty nan"
Kamar wanda aka soka axuciya haka yayi hanzarin dago kanshi ya bi hanyar da yake zaton tanan zamu fito,duk yadda nake danbe da idona amman yakasa bani hadin kai,saidai najini tsundum cikin kwayar idanunsa,wadanda suke neman su narkar dani in xube akasa warwas ban san silalewar sharifa ba saidai na ji babu hannunta cikin nawa,kasa motsawa nai banda sautin bugun xuciyata bana jin komi cikin kunnena,idona kuwa har time din yana cikin nasa,ya gaza kauda nashi haka nima na gaza,danayi yunkurin dauke wa sai inji na kasa,wasu abubuwa ne suka shigar mun jiki wadanda suke haddasamun jin zan fadi kasa,numfashina naja sosai kafin nayi nasarar runtse idona ina ajiyar xuciya,juya baya nai kurin don bansan me zanyi next ba.
Surutunsu sharifa kurin nake jiyowa jikina har wata yar rawa yake,shin koma wa zanyi kokowa isa zanyi,idan na isa mexan ce masa.?
Hanyar dining na nanufa naja kujera na zauna,ina bude womers din duk na rude har time din jikina bai dawo daidai ba,.............bakina yana yar rawa kadan najuya ban yi gigin kallonsa ba nace ma anty.
"Anty yau dai bakujin yunwa ko,nikam ci zanyi inbarku"

Murmushi tayi tana kallon kanen nata daya shige cikin kujera yana jan iska,idonsa rufe hannunsa yana cikin gashinsa yana cakudashi.
"Aa ni saina jira mijina tukun,ki xuba wa su sharifa nasu suwuce falon Abbansu suci."
To nace mata kurin ina kokarin saita kaina karna nai badai dai ba.

"Khalipa nace maka bakada lafiya kamun gardama,gashinan kuma yanayin ka ya nuna tunda ka shigo naga wannan uwar ramar nasan ba banza zaka dawo gida ba"

Muryarsa ta dishe yakuma kasa bude idonsa bai tashi daga kwantar da bayansa dayayi cikin kujerar ba,yace "nace maki ni lfya ta lau abunda yake damuna shine silar wannan ramar,kuma shine yake sakani ciwo."

"Ayya sannu kaji dan kanena,karashi to ka je dakika da kake sauka kadan watsa ruwa ga abinci can kan dining,kanwarka ta dafa maka.,"
Tajuyo inda nake tace mun "pretty ki hadama yayanki abincinsa cikin tire ki kaimasa kuma ko sannu da xuwa banji kinyi masa ba."

Jinai kurin bakina yana cewa "yo anty ban gane kowaye ba,tunanina fa ko bako kikai wanene yayana kuma."
Wannan maganar tasa yabude idonsa da suka canza kala,yana kalloni irin dai maganar ta dakesa.

Muna hada ido nai saurin dauke nau "anty tace baki ganesa ba kema ashe,yo ai dole wannan rama ai ta hana aganeka,khalipa ne fah"

Cikin irin abun nan na mamaki na zaro idona kamar gaske nace "laaah wai ashe shine sai yanzu na gane gaskiya kam ya rame sannunka da xuwa,andawo lafiya."

Anty kanta tayi mamakin basarwar danai na dauke kai na cigaba da tura abincina,badan ina cikin natsuwata ba.

"Wai tana nufin bata ganeni ba Anty?"
Yafada cikin jin ciwon abun,kafada ta daga tace"da alama kam,amman ai yanzu ta gane ko"
Jinjina kai yayi "nikaina bacin nayi dagaske ai bazan gane ka ba,katashi taje ka watsa ruwa ka sheme anan,zan hau sama xuwa anjima"
Tai gaba tabarsa nan ganin duk an yaye anbarni nidashi,nima na hanzarta na gama nawuce kitchen na dauko tiren dazan hada masa,na fara xuba komi daidai misali satar kallonsa nai naga yatashi yarike haba yana ta kallona,na kauda kai na cigaba da abunda nake na dauka na nufi hanyar da dakin yake,don naga daxun samha tana gyaransa.
Sauri kurin nake na ajiye na fito na ruga dakina,single room ne mai gado da yar wadrop guda daya sai karamin madubi,da toilet aciki can na jawo wani table na dora tiren sama,na juyo cikin sauri zan fice nakaro dashi bakin kofa............numfashina kamar zai fece don tsorata,kafa yasa ya tura kofar ta rufe ruf,ya tsareni da mayun idonsa kauda kai nake ina kokarin saita kaina,ya fara biyoni ina ja baya hannunsa guda cikin aljihun jeans dinsa blue,duk na dirirce saida ya kaini karshen bango,sannan ya dafa bangon da hannunsa ya tare hanyar da zan ruga.
Sai kauda kai nake ko kadan bana son kallonsa,daga najuya nan saina juya nan,sai numfashi nakeja kamar asthma ta zata tashi.............muryarsa naji har cikin yan hanjina wani irin yammm nakeji cikin jikina,dole na runtse ido........."kika ce baki ganeni ba???"
Shiru na masa ina motsa bakina kamar zanyi magana kuma nakasa,kallon lips dinta yayi wadanda suka sashi jin kamar zai haukace,banda masifeffen kamshin dake isarmasa har cikin bargonsa,iska yafesar har akan fuskata kauda kai nai ina jan ajiyar xuciya,so nake inyi magana amman kusancin yayi yawa,don numfashin mu yana haduwa gani nake dana buda baki bakinsa danau zasu hade.
Sake magana yayi kamar mai jin bacci "dake nake kika ce baki gane ni ba?"
Kai kurin nadaga ina sake runtse idona gam.
"Dodo na koma da bazaki iya kallona ba" still daga kai nai.
Cikin mamaki yace  "what!!"
Yadda yafada da karfi kadan yasa ni bude idona.
Jina muryata ta narke sosai nace"don Allah ka kyaleni natafi"
Runtse ido yayi yadda yaji sautin maganar kanshi abun ya dimautashi,wai taya duk ta canza masa haka kominta ya rikide ya koma wani special.wani irin feeling yakeji kamar ta bude tsakiyar kirjinsa ta sata yarufe haka yakeji.
Daker yayi magana cikin cool voice dinsa wadda dakaji kasan yana cikin wani condition,
"Dodo aina koma ko?"
Kin kallonsa nai na girgiza kai alamun eh.
Kafin nace"kwarangwal ka zama shiyasa banda kashi babu komi jikinka".
Kamar kartayi shiru intana masa magana haka yakeji, sake matsoni yayi nikuma ina lahewa jikin bango,bakina na rawa nace kamar zan kuka "plsss ka matsa na tafi,don Allah kadaina matsoni bana so"
Kamar wanda na tun xura shi saidai naji ya dora hancinsa bisa nau,wanda gaba daya tashoshina suka kulle daker na jawo numfashina muryarsa daker take fita yace"meyasa bakya so"
Idona har ya ciko da kwallah,nasa hannuna cicin karfi na turashi baya,aikuwa saigashi xube agado.
Nai mamakin hakan kwata kwata jikinsa babu kwari,zaro ido na kamayi ina juye juye nace"sorry kaine kaja ai."
Najuya zan fita ya riko hannuna ta baya "pls karki tai ki taimakeni da ruwan wanka banda lfy."
Samun kaina nai da kura masa ido,kamar yadda yatsareni shima,jinai kamar na zama ruwa saboda yadda jikina ya narke.
Girarsa ya daga kadan yace "plxxx"
Juyar da kai nai nasunburi baki,don sai yanxu kwalwata ta tuna mun ashefa banice matar tasa ba,wani abu mai kama da kishi yaxo mun wuya.
Na kwace hannuna nace"ka kira ta mana ba hurumina bane ai"

"Huruminki ne saidai kice ba zakiyi ba,."

Sai naji bazan iya tafiya ban masa ba vance komi ba na nufi toilet din nafara hada ruwan,ina jan tsaki.
"Ko ina yabarta daya taho shikadai,kamar kuma dagaske din baida lfy,kodayake ina ruwana ma can ta matse mashi."
Na gama hadawa na fito har sannan yana kwance yana kallon sama,idonsa rufe.
Na gasgata maganar sharifa cewar ya zama bature,tabbas yayi kyau fiye da yadda nasansa kafin ya tai,ramar sace kurin zaka gani kasan baya cikin kwanciyar hankali.tome yake damunsa?
"Ina ruwanki"
Cewar wani sashe na xuciyata.
Tsaki naja na kallai nace "na hada zan tafi"

Bude idonsa yayi yace "thanks,kije wajen anty ki anso mun PCM"
Juyawa nai na nufi kofar kurin,ashe dai din baya lafiya da gaske abunda nake ayyana wa kenan araina.
Jinai yace *"PRETTY"*
cak na tsaya ban jiyowa ba don sunan yashigeni yazo mun bazata,jinsa nai wani daban abakinsa.

Jiyowa nai muka kurawa juna ido lumshe nashi yayi kafin ya sake cewa.
"Jakata na falo kitaho mun dashi"
Bude kofar nai nafita kurin,ni wannan salon ban saba dashi ba gara yayi mun yadda yasaba kurin,zanfi ganewa don inbanda sake dilmiyar dani babu abunda yake,kuma nida nake neman hanyar rabuwa dashi baidace na biyewa xuciyata ba.
Amman taki bani hadin kai
Har part din Abban su sharifa naje nayi sallama suna falo ita dashi,saida tace na shigo sannan na shiga.

"Yaya dai mrs Khalipa"
Kunya naji don Abban su yanajin metace.
"Anty kidaina don Allah,bana so wlh"
Dariya sukai mun ita da Abban sharifa yace"barka nadeya maigida ya dawo"
Boye kaina nai bayan kujera nakasa ansa mashi,anty tace"menene to kodai yau kwana wajen oga"
Kamar zan kuka nace "haba Anty don Allah kidaina."
Tana dariya tace"na daina to gaya mun inji"

"Dama cewa yayi kibashi PCM zaisha"

"Okay inazuwa"
Tatashi tashiga cikin dakin su tajima kadan sannan ta dauko ta bani,sai tsokana ta take nasamu na fito nabarsu can.

Jikkar na biya falo na dauka na wuce dakin nasa,yana wanka na ajiye masa nan kan gado zan tafi sai nakasa inason insake ganinsa,inason insake jin dagaske baida lafiyar,bayan haka xuciyata zataso naita zama akusada shi saidai bazan iya biye mata ba,don inhar ya gane halin danake ciki ina ruwa iya wuya.
Harna kama handle din kofar zan fita naji fitowarsa,ban jiya ba nace masa dai "ga shinan kan gado saida safe"

Kallon bayanta yake yana jin inama inama,ajiye towel din dayake tsane kanshi yayi ya nufo inda nake tsaye.
Don dai yasa kaya three quarter ne jikinsa fari sai singlet.
Gaf da gaf ya tsaya abayana har inajin digar ruwan wankansa kan kafada ta,jinai kurin tsikar jikina tana tashi.
Na runtse idona kurin yanayin bugun xuciyata yana sake sauyawa.
Bakinsa ya sunkuyo ta wajen kunnena yace cikin wata murya wadda take neman xubar dani kasa.
"U don't missing me why?"
Saurin matsawa nai na kama handle din kofar zan bude ya rike hannun nau,yana sake matsoni masifa nakeson nayi bakin ya kasa 
Sai hadiyar miyau nake "answer me,bakiyi kewata ba."
Cikin karfin hali na juyo da masifa nace "ehy banba,bakuma zanyi ba akan me zanyi kewarka ma wai"
"Shiiiiiit,karki sake daga mun murya ke mai wayau don karna gane kina kewata harda cewa baki hanene ba ko?"
Kauda kai nai nace"wayau name,aiba karya nai ba,ni nariga na mance da mai kama dakai ma"

"Inhar gaskene kalli idona kigaya mun haka"
Kamar zan kuka na juyo nasa idona cikin nasa,amman na kasa magana saboda abunda yake shigata duk sadda na hada ido dashi,kasawar yasa naji kwalla sun cika idona na kauda kai nace.
"Badole saina yi hakan ba ,iya gaskiya ta kenan"
Najuya nabude kofar zanfita ya sake rikemun hannu.

"Karfa kifara sona tun yanxu,don karki manta kince mun duk duniya nine mutun nafarko dake ka tsana,so kiringa tuna wannan"

Maganar ta tsaya mun ga makoshi nadai daure ban fiddo kwallata ba,nace masa "ba manta wa nai ba,kuma bazan mance ba bazan taba son naka ba har duniya ta nade,kaji ko"
Nafixge hannuna na wuce nabarsa nan,tun ahanya hawaye suka fara zarya afuskata,ya zama dole na sake yaki da xuciyata.don yana gaf da gane halin danake ciki akansa,gashinan har yafara mun gori.insha Allah ko sonka zai kasheni ni nadeya bazan taba nuna maka ba,bazan tana furta ma ba...............na fada kan gado ina rera kukana,saboda feelings dina su dawo sabbi shiyasa banso dawowarsa ba,abubuwa xasu sake rincabewa.

Saida tatafi yaji haushin kansa,meyasa yace mata haka!gashinan ya bata mata rai har tana kwalla,dan abunda ya faru tsakaninsu kadai ya saukar masa da natsuwa,idan tayi fushi tadaina kulasa fa?
_daman ai neman hanyar dazaka yakiceta kake miye na damuwa_
Inji xuciyarsa dafe kanshi yayi dayaji ya cigaba da ciwo,daker ya iya sallar magriba da isha,i ya zauna cin abinci,wanda da yake zaton bazai iya ciba saigashi babu abunda ya bari sai shinkafa.wannan ba dandanon girkin anty bane,to waye yayi.
Wannan yarinyar dai banda kwanciya bata iya komiba balle yasa ran ko itace,haka yagaji da tunane tunanesa ya dan taba computer xuwa shabiyu bacci ya saceshi..................

Episode  .29

Ina gama sallar asuba anty tashigo wai karna koma bacci shidda nayi na wuce nashiga kitchen na hada ma mijina break,kuka ne kurin banba amman kuma banida yadda zanyi bazan iya yiwa Anty musu ba.
Duk baccin da nakeji haka na hakura nafito don jiya nasha kuka,karshe har saida kaina yayi ciwo kafin bacci ya daukan,ban wani wahalar da kaina ba kunun gyada ne nayi yasha madara,na hada bakin shayi mai na,a na,a da lemon grass da citta da kanunfari.
Sai na soya arish na soya kwai shikenan,na kammala nawuce nayi wanka na shirya na haye gadona don bacci nakeji.

*****

Tun asuba shima baikoma ba nura ya kirasa ya tura masa wasu document dake cikin computer dinsa,aikin yake amman hankalinsa yana gareta gaba daya baccin sa na jiya itace aciki.
Koda yagama abunda yake kan computer saiya kwanta kozai cigaba da baccin,amman kiri kiri idonsa ya kibashi hadin kai.
Dole ya ta shi yayi wanka yacanza kaya kananu ne marasa nauyi,don garin anfara zafi ba laifi.
Murmushi yayi yana kallon kanshi a madubi tuna wa dayayi da maganarta ta jiya.
_kwarangwal ka koma banda kashi babu komi jikinka_

Shafa wuyansa yayi yadda ya tabbatar da yaramen,amman daga jiya xuwa safiyar yau wani irin natsuwa da jin dadi yake jinsa aciki,kaso sittin na damuwarsa yaragu koba komi dai gashi agida daya da ita,kuma duk sadda yake bukatar ganinta zai ganta,saidai har yanxu wannan nauyin dake kirjinsa yana nan,kuma abu gudane zai saka yadaina jinsa inya furta kalmar data dankare awajen.
Abunda yakejin har abada bazai iya ba,atafi ahakan dai har sadda Zainemi abun ya rasa,turare ya fesa ya fito da dokin sata ga idonsa yayi alkawari bazai sake bata mata rai ba,don jiya baiji dadin yadda yaga kwalla ga idonta ba.

Anty ce keta kokarin ganin su sharifa sun wuce school kowane an shirya masa lunch box dinsa,sai sauri take don sun makara.
"Uncle good morning"
Cewar sharifa da ashraf.murmushi yayi yana mika masu hannu kowa ya kama,yace "morning my son and daughter"
Sharifa tace "uncle don Allah ka kaimu school da kanka yau"
Cikin fada anty tace mata"banson sakarci sharifa,ga driver can kuwuce maza salan ayomun ragin abinci."

Rakosu yayi har motar yace "sorry zamuzo nida Anty deyah mu dauko ku in antashi kunji ko,ayi karatu sosai"
Jin haka yasa suka saki ransu driver yajasu suka tai.
Wayarsa ke vibrating ya fiddo ta yaduba yaga asmau ce,da kamar bazai dauka ba ya dai danne ya yi picking..........."yanzu yaya dagaske acan ka kwana saboda Allah?"
Runtse ido yayi kamar ta soka mashi kibiya,babu ina kwana babu ya gajiya kawai sai tsogumi.
Tsaki yaja ya sauke wayar ya kashe,ya koma cikin gidan falo ya zauna ya kunna labarai yana jin haushin abunda tayi masa.
Wayar ce ta sake kara cikin kulewa ya dauki wayar zaiyi masifa yaji muryar inna tana cewa.
"Sadiqu lafiya kuwa?tunda ka sauke yarinyar nan ta ishemu da yan kuke kuke,maganganu barkatai ban ma iya gane metake nufi,yanxu kuma takiraka ka kashe ta kama kuka don Allah ka sanar dani abunda yake faruwa."
Runtse idonsa yayi wani bakin ciki yana cika xuciyarsa,ya samu ya danne yace mata.
"Inna maganar duk bata waya bace,idan na karya zan shigo gidan saimuyi maganar"

"Nifa duk kuntada mun hankali,inakaje ka kwana ne"

"Ina gidan Anty aysher"

"Toshikenan saika zo din."
Ya sauke wayar yana shafa goshinsa,yadda baya tunani asmau ta wuce haka,wane irin neman fitina ne haka..............."ni nasan kana cikin damuwa,kallonka kurin nai ajiyan nasan akwai abunda yake damunka daya jawo wannan ramar taka,amma dayake ban kai matsayin in sani ba baka iya buda baki ka gaya mun ba."
Anty data sauko daga sama take nufo falon take maganar.
Jiyowa yayi yana kallonta yana jiran isowarta,saida ta zauna sannan yace "wlh ba haka bane,kinwuce nan Anty kawai dai bansan ma ta inda zan fara maki bayanin matsalar bane."
Yafara bata labarin duk halin dasuke ciki shida asmau tun xuwansu can cyprus din,da kuma irin azabar dayake sha na rashin sabo da tashin hankali.

Jinjina kai kurin antyn take cikin bacin rai fyskarta ta nuna sosai tafara fada kamar zata tsiresa .
"Banson sakarci khalipa akan wannan abun zaka dauki damuwar duniya ka dorawa kanka,akan wannan jaririyar yarinyar ashe har akwai abunda zai zaizayeka irin haka bacin kalubalen daka fuskata abaya,wace irin wuyace baka shiga ba duk baka lalace irin haka ba sai akan wannan yarinyar,don ubanta don ita kadai akayita iyeh ince sadda akai auren kaida ita duk bakuso,kasada kayi ka aureta saboda yunkurin lalatata da saurayinta yayi,sannan kuma don samun waje take neman dorama hawan jini.take kokarin hada kanta da nadeya,duk laifinka ne kaine babban mailaifin da sonta yasa kake nuna ma yar uwarka wulakanci agaban ta tana matsayin kishiyarta,ba dole daga baya taxo ta nuna sai yadda take so ba.kishi haukane koko ita kadai keda hakki akanka ehy."

"Anty kibar fadan haka don Allah"
Cikin fusata tace "baxan bari ba wlh,ka gama break din kafata kafarka har gidan innar,ayita takare gabanta inma kana ganin kafi karfin pretty yanxu yanxu kabani takaddarta kaje can ku karata kaida Asmaun,shashashun banza da wofi."

"Anty niba yanxu na shirya rabuwa da ita ba akwai time din dana ware"

"Ubanshi time din,nace ubanshi kafita idona kabar ganin ina raga maka wlh akan nadeya zamuyi batatta nida kai,yau saidai ka zaba nadeya ko asmau na gama magana,ai daman auren manufa kayi don samun kan J daula to ba kanshi kadai ba harda kafafunsa duk ankame,don haka yau wallahi ko asmau ko nadeya ko takaddarta kaji na rantse maka."

Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un kurin yake anbata "anty abun har yayi zafi haka.don Allah ki fahimceni inna da baba sunyi mana hallaci,inbacin su bansan ya rayuwata zata kasance ba.da bansan inda nake ba ahalin yanxu"

"Nasani nafika sanin hakan,amman bazai yuwu saboda yarsu ka wulakanta yar uwarka ba wadda bacin ni baka da kamarta,ai hallaci sunyi mana muma muna kan yi masu don haka bazai yuwu yarsu ta hanaka zaman lafiya ba wlh,don haka data canza hali da karta canza uwarsu daya ubansu daya,yau saika zaba cikin su biyun."
Tatashi tabarsa nan yana xufa duk da karfin ac da fankar dake aiki,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,,ina corridor din dazai kawoni falo nafara jin hayaniyarsu,da bazan tsaya ba amman jin an anbaceni yasa na tsaya donjin me nayi.................kafafuna sunyi mun wani irin sanyi kalau,har na kasa cigaba da tsayuwa saboda jin abunda anty take fada bin bango nayi nakoma daki na kulle,xufa nake sosai duk na rikice ban zaci inhar na tabbatar da rabuwa ta dashi ba zan ji irin wannan tashin hankalin.
Jikina har rawa yake kuka na niyyar kubce mun,ban tabbar da irin son danake masa ba sai yau don gaba daya naji na rikice anya zan iya?yadda nake abun bazai misaltu ba,amman kuma baxan iya kunyata anty ba.kamar uwata nake kallonta don haka bin abunda take so kamar dolena ne .
"Innalillahi wa inna ilahir raju,un nabani Nadeya yazanyi wlh xuciyata bazata jure ba.ina sonsa ina kaunarsa rabuwa dashi tashin hankaline agareni.Allah ka kawomun mafita "

_idan ya zabi asmau yabarki fa saikice dole saiya zauna dake??_

Abunda xuciyarta take gaya mata kenan,dif naji kukan ya tsaya hankali na yana dawowa,tabbas abunda zai faru kenan bana cikin zabinsa bai dace na matsama kaina ba,ni kadai nake wahalar banxa ko mutuwa xanyi baishida asara.............hannu nasa nagoge hawayena na dafe xuciyata na runtse idona,amman haka bai hana wasu hawayen xubowa ba.
Cikin rauni da karyewar xuciya nace "ya Allah sadda ka dora mun wannan soyayya ban sani ba,ya Allah ina rokonka karabani da ita cikin salama.tarayyata dashi babu wani alkairi baya sona,Allah kai kana sona ka kawomun sauki,ka saukakamun wannan radadin danakeji.kabani kwarin guiwar goyama wannan baiwar taka baya.na mika duk lamarina ahannunka hakika duk abunda kazartar mani zansa hannu biyu inkarba."

Jingina bayana nayi da gadon ina ajiyar xuciya,nai relax sosai sautin bugun xuciyata kurin nake saurara.
Ahankali na fara samun saukin zafin da nakeji akirjina,na hana duk wani tunani shigowa ita kurin nake saurare har sadda wani sabon bacci yasake daukeni.

Duk yadda yakejin yunwa kasa yin wani break din kirki yayi,turawa kurin yake ko kadan bayajin test din sa gabakin sai nadamar sanar da anty ta kamshi,da duk wannan tashin hankalin baishige shi ba,har anty ta gama abunda zatai ta zauna jiransa yana nan yana lailayar cup din kunu.

"Inka koshi ka ajiye mutai inada abunyi asibiti"

Idonsa kamar zai kuka yace"Anty don Allah kibar maganar inzaba cikin subiyun,ni ban shirya rabuwa da kowace acikinsu yanxu ba wlh."
Kallon banza ta watsa mashi.
"Kana son dawo da maganata baya ko,to nariga na gama magana ni"

Baida yadda zaiyyi dole yaje ya dauko jikkarsa yagoge bakinsa suka fito,key dinta ta bashi ya bude motarta suka shiga sai gidan inna.

Suna zaune duk afalo sukaji sallamar antyn,sumy tatashi tana mata sannu da xuwa tare da gaidata.asmau na gefen inna kamar zata shige cikin cikinta.saida antyn ta zauna sannan ta gaidata gadaran gada han ta ansa tana maida kanta wajen inna suna gaisawa,tunda ya shigo suka gaisa da sumy yana  kujerar bakin kofa,kallo daya zakai mashi kasan yana cikin tension.
Gaida innar yayi tana masa yagajiyar tafiya,kasa ansawa yayi yadai sadda kai kasa kurin.
Anty tafara magana da innar cewa "inna akan matsalar dakike son sanin mecece yasa na tasoshi gaba mukaxo wajenki gaki gashi gani ga asmau.nafarko nasan bata gaya maki rashin da,ar da takeyiwa mijinta ba,balle sauran halayen data kirkiro takeyi,shiyasa nace zanzo na zayyane maki komi inkuma yiwa tubkar hanci."

"Ina jinki Aysher,don ni babu abunda ta gaya mani sai korafin sadiqu yadaina sonta "

Kallon banza antyn tayiwa asmaun kafin tafara rattaba ma innar duk halin dasuke ciki,da irin zafin kishin da asmaun take nunawa akan nadeya,tare da ikirarin inzai koma bazai tafi da ita ba,da rashin tarbiyyar da take nuna masa wanda kafin tafiyarsu duk haka bata faruwa.,.........sallalami kurin inna keyi tana tafa hannu mamaki da al,ajabin diyar tata ya kamata.
Har saida ta dungure mata kai cikin fada sosai tace"nizaki kunyata Asmau?ashe iskancin da kike aikata masa kenan?ina iliminki ina tarbiyar damuka hafu dashi dani da babanku muka baku,ashe kafa kika saka kikai watsi da su.innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un kin bani kun ya wlh kin shayar dani mamaki,har kiringa kiran mijinki da munafuki!!!kai kuma dayake solobiyo ne kasa mata ido take aikata duk wannan."
Muryarsa ta canza alamun baya cikin natsuwa yace"wallahi inna nayi iyakar yina,abun ya fi karfina kuma ba ita kadai bace kar inyi abunda zai jawo matsala,shiyasa nace zan maidota gabanki wata kila inkika fada mata taji.,kullin nuna wa take bana mata adalci,kullin maganarta ta nadeya ce nafifita nadeya fiye da ita,bacin kuma abayyane abun ba haka yake ba,ita lallai alaihin karna nuna inason nadeya,kokari take ta sani da bala,i saina daina kulata."

Anty ta anshe da cewa"to kidauka buktarki tabiya asmau,Khalipa bazai sake kula nadeya ba saike kadai,don haka nariga na yanke hukunci tunda daman ba auren soyayya sukai ba,auren wucin gafine shida nadeya to yau saiya bani sakinta,don daman koba yau ba yace zai rabu da ita.tunda dai bukata tabiya ka kama J da yayansa Alh Abbas,case na gab da shiga kotu ai magana ta kare,don haka nadeya ta yafe zama dakai asmau hankalinki ya kwanta."

Inna tace"aa Aysher ke babbace bai dace hakan ba,neman mafita ake kibar maganar yarabu da yar uwarsa,ga wadda zai koyama hankali nan itace zaiba takadda wata kila intayi hankali sai azauna lafiya da ita amman me nadeya tayi dazaki rabashi da matarsa"

"Kiyi hkr inna bawai naki jin maganarki bane,daman basa son juna ko anbarsu cutuwa zasuyi,shizai cutar da ita halinsa nasani.don haka kibari kurin yasaketa Allah zai bata wanda yafisa.don haka ina jiranka gida da sakon da daddare."
Tamike zata tafi cikin rawar jiki yamike shima cikin damuwa da kidima yace"haba Anty meyasa zakiyi haka,kibari aji daga bakinta mana kika sani ita bata so"
Harara ta maka mashi tace"nasani ai shiyasa na yanke hukunvin batare da sanin ta ba,damuwa tame zakai anrabaka da kaya."
Tasa kai tafice tabarsu inna sunyi xugum,asmau sai Zare ido take,bata san makomarta ba ita.

Biyota yayi bakin mota yace"Anty don Allah kibani dama inhar akaji ta bakinta tace ta yarda zanyi maku yadda kuke so,ni bazan so ace na wulakanta diyar Ammaer ba"
Wani sakaran kallo tayi masa tace"wulakanci kuma khalipa nanawa,badai asmau ka zaba ba,uhmmm gaka gata nadeya kam inada tabbacin bazata zabeka tabarni ba koda tana sonka,balle ba sonka take ba amma tunda ka nace kaxo zan tambayeta gabanka.matsa bani waje"
Tashige motarta tabarsa tsaye kamar anshuka shi.

Mom muhsen ce✍🏻

LOV U FISABILILLAH💝😊FISABILILLAH💝😊

Episode  .30

Ta inda inna ke shiga ba tanan take fita ba,fada take wa asmau babu kakkautawa saboda iyakar bacin rai takaita karshensa,bakinta kamar zaiyi tsini don fada.
Tun sumy na saka baki afadan har tazo tana bada hkr,cikin jin bacin rai innar tace mata.

"Karki sake bani baki sumayya inhar akan wannan maganar ne,ashe duk yadda nake bakin kokarina akanku don ku zama na gari na kirki abun ba haka bane,shuren iska kurin nake?zaki gaya mun ubanda yake baki shawara akai kosaina kafta maki mari shashashar banza da wofi."
Matsawa Asmaun tai tana matsar kwalla bakinta na kerma take cewa"inna don girman Allah kiyi hkr wlh babu wanda yake bani shawarar aikata wani abu,zuciyata ce kurin take kasa jurewa saina aikata abunda ta sani,kiyi hkr wlh na yi nadama bazan sake ba,kikira yayan nabashi hkr."

Filon kujera ta dauka tajefeta dashi tai saurin kaucewa cikin masifar innar ta cigaba da cewa.
"Wlh nidai Allah ya sani nayi iyakar yina,muddin yaron nan ya fita sabgarki wlh bana ciki,kixo muzauna ga yar uwarki nan sai muta ru mujera tunda haka kikeso,"
Tatashi cike da haushi tabar wajen sumy ta kalleta cikin takaici,tace "yanxu abunda kikai kin kyauta Asmau,duk cikin mu inna tafi sonki tafi baki kulawa kuma tafi kowa shan wahalar tarbiyarki,saboda yadda kikai ta shiga cases kala kala.duba akan case din salman kalubalen da kika jefata tun baba na nan,yaron nan kowa yasani ba dan mutunci bane amman kika ringa zagayewa kina binsa saboda soyayya,Akasamu kika rabu dashi akazo kan case din Abdul basir still d same matsala.inbacin inna da yaya da yanxu kina can kauye wajen baba labuda.kila kin hayayyafa biyar ko hudu,amman Asmau har yau ace bakiyi hankali ba to sai yaushe kenan?wace irin kulawa da soyayya ce yaya baya maki,hakkin nadeya nawa kuka taushe ita bata da xuciya koko ita ba mutum bace,da iliminki da komi keba jahila ba,kinfi son koda yaushe inna tayi fushi dake anya kina son rahmar ubangiji asmau,kin bata ran mijinki kin bata na mahaifiyarki kinsan irin bala,in da kike ciki kuwa?to in bacci kike kifarka wannan haukan babu inda zai kaiki sai kogin dana sani da tarin nadama,tun kafin damarki ta kare kigyara inkuma ba zaki gyara ba wanda baiji bari ba yaji hoho nima nafita sabgarki,abunda kika gadama kizaba ma kanki"
Itama tatashi ta barta tana kuka,hakika duk abunda ake gaya mata gaskiyace,kuma tayi kuskure babba inhar yayansu ya rabu da ita tashiga uku,maiyasa bazata iya saita xuciyarta ba har gashi tana neman kaita ga nadamar da bazata daina ba har abada.tabbas zata gyara dole zata gyara insha Allah andaina kuka da ita,wannan ne nakarshe kuma na har abada zata nemi yafiyarsu da alkawarin gyara halayenta insha Allah.,.......

     💕💕💕💕💕💕

Yinin ranar yau asukuku nayita nayi iyakar kokarina wajen ganin halin da xuciyata take ciki bai hanani sakin raina ba,amman duk da haka inhar kasan nadeya dole zakace bana cikin jin dadi.
Amma ahakan nayi duk aikace aikacena dana saba don kawar ma xuciyata da abunda zai sake jefata cikin kunci............na gama sanwar rana nida samha na shiga don nayi wanka,ina toilet naji kamar anshigo dakina amman dana saurara sai banji motsin kowa ba,na cigaba da wanka na kurin har na gama,na daura towel na fito...........mutun na gani kwance kan gadona ko cumbus dinsa bai cire ba,alamun fitowata yasashi saurin tashi yana kallona,kamar yadda nima nake kallonsa yanayin sa ya nuna baya cikin natsuwa kwata kwata,don gaba dayansa ahargitse yake mikewa yayi cikin hanzari ya nufoni,nai sairin rugawa zan koma toilet din,amma kafin na isa ya rikoni gam jikina har rawa yake don na dauka ko dukana zai.
"Shiiiittt ki natsu babu abunda zan maki magana nazo muyi dake"
Kokowar kwace nake ina cewa cikin tsoro tsoro 
"Nibana son magana dakai,kafitar mun daki cikani ka cika mun hannuna zaka ji mun ciwo"
Sakina yayi yana jan iska kamar wanda iskar taima kadan,yakura mun ido sosai da idanunsa sun canza kala yace cikin dishewar murya.
"Niba ba abunda Zan maki nace magana zamuyi dake,kixo kishirya inanan ina jira kigama"
Idona yayi rau rau nace "agaban ka zan shirya Allah ni bazan tube gabanka ba,kafita kurin"
Matseni yayi ga bango yace"nikuma bazanje ko ina ba inanan!kuma sai munyi maganar nidake sai muga wanda zai hkr da bukatarsa"
Ya fada yana auna mani wani shu,umin kallo yakoma ya zauna kan bed side dina yana bina da kallo,jinake kamarzan mutu don haushi da kunya.
"Waiko kin mance da sadakina nabiya kike ma mutane wani nuke nuke,wlh in naga dama saina yi abunda ba,a isa arabani dake ba."
Murguda bakina nai ina gunguni can ciki "saika xo kayi din tunda kafara shan giya".................."keee mekike cewa"yataso gadan gadan aguje na runtuma xuwa kofar bayina,tsaya wa yayi baibiyonin ba. yace"wallahi kina shiga saina biyoki,gara kifito"
Rigata ga hannu wadda na dauko zan saka,muryata kamar zan kuka nace"don allah yaya musaddiq kaje falo zan sameka,bazan iya saka kaya kana nan ba"
Jikinsa banda tsuma babu abunda yake,inya cigaba da biye ta zai aikata abunda zaiji kunya shida ita,don jiyake kamar magadisu tana jansa xuwa jikinta,baison daina kallon jikinta da ita kanta din shiyasa ya kasa fita.
"Wai bakiji rantsuwa nayi ba, tau kiyita tsayuwa anan,inkika kuleni wlh kinji na sake rantsewa nizan saka miki kayan."
Zaro idanuna nai yace"yes I'm serious"
Kamar zanmutu haka nakeji nafara sando nadawo wajen wadrop dina,na bude marfin na boye awajen wanda ba shakka nasan wlh yana ganina,Amma ya zanyi dole nafara kokarin saka bra don on ready tun a toilet na saka pant dina
Ina gama sa bra towel din yasulbe ya xube kasa sumane kurin khalipa baiba da yayi ido biyu da hallitar jikinta,jikinsa tsuma kurin yake wani irin abu yake masa yawo cikin jiki,babu abunda xuciyarsa ke ayyana masa illa yaje kurin ya rungumeta koya samu sauki,amman girman kanshi da taurin kai bazai barsa ba.saurin saka rigar nayi ina kokowar zage xif din,na kasa saboda sauri jikina rawa yake kurin,duk na rikice don idanunsa kurin nakeji suna bin jikina.
Ganin na kasa yasa na hkr na barsa iya rabi yadda hannun nau zai iya kaiwa.
Na rufe wadrop din na tsaya gaban mirrow ina kokarin shafa cream dina,hannuna sai rawa yake.
Saidai na jisa bayana zabura nai na jiyo ina kalonsa,idonsa sun sake lumshewa magana zanyi ya dora yatsansa saman lips dina,jinai tsikar jikina tana tashi wani yanayi yana bakun tata sauke kaina kasa nai.yazaunar dani bisa stool din ya saka hannu ya zage mun xif dina,sannan ya anshi man ya lakuto yafara kokarin shafa ma hannuna............"don Allah..........sake dora hannunsa yayi kan lips dina dole nai shiru,inaji ina gani yafara bin hannuna da man wanda nakejin kamar da gayya yake shafawa,hannunsa kamar me zan kirasa dashi dan taushi.kamar ba na namiji ba shiyasa wasu abubuwa suke ratsa ni ta ko ina,tilas na rufe idona don abun mai wahalar fassaruwane.gashi dana zo yin magana zai hanani,shi kanshi dauriya ce kurin amman gaba daya ya rikice,jiyake kamar ba fatar mutun yake shafawa ba,anya wannan yarinyar jikin mutane gareta?saida yayi iyakar kokarinsa kafin ya sake hannun ya kama gudan,kasan cewar hannun rigar karamine irin na shimi.shiyasa yake mun shafar har kafadata,jikina har ya fara yar rawa don ban saba da wannan abun ba,nakasa jurewa kafata ya dauko ya dora saman tashi yana janye rigata data rufe mun kwamrina xuwa cinyoyina,girgiza masa kaina nayi ina rau rau da ido zan kuka.........."kabarsa haka ngd,zan idasa don Allah kadaina bana so wlh"
Na fada kwalla na cika idona,lumshe idonsa yayi yana rungume hannunsa ya dage girarsa guda yace"oky continue"
Hannuna na rawa na cigaba da shafa man amman naki shafa ma cinyata,don karya ganin mani.
Na gama na ajiye farar powder na saka da lips tick kurin na daura kallabina na jiyo ina kallonsa da wani irin emotional feeling da nakeji tattare dani,shima canza salon kallon nasa yayi yana bin idona da kallon nashi,har na kasa dauka na sadda kaina.
"Kinyi kyau"
Najiyo muryarsa ta fada ban dago ba don bansan me xan ce dashi ba,tsayuwarsa gabana yasa na dago ya karkace kai yana mun wani duba na daban,yace "muje falonki muyi magana bana son wannan matar ta dawo ta sameni"
Harararsa nai nace "wacece wanan matar"
Wara ido yayi wanda saida naji wani xirrrr cikin jikina yace"mai nan gidan mana,"
Na wurga masa harara nace"saina gaya mata kazo har dakina kana tabani"
Murmushi yayi wanda zan iya cewa wannan karon ne kurin ya taba yimun irinsa.
Gaba nai nabarsa yana cewa"kinci sa,a ga tabawar kurin na tsaya"
Banjiyo ba don maganar tafi karfina.
One seater na samu na zauna daya zo saina zauna saman hannun kujerar danake,na tashi na koma wata ya sake biyoni.bata rai nai nace
"Wai miye haka ne"
Zamewa yayi ya zauna gaban kafafuna yana sanyaya muryarsa yace "nima ban sani ba"
Nadauke kai ina mamakin duk wannan salon,na rasa gane meke damunsa.samun kaina nai da murmushi nace masa.
"Ohhh wai ko kafara sona ne?"
Saurin kallona yayi kafin murmushi mai kyau ya subuce masa,yana shafa kanshi alamun maganar tasashi nishadi.
Zaman cin tuwo yayi agabana muna fuskantar juna,ni ina sama shiyana kasa.cewa yayi "amafarki  kika gani?"
Tabe baki nai nace"aa azahiri gashinan ina gani kana acting as kamar wanda yakamu da sona ne shine nake tambaya."
Still murmushin yana nan afuskar shi yace "to ki dauka bacci kike kika ga hakan,yanzu kin farka kinga kenan ba gaskiya bane.ko kin mance abunda na taba gaya maki cewar................"bana sonji plssss na sani ban mance ba,bana daga cikin matan da zaka kalla kaji sun burgeka,bana da abunda zaka so ajikin mace indauka cewar nida dakai babu vanbanci,koba sune kake son tuna mun ba"
Shiru yayi yanajin maganganun babu dadi kokadan,ashe haka yafada shi wlh yama mance ashe ita bata mance ba,inko hakane taya zai iya shawo kanta.
"Kayi shiru oky,abar wannan maganar muyi wadda kace kana son muyi ina jinka"
Shiru yayi yana jin wani guyawunsa suna sacewa,kwarin guiwarsa duk yana guduwa.
Hada ido mukai naga canji akan fuskarsa baice komi ba naga ya tashi yaje frig ya dauki ruwa mai sanyi,da cup ya dawo ya zauna.
Xuba ruwan yayi ya miko mun kura masa ido nai alamun na miye.
"Ki sha kedai"

"Saboda me xan sha  bana jin kishirwa ai"

"Saboda zuciyarki tasake yin sanyi yadda zaki iya mun abunda nakeso"
Ansa nai na kurba na ajiye cikin kosawa.
"Nasha saime kuma"

"Sai kibani hankalinki da natsuwarki"

"Na baka"
Murmushi yayi yana kallon yatsun kafata bansan me yake kallo jikinsu ba,naja rigata na rude kurin.
Shiru  yasake ratsa wa kafin na tsinto muryarsa cikin lumana da tausasawa yace.

"NADEEYA so nake idan Anty taxo maki da maganar rabuwarmu kice mata aa kurin"

Saurin kalonsa nai shikuma yaki yarda ya kaleni.
"What!mekake fada?akan me zance aa bacin nima jiran ranar nake"
Sai time din ya dago yana mun wani kallo mai cike da abubuwa dayawa,muryarsa babu alamun wasa ko wani abu makamancin haka yace"dagaske nake,inason kice mata aa kurin"
Shan mur nai duk da yadda xuciyata take bugawa,nace cikin raunin murya.
"Aa Yaya musaddiq kayi hkr,bazan iya abunda kake so ba."

"Meyasa ba zaki iya ba,bacin zaki iya din"
Juyar dakaina nai nace"hakan bashida anfani don me zamu nace wa juna bacin ba zaman gaskiya muke ba"

"Kika Sani yakoma na gaskiyar"

"Shiyasa tun kafin tunanin haka yazo maka dole zamu rabu,nagama maka anfanin da zan maka don haka kacika alkawarinka kawai,ai daman kace aurena dakai kayyadedden lokaci garesa,to lokacin yayi gara murabu kowa ya huta."

"Kowa ya huta kikace"ya fada kamar xai aman xuciyarsa,kasa kallonsa nai nace "ehy bakasona haka nim......nima.......din"
Na idasa muryata tana sizing.
Murmushin yake yayi don har fuskarsa ta sauya yace"meyasa kike gargada wajen cewar kema din,kuma kema din  me?"
Cikin hasala nace "nima din ban.....bana......sonka"
Nai saurin mikewa zan barsa nan,ya rike hannuna yana azaune bai tashi ba.
Kasancewar na juya baya hannuna nasa na goge hawayena,ina fatan karsu sake xubowa.

"Dawo ki zauna bamu gama ba"
Daker najawo maganar nace"ni na gama tawa taka kuma ka adana bana sonji"
Dole ya mike ganin bazan dawo din ba,gabana ya tsaya ya dago habata yana kokarin lallai saina kallai.
Nakiya nikuma yatsa yasa ya dangwalo kwallata datake ta son fita,yasa ga bakinsa cikin sanyin murya wandda take sake kasheni atsaye yace.
"Kuka kike?"
Girgiza kai nai nace "aa"

"Kuka kike mana,menai maki to,bakince bazaki iya cewa anty aa ba?"
Daga kai nayi yacigaba da cewa "to miye na kuka,kuma ai kince bakisona ko?"
Nan ma daga kai nayi ban yarda na kallai ba.
"Magana zakiyi mun"
Kaucewa nayi daga gabansa ya sake biyoni ya tsareni da kallo rungume da hannuwansa.

"Kisake maimaitawa nikuma zan kyaleki intafi"

Cikin danne kuka na da bacin rai amuryata nace"to miye damuwarka ne don bana sonka,kaidin sona kake kona zauna da aurenka anfanin me zaimun baka kaunata."

Najuya na kama hanyar barin wajen..........."kika tafi kika barni."
Yadda ya fadi maganar har cikin xuciyata ta shigeni,kuma ta kassarani kamar in jiyo amman nakiya na cigaba da kokarin ganin nabar falon.

"Baki fada ba kike tafiya,kina tsoron na gane kina sona kome?"
Cak na tsaya amman bazan iya juyo ba nace.

"Bana sonka yaya musaddiq,hakan yayi maka ko inkara fada."

Dajin muryarsa kasan cewar acikin wani yanayi yake,amman dauriya yasa bazaka gane ba sosai yace"aa hakan yayi nagode"
Kafinma na idasa kofar bed room dina yarigani ficewa falon,najiyo dai bugawar kofa..............

Kafin dawowar anty ba karamin kuka nasha ba har na gode Allah,amman sadda zata dawo bakace nice nashiga wani hali ba,don asthma ta kadan ya hana bata tashi ba.
Hidindumun mu muka kamayi matayi mun maganarsa ba,haka nima ban mata ba su sharifa basa gida suna dawowa daga school suka shirya suna gidan kakarsu mahaifiyar Abbansu,sai gidan yasake zamarmun shiru daman sune abokan hayaniyata.
Yau dai anty tayi mana tuwon semo da miyar danyar kubewa tasha nama,nako cisa sosai jana taitayi da fira tun bana biyewa har na saki raina na bita mukai tayi,har nake cemata inason na sake koma wa makaranta don inason nursing.
Taji dadin maganata sosai har tace insha Allah zata yiwa Abbansu sharifa magana yane man,muna cikin maganar ne ta sakomun tashi.

"Nikau bayan fitarmu khalipa ya dawo gidan nan"
Ban wani ja danisa ba nace mata "eh yaxo bayan azahar"

"Meyace maki ko kawai shigowa yayi yafita"

"Baicemun komi ba naji dai shigowarsa vansan fitarsa ba"
Jinjina kai tayi tace "fushi yayi kenan,"
Nan take mun bayanin duk abunda take faruwa,da yadda ranta ya baci akan nuna war dayayi yafison ma,u akaina,shiyasa itama taga yadace ra kwatar mun yancina kodan nan gaba yasan darajata,kuma nima zansan inada wanda zai kwatarmun yancina.
Hakika nasake jin kaunar anty,har xuciyata kuma banji bata kyauta mun ba,hakan da tayi mun yasani na sake yarda khalipa ba sona yake ba.kuma zanyi iyakar kokarina wajen ganin ban bata kunya ba,duk halin da zan shiga akansa zan jure inyi mata biyayya inhar akansa ne,nasan komi zai wuce daga randa naga mahaifiyata.

"Anty karkiji komi wlh hukuncinki shine daidai,kuma bazan baki kunya ba nima nafison hakan,zaman auren nidashi baida wani anfanu tunda baiso nima banso,kuma daman ba yisa don ya dore ba anyine don wata manufa kinga tunda burinsa ya cika ba anfanin rike igiyar auren dayake"
Jinjina kai antyn tayi tace"karki cuci kanki pretty kina sin mijinki kuma na rabaki dashi kiga bakina,ni kurin ina son yasan muhimmancin kine ba komi ba."

"Wallahi anty ba haka bane,niba wani sonsa danake kinga ai nasamu damar yin karatuna yadda nakeso"
Shikenan bari najira xuwansa................xuciyata bugawa take saboda nasan na kwareta,na hanata abunda takeso sau dayawa saika yi irin haka kake samun cinmma manufarka,yin yaki da xuciya ba karamin abu bane,sai wanda yajajirce akwai ciwo don ciwo amman in kasaka hkr saika samu riba,kuma abunda kake son ba lallai shine alkairi ba wanda xuciyar bata so shine alkairi,Allah yasa mu anfana da haka.

Karfe takwas sai gashi lokacin ni kadai falon ina kalon wani series film.
Tunda yayi salama na ansa baice mun komi ba nikuma nace masa "ina wuni"
Yaki ansawa saina kama kaina na cigaba da kallona harda karo volume,aikuwa na tunxura shi cikin fada yace mun.
"Zaki cire mun wannan karar ko sai ranki ya baci"
Kashe kallon nai nabar masa falon nima don ya kuleni,saida Anty tazo sannan takirani na zauna kasa nesa dashi kadan.

"Nadeya Zan tambayeki ki gaya mun tsakanin ki da Allah in kinason aurenku ya cigaba,inkuma bakiso shima ki gaya mun gaskiya."
Yadda xuciyata take amsa kuwa zan iya bin ra,ayinta amman kuma ina hango abubuwa masu muhimmanci,inda rabon muyi auren gaskiya nidashi zamuyi inba rabo Allah ya hada kowa da rabonshi.
Kamar ban damu ba afuskata nace "anty aimun gama maganar nan,kurin ki ce yasakeni ai lokacin yayi dayake kira zai rabu dani,nima daman jiransa nake.................."karya take anty,wlh yarinyar nan karya take karki yarda da maganarta tana sona kunyarki takeji shiyasa bazata iya gaya maki ba,karki rabani da ita tazo tana kuka dake"
Dariya cekurin ban ba amman anty saida tayi murmushi tace"nadeya bata mun karya,kuma inhar tana sonka bakaine zaka gaya mun ba nice xan gaya maka,don haka kaji ai yanxu da kunnenka biyu da Allah yabaka tace bata yi don haka ga biro nan da paper write her divorce"
Gaba daya ya rikice sai kallona yake na kauda kai nayi kamar ban gani ba,nan ko da za,a duba xuciyata kila jinina zaikai dubu don hawa.
Muryarsa kamar zai fashe da kuka yace"ke kifadi tsakanin ki da Allah,karfa nan gaba kiringa mun magiya na aureki"
Dariyar dai dole saida nayi nace"wai yaya musaddiq naci name kake,inhar kaidin ma ba sona kake ba to karabu dani mana,banayi nace"
Sakale yayi da ido yana kallona na dauke kai,jinjina kai yayi yace"inata kokarin inga na rufa maki asiri kina kaucewa ko?to kije din na sakeki saki daya ki auri duk ubanda zaki aura wanda yafini"
Yatashi kamar zai haukace anty tace "eh jeka Allah ya hada kowa da rabonsa"
Jiyowa yayi kansa har wata jijiya ta fito dan tsabar rikici da tension yace"ita keneman rabo nikam tuni na samu tawa rabon,yanzu saita je ta samu shugaban kasa gen muhammadu bahari ta aure karewar ta auri wanda yafini."
Yafice cikin bacin rai...................yake kurin nake ina dariyar karya anty na taya ni,har na samu na kucce na ruga daki.

Bazan iya kwatanta halin dana shigaba awannan dare,abunda nasani shine na farka na ganni sanye da oxygen.
Saida naji bakin sumy data xo ganina cewa anty ke gaya masu cikin dare kamar ance taxo bangaren da nake,ta dubani ta sameni nimfashi yana kokarin fita mafarin kenan na shiga wannan yanayin,...............shiru nai ina tsoron tuhumar da anty zatai mun,amman nasan bazan rasa abun kare kaina ba.
Aikuwa tana xuwa kawo mun dinner,taita hararata dariya nayi ina cewa menai mata kuma nida banda lafiya.
Don tun daren jiya nake bacci sai daren yau na farka.
Saida su sumy da inna suka tai take cemun.
"Baki kyauta mun ba pretty ko kadan,inkinsan kina son mijinki meyasa zaki boye mun.ni ina kokarin yi maki gata ashe kokarin kasheki nake"

"Don Allah kiyi hkr anty wlh............"karkice mun komi nasani,na gano kuma amman baki yimun adalci ba kema bakiyiwa kanki ba.yanxu wa gari ya waya kinsaka na tsaresa ya sakeki gashinan kina niyar macewa"
Dariya kurin nayi don nasan komi zance karyatani zatai,kuma karyar zanyi.
"Yanxu ina naga bakin kiran khalipa ince ya maidaki"

"Karma kifara anty anyi ya wuce atari gaba,haka Allah yaso daman bashine dawwamemmen mijina ba"

"Uhmmm sanin gaibu sai Allah nadeya."

Tunda abun ya faru ko gidan inna bai sake koma wa ba balle gidan antyn,wajen aikinsa yatai akwai dakunan ma,aikata acikin wajen nan yake rayuwarsa ciwone yayi kamar xai kashesa dole wani daga cikin abokansa ya kaisa asibiti,don cikin dare abun ya samai saidai can asibitin ya farfado,drip kanshi saida yasha leda biyar.
Likitan yama sa fada sosai akan halin dayake ciki,don yace inhar bai samar ma kanshi maslaha ba ciwon xuciya na iya kamashi.ya bashi magunguna yana sha yace yarage tunani jinin sa ya hau,kwanan sa uku abokinsa sulaiman shine kan hidima dashi,ya hana kuma agaya ma kowa sai ranar da za,a sallamesa ya saci wayarsa ya kira anty yake gaya mata.
Aikuwa ta tashi hankalinta daga wajen aiki can ta wuce harda kuka.
"Yanxu khalipa ashe zaka iya fushi dani har haka,kana asibiti bazaka gaya mun ba yanxu da wani abu ya faru yakake ganin zanyi.kai kadai nake dashi sai nadeya don Allah ka gaya mun abunda yake damunka,indai akan pretty ne na yafe maka ka maida matarka wlh"
Girgiza kai yayi yace "kuyita kaya bana so"
Kuka take amman saida tayi dariya.
"Wlh kana son yarinyar nan karka kashe kanka saboda ita"

"Sai yanxu kika gane ina sonta ko,sadda nake ta kokarin ban barki kika rabamu ba ai baki gane haka ba."
Ya fada cikin daurewar fuska 
"Wlh nasan kana sonta alokacin,amman ban san yakai zafin haka ba.inason nuna maka tana da daraja itama kamar kowace mace"

"Ai nagani yanxu naga darajar tata,ki jikata kisha anty ko kilika ga goshi kina yawo da ita"

Duka ta kai masa "don ubanka har ka iya gayamun magana ko"

"Uhmmm"

"Katashi muje gida ka ganta"

"Ki kyaleni don Allah,ni gobe ma zan koma Cyprus banga anfanin dawowa ta ba."

"Babu inda zaka,inkuma ka matsa saika koma narantse saidai kutai tare da pretty"

Aransa yace sadda zan fece ma bakisani don bazaku kasheni ba da sauran kwana na ba.

Dole gidan inna ya koma daga asibitin,amman ko kusa bai shiga shashenta ba don ma karya ga asmau.suduka haushi suke bashi daga nadeyar har asmaun,saidai inna da kanta taxo ta kawo masa abinci ta tsaresa saiyaci sannan ta bashi magani tatafi,da safe ta turo sumy ta gyara masa gidan.
Saida ya kwana biyar ia idasa gyagijewa sannan fa yashiga bangaren innar,don asubar fari jirgi zaibi yakoma wajen aikinsa.

Episode  .31

Ko kallon inda asmau ta ke zaune afalon baiba,duk da gaisuwar da take masa.,hh ya maida dubansa ga sumy yana mata magana bayan sun gaisa.

"Cikani haka nan to naji sainayi tunani,maganar nadeya kuma aibake kika aura mun itaba,sadda na aureta bakisani ba don haka ba ruwanki da tsakanin mu,in maidata ko karna maidata ruwanane bana kiba oya wuce kikoma gida dare ne."

Yaji dadin adduarta har ya mance rabon dayaji tattausar muryarta tana masa addua,jinjina kai yayi kurin ya shafi kuncinta yace"ki kula da babyna sumy ta ringa rakaki awo duk sadda zaki tai,zan bar enough kudi inkina bukatar wani abu"
