Ban masa musuba don konaja maganar da nisa bansan mezan ce ba,nace masa "to yaya good night"

"Okay by by"
Na sauke wayar ina ajiyar xuciya,iyakar maganganun da muke yawan yi kenan,shiru tafi yawa karshe inmuka kasa magana sai muyi sallama,amma duk da haka sabo da shakuwa ya shiga tsakanin mu,yawan wayar damuke na saki jikina dashi na daukesa yayana na sosai,haka shima yana treating dina matsayin yar uwa ta sosai da sosai,kuma hakan yana mun dadi sosai.

Agogo na kalla naga shabiyu tayi lokacin sallata baiba,saina bude shafin dana tsaya akaratuna na cigaba............

 _gabaki daya Abbas ya saki karfinsa ya bankada kofar dakin saigata ta bude,aguje ya shiga ya wuce wajen baban cikin kermar jiki don har naman jikinsa karkarwa yake,muryarsa ta dishe don tashin hankali da kuka jijjiga baban yake yana kuka matsananci yana cewa "baba katashi,baba narokeka karka tafi innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un Allah kasa mafarki nake ba gaskiya ba,baba baba katashi don Allah karka mutu.................amman inaaaaa baba ya ansa kiran mahaliccinsa,sakinsa yayi ya durkushe yana gunjin kuka,deluwa jikinta kerma yake ta kalli jafar sai huci yake yana zare idonsa kamar na kare,faska masa mari tayi mai zafi tace"jafaru!!!mahaifinka kakashe?ubanka daya kawoka duniya nashiga ukuna na lalace,jafaru kana cikin hayyacin ka kuwa,kalli fa kalli ubanka kwance ya zama gawa,kaine ka kasheshi" wurga idanu kurin yake yana xufa amman ko kadan baijin yayi ba daidai ba, don da dai atona masa asiri wallahi gara ya kawar da kowaye zaifiye masa zaman lafiya,don inhar asirinsa ya tonu karshe gidan yari zai kare rayuwarsa kokuma shima akasheshi,abunda baya fatan ya samesa ahalin yanxu,don yana fatan shinfida rayuwa irin wadda yake mafarki._

_kuka sukai tayi ita da Abbas wanda kamar numfashinsa zai fice haka yakeyi,haka suka raba dare da gawar baban agabansu,jafar kuma ko ajikinsa yakama hada kayansa kurin,ya gama ya fidda su waje ya jefa ta katanga don yau duk tsananin dare bazasu kwana ba,inhar akasan da mutuwar babansu hakan zai jawo bincike wanda hakan zaisa asirinsa tonuwa,ya gama duk abunda zai ya dawo dakin ya fixgi abbas ya tadashi tsaye,ya fidddo wata wuka cikin wandonsa yana jare masa ido ya nuna sa yana saita cikinsa,yace masa cikin kakkausar murya. "Wallahi tallahi billahi kaji dai rantsuwa irinta cikekken musulmi ko,wannan labarin ya fita saina farka yan hanjinka,wallahi kaima bazan barka ba duk inda kashiga saina bika na kasheka,don haka kagina rami ka binne abunda kaji kuma ka gani,don ba wannane na karshe ba kuma kana tare dani babu gidan ubanda zaka,balle ka samu damar tona mun asiri" hawaye suka xubo masa yana jinjina rashin imani irin na jafar hakika jafar babu digon imani ko kadan acikin xuciyarsa,mutumen daya kashe ubansa babu wanda bazai iya kashewa ba duk duniya.muryarsa cikin bakin ciki yace "jafar bazan taba tona maka asiri ba har duniya ta nade,ba kuma don inajin tsoron mutuwa ba aa saidan inason ganin ranar da Allah da kanshi zai tona maka asirin,sai inga shi yaya zakai dashi,xan zauna dakai bazan tafi ko ina ba inatare daku,don ina son ganin yaya rayuwa zatayi daku nan gaba kaida deluwa" dariya yayi yana sakin kwalarsa yace "ka nemamma kanka zaman lafiya don wlh gunduwa gunduwa zanyi da namanka inhar nasan kaine katona mun asiri...............kekuma basaina ja maki kunne ba don inhar kin tona bani kadai kika tonawa ba hardake,kinga zamu rayu agidan yari tare har karshen rayuwarmu"_

_tana sharben majina tace"bazan taba fada ma kowa ba wlh jafaru,amma kayiwa Allah karka sake kisa daga kan babanka,nasan nice na doraka bisa wannan hanyar to na rokeka kabarta haka,mubar gidan nan yanxu batare da kowa yasan abunda ya faru ba" murmushi yayi yace"ai kin bude mun hanya kenan deluwa,sai inda maina yakare,kwanan nan zakiji duk fadin garin nan da makewayinsa sunan *jafar daula* zakiji don kuwa daga yau na zama jafar daula." Shiru tayi don yanxu abun yafara bata tsoro,haka suka saka baban cikin buhu da tarkacen kayansu sukai dubarar da babu wanda zaisan me ke cikin buhun,suka bar gidan maigadi kurin yasan fitarsu,daker suka samu taxi biyu ta kwashe kayan suka nufi wata unguwa inda jafar ya siya masu babban gida irin na masu kudi,don kudin daya sata dakin Alhaji akwati gudane zasu isa yasiyi gidaje ashirin ma. Cikin daren suka yi ginin kabarin baba cikin lambun gidan sukai masa salla sannan suka rufesa,Abbas yayi kuka wanda yakejin tunda yazo duniya baiyyi irinsa ba,ranar idonsa bai runtsaba yana bakin kabarin baba yana kuka yana masa adduar dacewa,dacewa kam baba yayi don mutuwar da yayi ta rahma ce direct aljannah,inda kurin yakejin sanyi kenan amman hakika zaiyyi kewar baba don duk da bashine ya haifesa ba yana sonsa yana nuna masa akauna,don saika dauka shine dansa ba jafar ba._
hakika haka Allah yake lamarinsa zaka ga uba na kirki amma kasamu dan na banza,wannan shine ake kira ka haifi da baka haifi halinsa ba,duk yadda xaka ba da tarbiyya baka isa ka yimasa halayen kirki ba,Alah ya shirya mana xuria ya bamu ikon dorasu bisan tafarki maidaidaci,wannan kalubakene ga iyayenmu maza da mata.duk halin dakika dora danki akai tofa saiya linka abunda kike dorasa,karshe abun kanki ko kanka zai dawo Allah ka kiyashemu ameen.

_washe gari aka tashi babu su deluwa agida,wanda kowa yayi mamakin tafiyarsu babu bankwana duk maganar da yan aikin gidan keyi kenan,har sadda su Abbana da mamana suka fito don yiwa Abba kaseem rakiya xuwa air port alokacin sukaji maigadi yana maganar da wani ma aikaci mai wankin motocin gidan,Abbana ya kirasa suna cikin mota zasu fita ya iso yana durkusawa yana gaidasu,tare da yiwa Abba kaseem Allah ya kiyaye hanya. Tambayarsa Abba na yayi cewa "menakejin kana fada kaida mujitafa" cikin ladabi yace "yallabai akan tafiyar su deluwa ne,kowa yatashi yaga sun kwashe kaya sun tafi ba wanda yasan tafiyarsu,shine nake gaya masa aini na bude masu get karfe biyun dare suka fita da uban kayansu" kallon sa kurin Abba kaseem yake yana sake tariyo bayanin nasa,kafin cikin fada yace masa "baka da hankali ne ehy,ya mutane zasu fita cikin wannan daren bakaxo ka sanar damu ba,kasani ko akwai rashin gaskiya tare dasu,baka san aikin ka ba kokadan don haka wlh kujerarka tana rawa" abbana ya dakatar dashi da maganar ya sallami maigadin kurin,dirver yaja motar suka bar gidan sai lokacin Abbana yake cewa "kaseem nasha gaya maka kacire zargi aranka,tun da abun nan yafaru da su baba kowama zarginsa kake,miye don sunfita cikin dare daman ai mun sallamesu koda wane lokaci suna iya tafiya" cikin gardama yace "wlh yaya ban yarda dasu ba kokadan kuma din kaki bani dama ne kurin amma walahi da tuni suna hannun hukuma saisun fadi abunda suke boyewa,tun sadda sukace xasu tashi naji ban yarda ba,wallahi basuda gaskiya kobasu da hannu wajen kisan su baba to da alama kam akwai abunda suke boyewa." Murmushi kurin abba na yayi yace masa "nadai ce kabar maganar nan kurin,tunda sun tashi ai shikenan inma suna da wata manufa akanmu Allah yafisu,mudai mun zauna tsakani da Allah dasu munyi masu abunda su bazasu iya yimana ba,bakuma suda abunda zasu saka mana dashi,muma kuma badan su saka mana mukai ba,munyi ne domin Allah. don haka kabarsu da aniyarsu Allah ya raka taki gona ba shikenan ba"haka Abba kaseem ya hkr badan yayarda ba saidan bazai iya gujewa abunda yayansa yace masa akai ba,suka kaisa filin jirgi suka jira har time ya cika sannan suka rungume juna kamar bazasu rabu ba,don sai lokacin Abba yaji kamar yabisa sutai tare.daker suka rabu yana daga masu hannu yashige jirgin,basu bar air port din ba saida jirgin ya tashi sannan suka jiyo gida cike da kewarsa._

*Wannan kenan*

_jafar da deluwa sun bude sabuwar rayuwa tajin dadi,duk wani abun jin dadi da more rayuwa jafar ya samar masu cikin gidan nan,sai abunda suke so sukeyi harda samu yar aiki deluwa saboda daurewa karya gindi,shikuma ya samo maigadi inka gansu zaka rantse cewa jafar wani babban ma,aikacine na gwamnati,amman nan ko biyar bai jin shigarta wayarsa duk wata,balle ace aiki yake kudine suka sata vana wasa ba,Abbas kuwa ya zama dan kallo sai abunda akace masa,ya koma tamkar bawan jafar ta ko ina.alokacinne diyar deluwa maibin jafar ta dawo hannunta da zama watau halima,itama aci arxiki da ita don da tana wajen wata yar uwarta dake can wani kauye,rayuwar ta cigaba da tafiyar masu yadda sukeso jafar harda siyen mota maishegen kyau,ya kara wayewa ya zama maikudin karfi da yaji,abokai yayi irinsa yan karya suke sake dorasa bisa karyar daya ke da ita,kullin burinsa kara hauhawa yake soyake kurin sunansa yayi kaurw ko ina ana anbatarsa saboda yatara abun duniya,amma tayaya ta ina zaifara nemansu don jiyake kudi kota wace hanya ce zainema,kuma zai samu. alokacine ya hadu da *Abdulkadir shawai*_ (inkun tuna minister dake farautar ran nadeya)

_haduwasu ta kawo cigaba cikin rayuwar jafar domin kuwa shine ya dora akan wata shawara,wadda yakejin duk duniya babu wanda ya isa ya dakatar dashi daga aikata abun,yaji dadin shawarar sosai da sosai,amman ya dibar mata lokaci ba yanxu ba sai sungama tsara yadda abubuwan zasu kasance shida deluwa.sai suka fara sana,ar fashi da makami zasubi gari da rana ko safe su gama duba gidaje da mutanen da suka ga zasu samu wani abuu wajen su,sannan in dare yayi sutafi operation kuma Allah ya basu sa,a duk fita sai sun samo iyayen kudi,lamarin da yajefa deluwa cikin tsananin jin dadi,yayin da Abbas yaci gaba da saka ido baida bakin magana,kullin fata yake da adduar Allah yatuni asirinsu,amman shiru._

_cikin mamana yanata girma kullin cikin kirgar yaushe Abba kaseen zai dawo suke ita da Abba,duk da kullin sai sunyi vedio call dashi da daddare alokacin ne yake sanar da Abba ba cewar ai ya samu wadda xai aura,sunyi mamaki don baitaba nuna yana son wata ba,saigashi lokaci guda kuma yace yasamu wadda yakeso kuma da alama soyayyar tata ba karama bace wadda yake mata.dadi da farin ciki mama da Abba sun ji,har sukace yabasu labarinta yar Nigeria ce ko yar ina,yana dariya yace masu "nasan zakuyi mamaki ba kadan ba fa don wanda baku taba tunani bace" mama cikin doki dasonjin wacece zata zama yar uwa kuma abokiya aminiyarta,tace "kanen mijina na kosa kafada mun wacece inkana da hotonta nuna mani" dariya yayi don yana jin dadin sunan da take kiransa dashi yace"Anty matar yaya karki damu bari ki ganta"ya bude wayarsa ya nemo hoton ta ya kanga masu suka gani..........ido da hanci da baki suka saki suduka suna kallon hoto,mama kamar zata anso wayar daga hannunsa,bakinta yaki rufuwa tace"kanen mijina wasa kake wannan yar indiyar aitafi karfinka"Abba yana dariya shima yace"baka saba tsokanarmu akan abu mai muhimmanci ba kaseem,don haka gaya mana gaskiya wacece wannan" dariyarsa yake cikin jin dadi yace "yaya bana wasa dakai ai,ka wuce nayi wasa dakai akan abu mai muhimmanci,dagaske nake itace wadda muka daidaita marainiyace yaya tarasa duk iyayenta kamar yadda muka rasa,kuma musulmace kamar yadda muke musulmai,ita kadai suka haifa kamar yadda mukadai su Alhaji suka haifa,sunanta *AYSHER JUNAID KHAN* yaya pretty tana cikin matsala shiyasa na zabi na aureta domin cetonta da kuma zame mata aboki, uba,yan uwa nasan zaka tayani cika wannan burin,domin kuwa pretty iyayenta sunbar tarin dukiya wadda yan uwan babanta da mamanta suke hari,hakan yaja har ratuwarta ma tana cikin garari.yanxu haka gidan da aka bamu mu zauna nida abokan tafiyata na daukota ta zauna,tunda akwai mata saita zauna cikinsu,donma tayi bacci danaje na tadota ta gaida yayana kuma mahaifina.insha Allah tare zamu taho nan da wata uku masu xuwa"jinjina kai yayan nashi yayi cike da gamsuwa mama sai murna take harda yan kwallarta,tace"kace yar uwarta maijidda tana gaidata,kuma takosa tashigo family dinmu."yana murmushi yace "insha Allag Anty matar yaya kamar kinfada taji,yaushe wai baby zai isone na kosa fa"dakuwa yayan yayi masa yana dariya yace"kai yake jira ka dawo"zaro ido yayi yace "lallai anty matar yaya kina da sauran dakon cikin nan,duk girmansa ace da saura"cikin rashin jin dadi tace"wallahi wasa yake maka wannan watan nake saukewa asani addua sosai"farin ciki ya kamasa yace "insha Allah zantaya ki pretty ma zan sata tadage damaki addua,kinsan fa malama ce sosai wlh har karatu take mun.baffanta babban malamine sosai ta gadoshi"sun jima suna fira sannan suka rabu cikin jin dadin sabon labarin dayaxo masu dashi............_

_mama bata kara sati ba ta sauke baby girl dinta Anty Aysher,wadda taci sunan kakata marigayiya da kuma sunan sabuwar anty prettyn Abba kaseem,wannan suuna  ba karamin dadi yayiwa su Abba kaseem ba da Prettynsa,don siyayya kamar ta hauka suka ringa jidowa duk ta baby ce,saida Abba na ya taka masu burki sannan suka daina,don da sun siyo zasu nuna masu ta system idan sunyi vedio call,ana cikin haka bayan suna da wani lokaci akafara farautar ran Abba,duk fitar dazai inhar zaikai dare sai anbiyosa haikan za,akasheshi lamarin da ya girgiza mamana don kodayaushe cikin xullumi take,ta dage da addua Allah ya kare mata mijinta,ya nisanta shi da makiya._
_Bata samu natsuwa ba saida Abba kaseem ya dawo tare da pretty sai sannan suka tabbatarda ruwan kyanta,domin a hoton vedio gani suke ta rage kyau sai azahiri,zuwan *AMMER* yasake kara kawo masu budi da wadata,don ta danka duk dukiyarta hanun Abba kaseem tace ya hada da tashi ya cigaba da juyawa,da yace aa ta rike wajenta saida tamatsa harda kuka sannan ya yarda bayan yayi shawara da yayansa,ba,adauki lokaci ba aka daura masu aure burin Abba kaseem ya cika ya auri sanyin idaniyarsa,soyayyar su gwanin ban sha,awa haka muamalarta da kowa lafiya lau,ta maida Anty tamkar yarta inkaga taje wajen mama to nono zata sha,kullin cikin siya mata abubuwan kyale kyale take,data ga abu ya mata kyau na yar budurwa zata siya mata,tun mama na tsogumi har tadaina rayuwarsu gwanin ban sha,awa kamar komi bazai dagule ba nan gaba.ana nan kwatsam mummunan abu yafaru annemi Abbana sama ko kasa ba,aganshi ba aka kwana daya aka kwana biyu abu yayi gaba har kwana bakwai babu Abba babu labarinsa..................alokacin kuwa Abbana yana hannun J daula_

Mom muhsen ce✍🏻

Episode  .32

_duk inda Abba kaseem yake tunanin samun Abbana abun ya faskara,daga shi har su mama da Ammer sun shiga wani mawuyacin tashin hankali,basa iya cin wani abinci balle wani isashen bacci saidai barawo.Abba kaseem shida yansanda sunata bakin kokarinsu akan matsalar,har dai aka shigar da cigiyar gidan radio da tv tare da kudi masu yawan gaske ga duk wanda ya gansa,lokacin kuwa baba yana can suna gana masa azaba basu bukaci komi ba tukun,sunce sai yayi likis sannan ta yadda duk bukatar da suka xo masa da ita bazai bujire ba,fuskarsu duk arufe take baiga wanda ya sani ba,babu irin roko da magiyar da baiba akan sufadi ko nawa suke so za,a basu amma  basuce komi akai ba,saida yacika sati biyu cir duk ya lalace ya rame raunika bakin sa da hancinsa fuskarsa duk raunika,kafarsa guda baya iya takata sosai...............jafar ne ya zauna gabansa yana masa kallon iskanci,har da fama masa rauninsa na goshi har saida Abba yace "washhhhhh" dariya ya kwashe da ita yace "akwai zafi ko,karka damu ai yau zamu sallameka"baidai ce masa komi ba don soyake ya gano kowaye muryar yakeji kamar ya santa.wasu takaddune ya xube masa gabansa yace "malam Abbakar sadiq sonake kasaka mun hannu a wannan takaddun."kallon takaddun Abba yayi yana sake karantawa idonsa awaje don firgici da mamaki,domin kuwa takaddun companyn sa ne na Vaseline.kuma ba shakka cikin motarsa xasu samesu,don ya daukosu ne daga company zai maidosu gida ya adana.............cikin gigita da rudewa ya tattara hannunsa biyu domin roko yace"bawan Allah karka mun haka don girman Allah,kafadi yawan kudin da kake so wlh zan baka amma karka rabani da abunda nake kallo matsayin rayuwata" mahaukaciyar dariya suka kwashe da ita shida Abdulkadir,suna kashewa cikin ixgili jafar yace"nasan da kudin ai na nemi wannan,domin kudi zasu kare shikuwa kullin kudin zai ringa kawomun," ya daure fuska daman kuma idinsa kurin bude yaxare masa su cikin mugunta ya shake sa yace"zabi biyu zan baka,na farko ko kabani company nan kokuma nagama da rabin jikinka,kagane ai rabin jikin naka watau *Kaseem* kuma wallahi ba wasa nake ba saina kashesa muddin bakayi ba"jikin sa rawa yake da karkarwa don kuwa bayajin ya hada rayuwar kaseem da komi,kaf dukiyarsa zai iya mallaka masu inhar zasu kyale masa kanensa,cikin muryarsa da tashin hankali tayi rauni yace"zan baka amma kayi mun alkawarin bayan nasaka hannun zaku sakeni,kuma bazaka farauci kaseem ba inma kana son kari zan karama kabarmun kaseem din,narokeku kuyi mun komi karku taba kaseeem"kwalla suna silalo masa masu zafi don muddin ya rasa kaseem wannan karon bayajin zai ita cigaba da rayuwa............."karka damu kaji indai kanen kane bazamuyi masa komi ba,amma kasani duk sadda muka sake bukatar abu wajenka zamu dawo,oya sing" hannunsa na rawa da karkarwa haka ya saka hannu cikin wannan takaddun,kwalla na diga sama yana gamawa jafar ya dauke yana buga ihun murna yana kiran sabuwar rayuwa,yaransa yasa suka dauki shi suka sashi motarsa suka kaisa can bakin gari inda za,a ganshi suka barsa nan_

_bayan dawowar Abba babu yadda ba,ai dashi akan yafadi yadda abun yafaru ba amma yace baxai iya tunawa ba,shidai yasan yana kan hanyar dawowa gida danja ta tsaidashi yaga mutane sun shigo motarsa su biyu,daga nan baxai iya cewa ga yadda akai ba.,,,,Abba kaseem yasan cewar ba gaskiya yafada ba,koda ace ma gaskiyarce to ba iyakarta kenan ba har kuka yayi masa akan tunda yaki gaya ma yan sanda shi ya gaya masa as security nan gaba,juya masa baya yayi yana goge kwalla don kuwa baxai iya fada din ba,saboda rayuwar kaseem yakeso shi ba dukiya ba,kuma muddin yafadi wani abu da xa,a ganosu tabas yasan zasu aikata abunda sukace,don baisan iyakar yawansu ba.hkr yabashi tare da lallashi cewar tunda dai ya dawo komi ya wuce,cikin kunan rai yace "yaya tunda bazaka fadi ba adauji mataki,miye dalilin siyar da companyn ka?bayan nasan inhar zakai hakan zaka gaya mun."murmushin yake yayi yace xabun yazo gagaf ne,kuma daman ina da niyyar hakan karka damu bazan takura ba ai gidan kajin can yana kawo mun kudi sosai,kuma kudin dana siyar da company zasu isa na saka hannun jari awaniko"badan yayarda ba shina din, kandole Abba kaseem ya hkr amma still baibar bincike ba don ransa yana raya masa wani abu,zai nuna kamar yabar bincikena amma aboye baibari ba har saiya san gaskiya............._

Wannan yazo ya wuce.

_rayuwar ta cigaba yayin da kaunar junan su ta sake linkuwa,shakuwa tsakanin Ammaer da mama saika rantse yan gida dayane,ana yaye Anty Ammer ta ansheta ta koma wajenta saika rantse itace uwarta,anty har mancewa take da mama duk kallon Ammer take itace mamanta.rayuwar ta cigaba rashin kudi yazo yafara damun Abba,amman bai nuna ma kowa ba,domin kuwa baisan abunda zai saka damuwa cikin ahalinsa,amma baisan Abba kasem yana sane dashi ba yadai yi shiru yaga kozai nuna masa yana da bukatar wani abun,domin dukiyarsa shida Ammer kamar ana xuba mata yisss Allah yasaka Albarka sosai aciki.aboye Abba kaseem gini yake na zamani ginin da kowa yagani saiya ji yabala,in burgesa babu wanda yasani sai angama yayi masu surprise.gefe gudu ya tsanata bincikensa kuma yana ganin za,a dace ya kudiri muddin yasan gaskiya bazai daga kafa ba,ganin dagaske Abbana bazai gayama Abba kaseem halin dayake ciki ba na rashin kudi sai ya bude wani kafcecen store,babuce kurin babu acikin store din yaxo ya danka ma Abba na shi kyautar Allah saida sukai yar karamar rigima kafin ya ansa shima Ammer tasa baki sannan ya ansa,yana masa godiya.tun daga nan kuma komi yadawo norma suka cigaba da tafiyar da rayuwarsu,saidai gefe guda rashin haihuwar Ammer yana damun ta ita da Abba kaseem,don lokacin sun shekara uku da aure,mama ke kwantar mata da hankali tare da bata baki cewar haihuwa ta Allah ce,duk inda zakaje kashiga ka nemi maganin ta inhar bakada rabo bazaka samu ba,amman inhat kana da rabon ganin kwanka aduniya ba shakka Allah zai baka saidai bakasan lokaci ba,hkr shine maganin komi da haka take samun natsuwa.............._

_jafar arxiki yaci uban nada domin duk da wannan kwacen company da sukai wa Abba basu bar sana,ar su ta fashi ba,saida company sukai kudin yamasu kyakkyawar ajiya don yana da wani target nan gaba,baya son nuna wadatarsa acikin garin saiya gama tara uwar dukiya kafin yabar garin yaje inda babu wanda yasansa ya kafa kujerar mulkinsa,deluwa duniya sabuwa wajensa karenta kurin takeci babu babbaka,Abbas shima hardashi ake cin wannan gara don baida wata mafita data wuce yazauna dasu,yazama yaron jafar shine kan kominsa na dukiya....................deluwa da halima zaune suna cin gasassar kaza da lemu jafar ya shigo cikin ta kama yana kunbura,halima tatashi don ba shiri suke ba jama yayi yana binta da harara yace"deluwa kina jin dadin ki,koni dake shan wuyar nemowa banacin dadi irin haka"kallonsa take tana jin dadi tace "meyasa bakajin dadin to,naga lokacin mune mun samu dama saimu dama ko"dariya yayi yace "deluwa kenan,burina bai idasa cika ba tukun zan fara jin dadin amman ba acikin garin yola ba,saboda akwai abunda nake shiri. Aikatawa"ajiye naman tai tana kallonsa cikin tsoro "aa jafar ya isa haka,nace ya isa dukiya dai ta gidan duniya kasamu don haka kabarshi haka,kabarsu suyi rayuwarsu"dariyar yayi kurin yana mata kallon banza yace"au wai ke nan kin musulunta?banga alama ba don haka ni baki isa kice xaki dagatar dani ba,domin ko kekika dorani akai,karma kisake wlh"ya tashi yabarta nan yana kwallo da lemunta dake gefenta._

            Agurguje.

_su Abba sun koma sabon gida wanda duk wanda yazo wucewa saiya shiga don gidan ya dauke wa mutane dadama ido saboda haduwarsa,Abba kaseem yasha Addua wajan Abba na da su mama gidan kam ya hadu sai sam barka,lokacin mama takusan haihuwata don lokacin anty tana da shekara hudu da watanni.aikuwa cikin dan lokaci aka haifi *Abbakar sadiq musaddiq*,wannan karon ma kam kamar Abba kaseem akaima haihuwar don bakinsa kamar gonar auduga haka yake,har baya son fita gida saidole komi na haihuwa shine yayi itama Ammer ba,a barta baya ba ta takarawar gani sosai da sosai kowa sona yake ta ko ina gata nake samu,tundaga haihuwata har nafara wayau dafari nima kallon Abba kaseem nake matsayin mahaifina na gaske,saida nakara wayau na gane cewar tsabar sona ne kurin yasa haka,har xuwa lokacin Allah bai basu haihuwa ba kuma basu fidda rai ba,addua harmu damuke yara lokacin yiwa du Abba kaseem muke da Ammer Allah yabasu yar baby,lokacin rigima muke da Anty tace mace takeso Ammer ta haifa nikuma ince namiji,haka zamuyi ta rigima har sai ammer ta rabamu._

_kwanci tashi babu wuya wajen ubangiji saiga ciki ya bayyana jikin Ammer lamarin daya jefasu suduka cikin matsanancin marinciki,ba iyayen ba, ba mu yaran ba don lokacin duk mun shiga makaranta Anty ta gama primary nikuma ina aji hudu.renon cikin anty takeyi cikin kwanciyar hankali don kokadan bata wannan ciwon na masu ciki,ciki na shiga wata tara Abba kaseem yafara siyayya kamar hauka,ba kamar da aka tabbatar masa cewar mace ce,ganin haka yasa Abba ya dakatar dashi yace aa yabarsa yanxu turn dinsa ne,tunda shima sadda yayi hidimar mu bai hanasa ba don haka shima baison ko yar ficikarsa ya kashe,daker ya hkr amma duk da haka saiya siyo wani abun,don gani yake ya dorawa yayansa nauyi bacin yafisa hali.cikin yar dar ubangiji ammer ta sauke tsaleliyar babyn da kowa yake santi,duk wanda yaxo barka ko suna kyawun babyn yake tafiya dashi bakinsa,rigima muka ringayi da Anty akan babyn saboda kowa soyake ya dauketa,hakika ta gado uwarta da ubanta don suduka ba baya ba wajen kyau,ranar suna babyn taci suna *NADEEYA ABU KASEEM ABBAKAR*................._

zabura nai ina tashi zaune jikina rawa kurin yake babu kakkautawa,idona tuni ya lalace da hawaye toshe bakina nai don wani matsanancin kukane ya taho mun,ban iya hana kaina ba na soka kaina cikin filo ina rera abuna,ashe daman anty da yaya musaddiq yan uwana ne najini,kenan baba na da babansa uwa daya uba daya wa da kane suke?innalillahi wa inna ilahir raju,un to ina suke yanxu?meyasa bana ganinsu ina suka shiga inason ganinsu suduka,inason ganin Abban ka yaya khalipa saboda tsananin kaunar da yake ma Abbana,Allah kasa ba wani abune yake faruwa ba,nashiga ukuna me jafar yake aikata masu innalillahi wayyo Allah na,Allah kasa ba kashesu yake don inko hakane wallahi bazan kyalesa ba,bazan kyalesa ba daman duk abunda yake faruwa bashine mahaifina ba,biri yayi kama da mutun abubuwan dasuka ringa faruwa lokacin da ina gidansa abun dubawa ne.............kukana nasha mai isata inajin tsoron cigaba da karatun,don jikina yayi bala,in sanyi atsorace nake inajin tsoron jin cewar wannan marar imanin ya aikata masu wani abu,wayo Allahna mama da Ammer ina zan ganku..........tuna wai nai da photo album din dana dauko tare da diary din,na saurin dirowa daga kan gadon naje cikin wadrop dina inda na boyesa na daukosa.fitilar wayata na kunna ina bude wa ina bin hotuna da suka nuna alamun dadeww sosai,na gane kakannina saboda an rubuta granny's akasa,hakika naga fuskar yaya musaddiq jikin tsohon kakana kenan,naringa bi daya bayan daya ina kallo nagano mamarsu Anty wadda inka kalli anty sak da mamanta take kama,shikuma yaya musaddiq kamar Abban sa yayi kaki ya tofar,fuskata dauke da murmushi ina kallon iyayena,idona ya sauka akan Abbana wanda naga sunan sa akasan hotonsa shafa shi naringayi kamar zan masa magana ya ansa mun,kwalla ta silalo mun ta diga bisa hoton inajin wani kaunarsa tana huda kirjina da xuciyata,bakina yana rawa ya furta "ina sonka Abbana Allah yasada mu ko anan ko acan"............na share kwallata na cigaba da budo hotunan xuciyata ta tsaya cak da aiki saka makon matar da idona ya cikaro da ita,murza idon nasakeyi amma still ita nake gani,matar dake xuwar mun cikin mafarkina da bazan taba iya mancewa da ita ba.............sunanta naga ni kasan hoton *AMMER* wata wawuyar runguma nai ma hoton kamar zan tsaga kirjina nasaka shi,kuka nake sosai ina shafa fuskarta.
"Dama kece kike xuwa cikin baccina Ammer,hakika ke uwace ta gari nayi kewarki ina cikin kewarki Ammer,ina rokon Allah inhar kina raye Allah ya hada fuskokin mu da" kuka nai tayi sosai daker na ajiye bacci mai nauyi ya saceni

Ina gama sallar asuba nai azkar dina na gama naje nai wankan safe,na fita na hada tea mai zafi da kauri na sha da bread,don yunwa na tashi da ita sosai naci na koshi naje dakin samha nace mata in anty tafito tace mata "don Allah na koma bacci,bana jin dadine bazan iya aikin ba."tace mun to insha Allah,na wuce dakina badan kuma baccin zan ba,karatuna zan cigaba don inason jin ina iyayenmu suke,miye dalilin bacewarsu kashe su akai?ko kuma dai meyafaru.
Gadon na haye ina jawo littafin saidai kuma wayata ta kama vibrating,nasan yayane da wannan asubancin,inbashi ba banda mai kirana wannan lokacin amman kuma Lafiya?don baitaba kirana da bayan asuba ba.

"Assalamu alaikum,yaya ina kwana"

Mika yayi yana salati tare da hamma yace"morning how are u?"

Cikin marairai cewar murya nace masa
"I'm not feeling fine yaya"

Cikin rudewa yace "meke damunki?shine babu wanda ya sanar dani daga ke har antyn.badai Asthma vace ko"

Murmushi nai inajin wani sabon sonsa yana huda ko ina najikina,ba kamar yau dana kejin sabon feeling akansa,yayana kuma masoyina da nakeji dashi duk fadin duniyar nan.
Kara marairaicewa nai kamar gaske nace"uhmmm saida nai kuka fa,jiya ban samu bacci ba ko daya kaina ma ciwo yake,"
Xuciyarsa ta buga tare da wani irin sabon lamari dake sake addabar rihinsa ya sanyaya murya yace "deeya nah"

Lumshe ido nai nace "uhmmm yaya"

Tsikar jikinsa tatashi sosai har yana sake dunkule kanshi cikin bargo,ya lumshe idonsa saboda dadin sautin muryarta shigarsa yake ta ko ina ba sassauci..........."so kike na dawo lokacin dawowarmu bai bako"
Kamar ince masa eh amma saina fasa na sake makale muryata nace masa.

"To yaya menai maka,daga nace maka bana jin dadi"

"Kukan me kikai to?"

Shiru nai ina nazarin ansar da zan bashi har ya sake magana ban kai ga ansa shi ba,
"Kokina kewar yayanki inadawo yau din nan"

Girgiza kai nai cikin sanyi nace"aa karka taho saika gama komi,kurin dai na tuna wani abune"

"Miyeshi da ya saki kuka haka"

Kwallah suka silalo mun muryata ta karye nace"yaya anya dady shine mahaifina?anya ba tsinta ta tayi ba,inajin kewar ainahin iyayena inson in gansu kafin namutu.........na rushe da kuka wanda daman kusa yake.

Rudewa yayi sosai yana cewa "shiiiitt my deeyah don't cry kiyi hkr kidaina kuka insha Allah dana dawo zamuyi magana,amma kiyi mun alakwarin kin daina kuka"

Shashshekar nake kasa kasa ina goge hawayena nace "uhm na bari"

"Okay smile"
Ma kale kafada nai nace "uhn ban iya ba"

"Shikenan tunda bazaki ba saida anjima daman nai mafarkin kina kuka shiyasa na kiraki"

Bacin karya yake jiya kasa bacci yayi mai dadi akan kewarta,shiyasa har ya makara Sallah.

"Bye.......na kashe wayar ina sauke ajiyar xuciya mai nauyi na jawo littafina na cigaba daga inda na tsaya.

******

_kulawa da soyayya babu wadda pretty bata samu agidan nan,tun daga kan iyayen har xuwa kanmu nida Anty,don har danbe muke akan daukarta haka za,aita fama dani inyi kuka ingode Allah saboda anhanani goyata,mama ce kuma mai hanawa wai ban iyaba zan iya kadata,pretty tana kara wayau tana kara sanin kowa ya za mana tafi son xuwa wajena akan kowa,nikam dadi kamar yayi man me shiyasa komi na samu ita nake kawowa,koda kuwa bata iya ci ina nace saitaci sai mama tayi mun jan ido nake hkr,ana cikin wannan zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali mummunan lamarin yaxo ya auku,wanda banajin har duniya ta nade zan iya goge wannan memory din ba,bakuma zan daina kuka ba har abada._

_adaren ranar wata alhamis za,a wayi gari juma,a mun gama duk hirarmu da iyayenmu cikin jin dadi da raha,kowa yaje ya kwanta karfe biyu da rabi na daren mama tatashi tana sallar daren ta data saba duk dare kurin sai wutar gidan gaba daya ta dauke...........abunda baitaba faruwa ba,bajima wa kuma mukaji sautin wata bindiga wadda itace silar tashin kowa agidan.duk mukayo falon kasa hankali tashe pretty sai kuka take takiyin shiru,naje na ansheta na saba ta ga kafadata ina jijjigata,su Abba suka nufi fita daga falon don ganin meyake faruwa................amma kafin isarsu kofar falon wannan matsiyaciyar bindigar ta harbo harsashin daya huda kafadar Abbana,nan take ya xube kasa yana shure shure,gaba dayan mu kansa muka nufa cikin gigita da ihu da kururuwa,Abba kaseem ya gigice ya rude jijjigashi kurin yake bamuyi aune ba muka gansu sun cika falon,sun zagaye mu mudukan mu kowanensu ya saita kanun mu da bindiga,ogansu yaja kujera ya xauna ya dora daya kan daya......................_

Episode  .34

Kwanaki suna ta wucewa yayin sa koda yaushe xuciyata cikin yanayi take,tunda na gama sanin wacece ni da kuma dalilin zama cikinsu Anty,tunda nagama sanin komi na koma silent,duk da dama niba mai hayaniya bace ba dana kasance ni daya to kau zan xurfafa cikin tunani har ban sanin ina hawaye,saidai naji xubarsu kurin.anty ta lura da halin danake ciki da tayi shiru kozan gaya mata,amma data ga abun nawa gaba yake da kanta ta sameni da lallashi da tattausan kalami tace mun.

"Pretty"
Dago idona nai kurin ina kallonta bansan meyasa da nacika tsareta da ido ba sai inji kwalla ta cika mun ido,tausayinta da nawa take kamani...............girgiza mun kai tayi ganin alamun hawaye zasu xubo,bansan sadda na rungumeta ba nafara xubar da hawayena,itama matseni tai tana bubbuga bayana cikin sigar lallashi saida nai mai isata duk ba tace  komi ba,sannan na dago ina kallonta nace "Anty ashe haka rayuwarku ta kasance shekarun baya,anty wane irin azzalumin mutun ne jafar?anty tunda na gama sanin wacece ni nasan duk mafarin duk wata matsala datake faruwa ban sake samun kwanciyar hankali ba har yau,kullin abubuwan dawo mun suke kamar yanxu ake aikata su,inajin kamar in hadiye xuciyata in mutu kiyayyar J ta gama mamaye xuciyata jinake dazan ganshi gaba da gaba zan iya kashesa anty.............kamar....kamar yadda ya kashe mana su.........su Abba da mama"kuka ya sake kubcemun itama tuni hawaye sun gama wanke mata fuska,dora kaina nai kan cinyarta muna kukan tare,shafa kaina take tace.
"Pretty gudun wannan halin dakike ciki yasa dagani har yayanki bamuyi yukurin sanar dake ba,munbar abun ne acikin xuciyarmu har xuwa ranar da duniya zata san waye J,zaku ma tasan irin ta,addancin da yajima yanayi xuwa lokacin muna fatan ya fiddo mana Ammer da Abba kaseem,don inajin xuciyata har gobe suna raye,amma a ina shine ban sani ba.kiyi hkr shine abunda zan iya baki don kuwa tunda kika riga kika gano komi bazan iya hana xuciyarki kunci ba,domin nima tsawon shekaru har gobe inajin irin hakan,saidai dayake nasaba abun yabimun jiki baza kace muna da wata damuwa ba,kiyi hkr haka Allah yaso kuma da idonki zai mana sakayya."
Ina jan majina tare da goge fuskata nace"nakasa tausar xuciyata Anty,sai nakejin inama ban bincikowa kaina wannan tashin hankalin ba"

"Ina kika binciko sa,nasan dai yayanki bazai gaya maki ba don baya son tashin hankalinki dana wa"

Dauko mata diary din nayi da photo album din na Nuna Mata,ansa ta tana kallon hotunan murmushi yana kubce mata,labari take bani na lokutan da akai hotunan,su duk basuyi wayau sosai ba don shi khalipa ma yana primary 3 ita antyn tana js one.abubuwan ban dariya dana kuka haka taita gaya mun,sai najini cikin nishadi kamar karta daina,ba kamar yadda yaya yake rigima akaina da yadda yake nace wa wai saina gwaigwayi goruba, ina wata biyar lokacin wanda ko zawon hakori ban fara ba,nai dariya sosai daker muka saki hirar muka fita don na taya ta aikn dare,samha bata nan taje yawo garinsu.

Kwanaki suna ta wucewa ya zama na yaya baifi sati uku nan gaba ya dawo ba,kwatsam asmau ta haihu murna wajena ba,a magana ba zakace kishiyata ce ta haihu ba,ko daya gani nake ai dana nane kamar yadda babansa yake yayana,tattara kaya nai nace natafi zaman jego nida dawowa sai anyi suna,bata hanani ba aikuwa inna taji dadi harda sumy da ita kanta maijegon.yaro kamar ubansa yayi kaki ya tofar,sak banga ta inda yabiyo uwarsa ba nidai hayewa nai kan gadon na daukesa  nai masa pictures,nace don Allah subarni nayiwa yaya surprise don ranar akai haihuwar da daddare,nikuma nazo da safe.

"Anbarki matar yaya"inji sumy harararta nai nace karki soma ada kenan,karkija matar tasa ta gaske ta tsireni kamar tsire.

Dariya sukai Asmaun tace"aa wlh meyakai na tsirewa,in yanxu ba ita bace nan gaba soon zaki sake zama matar tasa insha Allah,"
Tabe baki nai nace "uhmm aa bana so akai kasuwa,mijinki ya tsufa aradu nikuma saurayi sabon jini nakeso"

Sumy tace"banda dai cika baki ehe"
Asmaun ma tace "yawwa yaya sumy gaya mata dai,don yaya babu ta inda zaki nuna masa kyau ingaya maki sardidin guy son kuwa kin wanda yara sa"

Dariya nake sosai nace "su kyau manja,aini banga wani kyau anan ba ma,u kedai dakika gani kiyita kaya nikam dai bana so ya zama gwanjo"
Inna ce ta leko tana rike baki tace"iyye!nadeya dan nawa ne gwanjo?"
Boye fuskata nai ina dariya nace "tuba nake inna ba ina nufin haka ba,wlh sune suka tasoni gaba wlh"

"Kin kyauta kuma kamar kinyi akunnensa ne"

"Inna kiyi hkr wlh sune suka fara"
Na fada cikin sigar ban hkr,murmushi tai tace to ku kawo yaron ga yan barka nan nashigowa,kun kadaddabesa kamar xaku maidasa ciki.
Na daukosa ina murmushi nace"inna yaron ne wlh kamar ka cinyesa tubarkalla Allah yamasa albarka"
Kowa ya ansa da amen nabata shi na dawo na jingina da gado nafara kokarin tura masa hotunan ta network,saida na tabbatar sun shiga sannan na danna masa kira,don daman kwana wajen biyar bamuyi magana dashi ba,da dai fushi nake amma yanxu na huce saboda babyna dayazo..............sumy fita tayi Asmau kuma ta shiga toilet,tajima tana ringing kafin naji ya dauka na natsu sosai don saurarensa,sai yanxu naji nayi mugun kewarsa murmushi mai tsananin kyane ga fuskata,don bansan meyasa ba duk sanda nake magana dashi,ko kuma nake kokarin kiransa to zokaga fuskata kamar anmun albishir din gidan aljanna,maganarsa najiyo yanayi na saurara inji............

"Banson Sa,a ido matarka ta koya maka gulma da ba haka kake ba.......ohhhh may god hello."

Murmushi nai nace"uhm su yaya manya"
Shima murmushin yaya yace "su deeyah manya"

"Kasan dai ai kayi mun laifi ko?"

"I know,and I'm very sorry, u know I'm busy saboda muna haramar conclusion ne"

Rausayar da kaina nai lokacin da asmau ta fito daga bayin,kalona tai tace"jawarinne kika kashewa murya"
Bansan sadda dariya ta kubce mun ba,lumshe kyawawan idonsa yayi yanajin wani xiririn abu yana bin jikinsa,dole ya silale ya zauna kasa saman tayils,yace "wacece ke magana"
Ban bashi ansa ba nace mata "aa ba jawarina bane,wannan dattijon mijin nakine"
Nai zaton ganin bacin fuska gareta amma sai ta saki murmushi tace"uhmmm kin gama ce masa gwanjon kin  kuma daga baya kina zagaye ws kina sauraren zazzakar muryarsa wadda nasan duk cikin jawarawanki babu mai irinta don dadi,"
Girgiza kai kurin nai nace "zan waiwayeki yarinya,don ni mijinki baimun ba"

Fita tai tana dariya shikuma yana jinmu,nasan yaji abunda mukace nace masa"yawaa yaya kasan me"
Shiru yayi yana jina saida na sake magana sannan yace"kin gama zagin nawa"

Zaro ido nai nace"waceni na zagi dan gold din yayana,wlh ba zaginka nai ba matarka ce take neman raina mun wayau shiyasa"

"Shiyasa kike kirana tsoho,dattijo ko"

Bata fuska nai nace"nifa wasa ne,kurin don tagane ni bana layin ka"
Kirjinsa yaji yayi masa wani iri,yace "ban miki ba ba kya layina write?"

Gabana ya fadi nai shiru na kasa cemasa komi,sake magana yayi muryarsa alamun dai baiji dadi ba.

"Answer me,ban miki ba kuma bakya layina na miki tsufa ni dattijone"

"Yaya niba wannan nakira na gaya maka ba,wlh abun murna ne yafaru fa kaduba what's app dinka na turo maka wani surprise kuma ina jiran kyautata"
Na fada don kawar da maganar,gabana yana duka.

"Okay thanks"
Jikina ya mutu don sanyi kamar zan kuka nace "yaya"

"Karki sake Kira na wani yaya"

"Menai maka to,daga albishir din shine nai laifi"

"Ohhh kin mance mekika ce kenan,shikenan sai anjima ina aiki"

"Yaya karka kashemun waya nifa nakiraka"

"Saina kashe din,mai kudin dud duniya nima ba rasasu nai ba ko ki kashe koni na kashe"

Naji haushi nace"ka kashe din,ba sai in nasake kiran ka ba ma,karma kadawo kaita zama nan"
Kafin ya kashe na rigasa inajin haushin abunda yayi mun,nasan abunda yajima haushi don nace masa baimun ba kuma shi dattijone,ohhh so yake ince yaya kaimun kuma kai handsome guy ne,ina sonka.........naja tsaki wlh bazan fada ba,aiba sakarai bace ba ni.

Inma sona kafara ka gama nuku nukunka kaxo kana lobbying dina.........nai murmushi ina tashi don koma wa falo kila yan barkar sun rage.

Daddadare ina kallon kiransa nai buris dashi,don nasan yaga hotunan babyn shiyasa yakirani,naja tsaki meka ce mun bayan ka gama yimun wulakanci,daya dameni saina dauka na mika ma asmau nace "ga gwanjon mijinki yana ta nemanki,wai layinki baya shiga" saikuma karyata ta hau don asmau wayarta ta lalace kusan sati kenan,cikin jin dadi ta dauka tana gaidasa numfashi yaja jin ba ita bace,amma baida damar kubcewa don yadan dake yace "ina kika saka wayarki" cikin sanyi da kwantar da kai tace"yaya ta lalace,kuma bayan haka ma ai ba kirana kake ba,saidai kakira inna inkun gama kace abani mugaisa"
Bai dauki complain din ba tunda har yau basu daidaita ba,bai sauko mata kamar yadda tasaba ba yace"yajikin naki,badai komi ko shine kuma baki kirani kota wayar innar ba kin gaya mun" cikin lallami tace"kayi hkr wayarce ta bace akaita nema,sai daga baya aka gani shine nadeya tace abari tai maka surprise" shiru yayi kafin yace "kishafa mun babyn kafin nadawo insha Allah next week zamu dawo"cikin doki da murna tace "dagaske amma naji dadi Allah ya dawo dakai lafiya" yace " ameen,ki kula da kanki zamuyi magana da safe maida mata wayar" cikin jin dadi ta miko mun don rabon da suyi magana mai tsawon haka harta mance,da alamun yayan yana hucewa ahankali,insha Allah sadda zai dawo komi ya wuce zan gyara kuskurena.
Ansa nai maimakon nayi magana saina kashe kurin,kallona tai tace"magana fa xai maki" harararta nai nace"nikuma banaso bah" dariya tai tace "kun gama gulmarku mu dai yan kallone"

 
******

Anyi sunan asmau lafiya yaro yaci sunan mahaifinsu Abdul majid don shazalli ba sunan sa bane na ainahi Abdul majid shine sunan sa na yanka,ana kiran babyn da  *muhin* yaya ya aiko da kudi anyi duk hidimar data dace,anyi yanka raguna uku manya kosassu,munyi shagli anyi hotuna sosai amaryar jego tayi kyau ba karya,anty ma duk da ita akaita hidimar ga kuma yayarsu kubra datazo daga kano,kamar kar awatse anata raha da barkwanci da hira gwanin ban sha,awa bayan suna da sati guda na tattara nace zan koma gida,ai sumy da asmau kamar zasuyi kuka har inna suka gamani da ita wai in hkr inzauna don Allah,nace aa can dinma inada abunyi,ganin ta wajen inna ban tsira ba yasa sumy ta kira yaya da wayarta wai zatace yayun magana in xauna,nikau ina ayya nawa bazan zauna din ba,don har lokacin bana magana dashi kullin zai kira saidai naba asmau,wanda da haka nasamu suka shirya awayar,duk sadda zai kira ita zan ba inta sata tana yimasa abubuwan da zaisa yaji ya huce,saigashi cikin sa,a sun shirya har dariya xakaji sunayi ita dashi,sautari zanji wani abu ya sokeni inji mema yasa nake basu wayata?amma saina saurin korar wannan tunanin,inyita kiran hazbunallahu wani imalwakil don kar xuciyata ta rinjayeni nayi badaidai ba............ina ta hada kayana sumy nata kiransa bata samu,nasan kuma bada layin dayake using take kira ba,dariya nai mata nace "kinyi kigama bazaki samai ba ai"

Asmau ta rarumo wayata tace"anshi taya sumy kirasa da layinta kila ke bakida no. Din dayake anfani,"ansa  tai tana cewa "shiyas take dariya don tasan ba samunsa xan ba"
Nidai kayana nake linkewa ina sawa cikin akwati,inajinsu bancee komi ba.
Xuwa can sumyn tace "ta shiga asmau ya zance masa"
Ansar wayar tayi tace "bani shi nan,"tana sawa ga kunnenta tana rungume da muhin yana bacci,cikin sa,a ya dauka cikin taushin murya cike da shagwaba tace "yaya"

Lumshe ido yayi duk zatonsa ma deeyah ce,ya bude ahankali yace "ya dai asmaun yaya" murmushi tayi tace"yaya don Allah kayiwa deeya magana kozataji"

"Maganar me Zan Mata menene yafaru"

Sake narke murya tai tace"yaya wai gidan anty xata koma,bayan kuma duk bama son tafiyarta munce ta bari ta kara mana koda one week ne saita tafi,amma takiya har inna saida tayi mata magana tace ita aa"

Shafa kanshi yayi babu abunda yake masifar so irin jin muryarta,ba karamin wahaltuwa yake ba cikin kwanakin da basu waya,yadan sha mur yace "oky bata wayar"

Inata aikina tamiko mun tana dariya "yace ki ansa shi ai yafada maki kiji"
Kallon wayar nai ina harararsu ita da wayar nace"hannuna ciwo yake ki kanga mun"
Dungura mun ita tai ga jikina tana cewa "banzan yi hakan ba yar raini wayau"dariya nai kadan ina daukar wayar kafin na daure nayi sallama.

"Assalamu alaiku" wata wahallar ajiyar xuciya yaja wadda har cikin kunne,hakan yaja naji kamar zan fadi na dake dai don ganin idonsu duk akaina yake,ahankali na rage volume din wayar karsujimu,muryata can ciki ban zaton suna jina nace.

"Sallamar ma sai anja mata aji kenan?"

Kasa jurewa yayi yabi lafiyar kujera ya kwanta yana sassauta muryarsa yace cikin wani sauti mai matukar kashe sassan jiki.

"Deeyah"
Don dolena lumshe idona nabude nace "I'm here u"

"Fushin ya isa haka"

Cikin muryata da ni kaina bansan sadda take narkewa ba nace masa "uhn uhn bai isa ba"

"Meyasa bai isa ba,yayanki ne fa"

Nace cikin ko inkula "nasani ai,kaima kasan da nice kayi mun wulakanci rannan ko"

"Sorry"

"Uhn uhn bana so"

"Plzzzzz"
Jinai kamar zan kurma ihu saboda abun da nakeji,na bude idona ina satar kallon su tuni sumy ta fece tana dariya,asmau ta sabi muhin tana cewa"kar ayi gabanmu nayi na nidai"

Maido hankalina nai kanshi yace "meyasa zakitai bayan suna son ki zauna dasu"

"Nafijin dadi wajen antyna"

"Na san da haka,amma atleast in babba yayi magana koda baso bi ake deeyah,kingani inna baxata ji dadi tayi maki magana bakibi ba,kiyi hkr kizauna kinjiko"

Turo baki nai nace "uhun"

"Kibari ma saina dawo basaina maidaki har gaban antyn taki ba"

"Ainasan hanya ko"

"Oh bakiso kenan"

"Saurayina zai kaini"

Shiru naji yayi kurin na duba naga bai kashe ba,amma yaki magana sai naji meyasa nace haka,na gyara muryata nace "uhm shikenan zan zauna saika dawo din yayi maka ko"

Daker ya ansa mun yace"thank you"

"Bye"

"Okay bye" ya kashe wayar 
Na silale kasa ina jan numfashi,yaya yana da salon dazai iya saka mutun ya kurma ihu baisan yayi ba,saboda feeling din dayakeji na bashi number yabo ta nan wajen,.

Ba yadda na iya dole na hakura da tafiya na kira anty na gaya mata,tace ba komi in zauna din sadda ya dawo sai inkoma nima,daga inna har su sumyn kowa yaji dadin zaman nawa,koba komi za,a zauna ayitq fira cikin jin dadi da annashuwa,muhin kuwa ya zamana koda yaushe yana hannuna ko wanka akai masa ni nake shiryasa,inna ta koya mun da tsoron saka masa kaya nake saboda gani nake kamar zan karya masa hannu,(😂lol)amma daga baya sai gashi nice kominsa,har dai su sumy da asmaun suka canza mani suna wai *MOM MUHIN*,kuma sai sunan yamun dadi ban ce su daina ba, nikuma nake kiransa da swt hrt sabo da yadda nakejin son yaron kamar nina haifesa.................ana cikin haka sumy ta shiga sch karkaso kaga murna kamar zata tashi sama,ranar hanamu bacci tayi da wuri don washe gari da safe zata fara xuwa attending lecture,dariya kurin nake mata ina cemata ba sabon ba tace ehy taji ba komi zata saba ai,adaren yaya yakira da wayata na mika ma asmaun kamar yadda na saba,dama kuma shima ita din zai magana da ita shima fushin yake kamar yadda takeyi.

"Gobe wani abokina dake lecturing school din su sumayya zaizo zai wuce da ita,sauran abunda ba,a kammala ba zai taimaka mata,ayi break dashi don ba nan yake ba daga abuja yake xuwa duk sati."

"To yaya insha Allah za,ai yada kace,to kai sai yaushe duk fa mun kagara muganka har da muhin ma"

Murmushi yayi yace "nima nakosa nataho Asmau nayi kewar gida damutanen gidan,ga kuma new baby boy dake jiran Dadynsa"

"Ai yayi fushi dakai"

"Aa haba dai saidai ke kiyi amman yarona baxai fushi da mahaifinsa ba,karma kisake ki koya masa kinaji na ko"

Dariya tayi tace"uhmm shikenan bazan koya masa ba,amma inka wuce wannan week din Allah zan koya masa yadda daka zo daukarsa yafashe da kuka,har saina anshi kayana"

Dafe kai yayi yace"oky bazan wuce ba insha Allah,ki shafa mun kanshi da fuskarsa kice masa I love you"

Shagwabe fuska tayi tace"to nifa?"

Murmushi mai sauti yayi yace"kema haka din to"

Cikin shagwaba ta sake cewa kamar zatai kuka "nima me to,wlh yaya bana son haka"

Dariya yayi yace "sorry mom muhin Ilove u too"
Lumshe ido tai tace "thank you,amma wannan sunan banau bane kar maishi taji kana kirana dashi"

"To na waye?"

"Na mominsa nadeya ne,kowa ma yanxu haka yake ce mata"

"Tofa sabon salo,keda haihuwa wata da anshe maki suna,toke duk kina me"

"Lalalala ba ruwana karka hadani da ita saida safe"
Ta kashe wayar tana dariya,na kalleta nace "lafiya dai?ko duk zakin kaunarce"

Wurga ido tai tana murmushi tace"ehy harda hakan ma,amma ba wannan yasani dariya ba,"
Ta gaya mata abunda yace naja tsaki kadan nace"uhmm kudai kuka sani kubar sani axancen ku ni kinga saida safe bacci nakeji"

Washe gari dama nida inna muke hada break da rana sumy tayi sanwar rana,inkuma tafita registration dole ni zanyi,muna gama hadawa nakoma daki ina bacci don bai isan ba,asmau daman tana gama wankan safe ida swt hrt suka koma baccinsu,sumy kuma tanata shirin tafiya duk da bakon ma baizo ba.

Sai a round ten yaxo sumy na wanka ni kadai ce lokacin,nayi wanka nashirya sai daukar kamshi na nake riga da wando ne jikina na Pakistan,light purple and light pink sunyi mun kyau ainun,qawar anty mai gyaran kai tabani su kala biyu,dan gyalene sirir nayi dauri dashi na zauna falo ina karya wa,costand ce da cheeps damukai sai Pepe chicken,inaci ina kallo kan dining naji an turo kofar falon da sallama cikin wata murya mai dadin amo,dago idona nai na sauke kan mai shigowar..............tsura masa ido nai kamar yadda shima ya tsura mani nasa,dogone zaikai yaya tsayi daidai daidai,kuma farine ba bakiba amma yaya yafisa haske sosai,fuskarsa ba laifi tana da kyau kallo daya zakai masa kace ya hadu,bakomi zai nuna haduwarsa ba illa iya daukar wanka,ma'ana ya iya dressing sosai bawai ina nufin Yafi yaya ba,aa kowa da inda kyansa yafi fitowa,amma gaskiya babu karya guy din ya hadu sosai............daker na dauke idona kansa ina ansa masa sallamar cikin dan sakin fuska nace"sannu da xuwa ka karaso"

Ayadda na karancesa kamar ya shagala da kallona,har numfashi ya sauke yana sakin sassanyar murmushi,daya bayyana dumful dinsa araina nace wow ya kara masa kyau,takalminsa cover yacire yana shigowa da wata sallamar bakinsa nasake ansa masa,nace ka iso nan ga break nan yana jiranka."

Still murmushin dai yake mun kafin yace "kin bari dai na gaisa da inna kafin kifara danna mun abinci"
Dariya kadan nai nace "to nabari,ina kwana anxo lafiya"
Rausayar dakai yayi yace "lafiya lau,kuma na iso lafiya"

Nace"Haka akeso,"
Natashi don kiran inna yabini da kallo,har ji nake zan kifa da alamun dai idonsa shegen kaifi ne dashi,gashi nan zaisa inje na fadi gabansa............walha ta gama tana karatun qur ani saida takai aya,sannan nace "inna ga bakon nan yaxo kifito ku gaisa"

"Kafin nan aikin bashi kalaci ko"

Nace"yace fa saiya gaidaki kozaici wani abu"
Tace"to muje" tana mikewa nikuma na dauke mata qur'anin naje na ajiye inda take ajiyewa,na linke mata sallayar.
Murmushi tamun tace"Allah ya maki albarka nadeya,ya kawo miji nagari"
Kunya naji saidai nai murmushi ina wucewa gaba tana biyoni baya,break dina na cigaba bayan nace masa gata nan xuwa da kai ya ansani yana sake bina da kallo,daurewa kurin nake don ina iya kwarewa banson yawan kallo har haka,to meyake gani tattare dani haka daya shagala.inajinsa suna gaisawa ashe ma tasansa don naji tana cewa yaushe rabo,shikuma yana kare kansa wai yaya ne ya yadasa na tabe baki kurin na gama na wuce ciki don yima sumy magana.

Kwalliya take yi ashafa nan agoga nan dariya nai nace"dakyau sabon shiga,duk tamiye kwalliyar saikace xaki neman saurayi"
Canza fuska tai tace "amma dai mom muhin kin gama dani,"
Rufe bakina nai nace "tuba nake ana aspe ya sumayya"
Dariyar tayi itama tace"yaushe kika koyo larabci malama"

"Hum zauna nan,ingaya maki yawan kallon series films din dasuke tashar Bollywood yasa wlh kalmomi dayawa nasan su kuma nasan mesuke nufi,nan gaba ma daina magana xan da hausa"
Dariyar mukai tayi har saida inna taleko tace"sababbu sarakan dariya,ke kuma ai kin leko ki gaidashi ko"

Tace"to inna ganinan"
Kaya tasa tafice tana dariyar nikuma na koma kan gado ina duba wani novel mai suna *soyayyar mafarki* labarin yayi masifar tafiya dani,duk da dai na turancine amafarki yarinyar ta hadu dashi,shakuwa da soyayya mai tsananin karfi yashiga tsakaninsu,wanda takejin duk duniya bata iya auren kowa sai wannan guy nacikin baccinta,duk wanda yaxo nemanta da aure zata nuna masa aljanu gareta,kuma duk wanda yace yana sonta xai aureta kashesa aljanin keyi,wannan dalilin kesa duk wanda yazo yake guduwa,duk tana yin hakan ne don kar tayi aure har sai ta hadu da wannan masoyin nata..........(kunji wata sabuwa kuma🤔)
Ina cikin karatun nai nisa sumy tace "mu zamu tai sai Allah yai mana dawowa,"
Nace"to adai maida hankali ga malami banda kallon hadaddun samari"
Dariya tai tace"ba rakiya to"
Dole na sauko daga kan gadon na biyota ina mata tsiya,har bakin motarsa kirar kia mai dan banzan kyau baka na rakota,saida ta shiga na daga mata hannu nace "adawo lafiya"
Jinai yace mun "saina dawo cin abincin dare"
Murmushi nai nace masa 
"Saika dawo kana cin tuwo aiko"
Dariya yayi yana jingina da kujerarsa kamar ba tafiya zasuba yace"abunda nafi komi so kenan,amma dai da za,a samar mun wani abun zaifi"
Nida sumy mukai dariya nace"oh alamun dai baka son tuwon,any way saika xo din byy"
Na daga masu hannu sannan yaja motar suka tai.

*****
Zan iya cewa tun daga wannan rana mahfuz ya maida gidan inna wajen xuwansa,da safe anan zaizo ya karya haka da dare anan zaizo yayi dinner,sai ya zamana ansaba dashi yashiga rankowa saboda iya zama da mutane dayayi,ga barkwanci da tsokana da iya zance saidai zan iya cewa ni kadai yakewa wani salo na daban,wanda na harara naga baya ma inna baya ma asmau,baya ma sumy to why me?tambayar danake wa kaina kenan kai tsaye bazan iya cewa mahfuz sona yake ba,don bai fada ba amma kuma ayyukansa suna nuna hakan,da wayau da dubara da daukar hankali irin nasa har yasamu layina bansan ya akai na bashi ba,kuma yace nice na bashi wayar ya dauka.

Gefe guda kuma har yau bamu shirya da yaya ba,don har nakan dan mance dashi inba yakira wayata ba.ban kuma dauka zan ba matarsa,abunda shikuma take tunxura sa kenan yakejin Ransa yana kara baci,kenan da gaske take bata layinsa waishi yazaiyi da ransa ne wai,duk yadda yake dauakar cireta cikin ransa da sauki abun yaci uban nan,yakasa control din xuciyarsa yau dai yagaza hkr yace ma asmau.
"Ina nadeya kibata wayar"
Batare da tasan fadar maganar zata saka wani abun ba,xuciyarta daya tace masa "yaya bata nan tana falo suna hira da mahfux"
Dammm yaji gabansa ya ansa da karfin Gaske cikin fada dabain yaxo masa ba yace"uban me take wajensa har kamar yanxu"
Jikin asmau tadauki rawa ta kalli agogo karfe tara fa duka,tana kermar baki tace"yaya bafa komi take ba hira suke kuma harda sumy da inna duk afalon"

Dogon tsaki yaja yace"ubanme yazo yi kamar yanxu nan gidan to"

"Yaya abinci fa yake xuwa yana ci,safe da dare"
Dafe kai yayi tabbas inba da wani abuba mahfux badon abinci kadai yake xuwa ba,don gidan da yake zaune anan ba shi kadai bane bale abinci ya gagaresa,yasan sa yasan halinsa sosai akwai wani abu aransa.
Cikin kunan xuciya yace "inta shigo kice ta kirani,sannan kuma gobe insha Allah jirginmu zai sauka karfe goma na safe,agaya ma driver karya bata lokaci yazo da wuri"
Cikin jin dadi da doki tace"to yaya Allah ya iso dakai lafiya mezan hada maka mai dadi"
Baya cikin mood din raha dole ya danne yace"komi kika hadamun zanci saida safe agaida mun muhin"

(Taufa ana wata ga wata🤔,nidai ba ruwana nayi nan🏃🏻‍♀️)

Episode  .33

_wata irin zabura Abba kaseem yayi yatashi ya shakare ogan nasu dake zaune,iyakar karfinsa yafigesa ya gamasa da bango jikake gammm,dan danan yaransa sukayo kansu zasu daki Abba kaseem,yayi saurin dakatar dasu yana dariya kamar sabon mahaukaci,cewa yake "kubarsa,ku kyalesa ya huce haushinsa tukun,"huci kurin Abba yake yasa hannu ya fincike hular daya rufe fuskarsa cikin karaji da xuciya yace"daman nasan kaine,watau kaji ina shirin kulleka gidan kaso shiyasa kace bari kaxo ka karar da ahalinmu ko,wane irin butulune ku wane irin mutane ne ku marasa imani,?bakusan hallaci ba bakusan karamci ba kurasa wadanda zaku saka shiga uku sai mu,shin jafar ka mance sadda aka xo dakai gidanmu?ka mance sadda kuke bara kuna bin titi da roba ga hannunku,kamance ranar da aka maku gata da sittira aka kawoku gidanmu,wane irin arxikine baku samu damu ba,shine da kuka tashi sakayyarku kuka kashe mana iyaye!!ehy nasani kaine kaine ka aikata wannan mummunan aikin,ada na hkr bazan bincike ba amma abunda kukai ma yaya yasa bazan iya hkr ba,watau ku haka taku sakayyar take ko?ciki. Ihu da kururuwa yake maganar ya cigaba da jijjigashi iyakar karfinsa...........mekuke so damu me mukai maku da zafi?.............axuciye jafar yayi jifa da Abba kaseem kasa kansa yana bugar kujera dakarfi,Ammer ta rarrafa ta rungume mijinta tana kuka.cikin kakkausan murya jafar yake magana yace"naji dadi duk da kasan cewar nine,shiyasa naxo na bayyana maku kaina kafin na shafe babinku,nayi alkawarin kominku saiya dawo nawa,natashi da burin mallake duk abunda kuka tara tunda nayi wayau nasan ciwon kaina,nake hassada daku nakejin ina ma ace nine Ku nafara neman hanyar dazan zama mai kudi irinku,saidai bazai yuwu ba inhar kuna raye dukiyarku nakeso kaseeem!!!"yafada har gidan yana ansawa yacigaba da cewa "nayiwa yayanka alkawarin bazan kashe ka ba,kuma bazan saba ba amman kuma cikinku dole sai daya yabar duniya,sannan zabi uku xuwa biyu zan baku,awa guda gareku ku tattaro duk wasu kadarorinku kusaka mun hannu,kokuma daga ku har yayanku na saka maku wuta ku xama toka,............Abba na dake nishi daker yana rike da hannunsa yayi kokarin magana Abba kaseem yana hawaye yace"aa yaya karkace komi,karka yi magana domin bazan taba yin abunda yace ba saidai inhar ya kasheni,"_

_dariya jafar ya babbake da ita kamar wani tabbabe yace"nikuma bazan kashe ba kaseem,amma zan gana maka azaba mafi munin azabobina,ya dora bindiga bisa kan mama yace kadarori kukuma rayuwarta,jikin Abbana karkarwa yake yatashi yana jan kafafu haka ya hau bene,jikinsa rawa yake da faduwa da tashi haka Abba kaseem yake kamasa,suka isa dakinsa,duk wasu takaddunsa na kadarorinsa ya kwaso ya xube masa,hannunsa har baya iya rike biro haka yaringa sa masa hannu yana kuka jini yana diga daga jikinsa,muduka kukan muke pretty tafikowa sautin kuka,Abba kaseem ya ansheta yana lallashi yana kissing dinta cikin soyayya da kauna,ga takaddunsa ya rike tamau,don ko jafar zai dauki wuka ya daba masa bazai saka hannun ba,Abba na yagama saka hannun yace"gasunan nasaka maka kabar rayuwarmu ta huta,kasani inba nan akwai can akwai ranar lahir................jikake dammmmmm sautin bingida yashiga ta goshin Abba har yabulle ta keyarsa,yace"katana da maganarka sai munje lahirar" (wa iya xubillah)nan take mama ta sume kwatanta tashin hankalin da muke abune da bazai fadu ba,agaban idonmu ya kashe Abbanmu ya kashe mamana,ya dawo kan Ammer dasuka mamuke juna ita da Abba kaseem jikinsu kamar anjona shock,dariya jafar yake cikin jin dadi yasaka hannu ya fincike pretty ya jefata saman kujera ta fasa kara,yace ma wani yaronsa ya like bakinta da selitif,abun gwanin ban tausayi kuka sosai muke kamar ranmu zaifira,yasa yaran suka daure Abba kaseem da Ammer daurin da ko anbarka bazaka iya kuncewa ba,yace masu "nasani rabin dukiyarka ta wannan ba indiyarce,tunda baxaku bayar ba zan dauke diyarku mafi soyuwa gareku,daga nan har ranar da zaku saka mun hannu ko kuma ita in reneta har xuwa lokacin da zata girma tayi hankali,ita tasamun amadadinku don haka daga yau nadeya she is my daughter,inkuma kun canza shawara ina saurarenku"_

_girgiza kai Ammer take cikin kunan xuciya kirjinta yana dagawa muryarta ta dishe,tace"rayuwarmu da tata bata da wanj anfani,gara kakasheta itama muma kakashemu amma dukiyarmu ta zama haramiyarka,insha Allahu duk wuyar da zaka gana mana Allah bazai baka nasara akan mu ba,nadeya kuma Allah shine zai shiga lamarinta ka dauka cewar muma mun mace.............dauketa yayi da wani matsiyacin mari har jini yana fitar mata ta hanci da baki,yace"bakusan jafar ba zaku sani yanxu" kudaukesu kusasu mota kafin nan,kurfe kofar falon kar wanda yafita cikin yarannan,kusama gidan wuta_

_Munaji muna gani nida Anty suka fice da su Abba kaseem da Ammer daga gidan suka barmu da gawar Abba da mama,ganin da gaske kuna mu zasuyi don tuni sun faffashe galolin fetur acikin Falon,suka ni daki daki suna tuttulawa anty ta kama hanuna muka durkusa gaban gawarsu Abba muka sunbacesu,muna kuka haka muka tashi tana jana muna gudu muna faduwa,ta wata hanya dake cikin babban falo,muka shiga wani single room ta bude mana tagar dakin tace indira,na dira saigani bayan dakunan mu itama ta diro kwarangar dake jingine da bango,wadda akai aikin dora tanki sati uku da suka wuce itace tace inhau jikina yana karkarwa kar mutanen su kama mu,haka naringa bi ina hawa ina gurjews ban damu ba itama anty tana biye dani,har muka iso wajen katanga.na hango kasan da nisa amma gaka bai saka naji tsoron dirowa ba,nayi shahada na dirko dakarfin tsiya wannan dirkowar saida nasamu gocewar karshi,ita anty harda karya.amma ahaka cikin wannan dare muka ringa gudu nida ita bamusan inda zamu ba,gudu muke muna haki mushiga na sakon mufita saigamu bakin titi..........zubewa mukai gefen wani gini muna haki,ga ciwo hannun anty yana mata azabar ciwo da kafarta,haka nima amma muka rungume juna bamuda mafita,kuka kurin muke idonmu biyu har garin Allah ya waye muka fito bakin titi muka zauna,daka kallemu dole mubaka tausayi mune har rana tafito muna nan bamusan abunyi ba,jiniyar yan sanda mukaji ta tsaya gabanmu yansanda ne sai kuma Alhaji muktar babban aminin Abbanmu,susuka daukemu muka wuce gidansa acan muka ga kawu shazalli shikuma kanen mamane,rungumemu yayi yana kuka sosai muma sai muka hau kukan,yan sanda suka ringa yimana tambayoyi muna basu ansa suka gama duk daukar statements dinmu suka tai._

_atakaice dai rayuwarmu ta dawo gidan Alhaji muktar nida Anty,mutunmen kirkine sosai da sosai shida matarsa hajiya mabaruka,munshiga kewa sosai marar misaltuwa basaina fada ba kowa yasan dole zamu shiga wani irin hali,kulawa babu irin wacce bama samu daga momi matar Alhaji,haka shima dadyn yana iyakar kokarinsa wajen kyautata mana,yayansu uku babban dansu yagirmi anty,masuna khalil sai maibinsa jalil sai karamarsu na girmeta sosai suhaima,haka suka hadamu suka ringa kulawa damu,gefe guda anyi binciken neman jafar babushi babu labarinsa balle asamu kabarin inda yakai su Abba da Ammerh.tun ana nacewa da nema har aka hkr,mun cigaba da karatu tare da yaran gidan ahankali muke dan sakin jiki dasu,yadda suma suke kokarin janmu ajiki,ina gama jss dina kawu shazalli yazo yace zaitafi dani gidansa yacigaba da rikona,dafari da muduka yace amma daddy ya hana,yace don Allah yabarmasa guda mafarin aka bar masa anty,munsha kuka da zamu rabu amma dole ba yadda zamuyi haka muka rabu mafarin kenan inna da kawu suka rikeni wajensu,sune komina har sadda na samu labarin jafar yana kano,nan fa namatsanta akan lallai kano zanje naba kawu labarin abunda nakeson nayi,yace nayi hkr ingama karatuna tukun dole na hkrn._

_anty tana can gidan Dady harta zama kamar yarsa,karatu take sosai na medicen lokacin ni kuma ina aji biyu jamia ina karanta computer,ina gamawa ko shikara biyu banba dady ya samar mun aiki watau *ISSS* means *INTELLIGENT STATE SECURITY SERVICE* ni nace masa inason aikin tsaro wanda da wannan aikinne zan gabatar da burina,don naci alwashi sosai akan J lokacin Anty har ansaka ranarta da babban dan Dady watau khalil,naji dadi ba kadan ba saboda koba komi dai nasan tana wajen da baza tayi kuka ba,lokacinne na matsa akan mukoma kano duk da kaduna akai posting dina,nace hakan ba damuwa bane zanyi yadda zanyi.haka muka tattaro muka dawo kano,nine na samar mana wannan karamin gidan,bakuma don babu halin babba bane aa plan dina nakeson yatai daidai,saboda alokacin dady dakansa ya bani gida kyautar Allah a wata unguwa janbulo (inda aka aje deeya kafin tahowarsu kaduna)haka na fara aikina cike da sa,a ni nake kula dasu inna da kawu da duk dawainiyar su Asmau,saboda lokacin karfinsa ya kare kuma baida cikakkar lafiya,ahankali nafara bincike akan jafar sako da lungu na garin,wanda nasha matukar wuya bayar karama ba.kafin na samu wani suna da ake kira J daula,anan natabbatar da cewar shine ya canza suna,xuwa lokacin ba karamar dukiya yatara ba yazama gawutaccen dan daba ta bangare daban daban,yashiga siyasa karfi dayaji.alokacin ne dady ya sameni yabani diary din Abbana, daya rubuta duk abunda yafaru na rayuwarsu,na zauna na kwafesa acikin nawa diary din,duk abunda yafaru zanzo na rubuta indai yashafi ne yashafi burina,kwatsam aurena da asmau yataso,wanda kawu ne ya hadasa saboda aganinsa asmau tana neman dauko masa abun kunya,kasancewarta mararji duk cikin yaransa Allah yai mata kyan da samari suke bibiyarta,yayinda kuruciya take dibarta afka cikin soyayyar wani tantari daya nemi ya lalata ta, gashi yaron dan masu kudi ya nuna mata soyayyar karya wadda hartaja bata jin maganar iyayenta,mafarin aurena da ita kenan.ahankali nafara kokarin neman labarin J,nasamu kuwa don duk gari inkace ja daula za,ace maka wannan gawutacen dan ta addan,ga kudi ga ta addanci nasamu bayanai sosai anasa dana jewa da oganmu dashi,anan nasan cewar ashe neman wanda za,a dora bisa case din ake,nace ni zan je inhar za,a bani kowa yayi mamaki domin kuwa kowa tsoro ya hansa ansawa zaije,anbani lanbar yabo akan jarumtata,kafin aka bani wata hudu inje infara aiki,lokacinne kuma kawu yaxo ya rasu wannan mutuwar ta tada mun mutuwar su Abba,nayi kuka har kamar babu gobe,don kawu da dady sune nake kalla matsayin dangina iyayena,tunda bazan iya cewa ga inda Abba kaseem yake ba shida ammer,haka nafara aikina wanda nake matukar shan wuya kafin  nasamu natura wasu bayanai na kirki,kwatsam muka hadu da wata mai aikin gidansa tazo tsallaka titi tsotsai yasa na bigeta,mafarin nakaita asibiti akai mata dressing ciwon nace tamun kwatancen gidan su nakaita,tace ai ba nan garin take ba aiki tazo gidan J daula,jin haka yasa dadi ya kamani nayi iyakar kokarina wajen shawo kanta,har dai biyanta nai akan aikin da zatai mun na kawo mun duk rahoton gidan,anan naji cewar pretty tana nan amma angurbata mata tarbiyya,yarinya tazama kamar yar kafirai hankalina ya tashi dana kirga shekarunta naga sun kawo lokacin da J zai iya yin komi akan samun kaddarorin iyayenta,abakin asabe mai aikin naji labarin aurenta yataho zata auri dan minister,jin wannan labarin yafi komi kara tada mun hankali,nakoma gida ina neman shawarar yadda zanyi nasaka afasa wannan auren,raba dare nai ina neman mafita karshe dai na yanke cewar nine zan zama angon,nafara bin hanyar da zan saka afasa auren wajen iyayen yaron jadid nasaka aka bincika mu,uban shine yashirya auren uwar bata so saboda ta raina tarbiyyar yarinyar,tace kuma muddin akai auren babu ita babu jadid,da wannan nai anfani nashiga nafita har samu jadid din yace shima bayayi,saboda nasaka anbuga mun jaridar karya guda daya mai nuni dacewar yan maza biyar aka kama sunyiwa diyar J daula fyade,nasaka aka kaimasa har office dinsa,wannan yasa ya bijirewa uban ranar daurin aure yayin da nikuma nasamu nashiga,nayi addua nayi istahara sosai akan wannan kasadar nabarwa Allah ya idasa mun nufina,saigashi aka ansheni hannu biyu batare sa dogon binceke ba.koda aurena da nadeya ya tabbata har cikin xuciyata banyishi da sunan dawwamemmen aure ba,domin bana da tsarin mata biyu kuma yadda tarbiyar pretty ta lalace bazan iya zama da ita matsayin matata ba,mafarin ina dawowa na rubuta rana da date din dazan saketa,don to me asmau ta isheni.............._

Kallon rubutun nai ansaka *15/1/2018* jinjina kai nai ina lissafa lokacin,nan da wata biyu cif masu xuwa kenan,meye anfanin sai ranar to.cigaba nai da karatawa donjin miye hujjarsa na bari sai sabuwar shekara.............

_dalilina na rubuta date din shine ranar da za,a gabatar da J daula gaban kotu kuma aranar nake fatan tasan komi sannan na sawwake mata,don in nacigana da aurenta zata cutu nima haka,da ace renon Ammer ce ba shakka zansota zan kaunaceta soyayya irin wadda saurayi zaiyiwa budurwa,kauna irin kaunar da miji zaiyiwa matarsa,amma kashhh nadeyar da nake fatan gani ancanza ta angurbata rayuwarta,abunda ya wahalar dani akan case din danake fama dashi shine rashin sanin inda Abba kaseem yake shida Ammer,tsorona daya ace ya kashesu tuntuni cikin yardar Allah na gama tattara duk wasu hujjojina da baya naina akan J da masu take masa baya,hakan yayi daidai da karewar lokacin da aka bani wajen aikina,mafarin nayi insisting dawowarmu kaduna alokacinne natsinci xuciyata acikin wani yanayi,wanda ko kadan ban kawo hakan acikin raina ba,hasali ma bana tunanin faruwar hakan............................_

Tashi nai zaune naringa budo pages din amman babu sauran rubutu ajiki,haushi kamar yayi mani me don naso jin wani irin abune gake damun xuciyarsa?wane yanayine yatsinci xuciyar tasa?dan danan nafara tunanin abubuwan dasuka ringa faruwa lukutan baya,zaro ido nai ina cewa"anya daman yaya ba yana sona ba,abubuwan da yake sunyi kama da haka,amma miye dalilinsa kasa fada mani"jingina nai da gadon ina kallon littafin kamar duk yanxu abubuwan suke faruwa,wasu zafafan hawaye suka silalomun wata guguwar tsanar Jafar daula ta cika kirjina da xuciyata,hakika bantabajin kasurgumun marar imani ba irinsa,koga labari koga film.hakika aduniya zakaga azzalumai kala kala,shin anya suna tuna mutuwa da kwanciyar kabari?anya suna tuna tashin alkiyama da ranar hisabi?lallai da ace suna tuna wa da wadannan abubuwan bazasu ringa aikata ta addaci irin haka ba,kuka riris haka naringayi har inajin numfashina kamar zai fece,jikina ya dauki zafi sosai nakoma na kwanta ina ajiyar xuciya.................kewa da tsananin son ganin iyayena yana sake kamani,yayinda tausayi da bakin cikin rashin iyayen su Anty yana cikani, jinake dazan ga J da idona ayanxu zan iya soka mai wuka kowa ya huta,lumsh idon nai ina sakejin soyayyar yaya tana nin kuwa cikin ruhina.banyi aune ba bacci yasaceni cike da tarin mafarkai kala kala..........

*mungama lbarin abunda ya faru baya,insha allah daga gobe labarin zai dasa har zuwa inda zamu kai*
