LOV U FISABILILLAH💝😊

Episode  .35

Sadda nashigo dakin na samu Asmau tana ta yiwa swt hrt shirin bacci,na karaso dakin na zauna ina ansar pampas din nace "shine ba,a jirana naxo na shirya babyna"
Rungume hannu tai tace "hirar ce taku ta yau naga tana da dogon zango shiyasa"
Murmushi nai nace "kinsan dai ai yadda yah mahfuz yake,duk saida su sumy da inna suka watse suka barmu,daker na korashi ya tafi"

Tace"uhmmm,ni narasa me ke tsakaninki da shi anya ba wata akasa"

"Aa ba akasa ba saidai asama,kinfiye saka ido"

"Uhmmm nidai ba ruwana,yayane yakira yana tambayarki,nace kuna tare da mahfux kinji yadda ya balbaleni da bala,i wai meyake xuwa yanayi."
Nagama saka ma swt hrt kayansa na bacci ina masa rawa nace"toshi ina ruwansa?"

"Kinga nadeya don Allah kidaina haka,abu kadan saifada keda yaya ni narasa meke damunku,yanxu dai yace inkinshigo ki kirasa"

Wani kallo na antaya mata nace "kira?uhmm bacci nakeji"

Tadawo kusa dani tana ajiye phone din kan cinyata tace"don Allah karki ja abunda zai sake hasalashi,tunda yace kikira ki kirasa din miye,nasan fadane da tambayoyi don Allah"

Na kwantar da swt hrt namike ina jawo towel don shiga wanka,ta rike hannuna tace"magana fa nake maki nadeya,koba komi yaya fa gaba yake dake kenan bai isa yace kiyi abu kiyi ba.?"

Cikin kosawa nace "ohhhh wannan mata kincika naci alqu,an hayyyy sakeni to naji zan kira shikenan,fadane inyamun Allah bai sake gan hakorina waje da sunan murmushi ko dariya"

Dariya tayi kurin tana gyara kwanciyarta ita da muhin,nikuma na wuce na shiga wanka,ban waiwayai wayar ba saida na gama duk wasu abubuwa dazan in zan kwanta,addua ce kurin na bari in na gama wayar sainayi.
Sadda nazo na hau makwwncina tuni su muhin sun wuce zazzau don bacci,naja filo na rungume kamar yadda nakeyi,sannan na dora kaina bisa guda nai dialing no dinsa,..............yankewa yayi sannan yakirani daga baya,jinai gabana ya fadi sai yanxu naji shakkar sa ta shigeni,Allah yasani bana son fushin nan dayake ina wahalatuwa,amma naga yaya inba haka nabi dashi ba bazamu daidaita ba.
Daker na jawo muryar dana iya masa magana kamar anshakeni.

"Yaya ina wuni"

Iska yaja kadan har saida naji cikin kaina,kafin yace "nadeya"

Nace cikin muryata data koma yar mitsit "na,am yaya"

"Meyasa kin rainani yanxu?kina ganin kin giga kina niyyar gagarata kome"

Sunburo baki nai nace "to wai menayi yaya?ni Allah yasani ban raina ka bakuma ban gagare ka ba"

"Tun yaushe nace agaya maki kikirani?tabas nasan Asmau ta gaya maki, don ki nuna mani ban isa dake ba saya sai yanxu"

Kamar zan kuka nace"to ai akwai abunda nake ko!kuma dana gama ai gashi na nemeka"

"Rufe min Baki anan,me mahfux yake xuwa wajenki yanayi?"
Ya mutsa fuska nai nace "yah mahfux kuma?"

Kulewa yayi ganin zata raina masa wayau yace"karki kara bata mun rai,nasani nasan inba wajenki ba babu wajen wanda zaizo,nasan sa nasan kowaye shi kifita sabgarsa karna sake jin ance yaxo,koda yake ina hanya gobe zanga ni da idona"

Jinjina kai nayi ina jinjina lamarinsa nace"to miye don yaxo wai yaya,hirace fa sai abinci dayake xuwa ci,kuma bakai ka kawosa ba"

"Nadai gaya maki kikuma fara hada kayanki gidan anty zaki koma tunda zaman anan yasa kin fara tara samari.sai inga inrashin kunyarsa zata sa yabiki acan"

Raina yabaci bana son nai masa masifa amma nakasa danne raina,muryata ta sake sanyi kamar tauba nace"ai daman kai kace nazauna,kuma tunda lokacin zaman nau yakare dole zan tafi,inka sameni gidan ma ai ko"

Dakaji muryar kasan mai ita kuka yake dannewa jikinsa yayi sanyi shirun da yayi yasa na cigaba da cewa.
"Ashekaruna in ba kula saurayi ba wazan kula,ko sokake na dawwama zaune babu mai sona ehy"
Na fada kwalla na xubo mun na rasa dalilin kukan.

Sanyaya murya yayi yace"nifa ba kuka nace kimun ba,inma aure kike so nine zan zaba maki wanda yadace"

"Bana so Indai kaine zaka zaba mun,nima ina da idon xaben kuma yah mahfux ne zabina."

Nafada don nasake kulesa aikuwa ya kulen yace"nadeya nikike fada ma magana haka,zamu gani nidake waye sama da wani.idonki yayi budewar da zakina maida mun magana ko,zanji idan shine yake koya mki rashin kunya.........."yaya......yaya kasaurareni magana nak.......kitttt naji ya yanke wayarsa na runtse idona ina jin yadda xuciyata take duka,wai duk meyayi zafi ne miye aibun yah mahfux inma don muna hira dashine,kokuwa don narabu dashi sai akace karna kula kowa.......... Nai yan kwalla na nagode Allah nai addua na kwanta,amma najima bacci bai daukan ba tunanin nika kala kala sukazo suka rufeni,sai biyu da rabi baccin ya saceni.

*****
Washe gari week end ne sumy da wata diyar mai dorama asmau wanka sukaje part din damuka zauna suka fara gyarawa,nidai sukuku na tashi duk kokarinsu na ganin sun sani walwala 
Amma nakasa sakewa,yau mahfux yana abuja yaje gida sai monday zai dawo,yace mun daya dawo zamuyi wata magana.nasan kuma tatsuniyar gixo bata wuce ta koki,amma bazan saurin tarar numfashinsa ba,asmau sai fara a ake yau miji xai dawo,tun asuba bata xauna ba nitabarwa muhin suka shiga kitchen ita da Inna.

Break na girma da wayewa aka hada masa akayi wanka akaci uban gayu aka zauna falo ana jiran miji,nikam daki na koma na shige cikin bargo,wani abu yake sukata wanda ban tabajinsa ba,nasan kuma bai rasa nasaba da kishi har kuka dai saida nayi,yadda naga asmau na yi shiyasa raina ya sosu,saida naita anbatar haxbunallahu wani imal wakil kanan na samu sauki da sukunun xuciya,inajin sadda motar driver ta tsaya saida kirjina ya ansa ina tuna rabona dashi,ina sake tuna xuwansa na wancen karon,ina sake tuna abubuwan dasuka wakana,bansan sadda kwallah suka xubo mani ba.
Saboda jin danai kaunar yayan tana sake ninkuwa linkin balinkin cikin xuciya ta da ruhina,lamo nai agadon don bana iya fita jinai ma bana son ganinsa baga daya,don abunda nakeji araina ba karami bane.

Part din inna ya sauka cikin shigar suit black din wando da maroon din coat Amman rigar cikin farace,ba karamin daukar sa kayan sukai ba,ya kara kyau fiye da wancen lokacin,fatarsa ta kara zama so smooth gashinsa kamar kaita shafawa karka daina saboda yadda yasha gyara yana daukar ido,yafito sak gashin fulani.
Asmau mutuwar zaune tai idonta kamar zai fado kasa saboda kallo da mamakin yadda yayan yakoma,murmushine dauke mai tsananin kawata fuskarsa yakeyi,inna sai lala marhabun take masa itama cike da jin dadi,sumy tana taya ta ya shigo yana ajiye side bag din Sa yayi ya cire sawu cikinsa,bai tsaya ko ina ba sai kusada asmau ya durkusa yana daukar dansa cike da soyayya,kissing dinsa yake ta ko ina yanajin sonsa yana huda ko ina najikinsa,shikau kamar yasan me akeyi yadanyi dariya irinta jarirai,cikin farin ciki yace "laaaa inna dariya yake wlh,har yafara wayau"
Itama bakinta washe tace"eh ai indai dariya ce da alama kam ya gado uwarsa nadeya"
Sumy tace"yaya ka zauna saika gansa da kyau"
Zaman yayi kusada asmau yana kalonta jitai kamar ta cinyesa don kauna,tace "yaya sannu da xuwa"

Cikin tsokana yace"ke kiba ma kikai da alama inna tana baki kulawa"

Tashi inna tai tace "bari a hado maka kalaci,sumayya taho ki dauko masa nasan bazai hau dining ba"
Sumy tatashi tabita suka cigaba da kallon juna shida matarsa,ga kuma yaronsu gwanin sha,awa rada yai mata yace "amma yau dai xaki koma part dinki ko,ai kin gama wanka hakanan"
Zaro ido tai tana rufe baki da dariya tace"laaaa yaya duka fa satin mu hudu kenan,akwai saura arba,in akeyi"
Ya dage kafada yace"kedai talatin ya isheki,inason jin dumin baby boy dina kusadani"
Cikin gatse tace "saina ringa aiko maka dashi ai kuna kwana"
a maka mata yana dauke kai kurin sumy ce ta shigo dauke da try ta ajiye kasa don tasan saukowa xai,tace "yaya kawosa ka gama sannan"
Girgiza kai yayi yace "aa jeki abunki bai takurani ba tare xamuyi"
Tawuce kurin tabarsu nan asmau tana saving dinsa cikin kauna da soyayya,yaji dadin ganin su sosai ashe haka yayi kewar gida.
Magana yakeson yayi yace ina deeyah amma baison yayin,soyake ya tabbatar tatafin ko tananan,amma baisan ta ya zai tabbatar ba dole ya share ya Saki ciki yana kwasar girki,tana masa hira.

Wanka na tashi nai don daman banyi da safen ba naga agogo har sha daya tayi,on ready kayana ahade suke dauka kurin zanyi,nafito kenan sumy ta shigo cike da jin dadi tace.
"Albishirin Ki"

Bana cikin mood din dazan sake mata har muyi wata dariya ko raha,nadai sakar mata fuska kadan nace mata "goro"

"Ingaya maki yaya ya dawo yana falo,bakiga yadda ya koma ba"

Na jiya baya kurin ina tsane jikina nace "sannunsa da dawowa"
Hakan da taji yasa dole ta kyaleni tashiga wata sabgar,shirina nake cikin kasala da rashin kwarin jiki,nagama shafar na saka powder da lipstick ko kwalli ban saba, daka ga idona kasan dai nai kuka kuma bana cikin walwala,amma duk da haka inka kalleni zaka ga kyauna yafito.
Nagyara gashina na nadesa sosai yayi kamar acuci,less ne nasaka milk da coffee anyi masa dinki ma kyau,riga da siket ne akai sun hau jikina sosai,shaf dina yafito bakuma su matseni ba.
Nai daurin kallabina simple na dauko coffee din gyalena marar nauyi siriri na sagala ga kafadata,na dauki trollyna nafito wayata rike ga hannuna sumy tai saurin biyoni.

"Wai badai tafiya ba nadeya"
Murmushin dole nai mata nace "wlh hankalina yayi gida shiyasa"

Tace"amma aida kin gaya mani dana shirya na rakaki ba dadi kitai ke kadai"

Nace "haba karki wani damu ai zan gane hanya,"
Haka muka karaso falo muna maganar banyi gigin kallonsa ba kamar yadda  kwayar idona keta bani umurni,nikuma nakiya nadaure iyakar iyawa nasaka fara,a kan fuskata nace masa ba tare dana kallai ba.

"Sannu da isowa yaya "

Tunda yaji muryarta kafin ta karaso yaji xuciyarsa tana rawa gabansa yana amsawa,gaba daya jiyai abunda yake taunawa ya wuce batare daya sani ba,shima din bai dago ya kalleta na yace mani.

"Yawwah"

Naji wani iri a kirjina amma na share na maida hankalina kan asmau dake rike da muhin,durkusawa nai nai masa kiss ga goshinsa nace "swt hrt xan tafi saika kawo mani xiyara kaida mominka"

Asmaun tace"wai tunda safe nadeya,baki bari xuwa dare haka"

Na girgixa kai kurin nace "gara natai din da yanxu da daren duk dayane ai,ina inna"

Ta rausayar dakai alamun dai itama bataji dadi ba tace "tana daki"

Namike na nufi dakin xuciyata cike da kuna amma na danne bana son agane.
Zaune take tana duba wani littafin addini na durkusa na gaidata tace"ina xuwa haka kuma"

"Inna gida zan koma daman munyi waya da anti jiya nace xan taho yau,na mance kafin ki kwanta in gaya maki"

Murmushi tai na manya tace"xuwan yayanku yasa xaki gudu ko nadeya?to ina ruwanki dashi nan ma fa gidane nima kamar mahaifiya nake wajenki."

"Aa inna ba haka bane Allah,kawai dai ina son koma war"

"Shikenan nadeya Allah yai albarka ya baki mijin da zaki natsu dashi,mungode kwarai dagaske ki gaida Aysher"

Natashi ina ansa mata da zataji insha Allah.

Nafito sumy na biye dani rike da akwatina har sannan yana nan zaune,nayiwa asmaun sallama tace "insha Allah zamuxo yawo damun yada wanka"

"Sai kunxo byy byy swt hrt zanyi kewarka"

Na wuce xuwa kofar fita inajin kafafuna kamar andaure mun su, ina bude kofar naji muryarsa cikin daurewa da shan mur,yace "dawo kijira na gama na saukeki"

Kamar nanjiyo nace banso ai nasan hanya,amma bakina ya kasa na jiyo nace masa "aa kabarshi Allah,zan samu abun hawa ai safiya ce"

Uffan baisake tanka mun ba asmau tace "kidawo nadeya kijirasa kema kinfi jin dadi tunda baki saba da hawan ta hayar ba."

Hannun kujera na samu na zauna ina kallon hannuna kurin,sumy ta ajiye trolling tace "sai munxo nadeya agaida anty sosai bari naje nai wanka"
Jinjina mata kai kurin nai bance komi na,don dana bude baki kuka zai iya kubcemun,haka naita xaman jiransa har na minty talatin, hirarsa ya cigaba da yi da asmau wadda ina kallo duk ta tsargu,kadan kadan ta dago ta kallan nikam kaina kasa yake raina in yakai dubu ya baci,ko idona ka kalla zaka tabbatar da haka,amma na cigaba da jurewa har saida yaga dama sannan ya yatashi.
Kafin ya fito narigasa fita jinake inama ina da fiffike da saidai kurin suga baninan,hadiyar kukan kurin nake idona yana son tara ruwa.
Motor rufe take saida yaxo ya bude sannan nashiga nasaka akwatin baya,na dawo gaban na zauna don ma karya nemi yamin magana in nazauna bayan.

Tunda muka fara tafiya ko motsi banba,idona yana kan yatsuna kurin ko inuwarsa banson gani,yadda nakejin bacin raina yana sake hawa,ni yaya zai wulakanta nizai ma rashin mutunci,uhmmm indai ce muje xuwa.

Muna cikin tafiyar ne mahfux ya kirani saida kirjina ya ansa,dana ga sunan kuma bana jin zan ki daukar wayarsa,domin nibai mun komi ba cikin karfin hali na daga wayar na kanga ga kunnena,ina kokarin daidaita muryata nace masa.

"Hello yah mhfux ina kwana............
Jinai kurin anfixge wayar najefar ta taga da karfin bala,I wanda dole wannan wayar tatashi aiki,cikin tsananin zabura na jiyo ina kalonsa da idona da suka riga suka ciko da ruwan hawaye,fuskarsa kamar an turo masa mala ikan daukar rai gudu yake kamar zamu tai sama ta daya,hawayena banda xuba babu abunda suke haka mukai parking cikin harabar gidan anty,atsiyace ya taka burkin  har inda nake ina jiyo numfashinsa,jikina har rawa yake saboda kukan dake cina,kasa dannesa nai don xuciyata har ciwo take,na sakesa afili kai daji kasan kuna anjima ana dannesa,bude kofar motar nai zanfita ya cafke hannuna,kin jiyowa nai ina kokowar kwacewa dayan hannun nasa gyalena narufe fuskata,kirjina har daga wa yake saboda kuka don naji ciwo da bakin cikin jefar mun da waya dayayi,bacin bakin cikin daya kunsa mun bai isai ba shine zai karamun wani,ganin yaki sakina raina yana sake hawa na jiyo idona jage jage,muryata yakoma sirirya cikin masifar da bansan ma taxo mun ba nace masa,da tsawa.

"Kasake mun hannu,koshima karya man zakai wallahi bacin kana da girma ga idona da mutunci,wlh yaya da bazan iya control din bakina ba..............kawai saka jefar mun dawaya!don kurin kar nayi magana da abokinka,to tsoron me kake ehy koko rayuwata ce kake son takurawa,bacin wulakantanin dakai gaban matarka duk bai isheka ba yaya"na fada kuka yana kubce mun yadda jikinsa ya ke kerma bai kai nau ba,fuskarsa tayi jaa bakinsa yake motsawa yana son magana yakasa,yaki sakar mun hannuna kuma.
Ina cikin kukan najiyo muryarsa data shake yana cewa cikin kerma..........."ban....bansan na jefar ma.....maki da waya ba,raina ne ya baci kin tunxurani nace kirabu dashi kin kiya,kina gaya mun cewar shikika zaba,ashe har bakinki zai iya furta mun wannan kalmar *PRETTY*"

runtse idona nai inajin sunan yana ratsa kunnena har xuwa xuciyata,amma dayake raina akule nake saiban biyeda hakan ba na bude idona kansa,yadda yake kallona haka nake kallonsa nace masa.

"Meyasa Zan rabu dashi?miye illarsa?inhar yana da aibu bazaka barshi da kanwarka sumayya ba,meyasa kake katangesa dani why"

Kallon idon juna muke yayin da nakeji. Alburusan soyayyarsa suna sukan xuciyata,lumshe su yayi ya bude yana shafa kirjinsa yagirgiza kanshi kadan kafin yakalli wani wajan yace.

"Kedin ce bana son ya rabeki,bashi ba duk sauran maza ma bana so"

Kalaman sai najisu kamar daga sama suke sauka,ahankali nake son zare hannuna yakiya ya cikani jin hakan danai sai naji inason sanin dalili,don xuciyata tafara bani ansa amman zanfi son insani ko inji da kunnena.

"Meyasa haka?saboda baka kaunata,saboda kafison na dawwama gaban antu nibanyi aure ba,saboda kai kariga kayi naka iyalin ka kafa naka family din,ni inmutu ni daya ba uwa babu uba ba da babu miji haka kakeso ko ya.........jin tafin hannunsa nai kan lips dina,idonsa ya sake rinewa,kansa jijiya har tafito bakinsa yadan bushe yace"bana nufin haka,ba don bana kaunarki ba,haka badan kuma inason ki dawwama babu aure ba ko babu family,kifahimceni plssss karki yanke man hukunci bacin ba haka nake nufi dake ba"
Muryarsa har rawa take na kauda kai nace "sakeni natafi tunda baka da lokacin da gaya mun hujjarka,inka hanani kula mahfux don yana abokinka wani zaizo,shima kuma zangani korarsa xakai."
Dakarfi na anshe hannuna na fice ko akwatina ban tsaya dauka ba.

Episode  .36

Acikin kwanakin da mukai da dawowarsa nakullesa kamar yadda ake saka kwado akulle kofa,idan yazo gidan anty inzai shekara zaune falo bana ko kallonsa,in anyi sa,ar ma ina falon don sautari danaji sallamarsa zan bar wajen,anty bata tace komi ba nima bance mata komin ba,haka zaizo ya gaji da zama yayi yan maganganunsa cewar yanxu na rainasa bana ko gaidasa,tace ai munfi kusa ita ba ruwanta.

Vansan ya akai ba rannan saiga yah mahfux yaxo har gidan Anty,nasan yagaji da kirana baya samu yaje gidan inna bai saman ba,kila kwatance zasuyi masa kokuma daman yasan nan din.
Saigashi kuwa suna hira da antyn kamar dai daman sun saba,anan naji ashe sun saba din tafiyarsa wani course yasa yadaina xiyartarta,bayan tabamu waje ne ankawo masa abun sha da na motsa baki,sai kallona yake yana murmushi tun ina hakuri har na gaji nace masa.

"Karfa ka kallo munina yah mahfux"

Sakin dariya yayi mai kyau yace "ai bakida shi,dazan ganshi da tuni na gani kina da kyau zan iya cewa ban taba samun macen da kyanta da natsuwarta ya daukar mun hankali ba irin ki."

Kalamansa sun tona asirin da yake cikin ransa sharewa nai ina miko masa lemu a cup nace"ya abuja dasu momy"

Ansa yayi yana cewa "momy tana gaidaki da sauran yan gida,tace ma in nazo nabaki ku gaisa"

Nazaro idona waje nace"momyn?aa nidai kunya nakeji kace ina gaidata nidai"

Murmushi yayi yace "badai sarakuwa kika dauketa ba?aikuwa indai hakane kece zakiji wahala momy ba ruwanta diya take dake"

Yamutsa fuska nai nace"wai mekake nufi yah mahfux?wace sarakuwar?"

Ajiye cup din yayi yana fuskantata sosai cikin serious face yace "nasan kin gane abunda nake nufi,amma duk da haka xan fada maki."

Jinai duk na susuce bana son jin abunda zaice domin xuciyata ta riga ta rufe,babu wanda zai iya shiga ciki bayan wanda kecikinta,amma bazan iya watsa masa kasa a ido ba zan bashi chance..........sauke ido na nai daga kallon kurillar da yake mun,yace "tun ranar dana fara ganinki lokacin da idona ya sauka akanki,lokacin naji wani duka da shigar abu acikin xuciyata,zan iyace wa naji farat daya sonki da kaunarki ya kamani,kamu kuma ba dan karami ba.naita kokarin ganin kin gane na jawo hankalinki sai naga kamar aikin banza nake,yaudai naji bazan iya shiru na dole zan bayyana maki sirrin xuciyata."

Gaba daya jinake na kuntace akujerar da nake zaune,na kasa daga ido na kallesa ashe koba kason mutun inhar akwai kunya tare dakai bazaka jure irin haka ba,jinake kamar na nitse don kunya.

"Ina fatan ki zamemun fitilar dake haska mun duk wani farin cikina,ina son ki kasance uwar y'ay'ana,domin Im sure zaki basu tarbiyyar da kika samu,wadda nake yabawa da ita,Allah yasa mahfux ya samu matsugunni...............ban kai ga basa ansa ba naji an turo kofar falon,tun kafin idona yakai kansa xuciyata ta ansa,kamshinsa ya wadatar dani sanin ko waye,dan dana sai xuciyata taringa wani irin halbawa.tsoro da shakku suka dabaibayeta,amma na dake ina son aro jarumta na yafa ma kaina,inason na kuresa inason na hasalashi inma akwai abunda yake boyewa akaina dole yafito,na gaji da wananna azabtuwar da yake mana............muryar yah mahfux naji yana ce masa.

"Ahhh mazaje iso mana,yau dai ka kamani banji dadi ba naso ace sai daga baya zakaji labari."

Koban kalli fuskarsa da idonsa ba raina yabani arikice suke,kallonsu wajena tashin hankaline........takusa naji yana isowa cikin falon wajen da muke,ya zauna hannun kujerar da yah mahfuz yake,idonsa akaina amma badani yake  magana ba.

"Yaushe rabonka da nan gidan?Me kake son fada mun?,me wannan yarinyar take anan?"

Dariya yayi yace"tambaya uku at d same time,easy mana mazaje.yau ai gani naxo kuma zan cigaba da xuwa ina bawa fulawata ruwa,ita kuma wannan itace fulawar tawa nan bada jima wa ba zan dauketa daga nan,na maidata gidana ta zama tau,saidai na ringa baku aronta ko princess?"

Jikina tsuma yake kamar na dade bakinsa da saltif,kasa magana nai sai murzar yatsuna nake nakasa sakewa ko kadan,........"ohhhh wai kana nufin zance kazo,kokuma kana neman soyayya?don ni bansan da maganar ba har haka,ba,ai shawara dani ba nimai bayarwar."

"Sorry babban yaya,tuba nake tuntuni nasoyin hakan,amma yanxu ma ban makara ba ka tayani lobbying din ta tabani xuciyarta,koda wajen bazai isan zaman ba,ahankali zan wala wata rana."

Jinai yayan yace "to kekinji mekikace"

Yake na aro nasaka ma fuskata nace "yaya tsakanin mune,kabamu waje kurin bazan iya fada kana nan ba"

Jinjina kai yayi idonsa yakoma red don bakin rikicin dake cinsa cikin xuciya,mikewa yayi yace "agama lafiya bari na shiga ciki"

Musabaha sukai ya wuce ya fita.

"Yawwa inajinki yatafi,hararar wasa nai masa nace ai ba ina nufin daya tai zan baka ansa ba,I need to think for some days"

Cikin sauri yace"tomorrow?"

Nagirgiza kai nace "not tomorrow,just 2 to 3 weeks nan gaba"
Dafe xuciyarsa yayi yace "plssss wlh yamun yawa,"

Nace"duk da can sauran kwanakin daga dauka basuyi yawa ba sai yanxu?nidai inhar dagaske kana sona,kuma  u are ready to marry me kabarsa ahaka"

Rausayar da kai yayi kamar zai kuka yace "oky Na yarda,amma bazan jure rashin jin muryarki ba ya za,ai kenan."

"Phone dina ya lalace in nagyara i will call u"

"No my princess,u can use this one, guda biyune dani kafin sadda takin zata gyaru"

Bana son maganar tai nisa yasa na ansa. babbar wayace kudinta ba kananu ba,sumsung galaxy s12 wayar tayimun kyau sosai na ansa ina yaba kyanta,take away nai masa da snacks din da baici ba nabisa har motarsa dashi,sai jana yake da raha da hira daker nake babiye masa har ya tafi,.........anata kiran magriba lokacin nashigo shikuma zai fita masallaci,da ban kauce ba tsaf yaya zai hankadeni,naja baya ina runtse ido don na dauka harda mari zan samu,haka ya wuce kamar walkiya.

Anty na gamu da ita falo tace "mikikai wa wancen yaxo kamar zai cinyeni danya"
Nace anty "karki barsa don Allah ya shigo dakina,wai don kurin yah mahfux yaxo wajena fah shikenan"

Dariya kurin tayi tace "kunfi kusa ai,nibana ciki in lokacin shigata yayi zan shiga ba yanxu ba"

Kamar zan kuka haka na wuce dakina jikina har rawa yake,na tsorata da yana yinsa.
Asabule na cire rolling dina ina rage kayan jikina,nabar top din da pencil din wandon daman sune nasa sai na dora after sama,na nufi toilet don Alwallah duk jikina ya yi sanyi bansan kuma mezai faru ba,yanayin sa ya firgitani wai meke damun yaya ne,idan abunda nakeji akansa shiyake ji why not da bazai hutar damu ba gaba daya,wannan haukar da fadace fadacen sun isan haka,alwalar nake cikin kasala haka na gama na fito,ina fitowa naji anbanko kofata garammm.............ya rufeta da karfi again jikina ya hau rawa nakoma jikin kofar toilet din na makure,gadan gadan ya nufoni da fuskarnan murtuk,azafafe yake yana isowa ya fincikoni daga jikin kofar yana riko kafaduna yawani irin girgizani,saida naji numfashina zai fece,idonsa kamar na rura wuta bakinsa yabushe,jikinsa har tsuma yake don fitina cikin wata irin murya kamar ba tasa ba yace mun cikin tsawa yana jijjigani........

"Mekika ce masa ehy?dake nake magana watau ni kin daukar sauna sakarai kome?uban me zakici dashi kewai baki ganewa ne?idonki baya gani kokuwa kwalwarki bata aiki."

Na rude sosai kerma kurin nake inason kwacewa don natsorata,kokarin magana nake ya sake katseni cikin tsawar........."ko kina son kicemun duk abunda nake baki sani ba,sokike ki kashe ni ehy?"
Yafixgi hannuna ya dora bisa kirjinsa saitin xuciyarsa hannunsa da nau rawa suke,wani irin duka xuciyarsa keyi, buga wa take da sauri da sauri,na dago idona ina kallonsa daya cika da kwalla shima ni yake kallo,yace cikin sanyin murya jikinsa yana tsuma.

"Kinji yadda take bugawa?kinji halin danake ciki kin fahimta yanxu ko shima baki gane ba,kina son ki karasa gawata?"
Hawayen suka silalo mani babu shiri,na girgiza masa kaina kurin yajawoni ya zaunar kan gefen gado,shikuma ya durkusa gabana yana rike da hannuwana,ya tattaresu waje guda kamar zai rushe da kuka yace"inhar bakison na mutu,inhar bakison nashiga cikin mawuyacin hali kice masa bakya sonsa,kikoresa kice karya sake dawowa,bashi kadai ba sauran maza ma karki basu kofa,ba wajensu bane kice masu akwai meke"
Duk yana maganar ne kamar zai suma numfashinsa yana sama da kasa,sadda kaina nai ina digar da kwallah bakina rawa yake,nama kasa cewa komi mezance masa to?dago habata yayi ya matso sosai yana saka hannu yana goge kwallar,amma wasu suna sake xubowa don kuwa ina kukan ne akan irin halin da nakeciki,shima yake ciki bazai iyacewa nadeya ina sonki ba,nima bazan iya ba amma zai iya sabbatu irin na kauna,na tuna sadda nake cikin halin magagin kaunarsa bana bacci,bana sakewa banda wani sukuni,lokacin da shikuma baisan inayi ba,sai yanxu don kurin wani yaxo mun,har ya rude haka ashe ni ba karamar jaruma bace,ashe bazai iya jure abunda yayi mun inyi masa irin saba...........muryarsa ta tsinke tunanina yace cikin sassanyar murya......"Pretty."
Lumshe idanuna nai kurin ina jin wani abu yana kewayeni,.

"Kiyi hkr na saki kuka bansan duk nai haka ba,bana cikin natsuwata kintayar mun da hankaline."

Shirun dai ita nai kaina yana kasa yana rike da hannuna yaji ya dora mun abu asama,dagowa nai ina kallon menene kwalin wayane sabuwa,na dago na kallai ya sauke ajiyar xuciya kafin yace "na siya maki wata ne,na siya maki sabon layi yana ciki,kiyi hkr please"

Kallon kwalin nai naga Iphone 11 pro ce bakina daker ya iya furta godiya.

"Nagode"

Ganin ya kasa sake cewa komi yasa na yunkura zan tashi yasaurin mikar dani,mamakinsa nake kamar bashi ba na ajiye wayar gefen filona,nace "zanyi salla"

Rungume hannu yayi yana kura mun idansa ban kallesa ba,inata murtsika yatsuna kurin duk naji wajen yayi mana kadan,.

"Kiyi sallar ki"

Ina wasa da hannuna batare dana kallai ba nace masa"katafi to"

Ajiyar xuciya yayi ya iso inda nake ya dago kaina idonsa ya fara washewa ba laifi yanitsa idonsa cikin nau,wanda jinai kamar zan haukace cikin wata murya kamar ya aro wani waje yace mun.

"Pretty"
Dauke kai nai kurin ina jan numfashi don sunan yana shigata yadda bana tunani,.

Iska ya fesa mun jinai kamar zan sume awajen,yace "zantai gida zan dawo gobe bamu gama maganar ba"

Juya baya nai sai lokacin natuna kayan dake jikina,nazaro ido abayata na gefe na rarumota na saka,ina muxurai hanyar fita yayi yana cewa "aikin gama ya gama ai"(🤭)

*******

Kwanaki bayan haka ta faru ban sake ganin yah mahfux ba,bai zo ba kuma bai kirani ba nayi mamakin dalilin hakan,kusan sati kenan gashi koshi yayan bana gani yanxu don sai yayi kwana hudu biyar baixo ba,bana iya dai cewa anty ya akai baixo ba.itama bata cemun komi akai ba,rannan saiga su asmau sunxo mana yawo,naji dadin xuwansu koba komi sun debemun kewa,don anty yanxu bata zaune shirye shiryen dawo da asibitin ta dake kano take,don tace zaryar ta isa haka nan.

Swr hrt yayi kiba ya kara kyau da wayau,kamanninsa sai kara fitowa suke sak dana babansa,sainakara jin kaunar yaron tana shiga raina,ban sani ba ko soyayyar danake ma ubansa ce ta shafesa har haka?
Da niyyar kwana suka xo aikuwa nayi murna sosai,muka raba dare muna hira nai zaton ganin yaya aranar amma still babushi,bana son tambayar wata cikinsu suga kamar na xaku akansa da yawa.,sai da daddaren yakira asmau kasancewar tayi waya anan nakejin wai baya na garin,yaje kano wani bincike ashe shirye shiryen shiga kotu ake,naji hankalina ya tashi komi yana niyyar dawo mun sabo game da iyayena da zalincin da J daula ya mana,nadai taushi xuciyata nai masa addua ubangiji Allah yabamu sa,a shikuma ya karesa ya bashi ikon yin nasara akansu.don ance sharia sabanin hankali,duk yadda kake tunaninta ta wuce nan,barinta mukai taita wayarta inata son inji yace abani amma shiru,har na fitar da rai na cigana da sauraren sumy labarin school da take bani,ina dariya sai xuwa can naji dariyarta tace "eh kajiyota ashe gata nan ai ta kama muhin ta kadaddabe anya ba nan zan bar mata shi ba yaya"

Banji me yace ba nadai ji tace "nidai ba ruwana,aa yaya ni ba wani nan Allah kau,toshikena bari kagani kuma."
Ta kalloni tace "mom muhin ga yaya yace abaki waya"
Na girgiza kai nace "kice masa nayi fushi"

Murmushi tai tace "yaya kaji wai fushi take,oky da yafi kam saida safe ka kula mana da kanka don Allah,Allah yabada sa,a"

Kashe wayar tai tana murmushi na ringa tsokanarta nace" mrs love kenan"
Tayi dariya kurin tana daukar muhin tace "nayi falo nayi kallo"

Nace"kallo karfe goma saura"

"Wani series ne nake bi wlh goma suke farawa minty talatin ne"
Ta zabga karya don azahiri gaskiya bazata iya control din xuciyarta ba muddin ta cigaba da zama in deeya na waya da mijinta,tanajin zafi ainun,kuma bata son abunda zai sake bata sunanta,tayi alkawarin kiyayewa da hkr da kauda kai,tunda taga alamar shiga tsakaninsu shike kawo sabani tsakaninta da shi,gara ta gyara kuskurenta.

Najin jina kai kurin nai sumy tace"jirani nima kinsan ai nima baya wuceni"

Binsu nai da kallo nace "nizaku yaye kubari,nima biyoku zan yanx............wayata dana dade da fara anfani da ita tayi kara,kafin na waiwayo kansu tuni sun fece,na kalli mekiran naga yayane nace "watau ni zaku mayar sauna ko,to me hakan ke nufi?"
Na tambayi kaina, wayar ta tsinke kiran ya sake shigowa.

Na dauka ina sawa ga kunnena tare da yin shiru ina jiran abunda xai ce mun.

"Amincin Allah ya tabbata agareki pretty"

Murmushi nai amma ba mai sauti ba,nace masa "tare dakai"

"Akace kina fushi dani?ai na dauka banda muhimmancin da zaki iya nemana,ban dauka rashin wanxuwa ta zai dameki ba"

Juya ido nai ina cewa "waye yace maka ya dameni?kawai dai naga tsakanin yaya da kanwarsa har yayi tafiya mai nisa bamu da labari"

"Ayyuka sun sha kaina sosai,sannan naga bakida lokacina"

"Yaya kenan,mekaje ka gaya ma yah mahfux?tun ranar da yazo bai sake dawowa ba,sannan ga wayarsa daya bani bai neman ba,karka haka zaifi kyau murabu cikin mutunci"

Cikin jin haushi yace"ohh kina kewarsa ne kome?kinji haushi baizo ba,kuma bai kiraki ba?"

"Niba haka nake nufi ba,karka ja abunda xamuyi rigima yaya"

"Oh ni rigimamme ko?"

Shiru nai kurin don naga da gaske so yake ayin.

"Ni ban gaya masa komi ba,tafiya ce ta same sa ta gaggawa baya kasar,inajin wannan ne dalilin."

Bandai ce komi ba sai ya sake cewa "yaushe zan dawo to inkinyi kewata"

Kamar ince nayi dakai nai kewarka amma sanin halinsa dan danan zai kunbura yasa nace "sadda duk ka gama abunda kake,"

"Ashe kuwa ba yanxu ba."

"meyasa to"

"Saboda case ne gabana deeyah mai hadari,idan ban maida kai ba komi zai iya faruwa,nasan kinsan kan abunda nake magana akan dadynki"

Lumshe idona nai inajin kiyayyar mutumen da gake kira da dadyna idona ya kawo kwalla nace cikin rawar murya.

"Kabar dangantani dashi yaya,inajin duk duniya banda wanda nakejin inason na kashe da hannuna kamarsa"

Shiru yayi kafin ya nisa yace"deeyah"

Daker na ansa masa nace "hmm"

"Kin karanta mun diary ko?"

Daga kai nai sai natuna baya gani nace "kayi hkr yaya xuciyata ce ta kasa jurewa,kullin kwana nake da son sanin ni wacece,har na kai ga yimaka bincike"

"Bakiyi Laifi ba,amma da nafison sadda komi ya kammala kisani,saboda gudun tashin hankalin da zaki shiga"

Kuka nasaka masa ina kwantawa don abun dawo mun yake nace"tabbas nashiga tashin hankali marar iyaka,nayi kuka wanda bana tunanin zan daina har xuwa randa zan hada ido da mahaifana,yaya kunyi jarumta kuma kunyi hkr hakika Allah zai saka maku,ina maka fatan alkairi aduk inda kake Allah yabaka sa,a akansu ya doraka akansu yabaka ikon ganin bayansu."

Shima jiyayi kamar zai kukan ya dai daure yace "kiyi hkr kibar kukan haka,komi yana da lokaci insha komi yaxo karshe."

Cikin kukan nace "Yaya Kar wani abu ya sameka inajin tsoro"

Ajiyar xuciya yayi yace cikin sanyin murya "bakison wani wani abu ya saman?"

Ina shashshekar kukan nace "uhm"

Cikin cool voice dinsa yace mun "oky stop crying."

Kokarin daina kukan nake yace"oya smile"

Dukun kunewa nai guri guda ina makale kafada kamar yana ganina,nace "uhn uhn baya zuwa ko nayi xuciyata ba dadi yaya"

Sake narkar da muryarsa yayi ta sake yin Sanyi da shiga jiki yace "yanxu zatai dadi kinaso tayi?"

Nace"uhm"

"What are u feeling now?"

"I don't know"
Na fada ahankali ina sauke ajiyar xuciya kadan,.

"U know,but you don't tell me,ni bari na maki explain din what I feel,kina sonji.?"

Uhm din dai na sake ce masa don ni kadai nasan ya nakeji din,na nemi kuka narasa sabon abu ya zo,ban san dalilin dayasa duk sadda nake jin muryarsa ba sai inji kamar inyi tsuntsuwa naje inda yake,inason naringa jin ta azahiri ina kallon sa cikin idon,bacin kuma ba iyawar nake ba.
Shiru nai ina saurarensa lamo nai kamar yadda najiyo sautin numfashinsa yana fita ahankali.
Shirun tashi naji tai yawa nace masa "yaya are u sleeping."

"Ina sauraren numfashinki ne"

Runtse ido nai kurin don da alamar yaya yafara susucewa ko ina girman kan da yakeji,bazai iya fallasa abunda yake ransa ba.

"Inajin kamar na taho gida ne,kuma idan naxo ke kurin zan gani na dawo"

"Meyasa ni kadai to"

Kai tsaye naji yace "saboda ke kadai zan gani xuciyata ta samu natsuwa"

Cikin son tabbatar wa nace "nidin?har nakai haka yaya?"

"Kin wuce haka ma,kedaya fa nake dake as my little sister from Abba kaseem,taya bazanji dake ba don ma dai bakijin magana"

Na shagwabe fuska "yaya nice banaji din,komi fa kace nayi inayi"

"Banda abu guda,kin kasa cire mahfux aranki"

"Yaya kowa fa da wajensa acikin xuciyata,kai fa babban yayana ne wanda nake matukar ji dashi,shikuma saurayi nane wanda yake cikin kila wa kala,ko cikin xuciyarma bai idasa shigaba tukun"

"Yaushe zai idasa shiga ciki to?"

Murmushi nai jin muryarsa cike da kishi nace "Allah masani."

"Saida safe pretty naji kamar ana taba get din gidannan"

Cikin rudewa nace "get kuma?babu maigadi ne yaya,kaga karka je karka leka don Allah."

"Kinatsu babu fa wani abu,wani ne danasa axo mun dashi"

Ajiyar xuciya nai nace "wash bakaji yadda xuciyata ta harba ba "

"Karki bari ta sake harbawa inhar bada sun..........kin idasa wa yayi yana sauke ajiyar xuciya yace"ki kwanta ki sunbatar mun muhin ama dadina."

Numfashi naja nace "kayi mun rowar magana nima zan rama,kuma bazan sunbatar maka muhin din ba"

Dariya yayi cikin nishadi yace "nasan zakiyi ai,koxuwa safe ne amma ga kuncinsa zakiyi yadda nima zan dora nau idan nadawo"

Saurin sauke wayar nai na kashe ina ajiyar xuciya,hamma nai na tashi naje nayo alwallar bacci nayi shirina na yi addua kenan saiga su sun shigo,yinai kamar ina bacci don nasan sai sunjani da magana,cikin kan kanin lokaci baccin kuma ua figeni.

******

Kwanan su asmau biyu suka koma gidan inna suka barni da kewarsu,gefe guda kuma mahfux ya dawo yaxo har gida munsha fira,na bashi wayarsa nace ai nasai sabuwa,tsaraba ya kawo mun tuli guda na kayan sawa na mata masu tsananin kyau,kasa kalonsa nai mace masa yadaina xuwa wajena,haka na kasa ce masa bana jin sonsa saboda karamcinsa gareni,ban nuna ma yaya muna tare da shi ba,don karna tsokano ma kaina bana yarda ko fira muke awaya na sanyo masa sunansa,sosai muke hira da raha kalamai masu saka natsuwa ahankali yake sanyo munsu,bakina yana nauyi inkasa maida masa don naga wayau ne dashi,soyake yajani nafadi abunda ban shirya ba,shiyasa nake nokewa na nuna babu komi araina.

Shirye shiryen shiga kara ake amma har yanxu bai dawo daga kanon ba,yace ma sai ana saura sati ashiga kotun zai dawo,ina kewarsa sosai kamar yadda yake fada cewar kamar yayi tsun tsuwa yaxo inda nake,yayita rokona akan zaikirani video call in hana don duk sanda muke wayar kayan baccine,kuma ni bana rufa duk sanyi.
Ahaka muke yaki yafurta sai wayau da zagaye zagaye,nima saina nuna masa na iya wayan,inta xuba masa shagwaba son raina,tun yana biye mun har idan yaji zan kaisa makura saidai inji ya yanke wayar,inyi dariya kurin ina jinjina taurin kansa.
Gefe guda yah mahfux ya matsa lamba akan yagaju da rashin samun ansarsa,kullin saidai ince yakara mani lokaci rannan har qanwarsa ya kawo mani wai ta shawo kaina,na rasa yaxanyi dole na samu anty da maganar don samun mafita..........

Kuyi hkr jiya kunjini shiru wlh sai ahankali ngd da uxirin da kuke mun.👏🏻

Episode  .37

"Uhmmm sai yanxu da ta kwabe maki kikeson nashiga maganar,ai da barinki nai wayanki ya fiddaki"
Shagwabe fuska nai nace "haba Anty kefa babbace ba,asan babba da tsogumi ba"
Rike baki tai tana kallona tace "au don gidanku tsogumi ma nake?kinje kun jone da khalipa shiyasa muke ganin fara,arki agidannan,kadan kadan waya kina narkewa shine kikeson ingaya maki yadda zaki kori wancen ko?"

Yi nai kamar zan mata kuka nace"anty nifa babu komi tsakanina da yaya,kurin hira muke kuma ni har ga Allah bana jin ina son yah mahfux,amma karamcinsa gareni yasa bana iya gaya masa,gashi ya matsanta mun wlh inajin nauyinsa ki taimaka mun antyna ta kaina."

Murmushi tayi tace "kedai khalipa ya tsara maki abunda zakiyi shine zakizo kina wani kalailameni"

Kwantawa nai bisa cinyarta ina shagwabata "don Allah Anty karki mun haka,plsss yanxu haka text yamun cewa yau bazai tafiba inyaxo sai yaji matsayinsa"

"Shikenan in yazo din munyi magana dashi,"

Cikin jin dadi na rungumeta nace"nagode masoyayita Anty,banda kamarki wlh,amma anty karya yasan cewar nina saki pls"
Dungure mun kai tayi tana mikewa tace"dayake ni mahaukaciya ce ba,wadda bata iya magana ba."

Dariya nai ina cewa "tuba nake nasan zakiyi koma miye don farin cikina,Allah yakara nisan kwana yabarki da Abban su sharifa har duniya ta nade"
Murmushi take ta girgiza kai ta wuce kurin.


Karfe takwas sai gashi yazo sai nakejin kuma duk babu dadi wlh,amma da naringa jamasa rai ina masa wasa da hankali gara ayita takare,amma har raina inajin babu dadi.............wani tunani ya fado mani ina adduar Allah yasa hakan ta kasance,nashirya tsaf kamar yadda nake masa in zaizo aka kaimasa abun tabawa,sharifa ta zauna dashi tana ta masa uban surutu kafin nafito.
Bari nai saida suka gama magana da anty bayan ta dawo sannan nanufi falo,cikin rashin kwarin guiwa inajin nauyinsa sosai.

Kaina kasa inata sunkuyar dashi na kasa kallonsa sosai na gaidasa yace "bazan ansa ba fushi nake tunda ko kallona ba kyayi"

Na dago ina kallonsa nace"tuba nake yah na gashi na kalleka"
Tsura mun ido yayi wanda bai taba haka ba,muryarsa tayi rauni yace "meyasa haka nadeya?ni ashe baki daukeni tamkar yayanki ba meyasa zaki boye mun abu mai muhimmanci irin wannan" 
Kallonsa nake da son karin bayani don ban gane meyake nufi ba,ganin bai cigaba ba yasa nace masa.

"Kayi hkr yah mahfux ba haka bane,yadda baka tunani wlh ka wuce nan wajena."

Ajiyar xuciya yayi yace"nasani karki damu,anty take gaya mun duk halin da ake ciki,amma har xuciyata ke din dai kika mani kece wadda nake tsara rayuwata ta cigaba dake matsayin matata,amma dole zan janye badan inaso ba.ita kuma sumayya zan duba lamarin xuwa sadda zan gama jiyyar xuciyata,kiyi hkr nadeya ubangiji Allah ya bayyana mahaifanki yasa ka maku akan zalincin da akai maku"

Gaba daya kaina ya kulle wai me anty ta ce masa ne har haka,na sadda kai ina wasa da yatsuna kurin nace masa "ameen yah mahfux nagode daka fahimceni,Allah yabaka wadda zaka natsu da ita fiye dani,ka dauka kila nidin ba nice matarka ba."
Jinjina kai yayi yace "hakane nadeya nagode sosai,Allah ya tabbaatar da alkairi tsakaninku da yayanki"
Ya mike kurin yana ajiye mun wata leda gabana yace"zan wuce zamu ringa gaisawa lokaci xuwa lokaci."

Daker na iya rakasa bakin kofa dani dashi duk babu dadi.
Haka na jiyo cikin gida na wuce naje na ajiye ledar saman mirrow nai alwalla nai salar isha'i,sannan na fito falon don ino son nayiwa antu tambayoyi meta gaya ma mahfux haka.

Sharifa na tadda falon tana kallon cartoon,da yake ita kadaice ashraf da yayansu da yaxo hutu suna can gidan kakansu,gidan su abbansu kenan.

Nace mata "an sharifa ta ina anty"

"Yawwa anty deeya dama jira nake kixo, bakiga chocolate din da uncle mahfu ya kawo mani ba,don Allah anty kizama matarsa yadda kullin kullin ina gidanku ina shan chocolate"
Duk yadda nake cikin rashin walwala saida nai dariya naja mata kunne nace 

"Ke nan kinsan kalmar mata ko?"
Dariya tayi tace "eh mana anty ashraf ma ya sani"

"Yanxu ba tambayarki nai ina anty ba,ki ka hau bani wani labarin na daban"

"Tana sama yanxu zata sauko,don Allah Anty deeya ki zama matarsa kinji"
Ta fada da fuskar tausayi shan mur nai nace "karna sake jin wannan maganar bakinki,maza cigaba da kalonki."
Ta tabe fuska ta haye kujera tana satar kallona.

Antyn na sakkowa na hau yimata tambayoyi............"kedai ba kinsani aiki ba,kuma nayi karki isan tunda dai bukatarki ta biya."

"Anty niban fahimci meyake nufi ba,atleast kinmun bayani"

"Na gaya masa in breif halin da muke ciki,sannan nace Akwai alkawarin aurenki da khalipa,amma sai angama zaman kotu in Allah ya bayyana iyayenki,sannan na gaya masa  sumy na sonsa ne,amma yadda  bazai ga rashin kunyarta ba tunda ba itace ta nuna masa ba,in bacin ma inna uwace mai maida kai ga sanin halin da danta yake ciki,aida bawanda zaisan komi bata nuna ma kowa ba innarce take gaya mun,ko akwai yadda zan mata nasiha afakaice shiyasa ta barsu suka kwana rannan,to nadai yi abunda zanyi sai kuma da kika zo mun da ita,yasa naga yadace na shigar da ita in Allah yasa aka dace saikiga sun daidaita,balle ina yaba wa da hakayenta tafi asmau dadin zama da kuma hankali."

Cikin jin dadi nace" aikuwa kin kyauta anty,zan taya ta addua Allah ya daidaita su anty ke ta musammance wlh."

"Uhm saiki shirya koma wa khalipan duk da naga ashirye kike kobabu sadaki"

Kwabe fuska nai nace "haba Anty yakike mun haka wai don Allah"

"Abun ne naki yafara wuce gona da iri,waya sani ko har kin gaya masa ma ciwon son sa yakusan kayar dake"

Shiru nai kurin don maganar ta bata mun rai,na tashi kurin na nufi dakina.

Maganarta najiyo tana cewa"fushi kikai wai,ni nafada ne kurin don ki rage zalkinsa shi namiji ba,a masa haka,kija ajinki yadda zai gane cewar kedin maitsada ce,ni ba hanaki ki sake dashi nai ba."

Najiyo nace "amma anty saikice mun haka fisabilillah,"

"To yi hkr yan matan khalipa karki shigar da kara kaina"

Bansan nayi murmushi ba,na juya kurin na wuce dakina.

******

Kwanaki suna ta turawa karshe tun muna waya da yaya sau uku arana muka koma biyu,muka dawo daya.yanxu saina kwana uku bamuyi magana ba,saboda ayyuka sun masa yawa don yakusa dawowa da yadawo za,a gabatar da sharia,ya samu lauyoyi har guda biyu manya masu hazaka da kwarewa akan aikinsu, yace ma zaizo da guda har gida zaimun tambayoyi akan yadda zamana ya kasance agidan dady,da kuma abunda nasani akan boyayyar dabi,arsa ta ta'addanci.

Ranar da anty ta gama kammala dawowar asibitin ta na kano nan kaduna,aranar shima yace yana hanya nadai yi irin abunnan kawaici,na nuna bandamu da xuwansa ba,banyi yunkurin hada masa koda sobo ba,nasaka ido ko anty zata mun magana.
Najira tayi maganar har nagaji zan shiga kitchen din kenan,ta sameni tace mun.

"Ai nadauka ma saidai na tsinceki kitchen kinhau aiki"

Girgiza kai kurin nai nace "anty kenan,nida ba matarsa ba zalki name, bacin nasan banan zai sauka ba,asmau nacan na hada masa gara"

Nina na fadi maganar amma xuciyata wani kuna take mun,saboda kishi.

"ashe dai kina da hankali,amma duk da haka dai kitashi ki hada masa koda pepe chicken ne da dan juice."

"Anty Ki kyalesa sadda zaizo nan ma fa cikinsa acike"

"Abunda zaici can daban wanda zaixo nan yaci daban,dan danon ba daya ba karfa kishi ya rufe maki ido"

Kamar tasan abunda yake cina kenan nace"aa bazai rufe ba anty zanzo na dora."

Tajuya tafita lamo nai agado ina imagining yadda zai samu tarba wajen matarsa,to ni kona masa ma ai ba burgesa zan ba.
Amma bazan ki abunda antyn tace ba natashi na fito na shiga kitchen din,on ready akwai kaji cikin frig na fiddi guda biyu manya nafara aikinsu.
Kafin na tattaro kayan da zan hada juice din dasu samha taje gida bata dawo ba,sharifa ce take tayani da firarta marar karewa.

"Anty naga duk sadda uncle khalipa zaizo kece kike hada masa kayan dadi,to meyasa haka anty"

Dariya nai nace mata"saboda nice karama,ai gani dai basai anty ta yimasa sanwa ba ko sharifa"

Tace"nasani ai anty,amma to kullin mai dadi kike masa ina shine zaizo ba baki yi mana sanwa mai dadi"
Ina dariya nace "keeee sharifa banda sheri,kenan wurin wai har kike fadar haka bakin ki yayi zaki dayawa gaskiya."
Itama dariyar take tace"yo anty ai ina shan chocolate da yawa shiyasa,kinga idan naje gidan daddy (baban su Abbansu kenan) uncle jalil yafita damu shopping saina fi su yaya sudes da ashraf cika leda,shiyasa kikaji bakina tsaki."

Dariya nake sosai ahaka muketa aikin da ita abani labarin wancen abani na wancen,karshe sai akadawo na school kuma da hirarta ta debemun kewar kuncin danake ciki,har na gama aikina na dora abincin dare faten dan kalin turawane yasha kifi da kayan hadi,sharifa cewa take "gaskiya anty kin iya sanwa,kinfi ummi iya abinci mai dadi kidan mani kadan don Allah"
Dariya nayi sosai nace saina gaya mata yarinya,ta kama kunnenta tace "kiyi hkr kartaji tace bata sake yimana,kawai dai ke naki na yan gayune wlh"
Sharifa badai shirme ba duk abunka inta yanko wata maganar saika dara.............

Yau malamin mu dake dora mana karatu yaxo,yadawo daga tafiyar da yayi ummarah.
Naje na dauki karatuna  duk da hankalina ba sosai yake kai ba,amma na daure na bada hankalina,xuwa karfe takwas na dare yatafi wanda xuwa lokacin yaya tuni ya tsufa cikin kaduna,don naji anty tana waya dashi wajen karfe shidda na yamma.
Inajin sunyi wayar na jawo tawa nakashe,duk raina ya jagule ban tabajin irin abunda nakeji ba.
Gani nake nafi kowa bukatar ganinsa,amma ko awaya ya kirani bai kira ba tun uku da suka wuce,labarin xuwansa ma bakin antyn naji yau.

Ko zaman falon banba nai zamana dakina sai kirkiro aiki nake don ya kawar mun da tunaninsa,amma abun ya citura kuma ban fasa abun danake ba,nice wankin toilet bacin na wanke da safe,na gama naxo na gyara gado bai komi ba na hau canza bed shit.
Na dauko tsintsiya na share na goge,sai tsaki nake nikadai na gama goge gogen na jawo wadrof na bude na xubo da kayan kasa da masu gugar da marasa gugar na hade su waje guda,sai ajiyar xuciya nake na zauna dirshen,ina gyaransu raina jagule............

Bai xo gidan ba sai karfe tara daidai lokacin su ashraf sun dawo suna ta kallo da wasansu falo,yayansu kuma yana danna computer dinsa,oyoyo sukai masa yaringa dagasu daya bayan daya.
Sharifa tace"uncle meyasa ka dade baka zo ba,ummi tayi fada anty deeya tayi fushi bata ci abinci ba,kasan me ma tayi maka wlh yau zaka sha dadinka...........kirufe mana baki akun banza,saidan banzan surutu ba a tambayeki ba."

Sudes ne yakatseta ta sauko tana kunbura baki khalipan ya dorata kan cinyarsa yace"sorry my angel,karki kuka rabu da yaya sudes baza,a basa tsaraba ba"
Ta washe baki yace"ina ummin da Anty deeya din suke"

Ashraf yace "gama ummin nan ai,anty tana dakinta"

"Sai yanxu kenan muna shirin rufe gida"

Yace"wlh inna ta rikeni dasu Asmau naso fitowa kafin yanxu,ina wuni anty ya kuke ya kwana da yawa."

"Uhmm muna lafiya babu abunda ya samemu kam,ya aikin da shirye shirye"

"To da godiya abubuwa sunso rincabewa amma karfin addua da rokon Allah yasa suka ringa xuwa cikin sauki,yanxu angama tana dar duk wasu evidence da hujjoji akan J,insha Allah sai ya wulakanta cikin dubun jama,a da ixnin Allah mune sa nasara zaman farko nasan bazai yuwu agama ba,amma insha Allah zama na biyu komi ya kare,sonake kafin koma warmu spain anyi solving din duk wasu matsaloli"

Tace"to Allah ya amunce yasa adace,Allah ya bayyana su ammer jikina sanyi yake idan na kirga shekarun da sukai tare da wannan azzalumin,sai inga kamar ya kashesu ma kurin."

"Allah kadai yasani saishi da Abbas don haka akotu zasuyi bayanin inda suke,ina pretty na"

Harararsa tayi tace"taka kuma yaushe?kasan da itan baka zoba tun daxu sai yanxu to tayi bacci"

Shafa kansa yayi yana sassauta murya kar yara suji yace "anty karkiyi haka,wlh tana raina magana muke da wani abokina da muka taho dashi daga can,saidai naga driver yayi parking gidan inna,naso nafara sauka ko ganinta nai"

Tadaga kafada "saika koma ka dawo gobe"

"Anty please bana iya bacci wlh inban sata idona ba,kinsan kuwa yadda nakeji"

Zaro ido tayi tace"yaushe ka zama marar kunya khalipa"
Dariya yayi yace"wlh nima ban sani ba amma jinake ko gaban waye zan fadi fiye da haka,kibarni na ganta"

"Allah kuwa fushi tayi,ba lallai ka sameta ba kila ta rufe kofarta,saboda xuwanka nasata tashirya maka abinci gashinan zai lalace"

Saurin tashi yayi yana nufar dining din yace"ai shiyasa lemu kurin nasha agidan inna,nasan zan samu something special daga gareku,nidai kiban ixini naje gareta zata huce nina gaya maki."

Tace"ga hanya nan ai,amma kayi sauri zamu rufe gida."

"Ai akwai dakuna da yawa meyayi zafi,don basan yashe zan fito ba in nashiga na ganta"

Yaran tayi wa magana suje suyi shirin bacci hakanan,sannan tace masa "banda dai wuce gona da iri,don baka bamu sadaki ba"
Girgiza kai kurin yayi yana bin hanyar dakin deeyah.

Na gama gyara kayan kenan naji anyi knocking kofata,tunani na anty ce yasa ban tambayi ko waye ba naje na bude na koma inda nake tsaye ina jera kayan,shirun danaji da kuma mayen kamshinsa shine yasani daskarewa inda nake,naki juyawa amma na kame hannuna rike da wasu kaya set guda zan saka acikin durowar,lumshe idona nai inajin suna cika da kwallah,bansan kayan sun xube kasa ba takunsa naji yana nufoni,yasa naiyi hanyar toilet dina don banshirya fuskantar sa haka ba.to kukan me nake kuma,kishi nake don yafara sauka wajen matarsa?matar sace ni kuma fa? bani da wani matsayi da zaisa yabar iyalinsa ni yaxo gareni,nasan haka amma xuciyata takasa hucewa kafin na karasa naji ya cafke mun hannu,kafin naji yajawoni da karfinsa wanda har saida na shige cikin kirjinsa,yabi da hannunsa ya rungumeni,kamar zai fasa kirjinsa yasakani aciki.

Xuciyata nake jin kamar zata fito waje saboda tsabar dukan da takeyi,numfashina ya hade da kamshin jikinsa yake sake haifar mun da wani sabon abu na daban,idona na lumshe sauran hawayen suna silalowa kan kuncina,dago fuskata yayi yana kokarin ganin idona na kullesa nikuma na hana,ya saka gefen kuncinsa yana goge kwallar da ta xu bo mani,muryarsa tayi sanyi takoma wata kala yace mun cikin kunnena.

"Why are crying pretty?gaya min wanda ya mun kyakkyawata kuka."
Juya masa baya nai inajin xuciyata tana amsawa,duk bugu daya jina ke kamar sonsa zaia keta xuciyata yafito waje.
Share fuskar nai na durkusa ina daukar kayana zan maida cikin durowar............ta bayana ya dawo ya sanyo hannuwansa ya rike nawa,yasake ce mani.
"I'm sorry na gano nine nasaka wadannan idanuwan masu haske kuka,im so sorry juyo kimun duk abunda kike ganin zaki huce zan jure."

Wani sanyi da sabon jin dadi yake hudani,amma na daure na dauke kaina na janye daga garesa na cigaba da saka kayan,van ce masa komi ba.
Ganin abunda nake bama tashi nake ba yasa yaxo ya rude wadrop din ya jingina da ita yana fuskantata,sai dambe nake da idona don karma na kallesa ya matsoni yana leko fuskata.

"Oh may god,please look at me pretty,look in to my eyes nasan dakin kalli idona zaki huce,zakiyi ma yayanki  uxiri ko?"

Najuya na koma bakin gadona na zauna,sai lokacin nai magana ba tare dana kallesa ba ina wasa da yan yatsuna kurin.

"Uxiri ai dole inmaka tunda wajen matarka kaje,to miye nawa aciki.?"

Gabana yazo ya zauna kasan kafet kamar wani dan yaro ya kama kunnensa yace"na tuba,inhar baki daukesa laifi ba ki kaleni irin kallon da zanji sanyi,da zafina nashigo gidannan ana cemun fushi kike naji duk guiwata tayi sanyi."

"Ni fushin me zanyi,mood dina ne haka bana jin dadi"

"Kardai kishi kikeyi?wayo Allah don Allah kice shine kike"
Yadda yayi maganar yasa dole na dago idona ina kallonsa,kyau naga ya kara mani kasa dauke idona  nayi,ya natsu shima yana kalon idona daker na janye nace ahankali.

"Saboda me zanyi kishi?nida ba matarka ba,ba budurwarka ba babu wani abu da zaisa naji ina kishi da matarka........akwai mana saidai kikifada saboda kinajin tsoro."

Nasha mur nace "miyeshi?"

"So,soyayyata ce zata saka kiji kina kishi da matata."

Na juya inda bai ganin fuskata nace"ninake son naka?ba gaskiya ba ne ni bana son......ban...san...k..........hannunsa naji saman lips dina ya jiyo da fuskata muna kallon juna,ya lumshe idonsa girar sa kamar irin tawa zara zara,ya bude yana kallona cikin wata salon murya yace"karkiyi karya,don bata dace dake ba bakinki gaskiya ya iya fada."

"Haka idanunki abunda suke nuna wa daban da wanda bakinki yake fada,ki taimaki xuciyarki karki cutar da ita."

Na sake mikewa nace "kaje falo anty ta hada maka abinci"

"Kin hada mani dai,sharifa ta tonaki tace kinmun abun dadi muje kibani."

"Bacci nakeji kwanciya xanyi"

"Inbake kusa bazan iyaci ba"

"Shikenan kahuta,daman bakada yunwa. tunda daman ai kaciwo can wajen matarka"

Dafe kai yayi yace "wannan matar tawa tabani yau,don Allah kihuce haka nan kinsan Allah banci komiba acan,naki nake jira"

Na dauke kai nace "gashi can ai saika je kaci."

Na haye gadona naja bargo na rufe har fuskata.

Jinai yace "washhhh Allah na cikina"
Azabure na tashi na sakko ganinsa nai yarike ciki ya duke,dan danan hankalina ya tashi nakasa tabashi,naduka kusada shi nace "yaya lafiya,meyafaru kaji?magana nake don Allah meya sameka."
Muryata har ta canza zanyi kuka ya dago yana dariya yace "kece ai,nace kixo muje yunwa nakeji kinki tashi"

Harararsa nai ina goge kwallata yaja hannuna muka mike muka fito dakin,har dining ya kaimu yaja kujera na zauna shima ya zauna,na fara xuba masa juice din sannan na xuba masa faten dankalin.
Na sa mashi pepe chicken din lumshe ido yayi yace "wai ina kika koyo sanwa,nidai nasan deyar dana sani bata iya ko dora tukunya ba"

Namasa wani kallo yace "yi hkr karkisa xuciyata tafito waje"

Yafara cin faten yana mun surutu,sai yaba wa yake  saida yacinye plet guda,yaja naman shima yaci sosai juice dinma saida yakusa rabin jug din dake gabansa,na jinjina yunwar da yakeji.

Yasauke ajiyar xuciya yana kashe mun ido "kinyi kokari Allah yabiya."
Na jinjina kai kurin yace "sonake natai banso natai kuma"

"Katafi dare yayi fa Abba fada zai"

"Wa zaima fadan badai niba,inya maki kuma zai sani"

Na dalla masa harara nace"gidansa kaxo fa"

"Nima inda gidan ai"

Nace"saika tafi karka sake xuwa tunda kana da gida"

"Nikike kora saina yi yajin aiki kikama kuka kuma ko"

Na sunburi baki "nibana wani kuka"

Tashi yayi yana miko mun hannunsa "xomuje kirakani to"

Ban musaba na mike amma ban bashi hannun ba na bisa baya,su anty duk sun kwanta nasan maigadi shiyake jira ya rufe get.

Bakin motarsa ya tsaya bayan ya bude ya dauko wani abu ya miko hannunsa yace "muga hannunki"
Na boyesa abaya ina makale kafada nace "meyayi kuma hannun nau"

Ya na murmushi yace "cinyesa zanyi naga ya iya sanwa"

Murmushin nai ban shirya ba yace"sai yanxu naji nasamu sauki,amma da kin hade fuska kin hana naga dariyarki"

Bance masa komi ba naji ya jawo hannun nau nabisa da kallo dayake akwai haske harabar wajen sosai,naga ya fiddo wani xobe sai daukar ido yake yasaka mun,kafin yakai hannun bakinsa ba shiri naji yamun kisses har biyu ga hannun,hada ido mukai ya lumshe nashi ya bude yana sake mun wani kalar kallon.kafin yace "kamar na gudu dake nakeji,ga tukuicin girki nan"
Na anshe hanun ina kallon xoben tabbas xinari ne,yayi masifar yima hannun kyau fuskata ta washe da fara,a nace "yaya nagode yayi kyau sosai"

"Nima nagode da nasamu tarba mai kyau"

Nace"ba wani nan"

"Dagake nake,da dai ace natai bakimun murmushi ba kila bazan bacci ba yau"

Na tabe baki nace "ntafi gida kagaida mun swt hrt"

"Nikenan"

N zaro idona waje "nace kai kuma,aa ni da muhin dina nake kai ai yayane"

"To yaushe zan zama swt hrt din"

Na girgiza kai kurin na fara tafiya kadan inajin idonsa akaina murmushi nake bansan kona miye ba,jinai yace "i like it"

Najiyo nace "mekenan"

Yace "tafiyar ki,kamar kina tafiya da hasken wajen nan"

Najuya kurin na shige nabarsa najima kafin najiyo fitarsa gidan,wannan rana najima ina juyi nidaya agado farin ciki yake lillibeni,naita bitar abunda ya wakana har barawo bacci yaxo ya saceni.

Episode  .38

       *Agurguje*

Lokacin gabatar da kara yayi don jibine ake shigar farko,yaya yazama busy sosai shiyasa saidai muyi waya itama yan second ne mun gama,gaba dayanmu hankalin mu yayi kan shariar don bamu san ya zata kasance ba,amma muna fatan samun nasara da samun galaba akansu.

Yayan ya kawo mani lawyer dazai tsaya akan case din mu shida mataimakinsa,sunyi mun tambayoyi da yawa kuma duk nabasu ansa gamsassa dazata gamsar dasu,xuwa wannan lokacin duk bama da natsuwa dagani har anty,su kansu su inna sun cikin xullumi da damuwar ya zata kaya.

Zuwa lokacin nasamu labarin xuwan mahfux wajen sumayya bakin asmau,abun yamun dadi sosai har kiransa nai muka gaisa nake sake nuna masa jin dadina akan amuncewa da sumayyar,domin sumy mutunce nasan zaiji dadin zama da ita sosai.
Yace mun "naje na zauna na karanceta sai naga tana da abubuwa da yawa da sukai kamanceceniya da naki,nikuma dama fata nake nasamu wacce zata biyoki wajen halaye da abubuwan danakeso,sai akai sa,a sumayya taci tamanin bisa dari acikin abunda nakeso matata ta kasance,karki damu zan kula maki da ita sosai."

Naji dadin maganarsa sosai har nake gaya ma anty itama ta nuna jin dadinta,.

Gobene ake gabatar da karar amma high court Abuja za,ayi ta ranar da dare duk kasa cin abinci mukai nida anty,yaran kuma ta tattara ta turasu can gidan dady,don bazamu baro abuja ba har sai an karkare kararkarar
Munyi xururu nida ita abbansu ya shigo ya ganmu haka ya xauna yana mana nasiha da kokarin ganin ya kwantar mana da hankali,har ya zauna da kansa yana ba antyn abincin ga baki yana sake lallashinta,don sadda yake mana nasihar kuka ta saka masa,yadora kanta bisa kafadarsa yana aikin lallashi,naji kunya amma kuma sun burgeni nai murmushi kurin ina kallonsu,sai faman ya mutsa julluf din hadaddar taliyar danai mana nake,tasha kifin gwangwani ga dan tarugu kadan da kamshin seasonings.muna haka yaya yayi sallama ya shigo anty ta dago daka kafadar mijinta tana kallonsa,daka kallesa zaka ga ramar da yayi saboda rashin samun hutu da natsuwa,shima yana cikin damuwar juriyace da dakiya irinta namiji yasa baka ganewa kaitsaye.

"Ka gansu nan Abbakar suna neman su tada ma kansu hankali akan shariar nan"

Ya Kara so kusadani ya zauna yana cewa "miye na tashin hankali,bacin kunsani mune masu gaskiya mutane dayawa sunsan haka,kuma mune da nasara da ixnin Allah,to damuwa da kuka duk namiye kusaka yakini da yardar ma ranku komi yaxo karshe"

Abban yace "wlh abunda nake gaya masu kenan,har gara nadeya gata nan tadan natsu amma dubeta ita kamar karamar yarinya."

Murmushi kurin khalipa yayi yana maida kallonsa wajena ni kuma kaina yana kasa ina tunani,tashi sukai anty da abban suka wuce sama b'angaren sa aka barni da yaya munyi shiru.
Kallona yake tayi yana nazartata ga taliya ta harta huce,ya jawo kula ya xuba wata marar yawa,ya xuba lemun dake gefena acup yace mun "anshi nan"

Dagowa nai ina kallonsa da idona daya canza launi saboda abunda nakeji araina.
Lumshe mun ido yayi yana dora cup din saman lips dina,banda mafita dole na bude bakina na kurba,ya ajiye cup din ya fara debo taliyar ya nufi bakina na langabe kai idona ya cicciko da kwallah zan kuka ya girgiza mun kai yace "bana so,kimaida su maza inkika bari suka xubo bata wa zamuyi"
Nasa hannu ina gogewa don on ready sun Fara diga,yasake mikomun na bude bakin na ansa babu musu,amma tauna wa nake kamar ina cin madaci bakina babu test ko kadan bana ra,ayin cin komi.
Ahaka ya tusani yaringa danna mun ita inaci cikin bacin rai,saida yaga na cinye yar wadda ya xuba sannan ya xuba shima yaci,ina nan zaune yaci gama yace mun.

"Ya kamata kije ki kwanta da wuri zamu tafi da angama sallar asuba,don mune karar farko da xa,a gabatar."

Kamar zan kuka muryata ta sirace nace"yaya bana iya baccin"

Ya mike yana cewa "bazai yuwu ba,bacci dolene yarinya tashi muje na rakaki."

Na tashi nayi hanyar dakin yana biye dani,jiri nakeji ma kamar zan fadi a haka muka isa dakin,na kunna glub din na wuce toilet nayo brush nayo alwalla don sanyi nakeji baxan iya wanka ba.

Sadda nafito har ya gyara mun gadon ya jawo mun bargona dana linke,na wuce naje na dauko rigar baccina na koma toilet nasaka,doguwa ce iyakar guiwa kuma bamai nuna jiki nace amma duk da haka sai na dauko hijab din sallata na saka,naxo na zauna gefen gadon.
Ya kalleni yace "da abayar zaki kwanta"
Na lumshe idona na girgiza kai kurin.
Murmushi yayi yace "bakin nauyi yayi maki ne"

Nace"aa"

"Okay pray"
Na cire hijan din naja bargo ina kare kirjina muka hada ido yayi murmushi kurin,yana cewa aransa "wataran da kanki zaki bude mun yarinya" (😳khalipa baida kunya aradu)
Na yamutsa fuska nace "kaketa kallona katafi to tunda ka rakoni" 
Yagyara zamansa kusada da ni yana fyskantata yace"saina saki bacci  kozan tafi"

Nace"aikuwa in kana nan bacci bazai zo ba Allah kau"

"Okay bari na gama kallo ki saina tafi"

Yace"kiyi xeroying mine dinki kinji ko pretty,insha Allah xuwa gobe zaki gana da Ammer da Abbanki,bana son kitashi kanki yana ciwo kina jina ko"
Cikin shagwabe fuska nadaga kai nace "yaya kaima ka rame"

Yashafa fuskarsa yace "na kuma rage kyau ba"

Na zaro idona nace "laaaa aa ni bance haka ba"

Yayi murmushi yana kallon xoben dake hannuna sai walkiya yake,ya riko hannun yana kallonsa jiyake kamar ya cinyesa don so da shawa,a na anshe hannun ina cewa "karka cinye mun hannu da kallo"
Wani kallo da yasani lumshe ido ya sakarmun na kauda kaina kurin,don har wani yammm naji cikin kaina.
Yace"son hannun nake sosai"

Nace"dauko wuka inyanke maka shi to"

Yayi dariya yace"aa ajikin ki nakeson sa"

Na langabe kaina nace "masanya zamuyi da matarka kenan?"

Yace "aa ajikinki dai nafi sonsa wataran zai zama mallakina"
Na zaro idanuna duka ina kallonsa yayi murmushi yana sake mun wani kallo kafin yace"hada wadannan idanun suma sonsu nake"
Ni kunya ma yabani wlh na juya masa baya kurin ina rufe fuskata da bargo nace"yaya don Allah kadaina,kaga darefa yanayi nidai ba ruwana inta rufe maka kofa"

Mikewa yayi yana cewa"saina dawo wajenki ke ai bazaki rufe mun ba"

Na jiyo ina cewa "ni asuwa,ba igiyarka kaina balle saidai ka kwana wajen maigadi"

"Watarana Zan Daura maki igiyar guda nawa kikeso?"
Na juya kurin na kyalesa nace "good night"

Inajin fitarsa naja ajiyar xuciya kurin naga ranar da yaya zai sauke girman kansa yace mun yana sona,duk wani kwane kwane da lafe lafensa nasan abunda yake nufi kenan,ni kuma bazan nuna masa nasani ba har saiya furta da bakinsa,kafin nasan mexanyi kuma.


               ******

*RANA BATA KARYA AKACE SAIDAI UWAR DIYA TAJI KUNYA*

kamar yadda yaya yace ana gama sallar asuba zamu wuce hakan akai,kasancewar jiya ban samu bacci sosai ba na kwana ina salla da adduar allah ya shige mana gaba,ya ansar mana hakkinmu wajen wannan azzalumin mutun,sannan in su Ammaer na raye Allah ya bayyana su cikin amincinsa da koshin lafiya.ban dade da baccin ba aka kira assalatu,shiyasa tunda natashin ban koma ba har sadda muka gama shirin tafiya.yar jikkata daya da kayana kala biyu,don nasan baza,a gama shariar cikin kwana daya ba. 

Dagani sai anty da Abban su ashraf,sai yaya da inna da su asmau sumy da mai saboda makaranta baza,a je da ita ba yaya ya kaita gidan nura wajen matarsa walida.

Munfito compound din gidan motarsu yaya ta shigo shida drivern sa,Abba da anty suka fara yin gaba sai yaya yaba driver motar yace yawuce dasu inna da asma,u.

Nace masa"toni k'asa zan tafi daka hana atai dani"

Gira ya daga mun yana murmushi yaxo ya anshi yar jikkata yace"ga bayana zan kaiki ko bakiso"

Najuya masa baya nace cikin sonyin kuka "yaya nidai banson wasa,don Allah kaxo mutafi."

Zama yayi saman wani dandamali dake gefen guiding din gidan yace "zoki zauna insha Allah yanxu zamu tafi mota za,a kawo mani,kila ma sai munrigasu isa"
Taho wa nai inda yake ya karkace kai yana kallona na bata fuska na tsaya ina rungume hannuna.

Murmushi yake yace"tafiyarki kara maki kyau take shiyasa nake kallo"
Nace "aikuwa anan zan tsaya bazan karaso ba"

Dirowa yayi yana tahowa "nizan zo na taho dake ai"
Ganin ya hato tasa nai sauri buxun buxun na isa na zauna ina shan mur,dariya yaringa yimani yace inhar ban saki fuska ba anan zamu zauna baza muje ba.

Dole na masa dariya mun jima awajen kafin aka kawo masa mota daga office dinsu,muka dauki hanya muma gudu yake sosai don time yana tafiya.
Har saida naga yayi yawa nayi masa kamar zan kuka sannan ya sassauta saigashi cikin ikon Allah minty goma ne tsakanin mu dasu Anty dasu inna.

Karfe takwas abuja tai mana wanda karar za,a gabatar karfe goma.
Sai muka tafi gidan Abban su ashraf dake nan mukai break kafin muka dawo kotun,sanda muka dawo ta cika makil harabar wajen har babu wajen parking din mota,mutane ta ko ina ciki ya cika wasu saida awaje duka tsaitsaya,mun samu wajen zama mu duka yayane ya tanadar mana.
Nidai gaba daya jikina amace yake,bakina banda haxbunallahu wani imal wakil bana furta komi,saidai inmun hada ido da yaya ya lumshe mun idonsa alamun kwantar da hankali.
Ina rakube jikin anty na dora kaina bisa kafadarta,alkali akejira sai kuma wadanda ake tuhuma din.
Kowa ya natsu lawyers kowa yaxo ya kame yana jiran afara bagatarwa,cikin lokacin da bai wuce minty talatin ba aka shigo da J da Abbas da minister abdulkadir.kotun ta dauki hayaniya har bakajin ma me ake fada,wasu daga kusa damu banda tsinuwa da sukewa minister da J daula ba abunda suke fada,ko dau daya banji wani wanda ya nuna tausaya warsa akan su ba.kallo daya zakai masu kasan cewar sun ci bakar wuya,daker aka samu kotun tayi shiru sadda  alkali ya shigo.
Tashi akai  don girmamawa,saida ya xauna sannan kowa ya zauna.
Mai gabatar da kara yagabatar sannan alkalin yace "lawyers suna iya gabatar da kansu gaban kotu"
Daga inda suke zaune daya ya mike cikin shigarsa ta lauyoyi yace "sunana brr kamala umar nine lawyer dake kare Alh jafar daula."
Yakoma ya xauna inda yake,lauyer mu ne yatashi yace "sunana brr. Shamsdeen marwan nine lawyer dake kare wadanda aka zalinta"

Bayan sun zauna ne alkalin yafara rubuce rubucensa ya gama sannan ya basu damar fara gabatar da karar.....................
Brr. Shams ne ya mike cikin natsuwa yace "ya mai sharia inason kotu tabani damar yin magana da Alhj jafar."

Alkali yace "kotu ta baka dama"

Yafito ya iso inda aka tsaresu su dukan su yana kare masu kallo,kafin ya saki murmushi ya dora idonsa bisa J yace.

"Mlm jafar muna son kayiwa kotu bayanin sunan ka da kuma aikinka."

Fuskarsa kamar an kona taya don tarin bakin ciki daker yace "sunana Jafar mani dauda,wanda mutane suka fi sani da J daula,aikina shine business da siyasa"

Jinjina kai brr. yayi yace"vayan su baka wata sana,ar kenan?sai inga kamar kana wata sana,ar abuye wadda ba kowane yasan kanayi ba kozaka gaya mana sunanta"

Aharxuke brr kamala mai  kare J ya mike yace "of junction my load,brr shams yana kokarin tirsasa fadan abunda bashine ba"

Alkali yace "brr shams mekakeson bayyana wa ne har kake masa wannan tambayar?"

Yace"ya mai girma mai shari,a inson na bayyana ma kotu da jama,arta cewar J daula business da siyasa basune kadai sana,ar saba akwai mummunan sana,ar da yake boyewa wadda baya son asani,wadda da itace ya aikata duk wasu laifuka da ake tuhumarsa dasu."

alkali yace "kana da gaskiya cigaba da aikinka."

Yajuya kan J yace "kai muke sauraro malam jafar"

Mutsumutsu da baki yake yana ciccin magani yana kauda kai yace"ni bana da wata sana,ar da nake bayan wadanda na gaya maka."

Brr shams yace "tunda kayi taurin kai ni bari na tuna maka inka manta.malam jafar kana daya daga cikin kasugumammun yan ta addan da suka addabi jahar kano,bama kano ba kafaro ne tundaga jahar data rufa maka asiri watau yola,da ta addancinka ya gawurta ka aikata aikakka kagudo kadawo kano ka bude sabuwa,inhar yanxu kana gardama akai yanxu zan bayyana maka komi."

Ya juya wajen alkali yace "ya mai sharia zanso nayi magana da Abbas dan uwa kuma yaya ga Alh Jafar."

Alkali ya bashi dama sannan ya karkata ga baba Abbas yace "malam Abbas muna son ka bayyana ma kotu gaskiyar alakarka da jafar,da kuma labarin ku kaida shi ina nufin labarin rayuwarku."

Nan fa baba Abbas ya Fara bawa kotu labarin rayuwarsu tun kafin xuwansu yola gudun hijira,halayen jafar tun yana karami da irin yadda aka sha fama dashi akan sace sacen abubuwan jama,a tun akauye,har xuwa lokacin da suka shigo yola gudun hijira.
Zamansu gidan su khalipa da irin hallacin da ake masu da mutun tasu da ake,komi daya faru wanda Abbas yasani yaba kotu labarinsa da duk ta addancin da J yayi har kawo barunsu garin suka shigi kano.

"Na kasa tona asirin sa ne domin inason tseratar da rayuwar mutane uku,nafarko yarinyar da bataji bata gani ba wadda yake riko watau nadeya,saifa iyayenta daya kai wani sako lungu yake azabtar dasu tsawon shekara da shekaru,ni kadain nasan inda ya ajiyesu na bari asirinsa yatonu kamar haka cikin dubbun al umma.jafar ba mutumen da za,a daga ma kafa bane zamansa cikin al,umma babban hadarine yatara dukiya wadda shikansa baisan iyakarta ba,kuma duk ta dalilin marigayi Alh Abbakar musaddiq da dan uwansa Alh kaseem Abbakar da matarda Aysher junad khan,dukiyoyinsu ne jafat yake juyi bisa wanda yake ganin basu isai ba saiya handame na wasu.kunji labarin mu wanda inban dani da uwata deluwa babu wanda zai bakushi yadda yake hakan."

murmushi mai kyau brr shams yayi yajuya ga alkali yace "kaji to ya mai sharia,abunda dan uwansa yace kuma hallau zanso nayiwa minister tambaya."

Alkali yace "anbaka dama"

Yajuya ga minister yace"rankayadade kabani labarin abotarka da jafar daula gashinan."

Ya yamutsa fuska yace"abokina ne na kasuwanci"

Brr shams ya girgiza kai yace "karkayi karya rankayadade,eh to zan iya yarda abokinka ne na kasuwanci amma kasuwancin ba wanda jama,a suke tunani ba.ka na nufin kasuwancin irin na shik'a da handama,kuna kashe mutane kuna hayewa bisa dukiyarsu.inajin wannan kasuwancin kake nufi"

Yayi shiru ya kauda kai brr shams yace"ya mai sharia ko ahaka kadai ya dace kagane cewar wadannan mutanen gigga giggan yan dabane,kuma nasan kafahimci cewar basuda gaskiya kota sile ina rokon kotun nan mai adalci zata gaggauta yanke masu hukunci tare da ansar hakkin marayu,da ceto rayuwar iyayensu"

Yakoma ya zauna mazauninsa.
Alkali ya gamaa rubutunsa ya dago yace "brr kamala yana da magana ne"

Kamar ya mutu don haushi yace "ehy mai sharia.,inason magana da deluwa"
Alkalin yace "anbaka dama,kotu tana son ganin deluwa gabanta"

Can na hango deluwa tana fitowa cikin mutane kowa yabita da kallo,gadai tsufa ya kamata don har da wani dan gyasa kafa haka ta karaso inda ake bukatarta.

Brr kamala yace mata "deluwa ke uwace ga jafar da Abbas,inason ki gaya ma kotu gaskiyar halayensu,sannan ki tabbatar mana da gaskiyar labarin daya bada."

Kuka ta farayi sannan tace "hakika nina haifesu dukkansu,saidai banbancin uba kowa dana sa,tunda na haifi jafar abbas yake hassada dashi,baya sonsa ko kadan duk abunda zaija ace jafar  ne yayi laifi abbas nemansa yake ya lika masa,nayi iyakar yina don inga na daidaita su abun yacitura,kwatsam abun nan yaxo yafaru na mutuwar iyayen gidanmu.ranar jafar bainan yake wajen kanwata bai kwana gida ba,da wannan abbas yayi anfani ya ce jafar ne.bacin nayi rantsuwa inya yiwa kanensa sharri saina tsine masa da tuni jafar yana lahira,wannan yaxo ya wuce abbas bai hkr ba yaje ya gayama mahaifin jafar cewar shine yayi kisa ga iyayen gidanmu sannan yayi masu sata,ta dalilin wannan abun mahaifinsa ya bar duniya.amma shine wannan yaro xai masa sharri gaban jama,a wlh abbas kayi asara."
Ta rushe da kuka wiwi hkr brr kamala ya bata yace"kibar kuka deluwa komi zaizo karshe"

Ya juya ga alkali yace "ya mai sharia kaji dai duk abunda mahaifiyarsu ta fada ga dukkan alamu abbas karya yayiwa dan uwansa,sannan kuma akwai wata hujjar da nakeson bayarwa."

Yayi ma yansanda magana aka shigo da wasu kowane daure da unka.
Mazan su hudune mutanen da khalipa yasa aka kama lokacin da suka baro kano zasuzo kaduna,harin da aka kawo masu.

Wajen tuhuma aka ajiyesu yaci gaba da cewa "ya mai girma mai sharia wadannan mutanen daka gani,ankama sune saka makon harin da Abbas ya turo su suyiwa wannan yarinya Nadeya,zan so kaji daga bakinsu."

Yajiyu yana kalonsu yace ma guda "malam kozaka gaya ma kotu waye kai"

Kai tsaye yace "sunana haladu,kuma ni jakariyar jafar daula ne tun shekara da shekaru,kwatsam rannan Abbas ya samemu da maganar zamu farauto masa wasu mutun biyu,inhar mukai hakan zai biyamu kudade masu yawa mukuma ganin yawan kudin yasa muka amunce,don bamu san ma cewar yarinyar maigidace yake hari ba."

Kotun ta dauki hayaniya na dan lokaci kafin alkali yasa akai shiru,brr kamala yaci gaba da cewa.

"Ya Mai sharia kaga kenan ba jafar kadai ne yake farautar rayuwar nadeya ba,abbas ma daga gefe yana kokarin ganin yaja nashi kason,don haka inason kotu ta sake duba sosai akan wannan case jafar goga masa laifi akeson ayi bashine da alhakin wannan laifukan ba,na gode ya mai sharia."
Yakoma ya zauna

Haka akaita gurxawa tsakanin lawyers din biyu kowa yana kokarin ganar da kotu gamai laifi,duk hujjar da brr shams ya kawo da gudu brr kamala zai rusata ta hanyar kawo gurguwar shaidarsa,ahaka akaita abu gudu har xuwa sallar azahar babu matsaya guda daya,hankula sun tashi sosai ganin anason afidda jafar daga cikin case ajefa baba Abbas aciki tsundum,shidai minister tuni ya bada kai cewar yayarda ya ansa laifinsa na ta addanci da suke yi akan kudi,kuma yace shi bashida wani aboki acikin harkar daya wuce jafar.

Amma nan take brr kamala ya karyata yake cewa ai hadin bakine shida Abbas suke son ganin dole Sai jafar ya fuskanci hukunci,abun yaki ci yaki cinyewa nidau kuka nake sosai ganin bama ta inda iyayena suke akeba,har yau ba,axo wajen ba ga kuma wata rigimar tana son kunnowa ana son fidda jafar daga cikin case din,bacin shine gawurtaccen munafuki kuma oga acikin tafiyar.karfe biyu na bugawa alkali ya buga gudumarsa cewar andage shariar xuwa nan da kwana uku,tare da fatan kowane zaizo da kwakkwaran shaidarsa da zata gamsar da kotu asan mai gaskiya da marar ita.

Haka akatashi hankulan mu tashe duk na fita natsuwata,ina jikin anty ina kuka tana lallashina tare da bani hkr.
Mota muka wuce ta shigar dani,yaya nacan suna magana da brr Shams daga yadda yake magana kasan ransa shima abace yake,don idonsa yayi ja fuskar nan ta baci da bacin rai,sai daga murya yake brr shams yana bashi hkr..........daker Abban su sharifa ya jawosa muka nufi gidan da muka sauka daxun,don ana zamu zauna har agama shariar.

Koda muka isa gidan akintse yake part part ne guda uku gidan sai kamshi yake mun samu masu aiki sun gama abincin rana,amma babu wanda ya kallesa da sunan ci.
Abban ne yaketa kokarin kwantar mana da hankali cewar insha Allah basu da nasara,mune masu nasara hkrin da mukai Allah bazai wofintar dashi ba,insha Allah zaman karshe gaskiya zatai halinta asirinsa zaitonu.

Nidai wani daki dana shiga naga yayi mun nashige kurin na je na watsa ruwa nai sallar azahar,na dawo na dan shafa mai kadan da turare na haye gado na kama kuka,saida nai mai isata sannan bacci barawo ya lallabo yaxo ya saceni.
Nice ban farka ba saida naji ana diga mun ruwa akan fuskata,ahankali nake bude idona na sauke su bisa na yaya,yana tsaye kaina da ruwa acup ga hannunsa.fuskar sa ta saki ba kamad daxun ba murmushi yake kadan yace mun.
"Kalli agogo malama sarkin bacci,bakiyi la,asar ba har ga magruba tayi."

Nayi mika ahankali tare da salatin annabi nata shi zaune ina jingina da gadon,nace cikin muryar bacci.

"Yaya wlh ban san baccin yaxo ba,kuka fa nake saidai kurin naji ina bacci"

Dariya yayi yana ajiye cup din yace"wannan bacci ya kyauta mun sosai,da yanxu ina nan ina lallashi.kije kiyi salla bari naje masallaci"

Yajuya ya fice nabisa da kallo ina sauke ajiyar xuciya,Allah sarki yaya nasan duk yafimu shiga damuwa amma kasancewar sa namiji ya danne ya nuna kamar babu komi,saukowa nai na nufi toilet din nayo alwalla nafito nayi sallar.
Sannan na koma nasake wani wankan,kamar wata kwado ko kifi na xauna na shirya cikin doguwar rigata ta kanti may kyau sosai na feshe jikina da turare na saka powder da lipstick,ban sa kwalli ba don darene sannan na dauki wayata da naga missed call din yah mahfux,fitowa nai ina kiransa nasaka wayar ga kunnena nafito mukai karo da yaya.................


*hallau da hkrin zan sake baku wlh ina cikin wani yanayi ne,ina rokon adduar ku akoda yaushe Allah ya daidaita mun lamurana,insha Allah kafin axumin nan zan gama kuyita mun addua.inkuma ban samu hali ba sai yadda Allah yai damu*
