*INA TAYA AL,UMMAR MUSULMI MURNAN BARKA DA SALLAH,FATAN MUNSHA RUWA LFY UBANGIJI ALLAH YA KARBI IBADUNMU YA NUNA MANA SJEKARA MAI XUWA CIKIN KOSHIN LFY,NI MOM MUHSEEN INA MIKO GAISUWATA GA KOWA TARE FATAN ALKAIRI*

Episode  .39

"Wash Allah na yaya zaka fasa mun kaina"

Gefe yaja ya rungume hannuwansa yace "kokuma ni ki fasamun kaina ba,ai kanki yafi nau kwari"

Harararsa nai kadan ina cewa "uhm naji wannan muje nasan abincine za,a matsa mun naci kuma ni wlh bana jin cin komi sai abu mai ruwa."

Hanya ya bani yana bina da kallo yace "muje komi kikeso sai kici amman bazan yarda da kwana da yunwa ba."
Langabe kaina nai ina wucewa ya rufa mani baya muka isa main falon gidan,asmau,inna da Anty sai Abban su ashraf duk suna zaune saman table kowa nacin abunda yayi daidai da ra,ayinsa,duk da dai har yanxu babu walwala ga fuskar kowa.

Jan kujera nai kusada asmau ina ansar muhin ina masa murmushi sai kallona yake kamar yasanni,lakuce masa hanci nai nace "wlh kul dinka inkace zakai koyi kyuya don naga abunda kake kokari kenan"
Dariya kadan duk sukai Abbansu sharifa yace "ato da alama kam yanxu yasan mamansa ta karfe"
Bata fuska nai ina kallon asmaun tana murmushi nace "abun kike karanta masa kenan ko?kin kyauta anshe shi to tunda nizaku yiwa rashin mutunci"
Na fada ina daure fuska anty ta ansheshi ita kuma tana dariya tace "inbada abunki me wannan yasani harda za,a karanta masa wani abu wayaune fa kurin yafara yana son tunaki ne ba wai kyuya yake neman yi maki ba"
Sai lokacin nasaki raina nace "auto yanxu naji bayani,ina jawo exotic na xuba cikin cup sai na xuba fefe chicken da akayi na xuba pies hudu,hira ake dan tabawa akan zaman karar da akai nidai na kasa saka baki,don duk kalami daya kara sosa man xuciya yake,tunda muka zauna yaya baice komi ba saidai kadan kadan mun hada ido dashi,abincin kansa tunda yayi spoon baifi biyar ba yature yana dan kurbar lemu ya jingina bayansa da kujera,bana son nayi shishshigi dana ce masa mai ya samesa saboda ga matarsa zaune itace ya dace takula da halin da yake ciki,amman banga alamun hakan ba.

Kallonsa nake shima yana kallona lumshe idona nai na cigaba da cin naman,badan inaso ba saidan karnaji yunwa cikin dare,anty naji tace ma asmaun.

"Maman muhin ya kamata kuje kufara shirin kwanciya da alamun dai akwai gajiya tare da abban muhin"

Idan kafahimci maganarta itama taga alamun rashin walwala tare dashi,tayi haka ne don ta ankarar da matarsa don ita tafikowa kusa dashi wata kila insuka kebe zai samu natsuwa.

Inna tace"haka kam ya kamata"

Daman tun daxun ta gama cin abincin ta anshi muhin ta saba ga kafada sannan tace "to saida safenku"
Duk suka ansa mata daker na iya budar baki nayi mata saida safe,don jinai wani abu yaxo mun wuyana ya tokareni,ba kamar ganin shima ya tashi yana masu saida safen yabi bayanta.

Daker na hadiye naman dake bakina na bisa da ruwan lemun na sadda kaina ina jin yadda kirjina yake bugawa,kowa saida yatashi yabarni anan nafi minty ashirin kafin na mike na nufi dakina nima.

Kamar kofarce tamun laifi na bankata da karfin tsiya,don da ace bata kirki bace saita lalace.
Nasake maidata na buga da karfi ina jan tsaki,ko rigar jikina ban cire ba na fada kan gado ina jan numfashi,kamar wadda tayi tseren gudu.

Rubda ciki nai daga inda nake kwance na lalubo makashin glub din dakin na kashe,naja filo na dora kaina sama badan inajin bacci ba babin tunani na bude son ganin iyayena nake,amman bansan ya zata kaya mana ba.
Abun yaxo ya hademun da kishin yaya suka taru suka hanani runtsawa,karshe na saka kuka naita kukana ni kadai,don ni kadai din nasan ciwon dake cikin raina,ji nake ma yau kamar ansabunta mun yayan acikin kokon raina,haka naita juyi ina shashsheka har banson yin imagining cewar suna can tare da juna,jinake xuciyata na ciwo kokari nake karnaji haushin asmau kokari nake shima na kauda shi cikin raina,inanan kwance tsawon lokaci naji phone dina na vibrating kafin na dauka na duba time karfe shadaya,yah mahfux na gani daure wa nai na dauka ina masa sallama.

"Amincin Allah ya tabbata gareki"

Lumshe ido nai ina son daidaita kaina ajiyar xuciya naja kadan sannan na ansa masa.

"Tare da kai yah mahfux"

"Allah yasa ban tsike maki bacci ba,ban duba time ba sadda nayi dialing no dinki"

"Bakomi wlh Allah yasa banyi baccin ba kila da bazan maji kiran ba,ina wuni ya gida dasu hajiya"

"Kowa da komi lafiya ykk ya kuma abuja,fatan shariar tana tafiya daidai tun daxun naso kiranki sai naga baki dauka ba,kuma baki biyoni daga baya ba sainai zaton ko baku dawo gida ba"

Mirginawa nai gefe ina cemasa "aa bana cikin natsuwa ne shiyasa naso na biyoka bansamu dama ba,sharia gata nan sai addua "

"Tofa ubangiji Allah yasa adace,Allah ya doramu saman makiya kiyi hkr kinjiko komi mai wucewa ne,insha Allah zakiyi farin ciki marar misaltuwa ki kara hkr kinjiko yar kanwata"

Sai naji na samu saukin xuciyata at least na Samu mai tausasa mun yana kwantar mun da hankali,godiya nai masa nace masa.

"Nagode yah mahfux kamar kasan ina bukatar wajen da zanyi kukana a lallasheni,ngd da alamun sumayya zatasha lele"
Na fada ina dariya kasa kasa.

Murmushi yayi yace"harda xolaya,to kinyi daidai ai girmanki ne bari na barki kiyi bacci saida safe,don Allah karki saka damuwar komi kibarwa Allah"

"Shikenan yah mahfux nagode Allah ya kaimu safen lfy"

Mukai ban kwana na kashe wayar,na juyo kenan naga kamar mutun bayana,wata irin kara na fasa ina fadawa bayan gado,innalillahi kurin nake kira.
Saurin kunna hasken dakin yayi yana zagayo wa ta inda nake,jikina karkarwa kurin yake na dunkule waje guda..............tabani yayi na buge  hannunsa ina kuka,don sai yanxun nagane kowaye.

"I'm sorry"

Ya fada yana miko mun hannunsa inkama intashi,banza nai masa ina kukana don ba karamar buguwa nai ga kuguna ba,hannuna na saman kugun ina shafawa saboda ciwon da yake.

"Akwai ciwone?"

Ya fada yana durkusawa gabana da niyyar riko hannun da nake shafa wajen da nakejin yana mun ciwon,afusace na sake bugesa nace"don't touch me again"

Na Kama gadon ina rarrafawa na hau naja filona na dora kaina ina cigaba da kukana,na juya masa baya abunda ban tsammata ba naji,hannayensa biyu yadora bisa k'uguna gefe da gefen,bansan sadda kukana ya tsaya ba jinai kamar ya sokeni da wani abu,ciwon da wake kemun na nemesa na rasa,runtse idona nai gamm ina jin kamar numfashina baya fita ba daidai ba.

Murzawa yayi bansan sadda na zabura na tashi zaune ba ina jan numfashi,kallonsa nake kamar yadda yake kallona shima,dauke kai nai na tattare kafafuna na dunkule cikin fada nace masa.

"Wannan ba daidai bane,kashigo dakina babu sallama ka bani tsoro,yanxu daka saka wani mummunan ciwo ya sameni fa,gaskiya nidai......."

Nai shiru ban idasa ba ina dauke kaina.

"Keda wa kike waya cikin wannan daren?"

Tabe baki nai don baisan haushinsa nakeji ba,kome yazo yiman adaki bacin ga matarsa can yabaro.
Nadage kafada nace "yah mahfux ne"

"Mahfux?uban me yake gaya maki,bai rabu dakeba kenan"

Cikin kulewa nace"wai don Allah yaya ina ruwanka,kai yanxu ba shabiyu bace tayi harda kwata amman ka shigo mun daki,bacin ga matarka can kabaro kai mekazo yi katashi kai tafiyarka.......... don bakasan haushinka nakeji ba."
Na idasa fada can kasa kasa.........ganinsa kurin nai kan gadona kamar wani zaki,atsorace na wulkita na dire kasa ina gyara rigata data ke zamewa daga kirjina............"dakin tsaya dakinga abunda zai faru,wlh saina hora wannan dan iskan bakin da yake iya gaya mun magana,"

Jikin bango na tsaya ina kunkuni............kallona yake kurin ina dalla masa harara,murmushi naga yayi yace "ohhh sorry nagane meke damunki,nasan zakiji ba dadi shiyasa naxo dubaki ko ykkeji"

Kallonsa nai cikin rashin fahimta nace"mekenan?"

"Oh baki ganeba kenan,kishina yasa kike jin haushina ko I'm sorry bansan zakiji ba dadi ba aida nan nataho na sauke gajiyata."

Dauke kaina nai nace"yaya miye haka,bana son irin haka akanme zanji kishinka kaida matarka halalin ka mezai daman,don Allah zoka tai inkunga dama kucinye juna karkuyi saura saboda kauna",na fada xuciyata kamar zata fito waje don kuna,hanyar bandaki nayi don kawai inason na bar inda yake,haushinsa karuwa yake cikin raina ga shi inban kuka ba banjin dadi budewa nai zan shige naji ya rikeni ya juyo dani,idonsa kuri cikin nawa daya fara tara kwallah..............kokowar kwace kaina nake ina cewa "sakeni,mezan maka wai ince nidin ba halalin ka bace karka sake tabani.................shiiiiiiit ya fada yana dora hannunsa bisa lips dina dake kermar kuka,dauke kaina nai inajin kwallar na silalowa bazan iya hanata xuba ba runtse idona nai kurin don bansan mezanyi ba kuma.

Hannunsa yasa ya jiyo da fuskata gabansa naki bude idona har time din hawayena basu tsaya ba,baida zabi sai yin hakan,don yarikice baisan taya zai lallasheta ba.
Jinai kurin yasa halshensa yana bin kuncina da lasa har iyakar inda hawayena suka tsaya,gudan kuncin ma yayi masa haka,maimakon su tsaya sai suka karu ya sake yunkurin lashewar,nai saurin juya baya ina gogewa da hannuna,zagayowa yayi yariko duk hannuwa na yace cikin sanyin murya mai ratsa xuciya.

"I'm sorry pls,I don't mean to hurt u, and banda wani zabi sai hakan kinki bani dama,ko mun samu natsuwa nidake please I'm sorry stop crying."

Kin kallonsa nai don bazan iya yin hakan ba,na anshe hannuwana na juya zan wuce bakin gadona.

"Say something pls, at least kila na samu nayi bacci naje na kasa yin komi."

Kalamansa narkar dani suke inajin kamar nabiye masa nayi aman nawa da nake tarawashekara da shekaru,amman bazan yi ba not now.
Zama nai gefen gadon ina wasa da yatsuna,har lokacin jinake kamar halshensa ne bisa fuskata,har jinake jikina na tsuma.

Sake cewa yayi "plssssss kinji"

Runtse idona nai nace daker "Good night"

Kamar wanda bashida lakka ya jingina da kofa yana kallona har na kasa jurewa na dago muka hada ido,girarsa guda ya daga mun dole nai saurin sauke idona na kwanta ina juya baya.

"K'ugun yadaina ciwo ko insake murza maki"

Wata irin kunya da takaici suka rufeni nace "plsss don Allah bana so"

Murmushinsa najiyo yace"oky by have a sweet night"
Kin ansa masa nai saida naji fitarsa sannan naja wata doguwar ajiyar xuciya,bansan ina murmushi ba nikadai boye fuskata nai cikin filo ina tuna duk abunda ya faru,kai yaya karshene wlh mayene wajen soyayya,duk randa ya idasa fallasa kansa ban san mezaifaru ba.

Acikin kwanakin da aka bada na sake zaman kotun sosai yaya da lawyer din suka zage wajen har hada hujojinsu,don daga wannan zaman shikenan kuma muna sakaran komi zai zama daidai insha Allah,bamu sake wani kebewa dashi ba ganinsa ma wuya yake mun saidai najiyosa kurin,to nima kuma ina kiyaye haduwar tamu don ba komi yake karamun ba illa zallar kaunarsa da soyayyarsa,bana son tawa tayi karfin da zan kasa boyeta har sai ranar da yagaji yafurta,asannan saina san mezanyi.

Acikin kwanakin mun dage sosai da rokon Allah ya bamu sa,a don wancen zaman ya tsorata kowa,don gani muke kamar kayar damu za,ai.
Abban su ashraf ma tattake yake wajen ganin komi yatafi daidai,har kaduna suka koma don sake samo wasu hujjojin daga kaduna kano suka wuce ranar inajin sai biyun dare suka dawo yaya da Abban ashraf,kasa bacci mukai muduka har saida mukaji isowarsu duk munyi xuru afalo inajin saukar motarsu na mike nace saida safensu.

Inna tace"kin tsaya kiyi masu sannu ai ko nadeya"

Yake nai nace "inna bacci ya cika idona daman rashin dawowarsu ne yasa banje na kwanta ba"

Anty ta dalla man harara tace "kenan bazaki tsayan ba,"

"Sorry Anty na zauna"
Nakoma na zauna inajin gabana yana duka,bakomi yasa zan tafi ba yadda naga asmau taci kwalliyar tarbar mijinta,bazan iya kallon wannan kaunar ba xuciyata ciwo zatai.
Muhin ta dora mani kan cinya tace "jeka momi kafin na gama da Abbanka"
Ansarsa nai ina yake sai ya washe mun bakinsa,biye masa nai naita masa wasa yana dariya,har suka shigo kasa kallon kofar nai ina ta ma muhin rawa yana dariya abunsa,sannu na juya nayi wa Abban ashraf ya ansa da kulawa,suka gaisa da inna sannan anty ta anshi jikkarsa suka wuce part dinsu.
Inna suna ta magana da yayan na mike na wuce sa muhin din dakina,inta gama tazo ta anshesa.

Wanka nai masa na barsa dagashi sai yar rigasa ta shan iska da pant saboda daman zafi ake,duk dai akwai ac da fankoki.naita danbe da xuciyata har nasamu na kauda abunda yake raina,naita kula da muhin har yayi bacci nima na kwanta kusada shi ban san baccin ya sureni ba.

Duk wata kulawa da yadace ta bashi tayi masa sai kokarin ganin ta nuna irin kewarsa da tayi take,yayin da hankalinsa gaba daya baya kansa,baisan ya akai tunda sukazo abuja kullin jiyake kamar ana masa allurar sabuwar kaunarta,amman baison ya gwale asmaun yana son bata damarta karya tauyeta,shiyasa suka mance da muhin soyayyarsu suka sha son ransu har yake mamakinta,yadda ta zage take sakar masa jiki duk kwana biyun da sukai basu hadu ba.

Kukan muhin din ya tasheni na duba agogo karfe daya nai mamakin yadda asmau zata wofintar da danta har kamar yanxu,tashi nai na sabesa ga kafada ta ina jijjigashi na bude kofa na fita,dakinsu na nufa ko kadan xuciyata bata kawo mun komi ba awannan lokacin aida bazan fara yun kurin shigar masu ba,na mance tunanin hakan bai xo mun ba.
Kasancewar single room ne babu falo tura kofar nai kaina tsaye,idona yayi wannan mummunan ganin,asmau ce kwance saman jikinsa ba riga jikinsa itama haka,amman akwai bargo ya rufe rabin jikinsu fuskarsu hade data juna salati naja nai saurin ficewa ina jan kofar,xuciyata kamar zata fito waje saboda yadda take duka jinai kafafuna suna rawa bazan iya tsayawa ba,idona na runtsesa gamm muhin dake hannuna kurin yasa ban silale kasa ba,..........salatin da sukaji anyi ya ankarar dasu sunga sadda tafice a 360 asmau tazabura tace "na shiga uku nadeya ce,ashe nabar mata muhin tun daxun,yaya wane abun kunyar mukai"

Duk da shikanshi yaji babu dadi sai yace "ba abun kunya mukai ba sunnace muka raya,itace ai tayi abun kunya."
Ya fada yana janyeta yana jawo dogon wandonsa ya xura ya saka singlet dinsa,yace" bari na ansoshi."

"Yaya bakajin nauyinta"

Bai saurareta ba ya fice bakin kofa ya ganni inajin motsin fitowar sa na dire muhin din kasa na juya kamar zan kifa nabar wajen.
Daukar dansa yayi ya juya yakoma,xuciyarsa na amsawa baiso ba duk da ita taja batai knocking ba balle sallama,bashida laifi tunda matarsa ce amma kasan ransa yanajin rashin jin dadin hakan.

Episode  .40


Washe gari muka tashi da shirin komawa kotu,don ranar ne alkali yace akoma.
Daren jiya idona baiga bacci ba ko kadan,naci axaba ba yar karama ba don bansan haka akejin kishi ba sai daren jiya,nayi kuka har nagaji  nai salla nayi karatun qur,ani amma jinake xuciyata kamar ana dafata.
Nayi dana sanin abunda na ganin ma idona,don har yanxun da nake break dakina,dana runtse idona sunake hangowa fuskokinsu hade bakinsu hade.
Dafe kaina nai dake sarawa nace "haxbunallahu wani imal wakil,lallai kishi bala,i ne Allah kabani ikon sarrafa xuciyata,karka sa nayi abunda zai zama abun kunya wajena."
Kurban tea din nake na kasa hada sa da soyayyar doyar da ke cikin plet din gabana,daker na ci uku kamar ina cin dusa haka nakejinta.
Tashi nai nafita rike da cup din tea d'in ga hannuna na samesu su duka saman table,daman bamu gaisa ba ina tashi kitchen naje na dibo break din na wuce daki,sannan duk basu fito ba.
Anty ta fara mun magana "lafiyarki pretty?"

Jinai kamar na fada jikinta nai kuka na daure dai,fuskata na yamutsa nace "kaina ke ciwo ko akwai mai PCM"

Inna tace "sannu nadeya Allah ya sawake"
Na ansa mata da kai kurin.

Asmau tace "ayya nadeya sannu bari na duba maki naga kamar akwai wani dakinmu"
Tatashi tawuce nabita da kallo kurin,kafin na jiyo saboda maganar da Abban su ashraf yake mun.

"Allah yasa ba damuwa kika dora ma kanki ba har taja maki ciwon kai"

Nagirgiza kai nace "aa Abba nadanyi masassara da daddare kila itace tasa kan nawa ciwo"

Khalipa kallonta kurin yake amman Allah bai bashi sa,ar ta jiyo sun hada ido ba.
Kallo daya yayi mata yasan cewar bata yi bacci ba jiya,karya ta shirga kurin.
Wani abu yaji ya darsa mashi har saida ya lumshe idonsa,addua kurin yake ta kallesa amman takiya.

Asmau ce ta dawo tace "ayya kiyi hkr wlh ban ganshi ba,Abban muhin ko kasha jiyane"

Ajiyar xuciya yadan ja yace "na mance wlh ashe nashanye shi jiya,kibari idan zamu fita sai mutsaya asiya kisha sannu kinjiko"

Banjin zan iya bude baki nace masa wani abu,duk yadda nake son na ture haushinsa abun ya faskara.
Ba tare dana ansa masa ba balle har na iya dubansa na wuce dakina na barsu,duk bina sukai da kallo.
Anty tace "tofa?mekai mata dagani kayi mata laifi"

Girgiza kaiyayi yace "ni sarki uban laifi ko anty,toni babu abunda nai mata muskilancin ta ne yatashi kuma taji da abunta."

Asmau dai kanta kasa don tasan tatsuniyar gixo bata wuce ta k'ok'i,ita kanta abun ya dameta amman ya zasuyi da tayi sallama duk da ba haka ba.
(Musani yan uwa sallama tana da matukar muhimanci,shiyasa addini ya wajabta mana ita,koda gidanka ne akace kayi sallama har a ansa maka sannan kashiga,balle ace gidan wani ko turakar wani ina fata zamu kiyaye shigar ma mutane dakuna ko gidaje kai tsaye batare da sallama ba,domin bakasan a irin halin da zaka iskesu ba,Allah kasa mudace Amen)

Doguwar riga ce jikina pich color tasha adon duwatsu da fukawowi,nayi rolling kaina da gyalenta takalmana plat shoes bakake sai side bag dina karama itama baka,nasaka black glass idona don ma kar atakurani.
Babu wani murmushi ko walwala daka kalleni zaka san banda walwala ko kadan,motar Abba na shiga abaya na kame abuna ina jiransu su fito.

"Aaha nadeya,kifito kibi khalipa mana nan inna zata shigo,"
Inji anty 

"Anty basaina bisa ba nan ma zai ishemu da innar,kibarni nan nidai"

"Nak'iya nace nak'ya,zaki fito kokuwa,akwai inda zamu biya ne kekuma ba lfy gareki ba kibisa yasiya maki ganin kujiramu a kotun"

Raina bace na fito naje na bude motar tasa nashiga,kamar zanyi aman xuciyata.
Asmau na xuwa tabude gaban ta shiga ta jiyo tana cemun.
"Yajikin naki"

Kamar na kwada mata mari amman na kauda hakan nace mata "na samu sauki sosai,ina swt hrt"

"Gashi can wajen inna"

Daga haka mukai shiru har sadda yaxo yashiga yana tada motar,ta center mirror yake kallona nai iskar kare dashi.
Tafiyar ta zama so silent babu Mai magana a cikin mu,har mukaje shagon da zai siya mun maganin ya fita yabarmu.

Alokacin na samu damar cema asmaun.
"I'm sorry for yesterday,nayi abu irin na jahilai mantuwa ce ba rashin sani ba,ina cikin bacci ne ko kusa ban dauka zan sameku haka ba sorry please"

Kamar jira take ta jiyo cikin damuwa tace "nasan mantuwarce nadiya,ai bazaki fado daki kai tsaye ba don Allah kibar bani hkr,hada laifin mu bamu rufe kofar ba.wlh kunyarki nakeji"

Na tabe baki nace"don't mind"

Bude wa yayi yashigo ya miko mun swan da maganin,na ansa na ajiye gefe.

Bata rai yayi yace"yanxu zakisha,inba sokike na kara maki bacin rai akan wanda kike ciki ba"

Cikin kulewa nace "bismillah"

Cikin mamakina yace "mekikace?maimaita don Allah"

Nadauke kai kurin yana ballo maganin, yaja kwata yace"dakinga yadda ake iskanci tunda abun naki harda rashin kunya."

Fixgar motar yayi muka bar wajen.

Yau ma kamar wancen karon kotun ta cika ta batse,harma fiye da wancen zaman hankalina ya sake tashi,gani nake shikenan munyi losing.
Waje na samu na kame nikadai babu wanda nake ma magana,kirjina duka kurin yake.

Duk wanda yadace ya hallara wannan zama ya hallara,alkali yaxo lawyers suma duk sunxo,aka fara karanto karar da inda aka tsaya.
Alkali ya bukaci lawyers dasu fara gabatar da hujojinsu don akwai wasu shariar dake bayan tamu.

Brr shams ya mike bayan yayi gaisuwa yace ma alkali "ya mai girma mai sharia zanso na bayyana ma kotu cewar,Alh Abbas ya fada mana inda jafar ya boye iyayen nadeya,yace wajene da dagashi sai jafar suka sani.amman babu wanda yake iya shiga wajen sai jafar,yamai sharia muna son yanxu bada bata lokaci ba zamuje da jafar da jamiai da abbas da duk wanda ya kamata ya zama ido ga kotu,don anfiddo wadannan bayin Allah domin rayuwar na cikin hadari."

Jinjina kai alkali yayi yace "kotu tabaka dama,kuma kotu zata wakilta mutane uku da zasuyi maku rakiya har wajen.amman kudawo kan lokaci sai adasa daga inda aka tsaya,"

Nan aka fitar da mutun ukun da zasu bisu da kuma jamian tsaro sai jafar da baba Abbas,da lawyer brr shams,sai yaya.

Ba bata lokaci suka dauki hanyar kano,don ma dai ta jirgine pravet jat shiyasa sai muka zauna zaman jiransu,hankalina tashe innace keta kwamtarmun sa hankali tana cewa naita anbaton haxbunallahu.

*****
Hakika duk mai imani da tausayi dole zai tausayama halin da yaga su Abba kaseem,don gaba ki daya wadanda akaje dasu wajen babu wanda bai xubar hawayensa ba akansu,khalipa idonsa kamar wuta saboda yasha kuka wanda yajima baiyyi irinsa ba,idan kaga kogon bazaka ce mairai zai iya rayuwa wajen ba,amma da yake Allah shiyake rayawa gashinan suduka da ransu,saidai daka kallesu zakasan basu da isassar lfy zakasan wahala tabi jikinsu ba yar karama ba,don idon Abba kaseem guda ya kankance.
Jikinsu kamar anyamutsa tsumma haka yakoma,basa iya tafiya ko kadan saidai jan kafafu,Ammer kuwa ta koma tsohuwar karfi da yaji,bama ita ba daga ita harshi Abba kaseem din.
Ai dole aka kira kiran gaggawa daga asibiti suka xo suka fara basu taimakon gaggawa,karshe ma acikin motar asibitin aka wuto dasu kotu,ganin da alkali yayi masu ya girgiza,yace sunfi bukatar asibiti fiye da zaman kotu,don haka awuce dasu asibiti.
Wannan kadai yatona asirin J,koda aka dawo kotu nidai idona har baya gani saboda kuka,bana jin zan iya yafewa jafar daula,bana jin ko ganinsa nai kabarinsa yana ci da wuta zan yafe masa,saboda ya cutar dani!ya cutar da rayuwata!ya cutar da iyayena mafi soyuwa arayuwata,tayazan iya yafe masa.

Nan fa brr shams ya Fara fiddo sauran hujjoji harda matar jafar daula cikin masu fitowa suna tona masa asiri,duk wasu munanan ayyuka data san  J dasu ta zayyane gaban kotu,haka kanwarsa halima taxo itama ta fadi duk abunda ta saniakan munanan halayensa,dana mahaifiyarsu deluwa tana rokon kotu ta sassautawa baba Abbas hukunci akan karamin laifinsa na kaiwa nadeya hari.

Brr kamala baki ya mutu duk ta inda yasan zasu tsere angama kamesu,shiyasa koda alkali ya tambayesa yana da magama cewa yayi babu,don kuwa karyarsu ta gama karewa,k'urungus kan k'usu daman yan karin magana sunce,rana dubu ta b'arawo rana guda tamai kaya,yau dai dubun J ta cika duk wani makiyinsa da wanda ya cuta yana cikin tsananin farinciki.

Nan danan aka shiga lissafe lissafen dukiyar daya handame,duk wasu takaddu na shaida dasuke wajen khalipa ya bayar,dole jafar yabada kai bori ya hau da kansa yake fadar irin yawan dukiyarsa,da kuma mutanen da yakewa kwace,duk wanda ya fada zaice ka iyakar dukiyarsa daya handame,haka akaita lissafi ana rubutu wanda alkali yabada umurnin tattaro duk wasu kadarori na J araba ma masu hakki hakkinsu,ya dora wani babban jami,i da lawyers akan hakan,ya kuma bada sati guda cub akammala tattarowa abawa masu hakki kayansu.
Kafin aka fadi hukuncin da kotu ta yankewa deluwa da J da Baba Abbas.

Rubutu alkali yayi mai dogon lokaci kafin ya dago yana kallon duk mutanen dake kotun,kamar yasan budar bakinsa kurin akejira.
Ahankali yafara da cewa.

"Wannan kotu mai adalci tayi duba da irin laifukan da tsohuwa deluwa ta aikata,wadanda dole saita anshi hukunci,domin ya zama ixna ga yan baya masu yunkurin aikata aiki irin nata,don haka wannan kotu mai adalci ta yankewa deluwa hukuncin daurin rai da rai agidan yari,tare da ayyukan wahala."

Dan danan kotun ta dauki hayaniya kamar kasuwa,na waiga na duba banga wanda ya nuna tausayi da jin kanta ba,domin kuwa deluwa ta dace da irin wannan hukuncin.

Gudumarsa ya duka don tsawatarwa,daker aka natsu kowa ido ga deluwa,wadda ta silale kasa ta some,babu Wanda yayi ta kanta.

Alkali ya cigaba da cewa "Alh Abbas kuwa kuto ta yanke masa hukuncin shekara guda agidan yari batare da aikin wahala ba,don ya zama ixna garesa ko wanin sa hakan zaisa ya shiga taitayinsa."

"Jafar daula kuma wannan kotu mai adalci ta duba tsaf taga munanan ayyukansa takuma yanke masa hukunci mafi dacewa ga mutane masu hali irin nasa,don haka kuo ta yanke ma jafar mani daula hukuncin *kisa ta hanyar rataya,agobe daya ga watan shabiyu shekara ta dubu biyu da shabiyu*

Yadda kasan gidan kallon wasan kwallon kafa anci kwallo haka kotun ta dauki shewa da hayaniya da murna wajen mutane,ba,a barni abaya ba kuka nake ina kuma dariya,don ban san wane zan bari ba cikinsu,don kowane ya dace nayisa  awannan rana ta farin cikina,rungume juna mukai nida anty muka saka kuka mai taba xuciya,babu abunda muke tunawa sai Abba Sadiq da mama,sai yau Allah yayi zamuga karshen wanda yayi silar ganin bayansu,wanda yayi silar tarwatsa masu family dinsu.

Hakika duk wani masoyinmu dole zai taya mu murna,dole zai yimana fatan alkairi jina nake daban yau,haka muka fito muna kuka hkr kurin inna ke bamu,itama tana share hawayenta asmau ma kukan take tana sabe da muhin,can wani waje muka samu muka zauna,muna jiran su Abba da yaya dun sun wuce can asibitin.

Saida muka gaji sannan mukai shiru,duk munyi xuru kowa da abunda yake ransa.
Nidai duk jina nake daban yau gani ga iyayena,idan na tuno yadda na gansu jinake ni da kaina zan iya kashe J,amma ba wannan damar tunda dai har shima an yake masa hukuncin da yadace dashi,mutanen da J ya kashe kan dukiya Allah kadai yasan iyakarsu.

Munanan zaune Abba ya dawo ya kwashe mu mu hudun amotarsa,inna ke tambayarsa yaya jikin nasu.

Yace"tau saidai saukin ba haushe,suna can dai ana basu kulawa idan muka ga abun yafi karfin nan din zamu san yadda za,ai mufidda su waje."

Anty tace cikin karyewar xuciya "eh Abban su don Allah kuyi wannan kokarin,hankalinmu zaifi kwanciya idan aka fitar dasu din"

"Ki natsu don Allah,insha Allah komi zai zama normal,inkina haka nadeya fa saiki kara tada mata hankali"

Nasiha yayi tayi mana da banbaki har mukaje asibitin,idona jage jage da hawaye nake leken inda suke,daki guda aka ware masu subiyun.
KO wane ansa masa oxygen,ga drip nan na shigarsu kafafunsu da kansu ansa masu wasu na,urori,gwanin ban tausayi haka na koma jikin bango ina shashsheka.

Yaya ne yadawo daga wajen likita ya iso inda muke,duk ya firgice ya koma wani kala dashi,sai naji tausayinsa ya kamani.
Muryarsa ta dishe daker magana take fita yake cewa "ku kwantar da hankalinku insha Allah komi zai zama daidai,likitan yace basa bukatar hayaniya akalla zasuyi kwana biyar ahaka,su likitocin kurin zasu ringa shiga wanjensu,sune zasu ringa basu abincin daya dace suci.
Don acikin aune aunen da akai Abba na da ciwon suger,so zasu kula dasu sosai iyakarmu muxo mu dubasu mukoma,sun ce inhar sunga da yuwuwar su turamu wajen zasuyi,don inhar aikin yafi karfin su,suna fatan bazaifi ba kuyi ta masu addua abunda suke bukata kenan ba kuka ba."
Ya fada yana jawo hannuna tare da tallabo fuskata,yasa hannu yana goge mun hawayen muryarsa adishe yace "don't worry insha Allah zasu samu lfy oky!"
Daga kaina nai kurin ina xamewa daga garesa,don duk idonsu akanmu yake ga kuma asmau nata kallonmu,banga alamun ya damu da hakan ba.

"Yanxu kenan ba zasu bari ko mutun guda ya xauna dasu ba,nidai gaskiya inajin tsoro"
Inji anty ta fada da hawaye fuskarta,yayan yace "don Allah Anty ki kwantar da hankalinki,dole akwai tsaro nasaka akawo wadanda zasu ringa tsaronsu,kuma karki mance Allah yana sane dasu shine ya karesu har suka kawo kamar yanxu,so ki natsu kibar kawo tunanin komi"

Inna ma ta kara bata baki sannan yace idan mun gansu mu wuce mutai gida dare yana yi,don lokacin karfe shidda da rabi mituna kadan za,a fara kiran sallah.

Munaji muna gani muka barsu nan asibitin,amman kamar xuciyata da ruhina nabaru gangar jikince kurin tare dani.

******

Haka muka kasance duk ranar duniya zamuxo mu dubasu,badai abari ashiga daga bakin kofar glass din nan zaka gansu ka tafi,har jikinsu ana kulawa dashi kullin akwai masu yi masu wanka,su gyare masu jikinsu.

Nikam nan nake yini sai anyi da gasken gaske sannan na tafi gida,likitocin sunce ana samun cigaba sosai mudai mudage da addua,don so ake yanxu kafafun su sudawo da aiki amman sai anyi masu wani aiki na kusan miliyan goma,amman ba nan kasar za,ai ba spain za,ayishi shima kuma sai sun dawo cikin hayyacinsu tukun,shiyasa ma suka kara kwanaki biyar akan wanda suka fada.
Ina kwance kan cinyar anty bacci ya daukeni ban sani ba,saboda dawowa ta kenan daga asibitin.
Can cikin baccina nake jiyo maganganu so sai na yi kamar baccin nakeyi,na cigaba da sauraren me suke fada,yaya ne da Anty suke magana akan aikin da za,ai ma su Ammer...............

"Kana ganin babu matsala,nifa inajin tsoro garin neman gira arasa ido,tunda dai sun da assurance din cewar banda kafafun jikinsu lafiya lau yake ni ina ganin ahkr da kafar kurin,muna sonsu ahaka"

Girgiza kai yayi yace "anty bafa sunce zasu mutu bane,babu wanda yasan ranar mutuwar wani,koma muma ba finsu rai mukai ba!likitan yace kafafunsu zasu iya cigaba da aiki amman dole sai anyi wannan surgery din,kiyi hkr kisama ranki natsuwa ki kuma bawa deeyah kwarin guiwa,tafi kowa bukatarsu ahalin yanxu so kiyi hkr kurin ayi din,tunda muna da halin biyan ayi fiye da haka ma muna da halinsu."

Ajiyar xuciya taja tace "shikenan,ubangiji Allah yasa ayi asa,a yasa iyakar wahalar kenan,ya batun tafiyar kenan?kana ganin inshirya muje tare koko ya kace"

Girgiza kai yayi kafin yayi murmushi yana shafa keya yace "nida deeyah zamu tai ku kunanan gida"

Wani Kallo take masa kafin tace "mekake nufi?ga matarka saika tafi da wadda ba matarka ba"

Murmushin yayi yace "ai itama zata zama matar tawa,kafin mutai za,a daura mana aure"

Xaro ido tayi tana kallonsa tace "ban gane ba,pretty tace maka zata aureka?kokuwa cewa tai tana sonka?"

"Wohoho Anty,ki goya mun baya mana,itama fa tana so kurin jaman aji take,kinga koda anyi borene zatai na dan lokaci ta dayaye saboda tana sona"

Duka ta kaima sa bisa baya tace "to ba dani ba,don haka fita daga idona inhar kai zaka to saidai katai da matarka,inkuwa bazaka da itaba itama deeyah bazata bika ba"

Marairaice fuska yayi yace "haba anty,haba anty,meyasa kikeson wahalar dani akan wannan yarinyar ne wai?koda yaushe ita kike goyon baya ni bandani,shin baki yarda dani bane kome"

"Ba wai ban yarda dakai bane,shaidan nake tsoro"

"Uhmmm Anty kenan,da ace shaidan zai galaba akaina saboda son danake mata,da tuni ankashe boss din baki sani ba,saboda har tsakar dare idan naji kewarta ina xuwa dakinta,na ganta harna tasheta abacci.kinga kuwa baki sani ba don haka nidai kurin kiyi wa Abbansu maganar nan"

"Musaddiq bafa zaka aureta babu yardarta ba,don haka sainayi magana da ita oky?"

Langabe kai yayi yace kamar zai kuka "wlh inkika biyeta cewa zatai aa,ba kuma don bata sona ba kurin sotake taga ina azabtuwa"

Rike baki tayi tace "wai yaushe ka lalace haka ina kunyar dana sanka da ita khalipa"

Dariya yayi ya mike yace "wlh itace tasa duk na koma haka,kawai kubani ita kuhuta kuma"

Kwantar da kaina tayi saman filon kujera tace"saina yi shawara da inna,da abbansu komi muka yanke zakaji oya out karka tadata"

Matsowa yayi kusadani yace "kibarni ko kissing din for head din ta nayi"

Filo ta dauka ta jefesa dashi yafice yana dariya.

Episode  .41&42

Tunda na samo na gudu daga part din antyn na kife kaina agado ina dogon tunani,tabbas ba tun yanxu yaya yake sona ba nasan wannan,girman kaida ganin kamar zan raina sa yasa ya kasa fada mun,nima na riga na yardar ma kaina cewar ina sonsa ina kaunarsa matuk'a,amman bazan iya ajiye kunyata ta y'a mace na fad'a masa cewar ina sonsa ba,wannan ba daidai bane aganina.
Sannan duk da tarin kaunar da nake masa bazan yarda mu sakeyin wani auren ba tare da soyayyar juna ba,don ban shirya yin zaman auren damukai abaya dashi ba.akullin ina hango mana rayuwa mai dadi mai cike da so da tsantsar kauna ce zata gudana tsakanin mu,don me bazai iya xuwa ya durk'usa yace mun yana sona ba,to nikuma ko zamuyi shekarun dabino ahaka nima bazan iya fad'a ba.

Kuma Koda aurensa akaina ko babu wlh saina je spain insha Allah,gani ga iyayena inajin guminsu bazan iya zauna wa suna wata duniya ni ina wata ba bansan halin da suke ciki ba,don haka zan samu anty da maganar ni bama na son ayi auren har sai iyayena sun warke abunda zance kenan.

Ban sake fita cikin gidan ba saboda daman sadda nashigo magrib tayi,nayi sallata,ban ma jima da gama magrib din ba aka kira isha,i sai kawai na tada sallar ina idarwa nayi karatun qur,ani sannan nayi wa su Abba doguwar addua ta neman samun nasara akan aikin da za,ai masu na jima sosai ina masu addua kafi nima nayi tawa na tashi na tub'e hijab din na wurgar naje na canza kayana xuwa na bacci.

Haka na kwanta badan ina jin baccin ba,gaba daya yaya ya kanai naye xuciyata ya hanani sak'at,tunda naji yana gaya ma anty yana shigo mun d'aki da dare yasa na sakama kofar key,don nasan farillane idan bai xoba yau.

Karfe daya ta buga banyi bacci ba sai juyi nakeyi,inmirgina nan in mirgina can,banyi aune ba naji kurin anbude kofata anshigo ahaukace na tashi da yar rigata iya guiwa mai siririn hannu,hasken daya kunna yasa na sauke wata nannauyar ajiyar,na dafe goshina na zauna dab'as gefen gadona,don takaicine ya kamani nama mance da irin shigar dake jikina.
Jingina bayansa yayi da k'ofa yana kare mata kallo,tunda yake da ita yaune ya ga zahirin jikinta,ashe daman haka take da kyakkyawan jiki lumshe ido yayi yana jin duk gab'b'ansa suna sacewa,jikinsa yana mutuwa baisan haka take ba da bai shigo ba,don bai tab'a samunta a irin shigarba...............d'ago kai nayi ina dubansa cikin jin haushi nace "tsakani da Allah yaya meyasa kake haka?kana sha,awar ka bani tsoro rayuwarka!shin ta yama ka shigo bayan nida kaina na saka key na rufe k'ofata?"

Ajiyar xuciya kurin yake ja don shikadai yasan abunda yake ji,kasa magana yayi ya xuba mata shanyanyun idanunsa da suka lumshe,gab'a daya ta canza masa key din dake hannunsa kurin ya d'aga mata alamun dai wani ya samo.

Nace "to me kazo kayi d'akina karfe d'aya yaya?ga matarka acan halalinka tunda nidai babu igiyar ka ko d'aya akaina"

Muryar sa ta zama kamar k'ank'ara yace "kiyi hkr nima sai yanxu nayi dana sanin shigowar,ban zaci samunki a haka ba"

Nai sauri kallon kaina gabana ya fad'i,daga ni sai rigar jikina sai pant don ko bra cirewa nai kamar mai ciwon wuya haka na d'ago ina kallonsa,idonsa ya sake lumshewa ya rungume hannunsa ga k'irji.

Girarsa ya d'aga mun na sadda kai kurin,don banda bakin magana kunyar duniya ta kamani,na wai waya kozan ga dan mayafi babu ko hanky akan gadona dole saina tashi naje wajen wadrop,kuma da d'an nisa daga inda nake zaune,cinyoyina kuma duk awaje haka k'irjina zai yi rawa idan nayi wani motsi mai k'arfi.

Daskarewa nai waje guda ban motsa ba,ban kuma sake d'aga ido na kallesa ba.

Ajiyar xuciya yaja da d'an k'arfi yace "magana naxo muyi mai muhimmanci,amman bazata yuwu yanxu ba mubata xuwa gobe ko?."

Kaina na d'aga kamar k'adangare yaja numfashi yace "rigar ta miki kyau"

Kasa ansashi nai ya bud'e k'ofar yasake cewa "I'm sorry pls"

Nan ma kan na d'aga yamun saida safe yafice yaja k'ofar,fad'awa nai kangadon ta baya fuskata na kallon rufin d'akin na dage kaina ina ajiyar xuciya.

"Ni shashasha ce,wohoho banji dadi ba ko kad'an,amman ai shiyaja nidai ai narufe k'ofata gobema yasake biyoni irin wannan lokacin.

Washe gari da safe ina gama karya wa nashirya zanje asibiti.
Anty tace "inkin dawo inada magana dake"

Na rausayar dakai nace "oky Anty na zan dawo da wuri ai,aramun key din motar Abba"

"Aikuwa kinyi missing yaje kaduna zai taho dasu sharifa sunyi hutu"

Cikin jin dad'in maganar nace "kai amman naji dad'i wlh,nayi missing dinsu"

Inna tace  "gidan ya kara dad'i yara ai rahmane agida"

Nace"eh inna wlh,to anty kiban na hawan taxi"

Asmau tace"kibari yaya yanxu zai fito can ya nufa sai kutai tare."
Nasha mur nace "ban xuwa dashi nidai"

Harara anty ta maka mun "toni kuma banda kudin taxi din,inbacin rigima ga sauki kike son saikin wahalar da kanki"

Na sunb'uri baki na wuce waje nabarsu.

Bakin motar na ja natsaya ina jiransa riga sa siket ne na atamfa,sunyi bala,in fitar da shap din coffee color ce da zanen flawers red sai d'igon fari ajiki,tayi mun kyau sosai na yafa gyalena red da takalmana suma red,wayace kurin hannuna.

Tunda ya fito ya kafeta da ido ya kasa d'auke wa yau d'in wani kyau tayi masa na musamman,wani sabo. Shaki yaji yana ratsashi ahankali ya taka ya isa inda take.

Kasa kallonsa nai don ban mance abunda ya faru ajiya ba,nadai kalli wani waje nace masa ina kwana.
Ya ansa cikin kulawa ya bude motar muka shiga,ya tada muka bar gidan.

Mun dauki hanya babu mai magana sai radio motar tana fidda sautin wakar india mai suna *hamari adhuri khahani* can kasa ahankali,irin mai shiga ran nan ga k'amshin freshener da mayataceccen k'amshin turarensa ya hadu suna bada wani yanayi na musamman,wani irin feeling ne yake ratsani wanda baki bazai iya fad'arsa ba.
Shirun muka cigaba dayi saidai time to time yakan jiyo ya kalleni ta juya,nima d'ina satar kallonsa amman ta gefen ido wayata kurin naketa juyawa,saboda na rasa abunyi jina nake kamar yau muka fara keb'ewa ni dashi.

Tsinto maganarsa nai yace mun cikin sanyin murya wadda ta mun mugun dad'i,har saida na kasa hkr na kallesa.

"Zaki dade asibitin ne?"

Rausayar da kai nayi nace " I Can't say, may be indad'e inaso nayi magana dasu ko yaya,saboda duk sanda zanje bacci suke"

"Oky"

Jin baice komi ba yasa nace "akwai wani abunne"

Yana juya sikiyarin zamu shiga kwana yace "kin mance nace "ina da magana dake,gashi kince bakida lokaci so zan bari sai kin samu"

Yadda yake maganar kamar wani abun tausai,gashi muryar tayi laushi ina kallon hannuna nace masa"shikenan idan na samu suna bacci sai muyi mgnr,naga kamar tana da muhimmanci"

Maimakon ya ansamun sa baki saidai ya ansa mun sa idanunsa,ya lunshesu kurin.

Mamakin sa nake wannan sanyin da yake duk namiye,ni baisan sake kashe mun jiki yake ba ahakan.

Mun isa muka samu waje mukayi parking,ya rigani fitowa yazo ya bude mun,kallonsa nai shima yana kallona gaba d'aya idonsa ta canza mun shima,sun koma wani iri kad'an kad'an ya lunshesu.

"Thank u"
Nace masa ya bani hanya muka nufi cikin asibitin,mamaki nake wai nida yaya yau duk mun koma kamar bak'in juna,meke damunsa haka?

Mun tadda baccin kau sukeyi.
Na yamutsa fuska alamun banji dadi ba na kallai cikin shagwab'a nace masa "yaya yauma baccin suke,nifa yana takurani wannan baccin nasu wlh"

Wani murmushi yayi mun da saida naji yan cikina sun motsa,kyau yayiwa fuskarsa sosai yace mun.

"Karkiyi kuka to don Allah,fuskarki zata b'aci nikuma bana so"

D'aga kai nai na samu waje na zauna ina kallon su ta glass,nurse ta fito daga cikin dakin muka gaisa nake tambayarta ya jikinsu tayi murmushi tace "madam yanxun nan sukasha magani suka koma bacci,naso kisame su ido biyu don nasan zakiji dad'i"

Nayi kamar zan kuka nace "amman kinsan zanzo ko!"

Tace"I'm sorry,time din shan maganin su naga zai wuce shiyasa kiyi hkr,next time insha Allah zaki samesu ido biyu har kuyi fira"

Jinjina kai nai kurin ita kuma ta wuce.

Yaya yace "bari naje naga likitan su kozaki rakani"

Nace "ehy"

Hannunsa ya miko mun,na kallesa sai yayi murmushi ya maida abunsa cikin aljihu kurin.
Sannan na tashi muka jera muka nufi office dinsa.

Bayani yayi mana sosai akan yadda jikinsu Abba yace xuwa yanxu sun gama duk wani check off da zasuyi masu inda suka ga matsala an maganinta,yanxu matsalar kafafunsu ce kurin kuma itama insha Allah da antura su spain din cikin sati biyu xuwa uku komi ya zama tarihi insha Allah.

Wannan bayanin yayi matukar samun farin ciki har saida ta bayyana akan fuskata,sukai musabaha da yaya muka fito.

"Kinga yadda kika kara kyau kuwa,dakin san yadda murmushi da dariya suke yiwa fuskarki kyau da baza kiso ki daina ba"

Hararar wasa nai masa nace "yaya ka k'ware wajen yabona,anya ba xolayata kake ba".

Key yasa yana bude motar yace "inma xolayarki nake xuciyarki ce zata baki ansa akan hakan."

Shiru nai ina nazarin sa nasan gaskiya ya fad'a,kobanyi murmushi da dariya ba nasan ina yin kyau amman idan nayi sun suna kara mun kyau,saidai ni banzan tantance irin kyan da suke yi mun ba har sai idan wanine ya fad'a.

Shiga nai na zauna sannan muka tafi har mukaje inda zamu babu mai magana,wani wurin hutawa ne ya had'u matuka waje ya tanadar mana maikyau ga kujeru nan na mutun biyu,wurin ank'awata sa da flowers ga grass Capet awajen duk inda ka kalla gwanin sha,awa.

Tunda na zauna duk sainaji ni wani daban,duk atakure nakejina nama kasa daga idona na kallesa.
Wanda nasan saboda kallon da yake munne yasa duk na rude,banda tsefe yatsuna babu abunda nake.

*"Nadeeyah"*
Najiyi sassanyar muryarsa ta fad'a,a slow na d'ago idanuna na sauke akansa,d'age girarsa guda yayi yana mun wanu shu,umun kallo,na juyar da kaina wani wajen nace masa cikin muryata data canza ban san dalili ba.

"Na,am"

Jingina bayansa yayi da kujerar da yake kai ya saka hannunsa ya tallabe hab'arsa,sannan yafara magana a tausashe.

"So nake muyi aure nidake"

Idona na xuba masa na dan lokaci kafin nace "yaya!aure kace?"

Ya bani ansa kai tsaye "ehy aure nake nufi"

Na jinjina kai nace "meyasa zamuyi aure?wannan karon kuma wace manufar kake son cimmawa akaina"

Tashi yayi ya xuba lemo a cup wanda yasa aka kawo mana ya turo gabana,kanan yace "wannan karon babu manufa aciki,hakanan nakeson muyi auren"

Ban san ma wani irin murmushi ya kubce mun ba na kallesa shima ni yake kallo,ya rungume hannu.
Saida na gama murmushina mai kama da dariya nace "yaya kenan,to miye amfanin hakan?naga nidakai ba mai son kowa to aure name zamuyi?aa ni bana so wancen ma da mukai ya wadatar."

Iska yaja sannan yace mun.
"Bafa wasa nake ba deeyah,da gaske nake kuma amuncewarki nake so.inso samune ayisa kafin tafiya spain din nan ta xo."

Nad'an sha mur nace "kaga yaya bafa zan amunce ba,ai na dauka kuma anwuce wannan maganar,tun wancen karon anyi baka so bana so kana da matarka wadda kakejinta duk duniya ba kamarta,to k'arin me kuma?inma zaka k'ara to ka nemo wata bani ba."

Tashi yayi yaja kujerarsa tazo daidai kusa dani har idonsa yafara canxa wa yace "meyasa badake ba?saboda baki sona?saboda ban cancanci zama mijinki ba kome?"

Yadda ya sunkuyo yake mun magana abun yana sani jin wani iri,na d'anja kaina gefe,bazan iya furta ban sanshi ba kalmar tayi mun nauyi awannan karon.

"Kikai shiru,bazaki iya cewa baki sona ba ko?right Amman meyasa nazo maki da tayi zaki watsa mun k'asa ga ido,kina sona meyasa kike nuna ba haka ba,kinga inhar fa ba aure mukai ba tofa bazaki bini spain ba."

Wani abu yaxo mun wuyana na b'acin rai nadai danne kurin,na jiyo cikin d'an bacin rai nace masa "yaya!na tab'a fada maka ina sonka?akanme xaka dameni akan haka,idan har kai kaji baka iya furta mun soyayya ka b'ige ga kame kame akanme ni zan furta,to bana wani sonka ni inma ka dauka sonka nake to xuciyarka ta yaudareka,baka ga daidai ba.kuma wlh saina je spain da tsiyama bada arxik'iba."

Iska ya fesar yana kallona yace "nifa ina nema maki mafita ne kurin,don inba da aurena akanki ba babu inda zaki saidai kigama haukarki ki hkr."

Nakai karshen hkrina najiyo nace masa "to bana yin auren dakai,ehy nace banayi kuma spain sainaje,bari kaji yaya bama kai ba kowaye bai isa na auresa babu soyayya tsakani na da dashi ba,so kake na amunce ka sake gana mun azaba irin wancen karon saboda baka k'auna...............yatsunsa biyu yad'ora bisa lips dina,idonsa yayi wani iri ya girgiza mun kanshi yace cikin wani sauti daya rikita mun lissafi.

"Plssssss don't say it."

Numfashi nakeja kamar asthma ta zata tashi nadaure saboda yadda yaya yake kallona yana narke idanu zan iya sumewa,kokuma na yi abunda ban niyya ba.

Kwace fuskata nai gefe cikin fad'a nace
"Akanme bazan ce ba,kaunar tawa yake?matarka kakeso kuma ban hanaka sonta ba,to donme zaka dameni akan wani aure,auren k'iyayua kakeson muyi?"

Sosai nake masifa ganin zan tara mana mutane yasa yace "shikenan abar maganar tashi muje gida"

Afusace na mik'e nama rigasa isa wajen motar na bud'e nashiga,ba komi ya tunxurani ba irin yadda yaya ya kasa fadar yana sona,wannan wane irin girman kaine da taurin kai,to wlh nasan matakin dauka akansa nagaji da wannan wahalar,idan yaga na kullesa dole yayi aman abunda yake cikin ransa.

Yashigo ko kallonsa banba saboda raina yakai k'ololowar b'aci,har muka je gida saidai ya d'an jiyo ya kalleni ya cigaba da tuk'insa.

Yana yin parking na bud'e afusace na buga k'ofar na wuce abuna,banma tsaya shiga can cikin gidan ba d'akina na wuce saboda bana jin zan iya sakarma kowa fuskata.

Ko ad'akin ma tunda na kwanta nake sak'awa nake kwancewa,har bansan nayi bacci ba saida akai azhar na tashi nai sallah sannan nafita don sama cikina wani abu,muka hadu da su sharifa nan fa labari ya bud'e,aka ringa bani labaran abunda suka faru da bamunan tun ina biyewa har na gaji na wuce dakin Anty,don tace tana son ganina.

Tana ta shirya kayansu ashraf cikin wadrop nashiga da sallama,ta jiyo ta ansa mun na zauna saman stool din madubi,muna gaisawa.

"Yaushe kika dawo?"

"Tare muka shigo da yaya daki na wuce bacci nai"

"Yajikin nasu yake"

Nai mata bayanin da likitan yayi mana,cikin jin dadi tace "masha Allah,ubangiji yasa adace daman akan maganar tafiyarce zan maki magana."

Ta fad'a tana rufe wadrop din ta zauna gefen gado tana fuskantata,nima na maida hankalina kanta. kanan tace "yayanki yace tare zaku tafi spain Amma idan anyi aurenku,nikuma nace ban yarda ba bazai aureki babu yardarki ba,don haka sainaji ta bakinki shin kinyarda daman kun jone ni ban sani ba."
Raina ya k'ara b'aci nace "Anty ba wani jonewa da mukai,yaya yawo kurin yake da hankulan mutane,ba wani sona yake ba kuma ni wlh bazan tab'a aurensa ba muddin bai sauke girman kansa ba yafurta abunda yake dannewa,ki rabu dashi anty dani yake magana.jira yake ni nace masa ina sonsa saboda ya daukan sakara marar aji,to ko sonsa zai kasheni anty bazan fad'a ba wlh!sannan zanje spain nai maki alkawarin kulawa da kaina yadda natai haka zan dawo insha Allah kiyarda dani"
Na fad'a ina riko hannunta.

Shiru tayi na d'an lokaci kafin tace "kina da gaskiya preety,don haka kija ajinki yadda kikeso nima nagoyi da bayan muddin bashine yafara furta yana sonki ba bance ke ki xubda ajinki na y'amace ba kifad'a masa."
Rungumeta nai don jin dadin had'in kan data bani,hira muka zauna yi tana cewa sotake taje kaduna anata kiranta a office,amma saita ga tafiyarmu nan da kwana uku masu xuwa.

*****

Shirin tafiya kurin nake duk da ba wasu iyayen kaya na tattara ba,kananu sunfi yawa don can bana kama saka manyan kaya ba,riga da wando sai dogayen riguna da jarcets dana had'a dasu.

Na tattara yaya nasaka acikin kwandon shara ko hakorina bana yarda ya gani, in abinci akeci ya ringa satar kallona kenan ina lura asmau duk hankalinta ya tashi,ni kam ko alamar na gane ma banayi,mur nakesha bana kula kowa balle ma yaga damar ganin fara,ata.

Duk Dan na kulasa haka zai ta jana da magana ko ya miko mun muhin,yadda zai samu damar mun magana ko yasa mu murmushina naki bada dama,ina gani rannan har asmau tayi fushi tatashi tabar wajen.

Saboda lokacin ina kusada ita shikuma yana gefenta muna breakfast,inna da Abba basa nan inna tana d'aki ta gama nata Abba kuwa yafice yaje kaduna.
Tea nakesha saina kware kamar walk'iya haka yataso daga inda yake zaune yaringa bubbuga bayana,ya xuba ruwa ya kafa mun ga bakina.
Ya ciro hanky dinsa yaringa gogemun bakina da rigata dana b'ata,saida na dawo natsuwata na turesa ina dalla masa harara.wanda on ready asmau tuni tabar wajen don ta cika ta batse da b'acin rai.

Har anty saida tai magana tace "ka kiyaye abunda zai tun xura matarka,kafin ka rasa gane kanta tunda Allah yasa girman kanka ya hanaka abunda ya dace"
Tana fad'ar haka tabar mu anan tafice.
Nima mik'ewar nai yayi saurin shan gabana ya zama kamar wani maraya.

"Kinga kema tafiyar zakiyi,wai yakuke son nayi da raina ne duk inda naje ba dadi"
Na wurga masa wani kallo na zagayesa na wuce abuna.

Episode  .43

Har xuwa ranar da zamu tafi bana magana da yaya,ba ma magana ba ko murmushina balle dariya ta bana yarda ya gani,na had'e masa tashoshi gefe guda Ma asmau itama fushin take dashi,ban sani ba ko bayan abunda yafaru rannan akwai abunda ya sake had'asu.

Duk inda hankalin asmau yake ya tashi ganin da gaske tafiya zai da nadeeya ba da ita ba,ta rasa gane son kai irin na yan gidan ga abun da ya kamata amman basa binsa,me zaisa ahadasa da nadeeya bacin ba aurene sukai ba,gata matarsa amman ya jibgeta kamar kayan wanki,ranta yakai k'ololuwa wajen b'aci,kominsa sai ya nuna nadeeya ta fita muhimmanci wai me nadeeya take dashi da har idonsa yake rufewa yake nuna ita ba komi bace.

Wanka ya fito yana k'ok'arin shirya wa ya zauna bakin gado yana tsane jikinsa,ya jiyo yace mata.

"Mom muhin d'an bani kayan shafa na pls"

Tsige muhin din tayi daga shan nono ta kwantar dashi,taje ta dauko cike da kufula ta dire masa kamar zata kwad'amasa mari.
D'agowa yayi yana mata kallon mamaki har ya kasa hkr yace "meke damunki ne"

Kai tsaye tace "hauka ne yake damuna"

Yamutsa fuska yayi yace "me?bakisan abunda kike fad'a ba ko?"

Cike da son neman rigima tace "Aa nasani!ai baiwuce kace haka d'in ba shiyasa na riga ka fad'a"

Girgiza kai yayi yak'i biye mata ya fara shafa mansa bai sake kulata ba,ganin bai sake ce mata komi ba yasa ta runtse idonta don abunda zatai dole saiya tunxura sa,duk da tana jin gabanta na fad'uwa..........dogon tsaki taja dauke kai.

Huking yayi daga abunda yake yatsaya yana kallonta cike da mad'aukakin mamaki,yace "Asma,u!Asma,u!Allah yasa badani kike ba,Allah yasa kuskurene kunnena baiji daidai ba."

Ita kanta saida taji tsoron abunda tai,don tana sa masaniyar babu abunda yafi tsana aduniya irin kamai tsaki.
Amman saboda son rigima da take dashi,tana son amayar da bakin cikinta kafin yatai yasa ta murje idonta tace "ba kuskure bane daidai kaji,nakasa hkr da irin abubuwan dasuke faruwa ne,haba!haba!! Meka daukan yaya?niba mace bace koko ni anfanina sai dare yayi,gaba ki daya ka gigice akanta ka susuce,inhar tana waje kowama banza ne agunka idonka rufewa yake,toni miye anfanina.tun ina kauda kai har xuciyata ta gaxa to nagaji,wlh nagaji gashinan kaiba matarka ba ba komi ba ka dauketa zakutai wata uwa duniya,Allah kadai yasan abunda zai faru acan............jikake tas tas tas har sau uku ya dauketa da kyawawan maruka guda uku,idonsa kamar jini numfashinsa har wani sizing yake yana huci.
Ta d'ago cike da kid'imewa hawaye share share tace "yaya ka mareni saboda ina fadar gaskiya?...........kamar zai cinyeta ya taso a harxuk'e yace "ehy Asmau na mareki,kuma ashirye nake dana kara maki wasu da sukafi wannan zafi,badai kina so ki gaya mun magana ba,badai idonki ya rufe da kishi ba kina fada mun maganganun da basu dace ba,to wlh tallahi zakisha mamakina kuma bari kiji idan da bakiji ba yanxu kiji da kunnenki,nadeeyar dai itace anan."
Ya nuna kirjinsa dake d'agawa ya cigaba da cewa"kuma duk yadda kike zatonta aciki ta wuce nan,tafiya kuma da ita zamuyi ba dake ba saboda iyayenta ne banaki ba,sannan kamar yadda kika dauka tafiki kima da mutunci ta fiyemunke sau dubu,don nadeeya bata iya kallona ta zageni,bata iya kallona ta fad'a mun irin munanan kalaman dakike gaya mun,badan bata da bakin fad'a ba aa ita d'in tana ganin girmana,tana ganin kimata kuma tasan kanta tana da tarbiyyar dakika rasa."

Kuka take sosai tana data sanin abunda tayi,don b'acin ran da yashiga ma yafi komi daga mata hankali,akan maganganunsa.
Ya zatai ina xatakai kishin nadeeya,kuma hakika yasan yana ba daidai ba akan abubuwa da yawa,ta gaza controlling xuciyarta ne balle tayi masa magana cikin lumana yadda zai fahimceta.
Durk'ushewa tayi yaja wani dogon tsaki ya wuceta ya idasa shirinsa,sannan yaje yadauko kaya yasaka ya jawo trolling d'insa da jikkarsa,ya durk'usa ya sunbaci muhin har lokacin ransa a matukar bace yake,yamuk'e yaja akwatin jikkar na rataye ga kafad'arsa ya nufi k'ofa ko sake kallonta baiba ya bud'e kenan yajita ta k'wak'umesa tana wani irin kuka mai tab'a rai,muryarta cikin kukan tace "kayi hkr,kayi hkr yaya zuciyata ta kasa hkr akan abubuwan dasuke wakana tsakaninku,sannan sau dayawa kana fifita lokacinta fiye dani kana fifita ta fiye dani matarka,taya kake zaton zuciyata zataji bacin nima inasonka bazan jure ba.bansan yazan ba karkatai kana wannan fushin.

Ya mance rabon da yayi wannan fushin,jiyake kamar zai aman xuciyarsa.
Bazai iya mata magana ba awannan halin ba,don kirjinsa yayi masa wani irin nauyi jiyowa yayi yarik'e kafad'unta ya sunbaci goshinta kurin sannan ya fice yabarta tana silalewa kasa tana cigana sa kukanta.

Duk muna compound din gidan yafito idonsa sanye cikin glass bak'i don ma kar aga idonsa ya canza sai yakara fiddo masa ainhin kyansa,duk da glass din bai hana aga bacin ransa ba.

Driver zai jamu nidashi saiya ya dawo da motar,don sai mun biya asibiti mun dauki su Abba tukun.
Jina nake cikin farin ciki yau zanyi magana da iyayena,cikon farincikina.

Tunda na hangosa sai naji gabana ya fad'i shan mur din dana gani akan fuskarsa,alamun dai ransa b'ace yake duk da haka yamun kyau sosai gaisawa sukai da su Inna da Anty,Abba ma suka gaisa inajin inna nacewa yo ina Asmaun,bai bata ansa ba.
Ya bud'e baya inda nake zaune ya shigo d'aga mana hannu sukai tayi har muka fice daga gidan.............sai naji ni wani iri yasha mur d'insa yaja can gefe ko kallo inda nake zaune baiba,don glass din tagar yake kallo kamar baisan da wani acikin motar ba.
Sai naji duk na muzanta gaba d'aya nima saina kama kaina,ban kulasa ba nadai daure na sauke girman kaina nace masa.

"Ina kwana"

Ba tare daya jiyowa ba ya ansa ni da.
"Lafiya"
Najin jina kai ina kara jin raina yana baci idan can ta had'osu ni miye nawa aciki,can kuje ku k'arata hanyar k'araye ai sunfi kusa.

Mun isa asibitin mun tadda har anfiddo su Abba suna kan well chair anshirya su cikin shiga ta alfarma,aguje na fito daga motar ban dire ko ina ba sai jikin Ammer na saka kuka na rungumeta tsam ajikina,jinake kamar na tsaga na sata aciki,ina kukan ina cewa "Ammer ashe dai darabon zamu gana,ashe idanuna zasuyi tozali daku?ban zaci zan ganku da rayuwata ba,nagodema Allah Ammer na godema Allah daya nuna mani wannan rana."
Shafa kaina kurin take tana bubbuga bayana Abban naji yana cemun cikin muryarsa ta manyantaka "toni baza,a rungumenin ba koko Ammern ki kadai kikai missing"

Sakinta nai ina hawaye had'e da dariya nafad'a kansa shima ina kuka hade da dariya,nace "Aa Abba ku dukanku nai missing,ina sonku ina matukar kaunarku kullin cikin begen ganin ku nake,nagodema Allah Abba wlh na godemasa."

Dariya yayi yana d'agoni yace ma yaya "Saddiq haka ka shagwab'a k'anwar taka da alamun dai ruwa ruwa ce"

Sai lokacin yayi murmushi yace "ai Abba indai itace tama fi haka"

Yar hira aka bude awajen ina jikin Ammer ina mata surutu,tana shafa kaina tana biyemun shima can shida Abba hirar suke.
Har likitan yaxo yasaka mana hannu ga wata takadda yayi mana fatan Alkairi,har bakin mota ya rakomu aka shigar da su Ammer a motarsu.
Muma muka shiga tamu aka nufi airport damu,yanxun ma bai kulani ba nima ban kulasa ba don yau jinkai yakeji nima kuma inaji da nawa.

Jirginmu ya tashi karfe goma na safe ina mak'ale jikin Ammer babu abunda nake bata irin labarin kalar rayuwar danayi agidan J,tun danai wayau har xuwa girmana da mafarkin da na ringayi da ita. Tace "Allah kenan pretty,yaga halin da muka tsinci kanmu sannan yaga ke din andoraki abisa turb'ar da bata dace ba kuma zata illata rayuwarki,shiyasa ya ringa kawo makini abaccinki har kika samu ilimin da kika rasa abaya,hakika ina yiwa Allah godiya duk da uk'ubar da muka shiga,tunda dai ke kina cikin koshin lfy kuma ya had'aki da y'an uwanki ya gama mana komi,daman ance komi yayi zafi maganinsa Allah yauga karshen J da mahaifiyarsa dake dorasa bisa hanya,inhar kana da hankali saikayi taka tsantsan da rayuwa domin kuwa yar k'alilance inkayi da kyau kagani a mizaninka inbakai da kyauba nan ma zaka gani,shi Allah ai ba azzalumin sarki bane gashi ya kub'utar damu kuma ya raya mu har gashi muna sake haduwa.ina alfahari da Aysher da sadiq sun mana komi,sun kula mana dake sun tsaya tsayin daka wajen ganin bayan mak'iyinmu,har sukai nasarar kaisa k'asa mukuma muka kub'uta."

Lamo nai ajikinta ina saurarenta tabbas haka ne su anty sun taka rawar gani acikin rayuwarmu nida iyayena,more especially ma yaya yashiga had'ari duk dan saboda mu,ya tsallake rijiyoyi da dama da bayansa.................jinai tana cewa "bamu da abun da zamu basu don sakayya,don kuwa bana jin kud'i zasu iya nuna irin godiyar da mukai akai,."

Nace"Ammer muyi masu addua ita kadai zata fi kuma suma sunyine don mukaidai muka rage masu,wannan kokarin inbasu ba ba wanda zai iya mana so kidauka xumunci sukai mawa sakayyarsu na wajen Allah"

"Haka ne pretty Allah yasaka masu da aljannah yajik'an iyayensu da rahma yasa Aljannace makomarsu"

Na amsa da ameen ahaka bacci ya daukeni har bansan time din da jirgin ya sauka ba.
Duk da natab'a xuwa spain lokacin ina ss one nida J na rakosa mukai wata guda,amman yanxu sai naga kamar ba k'asar ba don ansake samun cigana sosai,na jinjina kai nace kowace kasa na cigana banda nigeria komeye dalili?

Taxi mukai dauka guda daya,da taimakon mai taxi din muka shigar dasu amotar yaya ya shiga gaba,nidasu abaya sai kayanmu a cikin but.

Direct asibitin muka nufa don yau akeson gabatar da patient din ga likitocin,don adauki statement dinsu ayi documenting kafin su saka ranar da zasuyi aikin.
Muna isa suka tarbemu cikin karrama patient aka kawo well chairs aka dauki su Abba akai,muna biye dasu abaya har xuwa inda aka tanadar yaya yaje aka bude file sannan yaba likitan takaddar da wancen likitan na nigeria yabamu,yasa hannu shima sannan ya bada umurnin aba su abba daki.
Yaya ya tsaya wajen likitan nikuma nabi nurses din da zasu bawa su Abba d'akin.
Babban d'akine mai gadaje guda biyu gefe da gefe,har tv keda akwai ga toilet mai kyau komi na buk'ata ansaka aciki,ga Ac nan har biyu d'akin gwanin birgewa kamar bana asibiti ba.

Nurse suka dorasu sannan suka yi masu wata allura suka tafi,nace "nidai Ammer anan zan zauna daku bazan iyayin nesa daku ba wlh"

Abba yace "ina kika ga wajen zama nan daughter?ainan duk wanda kika gani lalurace ta kawosa,kibi yayanki kuje masauki tunda basa barin y'an jinya bama suda wajen kwanan su anan."
Na shagwab'e fuska kamar zan kuka Ammer ta shafa kaina cikin kauna da kulawa tace "kullin fa zakixo ki ganmu,miye na damuwa bafa asibitin kasarku bace pretty na!anan basu yarda da wasu y'an jinya ba.su nurses ne masu kula da patient d'insu,komi yakeso sune zasuyi masa kuma inkince nan zaki zauna ina zaki kwanta."

Kwanciya nai bisa cinyarta ina cewa cikin shagwab'a "ni Ammer anan kusada ke zan ringa kwanciya,nidai kibarni tare daku don Allah"
Na fad'a inajin kwalla sun xubo mani.
Hannu Abba ya mik'omun na isa wajensa na kwanta jikinsa,ina sake jin wata soyayyarsu tana shiga raina.
"Haba mana daughter na,yazaki d'aga mana hankali kinga mufa muna cikin koshin lafiya,inbacin kaffafun da basa iya mik'ewa bama jin komi tare damu,ki kwantar da hankalinki kibi yayanki zai kula dake muma kuma za,a kula damu basa wasa da ayyukansu,kuma yadda kikasan jarirai haka suke tattalin patient dinsu,inhar kina son mu samu natsuwa nida Ammernki to kigoge wad'annan hawayen ki amunce zakibi yayanki masaukinku"

Badan naso ba don ko kad'an bana son nabisa,saboda nasan kusancinmu zai yawa ina gudun shaid'an ina jikin Abba yayan yashigo,ya ajiye wata leda yaja d'aya daga cikin kujerun dake cikin d'akin ya zauna.
Ko kallona baiba yace wa Abba "ga wad'anan wasu magungunane zasu fara doraku akai na kwana biyar,sannan su fara maku wasu Allurai suma na kwana biyar d'in kafin su ga yanayin kafafun sai suyi aikin,sun buk'aci kudin abincin ku naje na biya da duk sauran kud'aden da suka buk'ata suma na biya,sunce kud'in aikin sai za,ayi sannan."

"Sannu sadiq,Allah yayi Albarka yakuma tsareka,kar Allah ya gajiyar daku akan ayyukan alkairan dakukeyi"

Ya na kallon kasa ya ansa da "Ameen"

Ammer ma tace "Allah ya biyaka da Alkairinsa kaji yarona"

Murmushi yayi yace "ameen Ammer,yanxu baku buk'atar komi sonake naje na d'an kwanta don kaina ke sarawa"

"Aa babu komi wlh,nasan yanxu zasu kawo mana abinci kuna iya tafiya ai lokacin hutun yayi dare yayi yanxu"
Inji Abba.

Tashi nai daga jikin Abban naje ga Ammer na rakub'e jikinta,mik'ewa yayi yace "to Abba saida safe idan mun shigo"

"To sadiq kama k'anwarka kutai don naga alamar rikici takeji"

Tun da muka baro gida har nan sai yanxu ya d'aga idanunsa ya kalleni,jinayi kamar ya sokeni a cikin xuciyata kasa iya kallonsa nai,don sainaji idanuna sunyi rauni.

"Sake ta kixo mu wuce."
Ya fad'a babu wasa acikin muryarsa rau rau nayi da idona ina kallon Ammer.
"Kibar kallona tunda bakijin lallashi ke"

Yar jakata na d'auka nayi gaba na barsu.
Abba yace masa "kayi hkr da ita don Allah"

Murmushi yayi yace "ai nasaba Abba da rigimarta karka damu saida safe"

Ina bakin k'ofar ina jiransa don bazan ma iya gane hanyar da muka biyoba,fitowa yayi maimakon yamun magana sai kurin yayi gaba,dole nabisa abaya har muka isa inda muka ajiye kayan mu trolley na danasa sai jikkarsa,ni daman trolley guda nai na tsakiya.
Yadau nasa yayi gaba nima naja nau nabisa,iyakar k'ulewa nakai amman na danne ina ta bawa xuciyata hkr,don idan nace zanyi magana yadda nakejin b'acin rai akansa abun bazai kyauba.

Bakin titi muka fito ya tari taxi muka saka kayan sannan muka shiga,kamewa nai gefe ina kallon hanya inaji yana gaya masa hotel zai kaimu,wani abu yatsaya mun.
Hotel sai kace wasu yan iska wannan ko kad'an bai dace ba,nidana zauna dashi muka d'ai na gwammace kwanan saman tayils din asibitin,amman su Abba sun kasa ganewa.
Inata zancen xucina nikadai har muka isa,sakin bakinai ina kallon hotel din haduwa kenan jinjina kai nai don ba karya dole kayi kyauyanci awajen.
Ban San ma tafiyar mai taxi d'in ba,saida naga yaya zai shige ya kyaleni sannan nai saurin binsa ashe banga komi ba saida muka shiga,yaje wajen masu biyan kud'in ina gefensa.
Naji yace masu magana da turanci daki mai 2 bedroom yake so,sai lokacin na d'anji dadi key aka bashi bayan ya biya sannan yayi gaba na cigaba da binsa kamar jela.

Babban dakine falo ne da kujeru masu kyau ga babbar Tv an k'awata falon sosai,ga haske kamar safiya akwai korido dazaka bi shine zai kaika har bedrooms din guda biyu,suna kallon juna inka d'anyi gaba d'akin motsa jikine shima yayi kyau.
Dakunan baccin gadone madaidaici mai lallausar katifa,ga mirror da wadrop ga kuma toilet ga manyan tagoge da zaka ringa hangen gari,komi need.
Wannan ce magana ta biyu da yayi mani.

"Which one do u like?"

Saboda haushinsa danake ji yasa ko kulasa ban ba nadai bude wanda ke kusada ni na shige na barsa nan.
Abunda ya tunxura ni kafin yau kullin neman hanyar dazai mun magana na kulasa yake,idan ina waje kokari yake yaga na kallesa ko nayi dariya koda badashi nake ba.
Amman yau daga ta had'oshi da matarsa shine zai had'a dani waje Fushin,saika ce sadda sukai abun ina nan.to ya dad'e bai daina mun magana ba har mahdi ya bayyana.

Kaya nake cirewa na watsar bisa gadon cike da haushi nashiga barth room din,komin wanka ne na cika shi da ruwa mai d'umi na fiddo sabulan wankana na xuba,na shige ciki nai lub.
Ban san meyasa fushinsa ya dameni ba,bana minty guda banja tsaki ba aganina ni menai masa zai had'a dani yana fushin,so nake yaringa bina ni kuma na cigaba da daure masa fuska har sai yagaji yaxo ya furta abunda yake ma girman kai.

Tunda yashiga dakinsa ya fad'a kan gado ya rude idonsa rigimarsu da Asmau take dawo masa,ransa yakai k'ololuwa wajen b'aci tabbas ta fad'i gaskiya yana fifita deeya sau da yawa akanta,bakuma don ita bai sonta ba,aa soyayyar deeya ce ta danne tata yakan kasa danne ransa akanta,idan yana tare da ita jiyake kamar xuciyarsa zata fito saboda bugun da take akan kaunarta.amman har yau ta kasa ganewa,sotake saiya mata explain da bakinsa sotake saiya roketa tasoshi, baisan yaushe ne hakan zata faru ba duk sadda yayi yunk'urin yin hakan zuciyarsa rawa take,shi baima tab'a tsintar kansa a irin halin matsananciyar soyayya haka ba.
Gashi yau b'acin ran asmau yasa yasake dorata akan fushin da take dashi,gaba d'aya su duka jiyayi haushin su yakeji yadda suke neman su rikita masa lissafi.
Amman kam yau Asmau takai sa kololuwa wajen bacin rai,ajiyar xuciya yayi yana tashi yaje yayi wanka yasa tree quater da body hug yafeshi jikinsa da turaruka,ya gyara gashinsa sannan ya fita don ya samo masu abunda zasuci.

Salolina nayi sannan na shirya duk da yunwar dake cina,
Rigata ta bacci nasa marar nauyi mai siririn hannu,nayi wankan turare na gyara gashina na daure da ribon,na saka yar hular rigar ta bacci mai raga.
Na haye kan gadon ina saka sabo kayi ga wayata don inkira su anty,ina zaune inata kokowar sakawa na kasa naji anbude kofara anshigo..............bin k'ofar nai da kallo muka had'a ido,nai saurin d'auke nawa na cigaba da kokowar saka layina.
"Assalamu alaiki"

Ban dubesa ba na ansa masa "wa,alaika salam"
Yatako ya iso cikin d'akin ya tsaya agefena yana ta kallon yadda nake kokowa da sim da wayata,ajiye plet din daya shigo dashi yayi ya zame ya zauna kusa dani gefen gadon,jinai kirjina ya amsa saboda makirin turarensa daya doki hancina,runtse ido nai na bud'e yasa hannu ya anshi wayar ya sake mikowa inbashi layin.mik'a masa nai ina d'a ja baya don rage kusancinmu,cikin abunda baifi second uku ba yasaka mun yana mik'omani.
Na ansa nafara setting din wayar,inajin mayun idanunsa suna yawo akaina karfin hali kawai nake,don kaina ya toshe ban sanma ina nake shiga ba acikin wayar,addua nake araina yabar kallona don yana rikitani.
Amman banga alamar hakan ba,saima gyara zama da yayi ganin haka yasa nai saurin mik'ewa zan bar wajen don abun ya isan.
Caraf ya cafke hannuna yadawo dani ya zaunar,numfashina naja kad'an ina kokarin ganin ban kallesa ba.

"Ga abinci nasan kinajin yunwa"

Kwace hannuna nai nace ina kauda kai "ajiye anan zanci anjima"

"Okay good night"

Kallonsa nai ta kasan ido naga yana murmushi ahankali,ban san sadda na d'ago na dalla masa harara ba.

Muryarsa da murmushin yace "menai maki kuma?karki sake hararta nidai"

Salon da yayi maganar dashi dole kaji wani abu,nasake hararar tasa na dauke kai nace "pls kana iya tafiya"

"Zantai basai kin koran ba,amman kiringa sanin kayan da zaki saka anan tunda kinga ba gida kike ba,kuma kinsan da wanda kike tare ai ko?"

Kallonsa nai shima ya xuba mun nasa,harda canza salon kallon.
Sai nakasa d'auke idona kamar ana jan idanuna cikin nasa,duk yadda naso d'aukewa na kasa saidai naji iska cikin idon sannan nasan abunda nai,ashe hure mun ido yayi.
Kunyar abunda nai naji na juya baya kurin ina jan numfashi,na runtse idona nikadan nasan yadda kirjina yake ansawa.

"kindai ji abunda nace ko,wannan shigar inkina yinta akwai matsala Are u hearing me"

Kaina na d'aga kawai nakasa jiyowa.

"Okay good night"

Banjiyo ba saida naji yarufe kofar sannan na kwanta ina jan ajiyar xuciya mai nauyi.
