Episode  .44

Saboda gajiyar da nayi ajiya da rashin baccin da nai da wuri yasa na samu dogon bacci sosai,har na mance da sallar asuba.
Inaji alam yana bugawa amman nakasa bud'e idona haka nacigaba da sharar baccin kamar kasa.

Shima bai samu yayi bacci da wuri ba saboda tunani da kuma ayyukan da yayi kan computer ya tura office akan course dinsu da zasu koma nan da d'an lokaci,saida yayi da gasken gaske sannan ya iya tashi yayi sallar don agajiye yake shima d'in.
Koda ya gama sai ya zauna laximi idonsa rufe gabaki d'aya suffar deeyah yake hararowa ajiya,don ba laifi rigar tayi masifar ansarta har da haka ya sa yakasa bacci sosai.
Murmushi yayi ya mike ya bud'e k'ofarsa yafita,alokacin ma har shidda da rabi saura y'an mintuna.
Yatura k'ofarta yaji bud'e sannan yasa kai aciki,tayi d'ai d'ai agado duk ta watsar da filoluwan ak'asa ta baje duk da rubda ciki tayi,hularta ta cire gashin shima duk ya watse agadon.
Wani abu ya tsikaresa yashafa kansa yana ajiyar xuciya,wannan yarinya sonta da shaukinta suna niyyar zautashi.
Wata mik'a da tayi saida gabansa ya yanke ya fad'i,ta mirgino gefen gado kad'an ya hana bazata k'undumo k'asa ba.
Matsawa yayi ahankali ya durkusa bisa guyawunsa,daidai saitin fuskarta ya tsayar da kansa yana karewa kyakkyawar fuskarta kallo,jiyake kamar ana fixgar ransa akanta.
Ahankali yafara hura mata iska akan fuska,yana zagaye fuskar da iskan bakinsa har xuwa kunnenta,yamutsa fuska tayi tana jijjuya kai bai daina ba ya cigaba,da hura iskan ko zaisa tatashi,amman har tsayin minty guda bata da niyyar tashi ya canza salo ya duk'a sosai yalalubo kunnenta yagantsara mata cixo..............ahaukace na tashi zaune ina murxa kunnena ina murtsike idona,kafin na samu idon ya washe na saukesu akansa gira ya d'aga mani yace "time for prayer madam"

Cike dajin zafin cixon da yamun kamar zan kuka nace "shine saika cijeni saboda Allah,wannan ai cin zaline"

"To kozaki ramane?kin wane baje kin saki baki da hanci kinata sharar bacci time din salla ya wuce,fuska duk miyan bacci" yafada da tsokana.

Shafa fuskata nai da bakina ina ya mutsa fuskar cikin kulewa nace "allah ya kyauta,wlh ni bana miyau sai dai idan kai kakeyi"

Na juya ina lalubo hulata nasaka nafara kokarin tattara rigata zan sauka.
Kureni yayi da kallo kamar zai cinyeni.

"To tunda ka tasheni saika fita ko"

Numfashi yaja yace "idan nak'iya fah"

Bance komi ba nasauko na jawo wata loka na dauki brush da makilin na saka sannan na nufi hanyar bayi,saida naje bakin kofar na jiyo na kallesa shima yana kallona nace "inka gama naka fushin dani ni yanxun na fara,kafin infito kabarmun d'akina malam"
Ban saurari cewarsa ba na shige abuna na rufe kofar nasa key.
Jingina nai da bayan k'ofar ina jan iska,gaskiya nidai zaman nan nidashi a wajen nan zai kawo matsala,nidai nikadai nasan menakeji muddin yana kusantoni,ni jinake ma kamar abunda nakeji akansa ada karuwa yayi yanxu.
Brush din na fara sannan nai wanka had'e da Alwallah na dauro towel dina na fito,kayana dana jera cikin wadrop na bude na dauko duguwar riga da abayar salla dana d'auko kafin na rufe naji an rungumoni ta baya..................kara na fasa ina runtse idona nasan ba wanda zaimun wannan abun inba yaya ba,fuskarsa ya dora gefen wuyana yana wani sassauta murya "wakike wa rashin kunya?kuma da wa kike fushin."
Gabaki d'aya na rikice don irin haka bata tab'a faruwa ba,ina kermar baki nace "badakai nake ba,kayi hkr wlh bazan sake ba kuma ba fushi nake dakai ba nadaina,ka cikani inyi sallah ka gani ni banyi ba."
Idona har yana kawo kwallah don tsoro ne yacika mun raina,har wata rawa jikina yake.
K'in sakina yayi ya jiyo dani gabansa,ga towel dina dake kokarin kuncewa na rikesa gamm,runtse idona nai bazan iya kalon idonsa a wannan lokacin ba.
Naji yana shin shinamun kumatu yace"nasan kin fad'ane kurin,amman baki daina fushin ba"
Bakina na kerma nace "na rantse maka yaya bazan kara ba,narok'eka ka cikani,kabar yimun wannan abun bana so kasani babu kyau."iska ya hura mani naji kamar bazan iya tsayuwa da k'afafuna ba,sautin murmushinsa nakeji sannan yace "oky duk naji wannan,oya open ur eyes"girgiza kaina nai don bazan iya kallonsa ba idonsa yamun nauyin da bazan iya kallon su ba.........."yaya kayi hkr bazan iya ba"sake matseni yayi,nabude baki zan kuka ya dora mun kuncinsa akai sannan yace "aiko inbaki budesu kika kalleni ba Nikadai nasan mezan maki,badai nikike ma rashin kunya ba."
Hawayena suka silalo nace "nadaina bazan sakeba narantse maka kau" yar dariya yayi yace "naji amman sai kin nudesu kin had'a ido dani yanxun nan" ina gunshek'an kuka hawaye na silalowa nafara bud'esu ahankali,na sauke akan fuskarsa dago hab'a ta yayi yasa idonsa anawa,yad'aga mun gira yace "sake fad'a inji,kar kuma ki sauke idonki."na kwab'e fuska kamar zan kurma ihu don bakina azai iya fasalta abunda nakeji ba,idanunsa kamar wani abune ke ciki yake fitowa yna ratsa dukan ilahirin jikina,dole na bude nace "kayi hkr bazan sake maka rashin kunyar ba,kuma nadaina fushin dakai"
Lumshe idonsa yayi ya bude yace " it's oky jiki sallar,bari naje nasamo mana abunda zamuci"kamar k'adangaruwa na d'aga kai kurin,don duk ilahirin jikina rawa yake zamewa nai na zauna kasa ina jan numfashi kamar dai antoshe mani makoshina,nakai almost five minute awajen kafin na iya tashi nasa rigar na kabbara salla,inayi ina ajiyar xuciya.
Saida na gama sannan nayi shafa inayi ina nazarin abunda ya faru,gaskiya bazan iya ba injin tsoron abunda zai iyafaruwa nan gaba.xuciyata nada rauni haka zalika cike take tab da tarin kaunarsa da shauk'insa,in irin haka na faruwa banda karfin xuciyar da zan dakatar da faruwar komi.
Jikina a kasalance haka na idasa shirina cikin riga da wando,na d'ora jarcet sama na yane kaina da veil,nasaka turaruka na fuskata tayi wani fresh kamar ina wanka da madara,powder kurin na saka da red din janbaki amman nai wani irin kyau sosai,sai kamannina da Ammer suka fito sak,murmushi nai inanan tsaye ya shigo amma daga bakin k'ofa ya tsaya rungume hannuwa yayi ga kirjinsa yana kafeni da mayatattun idanunsa,sadda kaina nayi na cigaba da tsayuwa jinai yace "wow masha Allah,tsarki ya tabbata ga wanda ya tsara wannan halitta,u look so cute pretty"
Shiru nai masa band'ago kaina ba sake cewa yayi "ohhh fushinne har yanxu"saurin d'agowa nai na girgiza kai nace kamar zan kuka "nadaina ai nace" lumshe idonsa yayi yace "oky to muje muyi breakfast xuwa goma sai mutafi asibitin"d'aga kaina nai nace "zanzo katafi" girgiza kai yayi yace "k'afata k'afarki inkik'iya kuma zan iya d'aukarki kinsan zan iya" dole na taho badan inaso mujera ba,kallona yaitayi har na k'araso ya d'aga mun gira nai saurin janye idona karna gani,hannuna naji cikin nasa yajani muka fita xuwa can falon.
Ak'asa ya jera mana kayan karin irin abincin nageria ne yasaka aka kawo mana,fefe chicken ne had'ad'd'e da chips sai kuma cuca oad tasha madara,ga faska faskan wainar k'wai zama yayi yana lank'washe k'afafunsa,yana fesin din Tv dake kunne wata tasha da suke nuna wasan kwallon k'afa.
Nai tsaye na rungume hannuna,remote ya d'auka ya rage volume d'in tv ya d'ago yana kallona.
Naturo bakina ina k'yaky'k'yafta idanu murmushin ya sakeyi yace "ohhh bakisan zama k'asa sai bisa cinyata ko?"
Zaro idanuna nai nagirgiza kai nace "ni wlh ba haka nake nufi ba." "Inba haka ba tsayuwar me kike" dole na zauna kusada shi inajin wani daban,xuba  komi yayi cikin plet guda ya xuba cuca oad d'in cikin cup guda sannan ya d'ago yana kallona. "Matso" kallon d'an filin daya rage tsakanin mu nai na maida idona kansa nace "yaya sokake na shige jikinka inba haka ba yanxu nan meyayi da zakace in matso" had'e girarsa yayi yace "Nadeeya ban son gardama fah,kinmun nisa sosai hannuna zai saurin gajiya wajen baki" dalalo idona nai nace "kabani fah kace yaya?saikace jaririya." Fork yasa ya dauko dankali hade da kayan hadin dake ciki ya nufo bakina yana cewa "au bakisani ba ashe?aike awajena kinma fi jaririya kamar sabon kwan da kaza tayi haka nake ji dake." Kallonsa kurin nai yadda shima yake kallona don yakashe mun baki,indai kalamaine ya iya bude bakinsa ya zazzagasu amman kalma guda ta gagaresa fad'a.
Batare da ina kallonsa ba nace "yaya nifa bana son wannan abun" lumshe idanunsa yayi yana ajiye spoon din muryarsa da sanyi yace "nine bakiso ko kalamaina?" Wani abu naji ya ratsani ba kamar yadda ya xuba mun shanyanyyun sa,kasa sukuni nai nasadda kaina ina wasa da yan yatsuna,bakina nake motsa wa nakasa samun abun cewa.
"Talk mana"
Dagowa nai ina marairacewa nace "yaya kaga lokaci yana tafiya kuma yunwa nakeji" baice komi ba ya mik'omun spoon din na ansa muka fara ci,ba ma cewa komi yana yinsa ya nuna kamar fushi yayi.ni gara yayi fushin ma don zanfi samun natsuwa da irin rikicin da yake haddasaman acikin xuciya da gangar jikina,har muka gama bai ce komi ba ya mik'e ya shiga ciki yabarni zaman jiransa.
Akalla yakai awa daya aciki xuwa can sai gashi ya fito cikin shigar kananun kaya,kanshi yasha gyara daga inda nake k'amshinsa ya dabai bayeni,ya hana hancina sak'at.
Yayi kyau sosai har na kasa kauda idona akansa,ya tsaya yana d'aura agogo nikuma sai aikin kallonsa nake,jinai kawai yace "karki lasheni don Allah."saurin kauda kai nai don nama rasa bakin kare kaina "tashi muje" nai xunbur na mik'e muka kashe komi sannan muka tafi.

*******

Cikin kwana ki goma da mukai spain kwata kwata na rasa gane kan yaya,tun ranar dana ce nibana son irin kalaman da yake fad'a ya canza mun,duk da ba wani daurewa yayi ba amman ya daina disturbing dina in asibiti mukaje hirarsa shida su Ammer ne kawai ko nayi k'okari namasa magana zai waske kamar baijini ba,duk sainaji na damu sabanin da danake ganin rafita harkata shine kawai,saigashi kuma ya daina damunan amman kuma banjin dadin hakan.ko gida muka dawo zai kirani muci abinci kowa da plet dinsa,yana yi yana kallo uffan bace mun.
Idan nice nai masa magana iyaka ya ansani atakaice koma yayi banza dani,sautari idan muna zaune ina ganin asmau na kiransa amman ko kallon wayar ba yayi balle ya dauka,najinjina girman laifin da tayi masa sai kuma naji ta bani tausai,koma miye dai tayi nadama kuma at least ya bata wata damar ta gyara kuskurenta.

B'angaren jiki su Abba kuwa komi yana tafiya successful,k'afafunsu sun mike lafiya lau saboda allurorin da ake masu da magungunan da suka fara sha,likitan ma yace idan akai sa,a akai masu gashin kashi na kwana bakwai inhar ya anshesu basai anyi aikin ba.
Wannan labari yaman dad'i sosai yini nai ina farin ciki tare da adduar Allah yasa adace,damun had'a ido da yaya sai yayi saurin kauda kansa tunda har yau yak'i sakarmun fuska yak'i kulani,daman k'arfe bakwai muke tafiya gida idan muka xo tun safe.lokaci kuma nayi muka fara shirin tafiya kamar ko yaushe sai narkewa nake jikin ammer,shikuma yana tsaye yana jirana.
Abba yace "dear Ke kike k'ara shagwab'a yarinyar nan,bakiga jiranta yake ba gashi garin nan baka sani sai kaji ruwa yadda nakejin iskar nan da wuya idan ba ruwan ne tafe ba."
Ammer tace "wlh ba laifi nabane kana gani dai inta korarta tana sake nanik'eman."
Abba yace "to shikenan,kaga sadiq jeka abunka zata taho daga baya."
Daman jira kawai yake abashi umurni don yaxo iyawuya tana sane take masa wad'anan abubuwan.juyawa yayi ya fice ina ganin haka gabana ya fad'i karfa yatai yabarni,kallon Abba nai ya harareni nace "kayi hkr Abba soyayyarkuce kullin idan nazo bana son tafiya" fada yake yace "ce maki akai bazaki tadda mu gobe ba?yaron nan yana jiranki saboda kin rainasa kike bata masa lokaci" na sadda kai nace "kayi hkr bazan sake ba insha Allahu" yace "bani zaki bawa hkr ba kitabbatar kin bashi hkr har yasauko" na d'aga kai kurin ina d'aukar jikkata na sunbaci ammer ga goshi naimata by by,shima naje zan masa ya kauda kai "bana so yau,fushi nake dake kullin aita fama dake kamar karamar yarinya" kamar zan kuka "nace fa bazan sake ba Abba" mika mata kuncinsa yayi nai masa sannan nafita.

Duk iyakar hangena ban hangosa ba ga garin iska ake ba laifi alamun dai ruwan zai sauka,ga hadari nan.
Tsoro ya kamani sai tarar taxi nake babu wanda yake tsayawa,daman suna da wannan matsalar da sunga ana hadadar kowa yake gida dawuri to sai suyita iskanci,bakamar insukaji inda zaka da nisa.
Inanan dai tsayen naji daga bayana ana kwala man kira da sunana danai anfani dashi a makaranta watau *NADEEYA JAFAR DAULA*,da sauri ma juya kallo guda nai masa na ganesa osman sharif,wani abokina ne da mukai export dashi department d'inmu d'aya tare muke karatu muke daukar overall a department dinmu,mun saba dashi sosai muna gama makaranta kuma abotar taja baya.
Washe bakina nai nace "osman sharif!!!"
Dariya yayi yana karaso wa kusadani zai rungumeni,saurin ja baya nai don muna makaranta don mun rungume juna ba wani abu bane,ina ganin it's nothing.
Murmushi yayi yace "sorry nariga na saba da wannan gaisuwar,naga kuma ke kamar kin mance to Assalamu alaikum" ya mikomun hannunsa.
Sai ban gwalesa ba nabasa nau muka gaisa yake tambayata menakeyi anan,na gaya masa na rako yayana wani aiki,naki gaya masa taka maimai abunda ya faru,yace shikuma yawon shaqatawa yaxo shekaran jiya kozara rakasa masaukinsa.
Gabana ya fad'i nai yak'e nace "aikuwa saidai wani karon yanxu yana gida gana jirana,kaban no dinka inyaso saimuyi waya"ba shiri ya bata card dinsa harda address din hotel din dayake zaune,sannan sukai sallama.
Ajiyar xuciya naja nace "da gaban yaya ne dana shiga uku"
Khalipa kuwa yana can cikin wata yar rumfa tsaye yana kallonta,har sadda osman yaxo duk abunda ya wakana yana kallonsu,duk inda ransa yakai ya b'aci hankalinsa yayi matuk'ar tashi,jiyake iska bata isar masa ga huhunsa,kirjinsa har d'aga wa yake runtse idonsa yayi gamm kamar zai mutu yakeji,bai aune ba aka shak'e da ruwa bakuma d'an karamin ruwa ba,xubasa ake kamar da bakin kwarya.
Wani mai taxi yafito daga shagon da yake tsaye kusada shi zaitafi,yayi saurin binsa yaroki alfarma ya saukesa masaukinsa,bai musa ba ya daukesa suka tafi ko gefen da take bai sake duba ba,shikadai yasan wutar da takeci acikin birnin xuciyarsa.


Jin ruwan na sauka jikina hankalina ya tashi na nemi wajen fakewa amma na rasa,duk wanda na tsaida bai tsayawa tsoro ya dabaibaye xuciyata,nai dana sanin rashin bin yaya da yanxu ina gida abuna,wani irin sanyi yake ratsani na rud'e ganin ruwan bamai tsayawa bane,duk inda naje na tsaida masu abun hawa basa tsayawa,narasa inda zan saka kaina jikina karkarwa yake saboda sanyi kayana kaf sun jik'e.
Na k'ank'ame jikina ina rawar sanyi ban san sadda kuka ya kubce mun ba,ban san iya lokacin dana dauka cikin wannan ruwan ba kafin Allah ya kawo wani mai tausayin yadaukeni,don bama ni na tsayar dashi ba tsayuwar motar naji gabana kawai,karkarwa kawai jikina yake har hak'orana suna karo da juna.
Cikin sa,a muka isa duk da ruwa baya bari muga hanya sosai hannuna na karkarwa nake kokarin fiddo masa kudinsa,naji yace na barshi godiya nai mai nawuce ciki kafafuna na hard'ewa,daker na hau benan daya kaini dakinmu knocking naita yi amman babu alamun za,a bud'e silalewa k'asa nai saboda sanyin da nakejin yana shigata da wuya idan banyi masassara ba,inma asthma ta bata tashi ba daga durkushen nake kwankwasa kofar amman yaya kamar bayajina,dunk'ulewa nai waje guda ina hawaye nakai minty talatin sannan naji anbude anjuya antafi.
Daker na tashi ina kerma nashiga yana kwance akan tree seater,ya rufe fuskarsa da filo.
Wucewa nai dakina cikin rawar jiki, banda abunda nakeso illa nacire kayan jikina saida wani abun tashin hankalin nayi iyakar yina kofar ta bud'u amman tak'iya,ga sanyin Ac na ratsani bakina rawa kurin yake turawa nake amman bata motsi,xubewa nai k'asa nasaka kuka sosai ina shashsheka,wanda yaya zai iya jiyoni daga inda yake amman bai xo yaji meyafaru ba,banga kuma alamar zaizo d'in ba.
Wurgar da jikkata nai namike daker kamar zan fad'i nakoma falon na tsaya daga bakin k'ofa nace masa.

"Ya...y.....yaya,k'ofata bat.......bata bud'uwa ka.....kak.....ka taimaka mun sanyi nakeji"
Daker ma nake fixgo maganar,amma ko motsi yaya baiba.
 Wajensa na isa na durkusa na cire filo daga kan fuskarsa,naga idon rufe na rik'e hannunsa ina cewa "yaya,don Allah kataimaka kabud'emun k'ofar d'ak..............."I said live me!!!"
Ya daka mun tsawar da bai tab'a mani ita ba tunda nasansa tsawon shekara guda da rabi kenan,jikina ya sake daukar wani rawa kaf kaf kaf haka nakeyi,saboda na rud'e na gigice ba abunda nake tsoro fiye da kima irin tsawa,dimautani take ja baya nake dashi har na matsa sosai daga inda yake.
Dunkuke kafafuna nai ina rawar jiki hawaye nabin fuskata,bai kyaleni haka ba ya cigaba da magana cikin fad'a kamar zai had'iyeni.............."meyasa kika dawo nan?nace meyasa kika dawo nan maiya hana baki bisa gidansa ko masaukinsa ba?imagine ki mik'ama wani k'ato hannunki yarik'e,mai yasa baki bari ya rungumekin ba ehy!!!?"
Ya idasa fada cikin wata irin tsawar data fi ta d'axun,soke kaina nai na fashe da kuka mai rikitarwa labbana rawa suke na dago ina kukan ina magana.
"Ni.......ni.........niban.........niban..........kwatseni yayi cikin masifa kamar zai kwada mani mari yace "ke uban me,nace ke ubanme?kina so kice kwatar hannunki yayi bake kika bashi ba?haka kikeson cewa,nace haka kike son kice?"
Nima cikin fadan ga kuma kuka share share inayi nace masa "aini......ai....aini ban bashi hannuna da wata ma.....manufa ba,gaisawa kawai muka.....Al....Allah ma yaga xuciyat.........."rufe mun baki ko na faska maki mari anan,ni kike gaya ma gaisawa?nace ni kike cewa gaisawa kukai?addininmu yace ka gaisa da wani katon namiji wanda ba muharraminka ba?dake nake addinimu yace haka?"
Ban iya magana girgiza kai kurin nai,saboda Sosai nake kuka har kaina yan ansawa.............dafe kansa yayi yana tafiya da baya da baya,ya halbar da d'an table d'in dake tsakiyar dakin jikake tas tas yafashe,toshe kunnena nai ina dunk'ulewa waje guda har lokacin kerma nake wani irin masifar sanyi nakeji,kaina kamar zai fashe gida biyu..............jinsa nai yana magana kamar zai fashe sa kuka dakaji kasan akwai ciwon da yakeji sosai cikin xuciyarsa.
"Kina yar musulmai jikar musulmai,wani katon Allah ta ala yabaki hannunsa kekuma ki ansa da sunan gaisuwa imagine!I'm so disappointed kin bani mamaki,dakin san kalar xunubin dakika kwasarma kanki sai kinji kamar ki mutu,sannan kina nuna kamar ba komi bane."

"Wallahi summa tallahi dakin sake kin had'a jikinki dashi alokacin nan,bazan iya kwatanta maki kalar hukuncin da zaki dauka awajena ba,wayanki bai anfaneki dakomi ba iliminki bai anfaneki da komi ba,wayewarki ta bance da wofi."

Yatashi ya wuce ciki yabarni nan ina kokowa da numfashina.

Episode  .45

Yadda nakejin numfashina yanayi ne yake bani tsoro,na sake yuk'urawa na tashi ina layi da rawar sanyi na sake komawa bakin k'ofata,na jingina da ita ina tsiyayar da hawaye ga sanyi da matsanancin ciwon kai dake addabata,ga tarin kuncin dake cike cikin xuciyata bana iya tuna when last danaji daci da kunci acikin raina irin yau,duk danasan inada laifi hakan bai hana xuciyata jin kamar zata fashe don tsananin b'acin ran abunda yaya yayi,silalewa nai bakin k'ofar ina soke kaina cikin guyawuna,daker nake jan iska tabbas idan nacigaba da kukan nan asthma ta zata tashi,to amman banda kwarin xuciyar da zan iya daina war,nikadai nasan irin pain din dana keji araina,gaba ki daya naji yaya yasire mun babu abu mafi bakanta mun irin tsawarsa da maganganunsa na k'arshe akaina.

_wayonki bai anfaneki da komi ba,iliminki bai anfaneki da komi ba,haka wayewarki ta banzace da wofi_

Na sake tsintar kalaman acikin dodon kunnena na runtse idona inajin wani kuka yana tahomani,na duke ina rera abu na ina cikin kukan naji ya bude k'ofarsa ya fito bana son ko kallon fuskarsa,shiyasa ma ban d'agoba na kuma cigaba da shashshekata,inaji yana kokowa da k'ofar taki buduwa yayi yayi amma takiya.
Yaja gefe yana jan numfashi kafin naji yace mun cikin dishashshiyar muryarsa da bata fita sosai.

"Tashi kishiga d'akina ki canza wad'anan kayan"

Idan kofar dakina ta ansa nima na ansa masa ya sake mai maita maganarsa amma kamar babu kowa awajen,na rage sautin kukan saboda yadda kirjina yake rad'ad'i,cikin kufula yasake cewa.

"Karki bari na sake maimaita maganata,wlh zan idasa juye sauran b'acin raina akanki"
Sai lokacin na d'ago fuskata da tayi jajawur idona kam ba,a magana ya rine sosai shima ina jan numfashi kamar zaitafi ya barni,nace cikin bacin rai nima.

"Meyayi saura kuma?Amma tunda akwai saura kajuye mun,amman bazan je ko inaba idan ma sanyin kasheni zai baka da asara ai,kaje ka rabu dani rayuwatace ba taka ba"
Na maida kaina k'asa ina  jan numfashina,inhela ta kurin nakeso nashaka inba haka ba bazan iya bacci ba,gashi wata makirar masassara na saukarmun ajikina.................iska yake fesarwa kamar wanda yaci yaji,ya kasa magana ficewa yayi daga dakin gaba daya bansan inda ya nufa ba.

Bajima wa sai gashi shida wani ma,aikacin hotel din, yasaka wani key ya bude yace "wajen rufe d'akin ne aka datsesa kuma anbar key din aciki shiyasa"
Godiya yayi masa ya rakasa yatafi yarufe kofar falon yaxo ya rabani ya koma dakinsa.

Da dafa bangon nashige na tura kofar toilet na wuce na cire kayan ina ta kerma,na had'a ruwa mai zafi nai wanka nayo alwalla sannan na daura towel na fito inata makerketa,riga doguwa na ciro nasaka na shafa mai daker sanan nai salla magrib da akai tun d'axun.
Anan saman abun sallar na kwanta na dunkule waje guda inajan numfashi,masassarar tana sake rufeni har lokacin hawayena basu k'afe ba,ina sake tariyo duk abun nadama kula osman ta cikani,nadamar bashi hannuna ta cikani nai istigfari saboda ko musulmine ba kyau balle kafiri osman Christan ne ba musulmi ba,kenan da na barshi ya rungumeni bala,in sai yafi haka,amma yaya ya balbaleni da bala,i ko lura da halin dana shigo baiba yasan inada lalura ya rufe idonsa ya surfaman masifa son ransa,sai yanxu yace zai taimakeni bana so bana son duk wani taimako daga wajensa daga yau,hawaye suka silalo mani jinake duk tsanarsa ta cika man xuciya,na nemi soyayyar da nake masa na rasa,kalamansa sunyi man matukar tsauri acikin kwalwata...............alamun shigowarsa naji ban motsa ba balle na kallesa inanan kwance na juya baya,naji ya ajiye abu akan table din dake cikin dakin,da alamun tsayuwa yayi bai fita ba don banji anrufe kofar ba.tsaki naja can kasan mak'oshina namaida idanuna na rufe inajin hucin masassarar har cikin bakina..............."ga abinci nan kitashi kici"
Kamar na tashi na ringa zazzaga masa masifa kozan samu sauk'i.

Daker ma nake maganar nace "na k'oshi"

Shiru yayina dan lokaci kafin yace "kin koshi mekika ci?kitashi nace kici kar ranki ya b'aci"

Hawaye suka silalo mani banson ma d'aga muryata sosai saboda kirjina dake ciwo da kaina,muryata ta karye cikin kuka nace masa "na k'oshi bazan iya ci ba banda lafiya".................ina gama fada kuka ya kubce mun wanda yake tahowa tunda ga kasan raina.

Abunda yaji dole tasa ya runtse idanunsa,wani irin feelings yakeji cikin xuciyarsa,yana jin jikinsa yana macewa idonsa yai masa nauyi,bakinsa har wani d'aci yake masa ga zafi da idanunsa keyi kamar an watsa masa barkono, daker ya bude idonsa yajuya kurin yabar d'akin.

Tunda nake arayuwata bantab'a ganin dare mai wahala da tsawo ba irin wannan,ga ciwo ga radadin xuciya da ruhi,ga   yunwa kuma bana jin zan iyacin komi.haka na rarrafa na hau gadona ko sallar isha,i da naji ankira tun kafin dare ya tsala kasa yinta nai,don har bana iya bude idanuna,masassarar ta rufeni ruf sai nishi nake.kaina kamar ana buga mun guduma(wayyo Allah deeya kinbani tausai over😭) har karfe biyu da rabi banyi bacci ba,naji bazan iya kwana da yunwar ba na sauko ina tangad'i na bude abunda ya kawo mani,ferfesun dankaline sai lemu da ruwa hannuna na rawa nafara ci,saida nai spoon biyar naji wani irin amaiya taho mani aguje natashi nai toilet naringa kwaraya shi,kamar zan amayo y'an cikina gaba ki d'aya k'arfina ta k'are nishi nake sosai kamar zan mutu,ni banma tab'a shiga mugun yanayi ba irin wannan rana.................da rarrafe nafito naje na hau gado na shige cikin bargo,kamar ana kad'ani haka nakeji.

Gabaki d'aya ya rasa abunda yake masa dadi,har yanxu xuciyarsa rad'ad'i take masa ga wani mahaukacin tausayinta daya cika masa xuciya da ruhinsa,kokari yake yashare da lamarinta amman xuciyarsa takasa bashi had'in kai,daya hango sadda wannan k'aton ya kama hannunta yarik'e jiyake kamar zai mutu.
Gefe guda yana yinta ya tsorata shi yasan ruwan da yadakera dolene saitayi ciwo,inma asthma ta bata tashi ba tuna sadda take gaya masa bata da lafiya yayi,dafe kansa yayi yaji meyasa yabarota bacin shikadai ne tafi kusa dashi da zai taimaketa,tashi yayi zaune yana sauraren bugun xuciyarsa,baisan kalar son da yake mata yakai girman haka ba,ashe yana da irin wannan kishin?da ya rufe masa ido yasaka *qaunarsa* kuka,ya gaya mata maganganu masu d'aci kuma ta gaya masa bata lafiya yagaza taimakonta,shin yayi daidai kuwa? Kar zafin kishinsa yasa wani abu ya sameta,wani ruwa yaji ya d'iso masa abun mamaki da al,ajabi hawaye yaji akan fuskarsa.lumshe ido yayi yayin da wasu suka sake biyo fuskar tashi,lallai ajinjina wa wannan soyayya ta nadeeya,arayuwarsa zai iya k'iga kukansa abubuwan da suka sashi kuka guda biyune rasuwar iyayensa da rashin su Abba kaseem,sai rasuwar mahaifin su asma,u bai dauka abu mai kama da wannan zai saka idanunsa xubar ruwa ba,yau abunda ya raina shine yasashi kuka.
Idan bazai mance bayasha kuranta kansacewar shi so bai kai girman dazaiiya hanasa bacci ba balle ya hanasa hud'od'insana rayuwa,togashinan yarinyar dayasha furta kalmar tsanarta, yasha cemata bata daga cikin jerin matan dazaiso,yau gashi tsundum cikin tafkin kaunarta har tana sashi kuka.
 daman hausawa sukace d'an hakin daka raina shike tsole maka ido.

Yana cikin wannan tunanin yajiyo kamar kakarin amai,saida ya saurara sosai yaji shid'inne sannan ya runtse idonsa ya mike afirgice ya sauko daga kan gadon ya bude kofar sa ya nufi dakinta.

Kamar anjona mani shocking haka jikina yakeyi,ban san shigowarsa ba nadaiji zamansa gefena,duk inda hankalinsa yakai ya tashi.
Janye bargon yayi cikin tsanain rudewa yace "deeya amai kikai?,masassara kike?"
Duk ajere ya jero mata wadannan tambayoyi cikin rudewa,hannunsa yasaka ya tab'a goshinta........."innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju,un sake saka hannu yayi zai tadani duk da zafin ciwon da nake ciki nadaure muryata yar firit don wuyar da nakesha nace "kar......kara sake tab'ani,ka.....kat...katai kabani waje ko mutuwa nai bana son kazo kusadani."
Sassauta murya yayi cikin lallashi kamar zai fashe mata da kuka yace "duk abunda zakice naji,ki adana fushinki zuwa wani lokaci kibari na samar maki sauk'i bakiji yadda kijinki yake ba."
Ina shashshekar kuka na tashi inaja baya dashi na jingina da gadon nace "bana so,katai bana so bana son ganinka ma ni"
Na tattare k'afafuna na rungume ina jan numfashi,na rufe idona saboda dagaske bana son ganin nasa.
Dafe kansa yayi dake barazanar tarwatsewa ya tashi yashiga toilet d'ina,bansan me yayo ba naji ya fito yaxo ya hawo bisa gadon nai saurin d'ago kaina muka had'a ido.
Wani suka naji axuciyata jinai kamar ya rik'emun idanuna,ban sanma ya kamo duk hannuwana ya rikesu ba firgigi nai nadawo natsuwata,nafara kokowar anshe hannuna amman nakasa saboda ba karamin riko yayi man ba.
"Saboda ni ba wannan katon abokin naki bane shiyasa kike guduna?amma naga shi ai dakanki kika mika mashi hannunki."
Fixgewa nai nace cikin kufula "ehy saboda ba shibane d'in ba, duk abunda zaka fad'a yaya ka fad'i mani,amma bana son wani taimakonka kabarni na mutu mana,kuma kai d'in da kake tab'ani hakan daidaine ga Addininmu"
Na idasa cikin wahala ina ta shashshekar numfashina.
Cikin sanyin muryarsa kamar zai kuka yace "ni kikema rashin kunya deeyah?saboda na nuna kishina akan abunda kikai?ashe zaki iya gaya mun wannan maganar" Kauda kai na nayi  nikadai nasan azabar da nake sha acikin jikina,masassarar da ciwon kai sunfi komi damuna.
Inaji ba daidai bane abunda nake yi amman dole na fad'a don nagaji na wannan nok'enok'en nasa.
Ina Jan numfashi daker don maganar ma kamar zan mutu nakeyinta nace "kishi?kishifa kace yaya! wakake kishin? akanme akan wane dalili zakai kishina iye?" Girgiza kai yayi ganin yadda take shashsheka numfashinta na sama da k'asa muryarsa na rawa yace "ba wannan ne mafi muhimmanci ba,lafiyarki tafiye mun wannan maganar,kitsaya na samarmaki sauk'i..........."bana so,nace banaso kafitar mu d'aki.........matsowa yayi kusada ni nai wata irin hantsilawa na fado k'asa,naji jiki kam sosai amman haka na mik'e na koma jikin wadrop ina numfarfashi daker,saukowa yayi ya biyoni na dunkule waje guda ina dukansa da hannuna ina kuka ina cewa "bana so,karabu dani banson duk wani taimakonka,karka tab'ani wlh inka tab.................jinai kurin ya rufemun bakina da lips dinsa,ya tsayar da idonsa cikin nawa yaringa girgiza mun kai,kamar anjonani jikin wuta haka jikina yake tsuma.lumshe idona nai ina kokowa da numfashina,su dukan idanunsu arufe ahankali ya ringa hura mata iskar bakinsa,tana taimaka mani wajen yin numfashi sosai haka yaitayi mani na tsawon minty biyar,sai nakejin ina samun sassaucin shashshekar da nake,numfashina yafara daidaituwa bansani ba acikin wannan yanayin ya rabani da rigar jikina,ya xuge xif din ta baya ya sauketa xuwa k'uguna,firgigi nai na janye bakina idanuna na xuba masa yayin da nasa suka rine kamar anxuba masa yaji,sannan sun lumshe kamar yana jin bacci,daga inda nake inajiyo sautin dokawar xuciyarsa,saboda kusancin mu yayi yawa bansan sadda hawaye suka silalo man ba,goshinsa ya d'ora kan nawa ya tallafo kumatuna,yace cikin wata irin murya da bansan sa da ita ba.
"Come down my dear,I don't do anything for you.I just help you okay?"
Idanuna arufe suke bakina ya mun mugun nauyi balle na iya juye masa rashin kunyar dana tattaro abakina,tunda na samu numfashina ya dan daidaita ba laifi saina yi shuru.
Tare muke jan numfashi muna ajewa tare,har lokacin goshinsa yana kan nawa,haka hannuwansa tallafe da kumatuna duka biyun.ahankali ya sauke hannunsa yamun wayau saidai kurin naji ya daukeni kamar jinjira ya d'oda bisa gadona,idanuna na runtse saboda tsananin nauyin ganina da yayi dagani sai bra,rigar ya zameta xuwa wajen kuguna.
Xuciyarsa ke wani irin buguwa idanunsa na kokowa da juna,ya kasa dora ido akanta jiyake xuciyarsa kamar zata fito.towel din daya jiko da ruwa toilet shiya dauko yasake jik'asa ya rasa ta ina zai fara gasa mata jikin,sai ya rufe idanunsa shima ahankali ya dora towel din bisa cikinta.
Wata wawuyar ajiyar xuciya na sauke nai saurin damk'ar hanunsa bakina na rawa nace "please..........yatsansa daya ya dora kan bakina yace da irin muryar d'axun.
"Bazan yimaki abunda zai cutar dake ba,ina kokarin na sama maki sauk'i darene balle na kaiki asibiti."
Kwalla suka silalo ta gefen idona nace da muryar kuka "karka kallar mun jiki pls bana so,ka rufe idonka"
Murmushi yayi ya bude idonsa ya kalle shafeffen cikinta xuwa kan kyawawan breast d'inta,dan danan tsikar jikinsa tatashi ya runtse idonsa yace "shikenan badai kison na kallemaki jikinki ko?"

Nace "hmm,bana so"

"To kitsaya na shafa maki ruwa kiji sauk'i,bazan kalleki ba"
 
Ya ringa bin jikinta da towel din ahankali kamar yana ma jariri,duk ya susuce dauriyace kurin da karfin hali,yadda take sauke ajiyar xuciya da jan numfashi kadai ya isa ya hautsina masa dukan lissafinsa,tun yana iya rufe idonsa har yakasa yabarsu abud'e kirjinta ya tsurawa ido yana jin jikinsa yana rawa yana tsuma,sake saka towel din yayi cikin ruwan ya matse,sannan yafaro mata daga kafarta yake matsawa ahankali,duk sadda ya dora towel din sainaja numfashi saboda sanyin ruwa dake ratsa ni.ahankali naji yana janye rigata daga saman cinyoyina,wani irin abune bak'o yaziyarceni na kama filo na rik'e ina jan numfashina.
Da wani irin salo yake mun wanda na rasa wane ma,auni zan dorasa,duk yadda naso tirjewa karya rabani da rigata abun yacitura,inaji ina gani ya ajeta gefe dagani sai pant dina kalar bra dina pink.
Wuyana yake dora towel din har saida nai wata shashsheka don sanyin dake akwai,har ya samu ya juyani nan ma abayan yaita wani nawa yana dadewa,saida na gaji na fashe da kuka saboda nidai takaicin duniya ya dameni,ya gama cusa mun bacin rai sannan yaxo yana taba mun jikina,daman abunda yake so kenan.
Ture sa nai natashi ina jan bargo na rufe daga kirjina xuwa kafafuna,na dalla masa wata uwar harara nace "daman haka kake sha,awa ka tab'a jikina kuma ka kalla,to tunda ka gani kana iya tafiya.kuma ban gode ba."
Durkusawa yayi guyawunsa kasan kafet din dakin,ya matso sosai jikin gadon yana gabana idonsa sunanan yadda suke,saima sake lumshewa da sukai,jinai ya rik'o tafin hannuna yasaka saman kuncinsa yaja ajiyar xuciya cikin sassanyar murya bata fita sosai yace *"NADDEYA BA SHA'AWARKI KAD'AI NAKE BA,DA HAR IDAN NA KALLI JIKINKI ZANJI NA DAINA JIN ABUNDA NAKEJI ACIKIN XUCIYATA DA GANGAR JIKINA,KIDAINA TUNANIN JIKINKI NE YAKE SAWA NAKE KUSANTO KAINA GAREKI,TABBAS SURARKI MA ABUN SHA'AWACE KUMA TANA BURGENI,TANA BANI SHA'AWAR AMMA BA SHINE MAK'ASUDIN DA YASA NAKE ADDABARKI BA."*

tunda ya fara magana jikina yake macewa tsura masa idanuna danai yanxu sai naji sun kasa kallonsa,ahankali najasu na sauke k'asa gabana sai bugawa yake,kalamansa suna sakani jin kamar zan sume sai naji bana son jin karshen zancensa,abunda nakeson najin yau kuma bana so,don wani irin nauyi da fargaba suka baibayeni.
Dago hab'ata yayi yace "ki cigaba da kallona hakan zai saka na idasa fadar abunda yake cikin xuciyata,idan baki kallona xuciyar rauni gareta akanki bakina bazan iya fad'a ba."

Lumshe idanuna nai na sake maidasu kasa na girgiza kai,inajin kwallah na cika idona bansan ko na miye ba nace masa cikin wata kasalalliyar muryata "kayi hkr bazan iya ba"
Sharrrrr hawayen suka silalo sake runtse hannuna yayi ya sake matsowa kamar zai shige jikina,yagoge mun kwallar wasu nasake xubowa.
"Plsss kidaina,menene yasa kike kuka don Allah karkice har yanxu nine"

Girgiza kai nai ina jan majina nace "kaina ne yake ciwo"
Shafa kan yayi yace "sorry zai daina,kibar kukan ko kinaso kiga nima nayi"

Saurin dago kaina nayi ina kallonsa lumshe idonsa yayi ya bude yace cikin kallon idona yana narkemun su.
"Yes akanki nayi kuka deeyah,nima nayi mamaki kamar yadda kikai,Amman akan ki komi nai bazanyi mamaki ba.ba kin tambayeni miyasa nake kishin ki ba,akan wane dalili nake kishinki ko haka kikacw?"
Daina kallonsa nai nace "aa ba haka nace ba"
Sautin murmushinsa naji ya sake dago fuskata muna kallon juna yace "banda wani dalili dayasa nake kishinki face *SONKI,EHY SONKI DA QAUNARKI SUNE DALILIN,GIRMAN SU ACIKIN RAINA YASANI XUBDA HAWAYE AKANKI,SABODA BAN TABAJIN IRIN ABUNDA NAJI BA AYAU SABODA WANI KATO YA KAMA HANNUN MATATA"*

kasa daina kallonsa nai yayinda duk wasu kafa na jinin jikina suna ansar sakonsa,xuciyata karkarwa ta ke yayinda hawayena suka k'i daina tsiyaya,bakina da lips dina sunyi nauyin da basa iya furta komi,lumshe idanuna nai ina shashsheka naji ya tallabo kaina ya dora bisa goshinsa,yashiga sharemun hawayen da kuncinsa,yaja iska yana magana kamar zai mutu.
"Na zaci soyayyarki hallakani zatai ayau,meyasa zaki bari wani ya tabamun ke?bacin kinsan kid'in babu maike saini,mallakina ce ke nadeeya insha Allah har gidan Aljanna, *inasonki!inasonki nadeeyah har karshen xuciyata,akanki har rayuwata sai na rasa muddin irin haka ta sake faruwa,don Allah kice hakan bazai sake faruwa ba,kice kuskurene bazaki sake mikama wani wanda bani ba hannunki."*

Rud'ewa nai jin ruwan hawaye na sauka akan fuskarsa,jikina ya kid'ime narasa inda zan saka raina ko idona nakasa budewa,hannuna na rawa na dago kansa ban yarda na kallai ba nace "kadaina pls,kana sani jin haushin kaina,akanme zaka xubda kwalla akaina ni wacece don Allah ka daina."
Na juya baya inajin xuciyata kamar zata fito,ashe duk yadda nake zatona aransa na wuce nan,meyasa yake mun iri wannan son?anya na cancanta.
"Ke wani sashene na rayuwata,ba kwallana ba har raina zan iya badawa akanki,rokona shine kiyi man koda kwatar kaunar da nakejin ina maki,karkice zakiyi fushi dani idan da najure ahalin yanxu xuciyata mai rauni ce akanki plsss"

Gani nake kamar wasa ba gaske ba,yayane yake durkushe yana gaya mun irin wadannan zafafan kalaman,har da yin hawaye akaina wace irin ranace wannan ban san ina murmushi ba,na lumshe idona ina ajiyar xuciya.
Gaba d'aya kunyarsa nakeji nauyinsa ya cika mun idona,sake dunkulewa nai ina jan bargo don har lokacin ba kaya jikina.
Ganin idan banyi magana ba bazai yi shiru ba yasa na daure nace masa "katafi dakinka bacci nakeji"
Zama yayi yana cewa "babu inda zani anan zan kwana,har sai kince mun kin huce daga b'acin ran sana saki"

Rufe fuskata nai ina murmushin kafin na shagwabe nace kamar zan kuma "Aa ni bazan huce yanxun ba,katai kurin ni bacci nakeji"

"Kina wasa ko,to bari kigani"
Ban idasa tunani ba najisa saman gadona yana kokarin bude bargon,saurin tashi nai nace "shikenan nayi hkrin amma katafi dakinka babu kyau"

" akwai kyau mana,ko bakisan na bayar da sadakin ki ba wai"

Harararsa nai ina dauke kai nasake jan bargon nace "ni ban sani ba,kuma ba asanar dani ba don haka ba gaskiya bane."

Fixgoni yayi jikinsa muka xube kan gadon ya tsurawa fuskata ido yana kallona,nikuma na kasa kallonsa ya shafa kuncina ahankali har xuwa cikin kunnena,zabura nai zan tashi ya rik'eni shinshina wuyana ya fara tsoro ya kamani kamar yayi maye yace "inhar babu aurena kanki bazan aikata maki wannan ba,kiyarda dani andaura mana aure kafin mubaro nageria,amman sai idan kin amunce kin yarda kina sona zaki zauna dani sannan zaki tare."
Sakina yayi na tashi zaune ina kallonsa with out say anything,tashin yayi shima ya riko hannuna ya sunbata yana leko fuskata,yace "ban zaci ganin haka tattare dake ba inkikaji wannan labarin,mekenan pretty?kina nufin baki sona bazaki zauna dani ba."

Girgiza kai nai nace "ba haka nace ba,meyasa ni baza,a gaya mun ba to."
 yace "nima ban sani ba my deeyah,wlh sai ana gobe zamu taho Abba yake gaya mun,anyi hakane kawai don gudun rigimarki."
Ya dago fuskata yana lumshe idonsa yace "I love u pretty,I really love u bazan iya rayuwa mai dadi ba inhar bana tare dake,xuciyata ta jima tana renon soyayyarki,don Allah karki gujeni ki saurari xuciyarki karki yanke hukunci kaitsaye,please and please my love"
Rufe idanuna nai daker na kakalo abunda zance masa "zanyiy tunani akai xuwa nan da mukoma gida,pls kaje dakinka zanyi bacci kaina na ciwo"
Shafa kan yayi yace "sorry zai bari ki kwanta da safe saimuje adubaki,don Allah karki cigana da fushin kinji"
Kai na daga masa ya sunkuyo ga kunnena yace "pls can I kiss ur lips" saurin janyewa nai nace "no bana so"
Cikin salo da son rudaman xuciyata yace "plz my happiness once"

Mak'ale kafad'a nai inajin wani feelings yana dawainiya dani,jiyowa inda nake yayi ya sake cewa "once my love pls mana"
Ban da wani option na bude idona ina kallonsa ya wani narkemun,yana dage mun girarsa bance komi ba nadai rufe idona ina saurarensa,hakan sa yagani yasa yayi murmushi ya matso sosai kamar zamu zama abu guda,ya tallabo keyata ya dora hancinsa kan nawa ya shaki numfashina,sannan cikin wani salo wanda ya rikitani ya dora lips dinsa bisa nau ahankali ya bude bakina yakamo halshena yafara sucking,dan danan naji gabaki daya jikina ya ansa salon da yake mun yasa naji kamar na shige cikin jikinsa karna fito,saida na danne abunda nakeji na kwaci bakina,saurin kwanciya nai na rufe kaina da bargo ina sauke numfashi.

Cikin nishad'i yace "thank you pretty ilove u so much saida safe"
Har ya fita ban bude kaina ba har saida naji shigarsa dakinsa sannan.
Wannan dare nidai banyi wani bacci ba,kaina yaita ciwi ga sabon al'amarin da yafaru hakika na dade ina jiran wannan rana,banzaci haka zata zo ba wai yau nice na zama matar yaya karo na biyu,yau nice yaya yake kuka yake fadar kalaman kauna akaina.............ban san iya lokacin dana dauka banyi bacci ba nadaiji ya saceni kurin.

Episode  .46

Washe gari nice banyi salla ba sai karfe bakwai na safe,saboda bacci bai daukan ba sai biyar na asuba gashi lokacin masassarar ta sake sauko mani,na kudundune cikin bargona daker dai baccin mai nauyi ya daukan,shima yayan nasan bai samu bacci daren ba don daya yata shi sallar asuba dole zai lek'oni.

Kamar had'in baki ina bud'e idona ya shigo dakin,na maida idanuna na lumshe ina sakin gajeren murmushi bansan kona miye ba,nasan dai bai rasa na saba da sabon al'amarin daya samemu nidashi.

Kara sowa yayi kan gadon ya zauna gefena kusada kaina,mayataccen k'amshinsa ya daki hancina na ji har cikin xuciyata k'amshin ya isa,na bude idona ahankali ina kallonsa yayi kyau matukar gaske gashinsa sai shalk'i yake,kasa hkr nai saida abunda yake raina ya fito fili nace.
"Yaya inason wannan turaren"
Wani kalar murmushi mai sanyi da burgewa yayi mani,yana kallon idona yace "inkinaso saina baki shi"
Nok'e kai nayi nace "ajikinka ai nake sonsa ba ajikina ba" yayi dariya yace "dagaske?"
Rufe fuskar nai da bargon jikina ina dariya kadan, yace "yajikin naki to"

"Da sauki amman har yanxu kaina na ciwo masassarar ma inajinta kad'an,ga numfashina har yanxun yana man wahalar shak'a"

"Ayya my dear sorry,kiyi wanka kishirya idan muka karya sai mu wuce asibitin aduba manke,ina shirin angwancewa amarya ba lfya ina magana"
Boye fuskata nai ina dukansa cikin shagwaba nace"nidai kadaina,abunda ma bance na amunce ba"

Mik'ewa yayi yana saka duk hannunsa aljihu yana cewa"zaki amunce inada kyakkyawan zato akan hakan"
Ya mik'omun hannunsa na kama ina kokarin tashi,na mance shaf ban kwana sa kaya ajikina ba haka na mik'e,saida na ankare nai saurin sakinsa zan koma na lullub'e.
Fixgata yayi yana matseni jikinsa ya rad'a man ga kunnena "karki man rowar kallon halal d'ina don Allah."
Fuskata akan kirjinsa ina shakar sassanyar kamshinsa nace"nidai kadaina banfayi salla ba"
Gashina yake yamutsawa dan danan muryarsa ta canza yace cikin shauki "muje na rakaki to"

Daukata yayi daga ni sai bra da pant dina ya tura kofar da kafarsa ya maida da kafar,bakin komin ya direni ya fara had'a mani ruwa acikin komin mai zafi amma ba sosai ba,ya xuxxuba liquid soap dina da turaren wanka na ya kad'a ruwan sannan ya jiyo yana kallona,daga jiya xuwa yau idanun yaya wani irin kallo suke man mai sakani jin zan narke,matsoni yayi ya sani cikin kirjinsa nikau sai nok'ewa nake don ba karamin nauyinsa nakeji ba matuk'a.
Hannunsa ya zagaya dasu bayana zai balle man bra dina,nai saurin rik'esa ina yin narai narai sa fuskata.
"Zan iya wlh.......zan iya cirewa.....na....nayafe"
Kansa yasa ya d'ago fuskata yana shinshina mun fuskar yace cikin kwaikwayona "uhm uhm nidai ban yafe ba kibarni kawai"
Hannuwana nasaka na rike rigarsa na k'ank'amesa don abunda yake mun yana fin karfina,inaji ya balle yana zareta kamar zan kuka nace "to ngd katafi lokaci yana wuceni banyi salla ba wlh."
Sakinsa nai na saka hannu na kare kirjina na jawo towel na rufe ina harararsa.
Dariya yayi yana huro mun kiss a iska na turasa waje na rufe kofar nasaka makulli,don ba karamin aikinsa bane ya sake dawowa.

Naji dadin wankan sosai don nasamu karfin jikina,kan ne dai yaketa sara mun.
Bayan na gama sallar nadan shafa maina nagyara fuskata na saka farar powder da d'an lipstick,daga jiya zuwa yau harna fad'a nayi yar rama,idona yayi xuru xuru.
Doguwar riga na saka mai nauyi saboda garin yau akwai sanyi,don uban ruwan da aka sha jiya,don har karfe daya na daren jiyana ana xuba ruwan.
Gashi bada suwaita ko d'aya jacket ne kuma basada nauyi,sosai na aka turaruka na gashina na gyara na yane kan d babban veil,sannan na dauko wayata dake kashe tun jiya na kunna na nufi fitowa.
Karo mukai dashi naja baya inace masa "sorry yaya"
Bina yake da mayen kalo kamar zai cinye ni hannuna ya kama yace "kinganki kuwa pretty na,kin kara zama wata pretty din ta daban amman kin rame kadan"
Ina murmushi nace "nima naga ramar ai nasha wuya,don Allah kabani suwaita inkana da ita sanyi nakeji"
"Muyi breakfast Saina d'auko maki nasan akwai yunwa tare dake jiya bakici komi ba,duk na daura laifin akaina I'm very sorry my love,bada niyya nai hurting dinki ba."
Narke fuskanai nace "nidai kabari,nace fa nayi hkrin"

Zaunar dani yayi saman kushin yajawo table gabana sai rawar jiki yake akaina,duk sainaji wani iri katseni yayi da cewa "me kikeso kici love dina"
Rik'e hannunsa nai nace "kadaina don Allah zan xuba dakaina"
B'ata fuska yayi "kibarni mana duk banine silar ciwonki ba,bari na xuba mana zakici dani ko"
D'aga masa kai nayi sannan ya xuxxuba mana ya mik'omani cup dina ya dau nashi,ahaka muka ringa break din yana yi yana sakar mun narkekken murmushinsa,yana jana da fira da fadar kalamai masu sanyi da sanyaya xuciya agareni,muna cikin haka wayarsa dake gefena tayi ringing idona yakai ga sunan dake blinking.
Habibty nagani ansaka jinai xuciyata ta ansa na dauki wayar na mik'a masa,dan danan naga ya canza fuska ya ansa ya ajiye gefe ya cigaba da karyawarsa.
Ce masa nai "meyasa bazaka dauka ba,tunda muka taho bana zaton ka ansa kiranta."
"Karki shi gar da kanki cikin maganar nan pls,Kibarni da ita har yanxun tana under punishment ne"
Inajin kishi yana nuk'urk'usata amman dole na fad'a masa abunda yake ba daidai bane.
"Komi tayi maka yaya kwana goma sha biyar baka daukar wayarta ai ya isa ace duk hukuncin da ka tanadar mata ya kare,at least kodan lafiyarsu kaji yasuke ka dauka,kodan muhin ka dauka kaji yayake haba yaya ba dadi irin haka,ashe nima idan kuskure yasa wani abu ya had'a mu zaka iya daukar Long time ba tare daka kulani ba,zaka iya ashe?"
Saurin jiyowa yayi idonsa yana narkewa da kallona ya rike hannuna yace "bana fatar haka har abada,kuma ke bazakiyi rashin hankalin da tayi ba."
Girgiza masa kai nai nace"taya ka tabbatar,duk da bansan meya had'a kuba ba shakka akan kishine?kana tunanin ni wataran kishinka zai iya sawa naimaka ba daidai ba,kayi mata uxiri duk wata mace dole tai kishin mijinta,don Allah ka dauki wayar nan ka kirata"
Lumshe idanunsa yayi ya kai hannuna ya sunbata yace "inasonki nadeeya,kuma zan kirata amma ba yanxu ba dak mundawo,Allah yasa kidore abisa halayenki na gari."
Araina na amsa da Amin.

Munje asibitin muka duba jikin su Abba muka tarar ana koya masu tafiya,lamarin ya mana dadi ba kadan ba kasa tafiya mukai wajen likita ya dubani saboda jin dadin ganinsu jikin yana kara warwarewa,nayi farin ciki ba kadan ba.
Daker yajani mukaje aka dubani,likitan yace asthma tace takeson tashi saboda ruwan daya dakeni,haryake tambayata wane magani nakesha idan tatashi,na gaya masa yace zai bani wani,idan nai dace banyi wasa dashansa ba zan iya rabuwa da ita.
Ya bani arubuce harda na masassarar mukaje pharmacy d'insu muka siya,ranar anan asibiti muka wuni saida abba yashiga toilet nake ma Ammer maganar aurena da Yaya.

Tace"ai munsani kece aka boye mawa saboda rigimarki."

Nace"yanxu kun sani ammer shine kuka kigaya man."

"To bagashi kin sani ba,abunda zan rokeki shine kitaimakemu kirikeshi da amana,kiyi masa biyayya karnaji karna gani,khalipa ya mana komi kizama matar dayake alfahari da ita ta ko ina,don Allah pretty kimun wannan alfarmar."

Nariko hannunta nace "Ammer basai kin hadani da Allah ba,yaya yacancanci yimasa dukkan ladabi da biyayya kuma insha Allahu zan zauna dashi da amana kodan alkairansa akanmu."

Addua tayi mani tana saka mani albarka,ta kuma jaman kunne akan yadda zan nuna kishina bana hauka ba,kuma karna sake zafin kishi yasa nadanne hakkin Abokiyar zamana,koshi naga yana yin badaidai ba tsakaninmu in nuna masa hanyar da tadace,don maza da yawa matansu suke jamasu shiga wuta,ke amatsayinki na wadda akafiso kina ganin yana danne hakkin abokiyar zamanki bazaki iya nusar dashi ba,saboda kina ganin hakan shine daidai saboda yana sonki,toke ba kaunarsa kike ba tunda har bazaki iya nusar dashi hanyar dazai gyara lahirarsa ba,saboda kishi kikai mijiki  wuta da kanki da sunan kina sansa,Allah yasa mugyara Amma mata akan kishi babu abunda bazasu iya ba.
(Kalubale garemu mata,muyi kokari mugyara alakarmu data abokan zamanmu,koba kishiya ba akwai abubuwa da yawa da yadace kiringa dora mijinki akai inkinga yana kaucewa,don ta haka ne kurin zaki taimakesa ba wai kisa ido kice abunda babu ruwanka yafi dadin kallo ba,yes abunda mata keyi kenan don Allah mugyara karya zama don kinada fada aji wajen miji kiringa azabtar da wadanda ke tare dake,inkinga za,a shiga hakkin wata kiyi kokarin dakatarwa allah ma saiya duba laifukanki ya yafe maki.Allah yasa mugane kuma mugyara sai muma Allah ya yafe mana)

Abba ma data fito yaji nasihar Ammer saiya dora tashi akai,da shawarwari na rayuwa mun dade sosai saida yaya yashigo sannan akabar chapter.

Kujera yaja ya xauna yanata so na kallesa na k'iya ina danna wayata,ganin bazai daina zaneni da idonsa ba yasa na dago muka hada idon,kashe mun ido daya yayi yana daga mun gira.
Na hararesa na juya wajen Ammer muna d'an tab'a hira.
Jinai yana cewa Abba.
"Abba daga wajen likita nake,yamun bayani akan yanayin jikin naku. yace kashin kafafun suna ansar gashin da ake masu sosai,insha Allah nanda kwana bakwai ba mamaki ku iya tafiya da kafafunku"
Cikin farin ciki Abban yace "Alhamdulillahi Allah abun godiya to ubangiji yasa adace,allah ya nuna mana da rai da lfiya"
Duk mukace Ameen,da haka Abban yayi anfani yake masa nasihar zama da mata biyu.

"Kaji tsoron Allah ka kwatanta adalci tsakani su,karka nuna sonkai da son xuciya atsakaninsu,kayi bakin iyawarka wajen ba kowace hakkinta.kuma saikayi hkr zama da mace sai ankauda kai,shiyasa akafi son hakurin namiji yafi na macen yawa,ba kuma komine ake saurin yin saki kansa ba karike igiyoyinka akwai hanyoyi uku na hukunta mata kafin ka iya sakinta idan tayiwa miji laifi,nafarko ka zaunar da ita kaimata fada da nasiha,idan bataji ba ka kaurace mata ashimfid'a,inhakan yak'iya ka kai maganar ga magabatanta,idan suma sunkasa daukar matakin daya dace shine fa zaka yimata saki guda kamar yadda addini ya tanadar,don haka kayi hkr dasu ka kara akan wanda kake dashi,ubangiji Allah yakawo farinciki marar yankewa acikin wannan sabuwar rayuwar,Allah yamaku albarka yakara bude maku kofofin arxiki ku."
Ameen duk mukace sosai mukaji dadin wannan ranar,munyi hira sosai dasu sannan muka tafi gida.
Xuwa lokacin na samu saukin jikina sosai saidai  rashin karfin jiki,shima nasan ahankali zai barni.

Yawo muka d'an tafi nida yaya don tunda mukaxo babu inda mukaje saboda da safe muna asibiti sai magrib zamu koma,to yau mun dawo da wuri shiyasa yace muje muga gari.
Acikin yawon na tabbatar da wani abu,cewar yaya ba karamin so yake mun ba gabaki daya hankalinsa akaina yake,bai barni koda na second nadaina murmushi ba ni kaina har mamakin kaina nake,nasan duk da banriga na furta masa martanin soyayyarsa ba,ya gane ina cikin soyayyarsa amman baiyi kokarin takurani dason saiyaji ba,hakan ya kara masa martaba da soyayyarsa araina.
Siyayya yamun ba laifi daya ga abun mata yamasa kyau zai daukarmun,saida na dakatar dashi sannan araina nace damunje gida zan rabasu biyu na ba asmau rabi,matsayin tsaraba.
Bayan isha,i muka isa gida duk na gaji kafafuna ciwo suke mun sosai,sai narkewa nake ina shagwabe fuska kamar zan kuka nace "yaya nagaji wlh"

Ajiye kayan cikin falo ya nufoni yana xaunar dani kan kujera yana kokarin ciremun takalmana cover dana saka yace "sorry my love kiyi wanka sai mudanci wani abu sannan ki kwanta da wuri nasan dole zaki gaji"

Na Kara shagwabe masa ina kukan karya nace "yaya kafafuna ciwo sukeyi fah"

Shafa kafar yayi yana jin kamar zai cinyeta yace "zan maki tausa kinjiko zata daina"

Duk abunda nai biyeman yake har tausayinsa nakeji,saida nadan huta sannan nace "bari naje nai wankan inyi sallolina,kaima kaje kayi to"

Gabana ya fad'i nagirgiza kai nace "Aa nidai bana so katai dakinka."

Tsoro naji ya kamani nace kamar zan kuka "yaya aa nidai kayi hkr"

"Kinga my love babu abunda zan maki,nasan tsorona kikeji ko"
Nokewa nai akirjinsa kamshinsa yana saukarmun da wani irin feeling mai kashe sassanjiki.

"Kayi alkawarin baza kaimun komin ba to."
Kunnena ya d'an ciza nai kara ina dukansa ga baya yace"abunda kike tsoron bazan makiba,nima zanfison sai kinbar tsorona sannan."
Turesa nai na ruga dakina don jinake kamar na nitse inyana wannan maganar.

Shaf shaf nai wankan nayi sallar da ta wucemu,na gyara dakin don da safe ban wani gyarasa ba.
Inayi ina smile yaya ne kurin cikin raina jinake kullin kara sonsa nake,ba kamar daga jiya xuwa yau kamar ana buga mun gangar kaunarsa,ban dai son bayarda kaina tun yanxu saina kara jaraba soyayyarsa,duk da bana jin akwai gaibu acikita.
Na gama na fita naje muka ci abubuwan sa mukayo shopping nasha magunguna na,sannan na jawo ledar kayan daya siyarmun na raba su biyu,na tura gabansa nace "ka kara cikin tsarabar da zakai ma habibty dinka"

Na tab'e baki don haushin sunan da yai mata saving ga wayarsa nakeji,nace "oho nidai gasunan sunmun yawa kabata itama,tunda banice kadai matarka ba."

Murmushi yayi zai jawoni na matsa yasake mika hannu zai jawoni na sake matsawa,yace "sorry kishi ba dadi ko?"
Dalla masa harara nai na tashi zan tai daki,ya rikemun hannu "don Allah karkiyi fushinan,menai maki kuma wai kinfi son inta wahala akanki ko"

Kamar zan kuka nace "toni menace kayi mani,kayane nace ka kaima habibty itama tasa kagani kaji dadi."
Dariya yayi yajawoni jikinsa yana shin shinamun fuskata yana zagayemun fuska da gashin fuskarsa,dan danan jikina yarude  tsikar jikina tana tashi wani abu yaringa tsarga mun har cikin cikina.
Nai nasarar fixgewa na tashi ina ajiyar xuciya,yayi gyaran murya yace "tunda sunan bai maki ba zan canza mata wani,wane kikeson nasaka mata wanda bazai bata mun ranki ba."
Tafiyata daki nai da nawa nabarsa anan,inajin yadda kirjina yake bugawa yaya mayen soyayyane wlh,anya zan iya daina tsoronsa wannan dare guda zai lallasani wlh Allah yabani juriyar daukar bukatunsa.

Ajiye kayan nayi can wajen wadrop na zauna gefen gado inajin kamar naita kuka,indai haka kishin yake nidai Allah ka rage mun,duk yadda naso karna yi kuka saida nayin.
Ganin har eleven bai biyoni ba yasa naje nai shirin baccina,raina duk ajagule na gama na kashe glup na haye gadona ina adduar bacci,korabi banba naji motsinsa nai saurin juya baya na runtse idona nai pretending as bacci nake.
Gabana das das yake tsoro nakeji,karya sab'a alkawarin da yayi.
Jinsanai kurin cikin bargona ahaukace na wintsila na tashi ina murtsike idona, "yaya bacci fa nakeyi" jawoni yayi ya dora bisa cinyarsa dole na hkr na kwantar da kaina akai,yace "ashe kina iya bacci yau batare dani ba."

Jin haka yasa na fashe da kuka nace "kace fa kayi alkawari don Allah nidai karkayi"
Bakinsa kurin naji kan labbana saida naji kamar numfashina zai dauke,magana yake mun yana lasar bakina wanda bakejin wani irin yanayi marar musaltuwa.
"Ni bance zanyi bafa,kawai inason naji dumin jikin matata ne kawai."
Nishi na sauke nace daker "ya.....yaya.....ni dai"
Kunnena yakama yana tsotsa ai bansan sadda na makalkalesa ba ina jan numfashi,jinai kamar zan suma daker nake fidda isa abakina.
Banji alamar yaya zai daina tsotsar kunnena ba,iyakar shiga yanayi kam na shiga,don abunda nakeji bantab'a jinsa ba,kafafuna nake murzawa kamar wadda aka danne kanta da filo.
Saida na kwace kaina sannan yadaina,nishi nake ina shashsheka.
Kwanciya yayi yana ajiyar xuciya,ya kalloni na dauke kaina don nikadai nasan abunda yakemun yawo ga jikina,hannunsa ya mikomun cikin kwantar da murya yace "zokiji tawan,babu abunda zan maki"
Nai raurau da ido nace "ehy mana gashinan kanayi mun,bacin kuma kace babu abunda zakai man,yaya alkawari fa kayi"
Murmushi yakeyi yace"Allah kuwa bashi zan maki ba,inason kurin na cire maki tsorona kiyarda dani mana."

Shashsheka kawai nake nace kamar zan kuma saboda abunda nakeji har yanxun.
"nidai gaskiya aa yaya."

"Baki yarda dani ba kenan?ashe har masoyi zai iya cutar da masoyiyarsa,don Allah kixo inason na gane inkina sona ne"
Ya matso inda nake yariko hannuna yana lumshe idonsa,yace "nine fa my love bazan yimaki abunda zai cutar dake ba"
Gabana bugawa yake tare da wani matsanancin shak'i dake shawagi acikin jikina,runtse idona nai nace araina "menene wannan abun da nakeji?mekenan nashiga ukuna"
Jinai yace "kauna ce da tarin shak'i da sha'awa bakomi ba."
Zaro idona nai ashe afili nayi,rufe baki nai narasa inda zan saka raina don kunya.
Fashewa nai da kuka ina rera abuna don narasa abunyi,jawoni jikinsa yayi yana shafa kaina cikin dishashshiyar murya yace"shikenan tunda bakiso bazan sake ba,kiyi hkr banyi haka don nasaka hawayenki xuba ba.don Allah ki daina"
Sai naji kuma yaban tausayi,kamar ban kyauta ba.
Shirun nayi nai lamo jikinsa kamar yar mage,hulata ya lalubo ya samun yagyara man kwanciya muryarsa daker take fita yace "kiyi baccinki bazan kara tab'aki ba."
Nauyin abunda nai nakeji tsoro ya shigeni kuma,karfa na jama kaina xunubi amman yaxanyi da abunda nakeji.
Shiru d'akin yayi nidashi babu wanda yasake magana,kuma idonmu biyu ba bacci mukai ba duk sai naji ban kyauta masa ba,da alama abun ya b'ata masa bazai dai iya gaya man ba kar raina ya b'aci.ashe har shi zai guji bacin raina ni bazan iya gudun abunda zai bata masa nasa ba,anya banso kaina sa yawa ba.
Xuciyata bugawa take naruntse idona don inji idan zan iya abunda ke raina,don fidda sa daga wannan mode din.
Daurewa nai na cije muryata asanyaye na shagwabeta na matsa jikinsa,kirjina sai bugawa yake nace "uhn uhn yaya kaji"
Jiyowa yayi cikin kulawa yace "menene pretty na"
Cikin narkewa nace nan yana mun k'aik'ai ka sosa mun,matsowa yayi jikina kamar zamu hadiye kanmu yana shafa fuskata,cikin zak'uwa yace "inane nuna man ingani" yadda gabana yake faduwa kamar zai fito waje,nakama hannunsa na dora bisa wuyana."
Ajiyar xuciya yaja nannauya,ya soma sosamanin kamar yadda nace masa.
Yace"ya daina ko bai bari ba"
Numfashi naja na sake shige masa nace "uhn uhm bai daina ba,niba da hannu nakeso ba"
Kamar na tunxura sa ya dora fuskarsa akan tawa,muna musayar numfashi yace cikin kasala da shauk'i "dame kikeso gaya man my love zanmaki koma dame kikeso"
Ban iya bashi ansa da bakina ba saboda zazzafan kiss din daya fara aikamun dashi,hannunsa na dora akan wuyan aikuwa ya samu abunyi,haka yaringa sarrafa halshena da shafa duk jikina,nidashi banda shashshekar numfashi da nishi ba abunda muke,jikina ya rude matuk'a yanxu na tabbatar sha'awace ke dauwaniya dani bakomi ba,kuma abunda yaya yamun ne dalilin tasowarta.
Duk mun yamutse gadon,yadda yaya yake mun dolene yasa naji abunda nakeji,har jinai kamar zanyi fitsari so nake na dakatar dashi hakanan amman banda wannan damar tunda nina jawosa.
Ban haukace ba saida ya rabani da rigar dana saka ta bacci,wadda daga ita sai pant daman nasaka.
Nidashi bansan waye yakai wani haukace wa ba,nidai nawa na sani don wata irin mik'a nakeyi kamar zan balle jikina,tunda ya kama breast dina bai san menake ciki ba,kamar zai ciresu daga kirjina kamar naci yaji haka nakeyi,na rike kanshi gamm ina murza kafafuna agadon,daker nake jawo iska saboda dadin dake ratsa ko ina najikina,duk wani tsoro danake jin inayi na rasasa,saida naji yana cire mun pant d'ina na dawo hayyacina,na rik'e hannunsa daker nake fixgo maganar nace "a....aa ya......yaya,k......kaifa.......kaifa kace bazakai ba."
Shi baima iya bani ansar ba saboda bana jin koyayi maganar zata fito,sakin pant d'in yayi yana jawoni ya matse kamar zai b'ab'b'allani,ajiyar xuciya kawai mukeja nidashi har lokacin hannunsa na saman breast d'ina yana luliyata.
Kunnena ya jawo yace mun cikin fidda numfashi daker.
"Can I sucking u"
Girgiza kai nai inajin kamar zan mutu,amman bazan iya bari yayi mun haka ba don yana iya zarcewa,gaba dayama ni tsoron ya dawo mani kamar zan kuka nace "kabarni haka,don Allah kabarshi haka."

"Okay shikenan thank you pretty,I love you for ever xokiyi baccinki ajikin mijinki zaki kwanta ko."
Na d'aga masa kaina ya sunbaci bakina yana shafa kirjina yace "na kosa swt hrt,nakosa lokacin yaxo I need u more and more"
Shiru nai masa ina ajiyar xuciya,inajin bazan iya sake hada ido dashi ba.
Ahaka bacci mai nauyi ya suremu mudukan...............

Episode  . 47

Na rigasa tashi don mun makara bamuyi sallar asuba ba,ahankali na zare daga jikinsa na sauka na shige toilet wanka na fara sannan nayo Alwalla,saida na shirya cikin riga da wando pink da blue sunyi masifar yimun kyau,na jira doguwar hijab dina na salla na shimfid'a abun salla na kabbara,saida na gama nai azkar  sannan na d'ebo ruwa ga hannuna na bud'e fuskarsa na watsa masa.
Aikuwa afirgice yatashi yana ajiyar xuciya,dariya na shiga yi masa na matsa nesa dashi don inyakamani zanji ba dadi,kallona ya tsayayi kamar zai cinyeni.
Muryarsa ta masu bacci yace "keko,zan kamaki ne yarinya.shine kika tashi baki tadani ba kin kyauta."
Jingina nai da tagar dakina ina kallonsa,sainaga ma kyau yayi mani da safiyar nan gaba d'aya jikinsa kara mulmulewa yake,ko hada canjin weather oho.
Katseni yayi da cewa"kibar kallona kokuma naxo na cinye idanun."

"Yaya time fa yana wucewa nidai ba ruwana wlh"

I wonder duk yadda raina ya b'aci dashi ashekaranjiya amman yana xuwa dakina ya kalailayeni bansan inda haushinsa da b'acin ran suka nufa ba.
Inama nikadai nakeda kai,inama nice nafara samunka,inama bani da reble acikin xuciyarka da nafi kowa sa,a arayuwata samun miji irinka babbar sa,ace ta rayuwa.
Amman duk da haka nagode ma Allah,kuma xan cigaba da gode masa zanyi iyakar yina wajen ganin na kyautata maka,bazan bari kayi kuka dani ba zanyi iya yina wajen sarrafa kishina,bazan bari ya lalata tsakanina dakai ba.

Yau mun dade bamu fita ba kuma duk yaya ne ya b'ata mana lokaci,saida nayida gaske sannan yatashi muka fito,don ya mak'alemun sai wata shagwaba yake kamar muhin,ni mamakinsa ma nake wai daman haka yayan yake,haka yakema asmau koko ni kadai yake mawa.
Tuna wannan danai har saida naji d'aci kan bakina,naja tsaki ina kauda kaina gefe lokacin muna cikin mota zamu asibitin.
Kallona yayi yace "what's happen love?"
Nace"na k'osa mu koma gida ina missing anty,gashi sau biyu mukai waya tunda mukazo"

Kaina na d'aga kurin don banda abun cewa bayan haka.
Har muka sauka ban sake magana ba,daman inji idan sunyi tunda sunyi d'in ni nafita koba sabulu.

wata arniyar juyowa yayi da gaba dayan jikinsa,da wata irin sassarfa ya nufoni,lumshe idona nai inajiran isowarsa sai jinai ya daukeni yayi sama sani yana juyawa dani acikin dakin,muryarsa da tsananin farin ciki marar misaltuwa dariya yake wadda bazance na tab'a ganinsa yana yinta ba sai yau.

Haka yaringa man har saida nace nidai ya saukeni juwa nake gani,sannan ya direni agado yana sabbatun farin ciki karshe wannan daren bamuci abinci ba,don haukace mun yayi wani irin mahaukacin romance yake aika mun dasu,zafafa wadanda bakina bazai iya misalta yadda nakejinsu ba.
Iyaka dai nasan nafita daga wannan duniyar ya kaini wata,mai cike da dadi da tsantsar kauna sa soyayya,saiga ni ina kuka riris saboda matsiyacin feelings a wannan lokacin da yace sai shigeni bazan hana ba,saidai idan naji azaba na nemi guduwa amman nasha wahala,daker ya kyaleni muka lula duniyar bacci.
Na mance da wata asmau can da b'acin ranta....................

*Alk'awarin Allah tabbatacceni*

Ana gobe zamu tafi gida munje ganin su Abba asibiti muka tadda abun farin ciki,Abba da Ammer tsaye bisa k'afafunsu tafiya suke kamar yadda mukeyi,har gudu aka sasu tsabar farin ciki rungumesu nai nakama kuka,kuka riris haka nakeyi ina kallon su daya bayan daya gani nake kamar basu bane...........daker na iya yin shiru ranar kowa ya ganmu yasan muna cikin farin ciki marar misaltuwa,har kafafun nasu nake shafawa gani nake kamar ba gaske ba.
Abba yace "ikon Allah kenan,babu abunda yafi karfin ubangiji hakika adduar da muka shekara aru aru munayi akan jafar Allah ya tabbatar mana da ansuwarta tun ranar da asirinsa ya tonu,haka wadda muka ringayi akanki nadeeyah itama Allah ya karb'a mana ya raya ki har xuwa lokacin da mukayi ido hudu dake,sannan wadda mukai akan k'afafun nan itama gashi nan ya ansa mana,mezamuce da ubangiji hakika saidai muce Allah yak'arama annbinmu dubun salati,kuma mukara godema Allah Alhamdulillahi ala kulli hal,lallai babu mai bada lafiya saikai babu mai kashewa da rayawa saikai,babu mai iko da jikinmu sai kai mungode maka ya Allah,ka kara mana ni'imominka."
Duk muka ansa da amin Ammer tana godewa Allah tana hawaye,nikaina har lokacin hawayen nake ganin yadda ubangijinmu yake warware mana dukkan matsalolinmu,banda godiya mezamuyi masa.
Daman ance bayan wuya sai dad'i,dukan wani tsanani yana tare da sauki.

Aranar aka bamu sallama muka wuce hotel dasu na barmasu d'akina zamuyi anfani dana yaya,ranar bamuyi bacci ba sai after 2 hirace ta abubuwan da sukaita faruwa akaita tunawa wani muyi kuka wani muyi dariya,saida Abba ya tsayar da firar sannan mukaje muka kwanta.

Ina cikin jikinsa na lafe yana wasa da gashin kaina yace mun "nifa komun koma bana son wani taro don karma ace za,a Hanani sakewa dake,walima ce kurin za,ai gidan dana gama ginawa inda zamu koma."
Nace"gaskiya yaya aa nidai"
Ya na cusa yatsunsa cikin kaina ina lumshe ido yace "kiyi hkr love amman gaskiya babu wani biki da za,ai yadda tsarin addini yace haka za'ai kiyi hkr kinjiko love"
Nad'aga kaina nace "shikenan,yadda kace haka za'ai babu jayayya nidakai yaya."
Kiss din goshina yayi yace "thank you matar kirki Allah ya maki albarka,Allah yasa haihuwan farko ki haifo mana baba da mama."
Duk'unk'unewa nai jikinsa ina jin kunya ina dariya nace "nidai ka bari"
Dariyar yayi yace "I'm serious Allah,kice Ameen"

Na mak'ale kafad'a yace"ko kice kokuma nai maki abun jiya."
Na zaro idona cike da firgici nace "aa natuba indai ameen ce nace Ameen shikenan ko"
Dariya yayi don yasan ajiyan baimun da sauk'i ba,sucking d'ina ya dungayi saida nai fitsari,naringa rokonsa yadaina saida yaga ina kuka wurjanjan sannan ya kyaleni,don da har ya nufo ni zai kashe boss din aguje nashige toilet na kulle saida ya rantse bazai mun komiba sannan na fito.

"Aidai duk wayan amarya sai ansha manta,kuma inanan ke zaki ringa mun kuka kina cewa yaya kaxo kayi,don Allah kayi na k'osa."
Kukan karya nasaka masa nace "Allah ya kyauta man,ni Allah bazan ba kuma nidai kadaina fadar haka bana so"
Hannunsa yasa ya kashe bed side lap yana cewa "shikenan bani madarata nasha sai muyi bacci"
Narkewa nai nace "Allah kuwa wayau zakai mun,jiyama ba haka kayi ba."

Kamar dan yaro yace "jiyama fa love kece kika tun xurani,wayyo Allah nidai kibarni"
Ina mashi kukan shagwaba nace "to karka cire mun pant kayi alkawari"
Murmushi yayi yace "nayi shikenan ko"
Na daga kai kirjina yana duka,har tsoron yafara wasa dani nake don yaya karsheni wlh,harjinake zan bar duniya numfashina yana sizing.
Fuskarsa yasa saman breast dina yana wani murzawa,cikin wani irin motion na bank'are ina turosu sama inajan iska,kafin ya cafki guda na hannun dama yafara jansa yana tsotsa wani marayan numfashi naja,ina rik'e kansa idona har wani marmar yake saboda yadda nakeji.
Azafafe ya cigaba sa aikamun sakonnin da bazan iya maida reply ba,yayi mun pieces da kaya ya watsar dasu,kuma abun mamaki nice nake taimaka masa ake cirewa banma san inayi ba,a wannan dare nasha kukan wuya dana dadi,har fada mukai saboda nakiyarda yayi mai gaba dayan.
Kokowa kam mukai watsi da gadon da fuloluwan,daker nasha kasa muka idasa kwana muduka,saida sanyin asuba ya tadamu muka koma kan gadon.
Daker mukai sallar asuba muka koma bacci,karshe dai muka makara don time kadan jirginmu zai tashi, bamu tsaya break ba mukatai airport,aciki muka samu abun sawa bakin salati.
Zuwa karfe biyar na yammacin ranar litinin muka sauka garin kaduna,kaduna garin gwamna.

Mun samu tarba ta musamman agidan Anty,don nan muka sauka muka tadda duk su inna nanan basu koma gidansu ba,rungume juna mukai Da sumy tana ware baki cike da mamaki tace "lalalalala kingani kuwa?keda kukaje jinya Amman harda wasu kumatu kika tara"
Dariya duk aka sanya kowa farincikin ganin Abba da Da ammer cikin koshin lafiya don gabaki daya jikinsa yayi wata irin mulmulewa cikin kwana talatin da mukayo,ba zaka ce sune lokacin da akafiddo su daga ramin da J yasaka su ba,tunda nai ido hudu da anty na gane cikine da ita,natuna sadda naji muryarta ta canza,ashe duk sabon bbynmu ne ya canza mata yanayi.
Oganniya asmau ba kunya ba komi taje ta lik'e jikin yaya,kugu da kugu niko gaisawa da ita bamuyi ba,bama ta kowa take ba ta mijinta take bana jin kosu Abba ta gaida,naji abun ya mani ciwo don ni bazan iya yiwa nata iyayen haka ba,ga inna nan kamar Ammer haka na dauketa,amma ni iyayena sunyi tafiya basuda lfy tsawon wata guda,gashi mun dawo da nasarori bazata iya yimani murna ba balle su,ai gaisuwa ma wani abuce.

Inna tace "iyye kina da masu jinyar baki mance da tsaraba"

Nabude na fiddo masa su cikin wata leda mai kyau duk na had'asu donma karna mance wani abu,naba inna nace duba kigani inna zasuyiwa mijinki kuwa."

Muna nan zaune Abban su sharifa da Abbana da yayan suka shigo,lokacin ma Ammer da anty suma suka fito falon ya cika aka zauna ana fira,duk yadda yaya yaso na kallesa hana idona nai,don inhar na kallesu zanga abunda ya b'ata mun rai.
Nafiddo kayan su sharifa  danata ita kanta antyn na bata. aikuwa taita jin dadi tana yabawa,abbansu kuma agoguna ne da turare na basa,duk wanda naba ya nuna man tsantsar murnarsa yamun addua,amman asmau koga fuskarta bangani ba nayiwa d'anta siyayya,duk cikinsu kayansa sunfi na kowa yawa da tsada,so don nasan badan ita naiba shiyasa ban damu ba ko akwalar rigata.
Bajewa akai cikin babban falon aka saka darduma katuwa,aka dauko abinci aka shiga saving juna.Ammer tatashi ta xuba masu itada Abba,anty ta saka masu ita da abbansu sharifa Asmau ita da yaya
Ni na saka nawa ni kadai sumy kuma ita da inna.
Wajen yayi shiru sosai kowa na xuba ma cikinsa,turawa kurin nake don wani abu mai kama da kishi duk ya dabaibayeni,daker nake had'iye lomar danake kaiwa.
Ina jiyowa idanunsa suna yawo akaina,amman bazan iya dagowa na dubesu ba,don ga baki take nasa asmau ko kunyar idon mahaifansa bataji,kai jama,a duk xalk'inka aikabari ku ware.

Nidai ko rabin wanda nasaka banba na ajiye nasha lemu naja gefe ina danna waya.
Kamar daga sama najisa yana ceman"mekikaci anan har da zaki ajiye,bacin ajirgi ma banda cake da lemu babu abunda kikaci,maza daukarsa kici inbakisan na sab'armaki."
Jinai zan kuka don daman kan gab'a nake nace cikin muryar kukan nace"wlh na k'oshi yaya bana jin yunwa sosai"
Abbana yace "ban son musu maza ki dauka ki kara ci ko yayake,sokike yunwa ta kamaki."
Anty tace "kobaki da lafiya ne?"
Nai narai narai nace "bakina babu testing"

"To kiyi hkr kikara ko yayane"
Ba yadda na iya haka na dauka na cigaba sa cusawa raina bayaso,dana zo zan ajiye zaice saina k'ara da haka harnai rabin abincin sosai sannan yayarda na ajiye.

Bayan kowa ya gama ne Abbana yayi addua sannan yafara da yiwa su inna godiya akan alkairinsu garemu,tare da kara jinjina kokarin yaya yayi masa babbar kyauta ta wani company sa na sarrafa auduga,kowa ya tayasa murna yashima kuma yayi masa godiya sosai,on ready daman duk takaddun kadarorin suna hannun yaya,kuma duk abunda J ya mallake na iyayen su yayan,kotu ta anso masu kayansu tuni Abban su sharifa yaje ya ansa suna hannunsa.

An d'an taba hira wadda yawanci nasiha ce da jan hankali akan zumuncinmu da tuna abubuwan dasuka wuce,gajiya nai da zaman na tashi nayi masu saida safe.

Bayan kowa ya watse yatai dakinsa yaya na nan zaune asmau da muhin sunta masa hira,har saida tace "katashi muje mu kwanta koba gajiya tare dakai"

Baice komi ba ya dauki muhin d'in sukayi gaba,binsa tayi baya yau jitake ba wanda ya kaita farinciki gata ga habibin mijinta,iyakar cika cike yake da ita don duk abunda tayi yana sane,baiga inda ta gaida su Abba ba,baiga inda tayiwa deeya mgn ba balle tayi mata murnarn samun lafiyar iyayenta.
Kauda kansa yayi bai ce mata komi ba akan abunda tayi,yabarta tayi d'okin dawowar tasa akwai lokacin da zai wanketa.
Aikuwa yaga d'oki sai nan nan take dashi,wanda gaba d'aya hankalinsa yana kan love d'insa,yasan yanxu haka kuka take abunda yakeji kenan aransa.gashi babu damar yace zaije inda take,wannan dare dai Asmau ta cire kunya tabawa mijinta dukkan wata kulawa,kuma shima bayyi k'asa guiwa ba ya sakarmata jiki yakuma tayata sun more wannan dare,wanda deeya takejinsa kamar daren mutuwarta.don kirikiri idonta bai runtsa ba,bayan kukanta da tasha saiga masassara ta rufeni don daman tun ajirgi nake jin kaina yana ciwo.
Sai gaf da asuba baccin wuya ya saceni.

****
"Ki tashi kiyi sallah baki ga lokaci ya na wucewa ba."
Khalipa ya fad'a bayan dawowarsa daga masallaci,mik'a tayi babu riga ajikinta ta wani bank'aro kirji cike da salon jan hankali tace "wlh na gajine yaya,don Allah ka kaini toilet cinyoyina ciwo suke"
Baice mata komi ba ya sab'eta yaje yasauketa cikin ruwan da ya had'a mata,don gabaki dayan hankalinsa yayi wajen love,direta yayi zai juya tai saurin rikoshi tace "to ka tayani don Allah."
Janye hanunsa yayi yanufi k'ofa cike da haushi tace "saurin me kake wai,kuma shariyar fa tamiye."
Kai tsaye yace mata "wajen nadeeya zanje indawo"
Wata zabura tai tafito daga cikin ruwan tasha gabansa cike da haushi tace"nadeeya fa kace?meza kayo dakinta da asubar nan,wai yaya mekakeson zama ne ko bakasan babu auren ta akanka bane kake acting like miji da mata."
Wata irin jiyowa yayi saida taja baya yace "karki sake,karki fara cewa zakimun rashin kunya wlh tallahi ashirye nake da daukar mataki akanki wanda zaki dawwama kina nadama, *nadeeya she is my wife* kuma abunda nai maki jiya shizanje itama nai mata,ba dai tambayata kike mezanje nayo ba to aikinji,inkina saita xuciyarki ki saita babu abunda zai canza,bara kiji abunda baki sani ba asmau"
Idonta yana shatatar kwallah take kallonsa ya cigaba sa cewa,"kamar yadda nake sonki haka nakesonta kamar yadda aka daura aurenmu dake haka aka dauda mana,sannan kisani iyayenta dakika raina har kikejin baki iya gaidasu balle kiyi masu murnar lafiyar da suka samu,nima iyayena ne duk duniya banda wasu iyaye kamarsu,idan kin darajasu nikika daraja inkin wulakantasu nikika wulakanta,zabi ya rage gamai shiga rijiya inkina iya gyarawa ki gyara inkuma ahaka zamuyita tafiya tofa ina gaya maki bazanji wuyar sawwake maki ba,so kishiga hankalinki."
Yafice yabarta anan daga ita sai dan pant zubewa tai cikin bayin tana rusa kuka kamar Allah ya aikota,tunda takejin tsanar aurensa da nadeeya bata tabajin abu irin na yau ba,ita duk maganganunsa babu wacce tayi mata zafi irin kalamar _nadeeya she is my wife_ kamar ta shek'e kanta haka takeji,ko kadan ta rasa yadda zata daina kishin nadeeya,bata san ma kishin yana da xafi ba saiyau.
Cikin kuka take dukan tayils din kewayen tana cewa"meyasa zakaimun haka?da kasan zaka hadani da wata meyasa ka aureni,meyasa zaka yiwa xuciyata kishiya bana iya jurewa,bazan iya ba zafi nakeji ga kirjina muddin kana tare da nadeeta xuciyata bata tabayin sanyi,"
Haka taita hauka acikin bayin har ta tada muhin,yaringa tsala kuka amma ko gezau batai ba,don haushin ubanshi yasa taji shima haushin yake bata.
Ko kadan kukan baidamun ta yau saida ta gama nata sannan ta fito taje ta daukai tana dungurinsa.(😂😂allah sarki muhin bakai ka kashe xomun ba)

********

Ahankali ya tura k'ofar dakin yajita kuma bude yasaka kanshi aciki,dund'um yake ko ina rufe ya kunna hasken dakin can ya hangota cikin bargo ta rufe har da kanta,cikin sauri ya isa wajenta yana bud'e bargon.
Wani uban huci ya dakesa saida gabansa ya fad'i afirgice ya dagani yasaka cikin jikinsa yana sauke ajiyar xuciya,ya tallabo fuskata muryarsa na rawa yace"deeya na,bude idonki ga yayan yazo baki lafiya shine baki sanar da kowa ba,yanxu da wani abu ya sameki fah"
Ina nishin wahala na shafo fuskarsa nace "yaya shine katafi kabarni ko?kasan bana iya bacci mai dadi inba tare dakai ba,kuma shine ko kaxo mun saida safe."
Sunbatar duka fuskata yakeyi yana cewa"I'm sorry,I'm very sorry love bansan hakan zai bata ranki na,nasan kinyi kuka jiya ko?"
Bakina turo na daga kaina shafa kaina yayi yace"I'm sorry dole haka zanyi banda wata mafita,kamar yadda kike matata itama matata ce kiyi kokarin kauda duk abunda zai b'ata maki rai,inbacci kikaji ya dauke maki ki kama karatun qur'ani,ko kiyi salla don Allah karki biyewa xuciya kinjiko love"
Lamo nai ajikinsa na d'aga kaina nace "zanyi kokari na kiyaye,ina sonka dayawa yaya shiyasa basan lokacin da nakejin zafi yana taso mani daga xuxiyata ba."
Kissing din goshina yayi yace "kiyi hkr da Asmau koba komi akwai kuruciya tare da ita har yanxu,kibata lokaci zata gyara bana son fad'a yana shiga tsakaninku,idan tayi maki karkiyi saurin biye mata kitarata sannan ki sanar dani,aman karki biye mata balle kuzama d'aya kinjiko love"
D'aga kaina nai yasake soka kanshi cikin fuskata yace "intai maka maki kiyi wanka?"
Lumshe idona nai nace "sanyi yaya"
Yace"ruwan zafi zan had'a maki,bazakiji sanyi ba kinyarda"
Banda kwarin jikin tashi dole na amunce masa,ya idasa yaye bargon sannan ya daukoni kamar jaririya muka nufi toilet din.

Kuyi hkr ban fara da wuri bane wlh,ayyuka nai shiyasa.👏🏻

Daker ya fita nikuma na cire pad din jikina na saka inda nake tara shararta,nai tsarki da ruwan d'umi wanda na xuba masa liquid soap din clove da farin miski saida na gama sannan na shiga na fara wankan,nai lum acikin ruwan inajin dadinsa wannan masassarar bai wuce ta period bace don duk inzanyisa sainayi,har na gama sannan zata daina addabata.

Sumy ce tace "inna bazan fasa baki hkr ba,asmau tayi laifi koma ince tana yin ba daidai ba,amma ke uwace karkice haka kiyi mata addua."
Cikin fad'a tace mata "na gaji haka sumayya,fad'a na nawa zanyi mata ai bata daukan uwar ba ita data daukeni da batayin abunda takeyin,mutanen nan bamuda kamarsu idan ta bari khalipa ya rabu da ita ubanwa zata samu dazai mata abunda yake mata,na gama magana karki sake kokarin kareta ko nemar mata afuwa."
Janjikina nai nawuce d'akina inajin babu dadi,Asmau zata tafka kuskure arayuwarta domin uwa ba abun wasa bace,bakin uwa masifane muddin kasake tafurta maka kalma marar dadi kashiga ukunka,meke damunta da har kanta yake toshewa,insha Allah bazan bari haka ta faru ba,aurenta da yaya bazai rusheba kodan samun kwanciyar hankalin innar,hakika inna uwace tagari kuma da ace ana haifar halin mutun,to kuwa asmau bazata yi abunda take ba.Allah ka saka mu iya sarrafa xuciyarmu,kasa mufi karfin shad'an.

Adaren sumy ta rakani wajen Anty fatima acikin daren ta fara mun wankin jiki,wani ruwan turarene taringa mulmule jikina dashi sak'o da lungu,wani daki muka shiga na zauna dagani sai pant da bra.tafi awa guda darabi tana mun abuguda,muna tab'a hira duk akan yadda zan gyara kaina,da kulawar da zanba mijina salo salo kala kala take dorani akai.
Tun inajin nauyinta har na dayaye mune bamu bar wajen ba sai after ten.
Yaya yakirani yana fad'a ina natafi har kamar yanxun,hkr na bashi nace gamu nan kan hanya.dajin muryarsa ransa a b'ace yake nace araina Allah yasa banice silarsa ba.

Koda mukaje gidan bakin get na gansa zaune kan wani benci ya dafe kansa,sumy tayi cikin gida ni kuma na tsaya wajensa.
Cikin d'ar d'ar nace "yaya kayahkri don Allah"
Dagowa yayi idonsa  yayi jaa muryarsa ta dishe yace mun "bakomi love muje ciki,har kun iso ina sumayyar"
Ina kallonsa cike da tausayi nace "ta wuce ciki,yaya baka da lfy ne"
Yagirgiza kai kurin yaja hannuna muka tai d'akina.
Zaunar dani yayi kan gado shikuma ya kwanta ya d'ora kansa bisa cinyata.ganin haka yasa na cire hijab d'ina na gyara masa kanshi ina sosa masa cikin gashinsa,lumshe idonsa yayi baice komi ba tsawon lokaci,nima kuma ban takura masa da magana ba na cigaba da massaging kansa,munkai minty biyar haka ya bude idonsa yana kallona cikin damuwa yace "kiyi hkr pretty"
Shafa sajensa nai nima ina kallonsa nace "mekayi mani yaya,komiye na yafe maka."

Ajiyar xuciya yaja yana riko hannuna ya sunbata sannan yace "kiyi hkr zamu tai da Asmau Cyprus"...................
