Duka kirjina yayi wani abu yaxo mun iya wuyana mai d'aci,amman saina kira haxbunallahu wani mal wakil,na sake nanatawa kusan sau uku na lumshe idona sannan na iya bude bakina danaji yana d'aci saboda maganar da yayi mun nace.

"Yaya duk yadda kace haka xa'ai inhar kana ganin da ita din zakutai wlh ni bazan d'aga maka hankali ba,nasan dai akwai lokacin da zaku dawo."

Wani irin mugun sonta da tausayinta ya narke masa acikin kokon ransa,tashi yayi zaune yana tallabo fuskarta cikin wani irin shauk'i dake d'awainiya dashi muryarsa ta sake komawa so cool yace "nagode maki,nagode dakika amunce love don da ace baki amunce ba komai zai faru saidai yafaru bazamutai da ita ba,nasan abun zai maki babu dadi kiyi hkr don Allah."
Had'iye b'acin raina nayi saboda iyakar kulewa na k'ule me asmau take tak'ama dashi,to kuwa zata sha madarar bak'in ciki indai kishi takeyi tukuwa tunda ta dage saita bimu zata kwan kwad'esa har tai amai.
Cikin nuna rashin damuwa nace masa "haba yaya miye aciki,taxo muje kaima inhar kabarta anan din hankalinka rabuwa zai,so ba wani abubane."

Sai lokacin ya saki ransa yace "ban san meyasa asmau tafaye son kanta dayawa ba,bata son na zauna lafiya asmau ta daina ganin girmana matsayin mijinta,bata darajani kishi da sonkai ya rufe idonta amma tabbas hkrina akanta yazo karshe,ingaf da daukar mataki sosai akanta."

Nagirgiza kaina nace "karka biyeta,har yanxu akwai yarinta atare da ita,kuma karka duba halinta kaduba yadda inna zataji inhar kace zakayi abunda zai kawo batanci.ka kara hkr zata gyara lokacine."

"Uhm Allah yasa amman tabbas takaini bango kowa zaiji babu dadi nagaji haka,kifara shiri insha Allah nan da kwana shidda zamu wuce."
Nace "Allah ya kaimu ranar,yanxu ka tashi kaje ka kwanta dare nayi."
Jawoni jikinsa yayi yana cewa "nifa anan zan kwana jikin ki love,kwana biyun nan bakiji wuyar danake sha ba bana tare dake."
Har lokacin raina babu dadi na dai danne abunda nakeji nace masa "nidai ba ruwana katashi katafi,dadin baki kurin zakai mun."
Natashi ina turesa jikina yayi saurin riko hannuna yana sake zaunar dani akan cinyarsa ya kanainayeni,yana cewa cikin muryarsa kamar yasha kayan maye.
"Wai wani sabon k'amshi nakeji tare dake mekika shafa"
Ya fad'a yana soka kanshi cikin wuyana yana suca hannunsa cikin gashina yana wani irin yamutsawa,hade da fiddo halshesan yana lashe mun wuya,tare da sauke mun wani irin numfashi dauriyace kurin nakeyi,don jikina tsuma yafara saboda kafin na baro wajen anty fatima tabani wasu abubuwa kala uku na shanyesu acan.
Wata kila suke aiki ajikina don ada inyana tab'ani bana jin wad'anan alburusan abubuwan namun yawo ajikina,ahankali yakwantar dani ban ankare ba.
Yaringa goga fuskarsa awuyana xuwa kirjina da suke cikin rigata mai sulb'i,so yadda yake juya kansa da fuskarsa awajen dole ta sani sakin wani ma wuyacin numfashi,dayake doguwar rigace sai yaringa tattarota tadaga k'asa yana shafo cinyoyina har xuwa saman pant d'ina,azabure na wuntsila gefe ina nishi saboda banyi wanka ba bazan narsa ya tab'amun gaba ba.

Bansan nayi dariya ba nace "nifa ba komi donka kwana biyu baka tab'ani bane yasa haka,amman ni babu wani sirri."

Mik'a yayi yana sauke numfashi yace "wlh kin kwad'aita mun kanki don Allah inmuje Cyprus karkimun rowa,kibarni nasha garata yadda ya dace"
Boye fuskata nai ina jifarsa da filon gadona nace "wayyo Allah hubby kadaina,bana sonji wlh."

Dariya yake mun yace "oky sorry my love bari natafi nabarki kiyi bacci,amman fa zan dawo da asuba karki rufe mun k'ofa pls"

Walimar tariyar su Abba gidansu data auren mu tayi yadda akeso,abokan yaya da abokan aikinsa da matansu duk sunxo,harda ogansa na wajen aiki.
Haka ta b'angaren anty ma mutanenta sunxo sosai ba kamar y'an wajen aikinsu da mutanen unguwarsu,sai surukanta mahaifan abban su sharifa,don inbaku manta ba nace maku abokin abban su anty ne,so duk dasu akai zaman kotu,yanxu ma gasu sunxo walimar tarewarsu Abban.
anci ansha anraba take away da qur'anai yan hixub biyar biyar,sai turaruka masu k'amshin gaske,nidai ta wajena babu wanda yaxo don J daula bai barni nayi kawaye ba,acewarsa baya son ana fitar dani wani waje inba makaranta ba,duk wayonsa kar wani yasace ni,sai gashi hakan bata anfanar ba.
hakika duk wanda yaxo yaga gidan yana yabawa cike da sambarka,plat House ne mai part biyu kowane nada three bed rooms,ga daining room da waiting falo da kuma main falo babban kenan,akwai wani dan waje da zaka na zama kana shan iska ko kayi ayyukan ka assignment ko aikin office kuma inkashiga baka san fitowa saboda had'uwarsa,an k'wata gidan da shuke shuke na flowers kala kala.
Ga wajen parking space gidan fa yayi sosai,ba atashi daga walimar ba sai bayan magriba kowa ya watse sai yamu yamu.
Ganin duk zamu tafi muma mubarsu.
Abba da Ammer suka rok'i alfarma akan inna da sumy su zauna tare dasu,gidan zai masu girma su kadai yaya yace "to inhar zasu zauna zanfi son hakan,gasunan kuji ta bakinsu ko anty."
Tace "sosai kuwa kaga In abba baya nan Ammer ta bazata na zama da kewa ba,duk da zamu samo masu aiki don ayyukan suyi mata sauki."

Yaya yace"shikenan Ammer yarda kikeso haka za,ai ga kuma su inna akusa dake sai suxauna wancen part din,ita da salisu don sumy ma kwanan nan zan badata tunda ta samu mijin daya dace."

Yaya yace "daman abunda zance maka kenan,Allah ya taimaka sannan game da tafiyarmu Cyprus mun yake cewar zamutafi muduka,inyaso zan samu aimun canjin gida dazai ishemu har sadda zamu dawo,in muka dawo kuma can tsohon gidan inna zan ajiye asmau,nadeeya kuma akwai gidan dana gina unguwarsu anty zan barta acan."
Asmau kamar ta mutu jitake kamar ta fashe da kuka tace bata yarda ba,bata son tsohon gida sabon takeso ai itace babba akanme zai banbantasu,amma saitayi shiru taji me zasuce akai amman abun haushin ba wanda ya nuna hana akwai wani abu.
Anty tace "hakan ma yayi daidai,don daman bazan so ka hadesu gida guda ba,gara kowace da nata zaifi."

Har za,a tashi antyn tace "yawwa inaso kabar mun pretty wajena har sadda zaku tafi,"
Bata fuska yayi yadauke kai zai rashin mutunci yace "gaskiya kiyi hkr gida zamu wuce daga nan nidasu"

Kunyarta yaji yana sosa kai yana kalloni nikuma na yi kamar ban ganshi ba.
Yace"shikenan gata na anbar maki"
Aka saka dariya da haka kowa ya watse aka bar inna da Ammer da Abba sai sumy da salisu dayaxo yau daga kano makaranta.

Nida anty da yara muka wuce gidanta,on ready daman abban su ana gamawa yawuce yana da wajen xuwa.

Yaya Shida asmau suka dauki hanyar gidan inna nada,babu alamun sassauci akan fuskarsa tamau haka ya daureta saika rantse bai taba sanin wani abu wai dariya ko murmushi ba.

Tunda suka d'auki hanyar gidan fuskarsa babu annuri,ko kadan baka tab'a cewa yasan wani abu wai dariya ko murmushi,tamau haka ya d'aureta baka cewa yasan da wani acikin motar tasa.
Asmau kau ko ajikinta duk da haushin za,a barta gidan inna tsohon hakan bai hanata jin farin ciki ba,zasu kasancw su kadai awannan kwanakin,koba komi zasu wala inta dage ma ta mantar da shi waccen mayyar,ta rasa dame nadeeya tafita har da yake rufe idonsa yana shuka mata rashin mutunci,kwarkwasa da kwalliya yimasa take girki ta iya haka tana kula da jikinta da tsaftar muhallinta,bata gaza masa wajen kwanciya ba to meyasa yake mun wulakanci duk saboda ita?to bazan kyale ba wlh bazan zuba ido ina kallo arabani da mijina ba,kota tsiya saina kwatowa kaina martaba ta da yancina,idan inna bata kishina ni ina kishin kaina.
Satar kallonsa tayi ta dauke kai don wannan fuskar tasa saita sata taji fitsari,aranta tace zaka saukone damunje gida ni nasan ta inda zan biyo maka saika mance da wata mai kalar sunanta.

Yabarta da daukar muhin ga jikkarsu ta kaya,daker daker haka taja jikkar tana sab'e da d'anta har cikin falonta,agajiye ta jibgeta gefe ta nufi d'akinta da muhin ta kwantar dashi kan gado tashiga wanka,tana fitowa shima tayi masa suka hau shirinsu tanayi tana masa wasa suna dariya tare.

"Karki yarda da raini daga wajen Asmau,don tana uwargida hakan bashine zai saka kibarta tana raina ki ba,don agirme kin girmeta bazai yuwu ki saka ido ta rainaki ba,bandai ce kijata da fitina ba babu ruwanki da shiga sabgarta,inkinji zaki iya mata magana inta kama kiyi inkinji bazaki iya ba ba dole kowa tayi sabgar gabanta,tunda naga bata son zaman lfy.ki maida hankalinki wajen kula da mijinki cikin sati guda dazai d'akinki don Allah kitabbatar mata da cewar akwai banbanci tsakaninku,tahaka zata gane bata da wayau nina gaya maki da kuka zata baki hkr akan abubuwan dake faruwa,so ki kula ki kuma maida hankali wajen kula da jikinki shi jikin mace d'an gyarane baya son a wofintar dashi,kinga dai abubuwan da nake doraki akai so ki dage wlh zaki bani labari,wadannan abubuwan zasu siyomaki martaba da soyayya wajensa,kamar super glue haka zai ringa nane maki."

Kamar kar mu rabu haka naji,gashi dan nan naji bala'in kewar antyn tana ratsani tun bamu tai ba.

          *Last page*
Kasa hkr yayi saida ya taso ya iso inda nake ya makaleni ajikinsa yana sauke ajiyar xuciya,yace "love u want kill me ko?"
Dariya nai masa nace "ayya sorry hubby taya zan iya kashe ka,to inrayu tare dawa?ai nidakai abadan da'iman insha Allah"
Kissing wuyana yayi yace "Allah yasa haka,wannan gayun duk nawane ko,kice yau shansha shagalina don Allah love banda raki"
Turesa nai ina shagwabe fuska bayan na gama duk shirina,sannan nace"to ba kaine baka san kayi kad'an ba,wlh har cinyoyina rik'ewa sukeyi."
Biyoni yayi kamar wani sakarai yana kama k'uguna ya rik'e yace "bazan bari su sake rik'ewa ba na rantse,nidai yau kibani dama jiyafa banyi ba,kitausaya mun mana"
Nace "aidai gobene zaka koma wajen matarka saita baka"
Tsaki yaja shikanshi baisan ma yayi ba,ko sunanta baya son yaji kwanciya yayi yana fushi,nai murmushi na kashe mana wuta sannan na haye saman cikinsa na fara masa cakulkuli,aikuwa kamar zai hauka saboda dariya yana rok'ona na daina,nima dariyar nake nace "bazan bari ba saikece mun kadaina fushin"
Yana ta kyalkyala dariyar har yana rike ciki yace "n....na.....nadaina.......wlh......wlh love bazan sake ba."
Sannan na daina masa na kwanto saman cikinsa ina ajiyar xuciya shima yanayi,nace ahankali yana shafa sajensa "hubby babban tashin hankaline shine naga fuskarka ta canxa akaina,ma'ana kana fushi dani bana fatan nayi maka abunda zai saka hakan tafaru,kullin so nake mukasance cikin farin ciki nidakai da mom muhin,inason muzama family mai tattare da annashuwa da farin ciki,duk wanda yaxo ya xauna damu ko na wuni gudane zai tabbatar da cewar akwai zaman lfy sosai acikin wannan ahalin,so hakan bazata tabbata ba sai da had'in kanka dana Asmau,don idan muka cigaba da tafiya ahaka zuria zamu tara,yaranmu zasu taso da wata d'abia da hausawa sukeyi ta taya uwarsu kishi,bazasu dauki matar babansu matsayin uwa ba,saboda ganin bakin halin kishi da sukewa juna.
Amma inhar suka taso suka ganmu kanmu ahade bamu dauki juna abokan gaba ba,so zaka ga gidanmu kullin cikin farin ciki.abunda nakeson rok'onka hubby shine punishment d'in dakakewa mom muhin yana damuna,kuma ya isa haka ya isa ace ka saukko haka nan,wlh ta canxa ta gyara halinta ka sake bata wata damar duk dan adam ajixine,tausai take bani in nice cikin halin da take wlh xuciyata zata iya bugawa,domin bazan iya juriyar haka daga gareka ba,yadda nakejin soyayyarka ta gama mamaye mun dukkanin xuciyata da sassan jikina,so bana fatan hakan akaina kuma ina rokonka itama ka sauko haka nan kozata samu natsuwa da farin ciki."
Shiru yayi sosai har na zata bacci yayi,saida na lek'a fuskarsa sannan naga idonsa biyu.
Gyara man kwanciya yayi saman sa yana shafa kaina cikin kulawa da nuna soyayya sanan yafara magana cikin sanyin murya mai dadin gaske.
"Hak'ik'a samun mata kamarki dacene na duniya dana lahira deeya,ban tab'a kawowa cewar kece zan samu natsuwa dake ba awancen lokacin,sai gashi wadda nake zaton samun natsuwa da farin ciki tattare da ita itace take k'unxuga mun bak'in ciki,tabbas asmau ta shayar dani bak'in ciki wanda har bana son tunawa,hakan yajawo kaso tamanin nacikin son da nake mata yabi ruwa,nima bansan ya hakan ta kasance ba.ko kusa bana tunanin daidaita tsakaninmu da ita don yanxu,don banga alamar tagane kurenta ba,bata baki hkr akan abubuwan datake maki ba,bata canza halinta gareni ba amman ke duk da abunda yafaru kina nemar mata maslaha,kina son kushirya ku zauna lafiya ban san wace irin macece ke ba deeya,tabbas kina da xuciya mai kyau bakiso kanki ba kamar yadda koda yaushe ita take son kanta,da ace kece kike cikin wanan halin uhmm wlh asmau bazata tab'a nuna mun hakan ba daidai bane,tunxurani zatai tayi.da ace duk mata haka suke kamarki da aurarrakin duniya ya zauna lfy,da ba'asamu yawaitar sakin aure ba nagode ma Allah daya banike zan kuma cigaba da gode masa,Allah yayi maki albarka ya bamu xuria mai albarka yasa ki dore akan d'abiarki,sannan asmau zataci darajarki zata ci albarkacinki zan bata dama ta karshe inhar ta rik'e da mutunci mucigaba da rayuwa mai kyau inkuma akasamu akasin haka nima bazan ce ga abunda zai faru ba,amma tabbas bashida kyau."
Ajiyar xuciya na sauke inajin na sauke nauyin da yake kaina,inajin hakan danai shine daidai shine maslaha domin ina ganin inada laifi acikin lamarin,bana jin dadin hakan tana faruwa bakuma don bana son mijina ba,aa inasonsa son danake masa da kaunarsa yasa nake jiye masa tsoron fushin Allah,Allah yabani ladar ya kuma cigaba da bani ikon sarrafa kishina da xuciyata.

Daga nan ya burkiceni muka lula duniyar da sai ma'aurata kadai ne zasujeta cike da natsuwa da farin ciki da jiyar da juna dadin da keciki,aduk lokacin da muke kasancewa tare nakan ji ina shawagi asararin samaniya,nakanji babu yani acikin mata domin yadda yaya yake tafiyar dani afagen abun sone da bukatuwa,shiyasa ina jimawa kafin na gaji wanda shikuma alokacin ne yake jinsa asama.
Sau dayawa ma kuka yake mun sosai idan abun yana kaimasa yadda yakeso,don gaskiya bana zama haka nan kullin cikin tattalin jikina nake ina gyarawa,saboda shima yaji daban kuma na samo hakan daga anty,duk sanda mukai waya abunda take dorani akai kenan har takai ga na kware kuma na saba,na daya daga cikin abunda yasa yaya yake k'wak'wata kamar ya had'iyeni..............

Washe gari da safe aka aiko mana da breakfast daga hotel,dayake tunda mukaxo kullin daga can ake aiko dashi yayan yasaka ake kawo wa shikuma yake biya,munsha kwalliyarmu as usual sai k'amshi muke yi falon kasan ba kowa,kamar ma ba a lekoshi ba tunda gari ya waye.
Saida ya zauna nace "bari na kira mom muhin mu karya"
Yace mun to na juya na nufi hanyar bedroom d'inta nayi mata knocking,shiru na d'an lokaci ba'a bud'eba na sakeyi sau uku sannan tataso naji alamun tana budewa.
Kallon juna mukai mamaki ya kamani ganin wata irin rama ta sosai ta bayyana tare da ita,tayi xuru xuru kamar wadda ta tashi aciwo.
Banyi zaton samun tarba ta mutunci daga gareta ba,shiyasa ban saki fuskata ba nace mata "kifito mu karya rana nayi"
Na juya zan tafi tai saurin riko hannuna har hannun nata yana rawa haka ma muryarta tace "pls nadeya inason muyi magana"
Na d'an saki raina nace "later,yanxu dai kifito muyi break sannan"
Ta d'aga kanta ta sakeni najuya ina tsananin mamakin canjin dana sameta dashi ayau,asmau masifaffa da girman kan tsiya itace ta koma haka cikin sati biyu xuwa uku.
Kallona yayi yace "tayi maki halin nata ko,ai dan karna hanaki kiyi mun tsogumi yasa nabarki nasan ba zaku kwashe lfy ba."
Murmushi nai ina matse masa hanci nace "to ba haka bane lafiya lau muka gaisa tana tahowa ma yanxun haka"
Murmushi yayi badan ya yarda ba yace "nizaki yiwa karya love"
Na rufe baki ina zaro ido "wlh hubby ba karya aciki bari tafito ma ka tambayeta kaji"
Hararata yayi yace "waye zai tambayetan?mts"
Yaja tsoki yana cin abunda yake gabansa ina masa dariya spoon ya dauka ya ringa samun ga baki ina masa hira,idan ya bata mun gefen baki ya sunkunya ya lashe da bakinsa.
Yaya indai jikina ne babu inda nazai dora bakinsa akai ba,kuma baya jin komi.
Idan muna tare da shi kamar ya bude xuciyarsa ya sakani haka hakeji,nima kuma inajin hakan sosai kullin jin juna muke kamar yau muka fara soyayyar,ba jimawa saiga asmaun ta fito tayi wanka ta shirya cikin doguwar rigar atamfa ba laifi tayi kyau,tunda itama ba bayaba da kyanta muhin ne sabe ga kafad'arta shima anshirya shi yayi kyau sosai.
Yana hangoni ya fara zalkin tahowa wajena,murmushi nai masa nace "ohni swt hrt har ya ganeni to taho"
Na mik'a masa hannu ta bani shi tana cewa "ai d'axun idonsa biyu da kikaje yana jin muryarki har lekowa yake"
Mukai dariya tare da ita sannan ta zauna tana cemun "ina kwana nadeeya"
Wani iri naji maganar don hakan bata tab'a hadani da itaba,ko nayi mata barka da safiya ko dare ko rana bata ansani saima harar da zata biyo baya.
Nai yak'e nace "antashi lfy ya boy na"
"Gashinan sai kiriniya tunda ya kware wajen rarrafe da mik'ewa tsaye"
Nace "da kyau swt hrt insha Allah sadda zamu koma gida ka take gudu ta ko ina"
Ta ansa da ameen.
Shiru tayi ni kuma na cigaba da masa wasa ina cin fefrsuna,yaya kau kamar babu shi awajen ahankali naji tayi masa magana.
"Dadyn muhin ina kwana"
Wannan karon ma nai mamaki don satin mu na uku muke ciki ban tab'a jin ta gaidasa ba sai yau,lallai ta sakko kuma ta canxa Allah yasa har xuciyarta hakane,nidai ban nuna ma ina saurarensu ba na cigaba da abunda ke gabana.
Kamar anmasa dole yace "lafiya"
Daga haka bata sake magana ba ta xuba abunda zataci sanan ta fara break d'in shikuma yana gamawa lokacin ya mik'e.
"Zan fita wajen aiki"
Nace "adawo lafiya Allah ya bada sa'a ya tsare mana kai"
Yace "Ameen,babu rakiya yau kenan"tashi nai dauke da muhin najera dashi muka nufi k'ofa,naji tace masa "saika dawo Allah ya tsare"
Yanxun ma a takaice ya ansa da "Ameen"
Saida na rakasa na dawo ina kintsa wajen bayan na zaunar da muhin kasa yanata rarrafensa yana zagaye falon.
Tasowa tai tana tayani har muka gama,muka dawo falon duk muka zauna kowa yayi shiru,nidai wayata nake dannawa ina kiran anty don tunda itace tace tana son magana dani ita zata fara fadar abunda take son fadi,basaina tsareta ba.

"Mutanen Cyprus ne yau"
Nai dariya nace "wlh kuwa anty kwana biyu bamu gaisa ba,ina kwana yagida dasu sharifa"
"Lafiya lau wlh,ga sharifa nan bari na bata."
Nan muka ringa hira tana mun shirmenta,saida antyn ta anshe wayar sannan ta dakata.
"Ina khalipan koya fita"
"Eh anty baijima ba ma,yasu Ammer kwana biyu bana samun layinta,har gara abba shi tana shiga amman bana jinsa sosai."
Tace "sunanan lfy laifin network ne nima haka naita nemanki jiya har dare ban samu ba,to yaya kuke ina kishiyarki"
Dariya nai nace tana lafiya bari na bata ku gaisa"
"Keee bana so kyaleta,intayi niyyar kirana aitana da waya"
Shashantar da maganar nai muka hau wata hirar mun jima muna hira sannan muka rabu,har tace kila sadda samu dawo ta haihu,ban so ba naso ace saimun koma yadda za'ai suna damu.

Ina sauke wayar muka had'a ido da ita tace "fushi take dani itama ko?"
Murmushi nai nace "bazance aa ba gaskiya,saboda ai kinsan kinyi mata laifin"
Sadda kai tayi tana wasa da hannunta kafin ta d'ago cikin sanyin murya tace"nasani,shiyasa kunyarta ta hanani nemanta tunda mukaxo,don Allah inkun sake waya kibata hkr zan nemeta nima saina sake bata."
Na jinjina kai nace "ba damuwa zan gaya mata komi zai wuce"
Shirun ta sakeyi nima kuma bance komi ba,kafin daga bisani ta fara magana kamar zatai kuka don har idonta sun cika da kwallah.

"Nadeeya kema nai maki laifin na saudaya ba,ba sau biyu ba.
Don Allah kiyi hkr ki yafe mani sharrin xuciyane dana rud'in shaid'an,wanda na kasa yak'i dashi balle har na iya aikata abun kirki,ban kyauta ba hakika ban kyauta ba,nazamo mai son kaina akoda yaushe na zamo mai haddasa fitina tsakanina da mijina,duk don ta dalilinki dana kejin baki isa ya soki ba bayan gani,bai isa ya daukeki yaje dake wani waje ba bayan gani,haka bai isa ya nuna soyayyarsa akanki ba saini.idona yarufe da matsanancin kishinki,har hakan yasa na bata da mutane da yawa masu muhimmanci arayuwata,na b'ata da mahaifiyata data kawoni duniya,na b'ata da mijina da aljannata take k'asan kafarsa,na b'ata da yayarsa da ta wajenta zan kara samun kauna wajen mijina,naxo na b'ata dake da kika dauki uwata kamar taki uwar,ni kuma na kasa daukar naki iyayen matsayin nawa,na kasa yi masu kwatan kwacin abunda kikewa tawa uwar.tur da halina tur da biyewa xuciyata danai,wlh ba halina bane ba haka nake ba bansan kuma ya akai nakoma haka ba,don Allah nadeeya kiyafe mani na daukeki abokiyar gabata bacin ke baki daukan haka ba,kin daukan kamar kanwarki uwa daya uba daya,baki daukan kishiya ba saboda baki kishin dani sainice nakeyinsa dake,kina kokarin ganin kin gyara alakarmu nidake da yaya,meyasa idona ya rufe meyasa na kasa fahimtar komi yadda yake...............ta fara kuka kasa kasa tana shashsheka maganar ma ta kasa fitowa.
Shiru nai ina saurarenta sai naji tausayinta yayi mugun cika raina,hakika ba wani d'an adam da baya kuskure kaima baka wuce ka aikata hakan ba saboda baka wuci karfin shad'an ya ingiza ka ba,sai idan kayi yak'i dashi bakuma karamin yak'i ba,don shi baya raina kofa koyatake..............

Ahankali na matsa inda take na riko hannunta cikin tausasawa nace "kibar kuka haka asmau,komi ya wuce na yafe maki duniya da lahira ko kusa ban rik'eki acikin raina ba,ba wanda baya kuskure saidai idan munyi muyarda munyin ba wai mukasa yarda cewar munyi ba,kinyi laifuka da dama wanda babban ciki shine sab'awa mijinki da kika ringayi da kuma sab'a maganar mahaifiyarki.
Don haka nidai 2me na yafe wlh komi yawuce atari gaba,sukuma saiki maida hankali kinemi afuwarsu suma su yafe maki din."

Tana share hawaye tace "wlh ina kokari wajen yin hakan,amman yaya baya bani dama.kullin saina rokesa ya saurareni baya kulani,yace idan na matsa masa da magana zai barmun d'akin to ya xanyi,duk yadda naso na bashi hkr toshe kunnensa yake haka inna tunda mukaxo innakira ta saidai sumy ta dauka bata mun mgn,wannan masifar ce ta addabeni nadeeya kisa baki wa inna da yaya su saurareni wlh na gyara bazan sake bakanta masu kamar wancen karon ba."
Ta cigana da share hawayenta cikin kunan xuciya da dana sani,na sake ce mata da kulawa.
"Zan magana da inna insha Allah,shi kuma yaya mun wuce wajen dashi kedai ki dage karkiga yana fushi kice saiya huce tofa bazai huce don yanxun ba,kinace masa har saiya sakko kuma kiyi masa alkawarin anyi na farko anyi ma karshe."
Rik'e hannuna tayi sosai tace "ngd ngd nadeya Allah ya saka da alkairi,ban zaci samun haka daga gareki ba ban dauka cewar bazaki maidani makiyarki ba,sai naga cewar ke baki daukan yadda na daukeki ba ngd insha Allah bazan sake maimaita wannan kuskuren ba."
Na murmushi nace "shikenan Allah ya yafe mana gabaki daya"

******
Daman ranar nake fita girki don haka duk abunda na sabayi ranar da zan fita nayi,ina jin kewar yayan tun kafinma naje kwanciya bacci,don gaskiya munyi wani irin mugun sabo da koyaya d'aya baya tare da d'aya saiyaji ajikinsa,don haka zai sani na cire kayana tas yace ahaka zamuyi bacci,dole kuma ahakan zamu kwana tun inajin kunya da nauyi harna saba,shima na sabar mashi da hakan.haka wanka safiya nayi zai had'a mana ruwa saimuyi awa da rabi muna wanka,muna wasanni saina matsa sannan mufito.
Kullin jinake inama nikadai kedashi inama babu shamakin wata,da jin dadin saiya fi haka,amma duk da haka ina godema Allah daya mallakamani xuciyar mijina batare da wani karfina ko dubara ta ba,domin kuwa abunda mata da yawa suke nema suka rasa kenan,wasu har ga malamin zaure da boka malamin tsubbu mushiriki makiyan Allah suke kai mazansu don su samu xuciyarsu su mallakesu,mu sani ba wannan kesaka ki mallaki xuciyar mijinki ba,kulawa da tausasawa da biyayya da hakuri kauda kai,iya magana da tsafta ta wajen girkinkine makwancinkune jikinkine duka wadannan sune jiga jigan samun miji ga hannu,ba kamar inkika hada da girmama iyayen sa da yan uwansa duk wani nashi ki daukai da muhimmanci,zakisha mamaki sannan ki kauda kai ga abun hannunsa karki zamo mai saka ido akan dukiyarsa,sannan inda hali ki kama abunyi zakiyi rayuwarki cikin farinciki da zaman lafiya.
Allah yasa mu gyara mudauki dukkan abunda yake daidai.

Ina falona ina kallo na gama sallar isha'i dama ashirye nake nai wanka na na tsuke cikin kananun kaya riga da wando sunyi masifar fiddo jikina,gashi sai k'amshi nake haka falon shima kamar anyi b'arin turare.
Jinsa kawai nai ya daukan yasoma zagaye falon dani yana mun dariya,dafari tsoro naji dana ankare shine nai ajiyar xuciya nai lub jikinsa ina shagwabata,direni yayi sannan ya zauna yana dorani kan cinyarsa.
"Love irin wannan kyan ai saki saka ma kaina ya kwance"
Nayi fari da idona yadda nasan yanaso nace "ai haka nakeso,koda yaushe kanka ya kwance akaina hubby,to ya aikin yau fatan babu gajiya."
Kissing lips dina yayi yana wani narkewa yace "babu,amma danai tozali dake sai tasaukko don haka muje aimun wanka a shiryani saina ci abinci ko"
Kallonsa nake cike da mekake nufi ya hure mun idona yace "nasan abunda zakice,kuma ba gobe bane ba."
Na dalla masa hararar wasa nace "Aa hubby ka mance jibine,to wlh ba ruwana nidai don haka katashi kaje matarka tashirya maka tarba ta musamman"
Dandanan ya had'e fuska yana tureni jikinsa zai wuce d'akina,saurin shan gabansa nai ina canza yanayin fuskata nace"yaya I'm serious ba anan kake ba,kuma a wannan lokacin bai dace ka sauka nan ba,don haka ka wuce can kayi wankan ka da shirinka nidai ba ruwana."
Ban zaci ransa zai b'aci akan maganar ba saida ya tsareni da idanunsa muryarsa harta canza yace"inkina son kinuna gajiyawarki akaina basai kin ringa korata ba,don ba nan nake ba sai akace karna zo inda kike,sai akace karnayi wanka dakin ki karna shirya,tsoronta kikeji ne kome?au har kin daina son nawa kenan har kike korata."
Lumshe idona nai inajin hankalina yana tashi nai saurin rik'o hannunsa zanyi magana ya fixge ya wuce d'akin nawa,da sauri na bisa yana cire kaya zai shiga wanka nafara tayasa yasake fixgewa,inata danne raina kar nima ran nawa yab'aci.
Banyi fushi ba na sake kai hannu zan tayasa cire belt dinsa zayayi zanzaro dashi ya fixge yana cewa"ai kindai barwa wata ni ko?to kije kiyita sha'aninki ngd"
Yashige toilet yabarni tsaye kamar an shukani,raina yab'aci har naji d'acin kan halshena,miye laifina anan ne wai?don ina kokarin ganin na fita hakkin wata,ina kokarin na kaisa hanyar da zata kaisa aljanna,shine laifina badan raina yanaso ba nima saidai inba hakan nai ba,bazai daina kokarin danne mata nata hakkin ba.
Nan bakin toilet d'in na silale na jingina kaina jikin bangon ina kuka kasa kasa,sosai nai kukana na godema Allah na saka kaina cikin guiwata har bansan fitowarsa ba,sai naji ruwa akan bayana yana d'iga.
K'in d'agowa nai don nasan shine yake tsaye gabana,tsugunninsa naji gabana yana son ya d'ago kaina na k'iya ture hannunsa nake inajin wani kukan yana taso mani,na rushe da kukan kau nace.
"Kara bu dani,kadaina tab'ani kaje abunka bana son ka kusadani"
Na sake saka kukan dauka ta yayi ya ajiye kan gado ya kwanto jikina cikin tausasa murya yace "I'm very sorry love,nayi hurting d'inki raina ne ya b'aci kina kokarin sadaukar dani inda ba'a san darajata ba,bana son duk abunda zaisaka nayi nesa dake kiyi hkr nasan ban kyauta ba kidaina kukan,inajinsa har cikin raina inba sokeki nayi nima ba kidaina."
Nasaka hannu ina turesa nace "ni ina kokarin ganin nayi abunda yadace kai kana sagemun guiwa,so kake na xuba maka ido kacigaba da danne hakkinta ne?duk laifin datayi ai ya isa ace ka sakko haka nan,bafa don bana jin kishin nan ba inajin ma inda zan fito da nawa fili saitace ita ba kishi take ba,amma dayake ni bana son tashin hankali bana son abunda Allah zai kamamu da laifi yasa nake dannewa ina nuna komi ba komi bane,meyasa zakai kokarin dakushe kokarina?muddin kanayin haka wataran zaka rasa gane kaina zaman lafiyar da muke fata bazai wanxu ba."
Rungumeni yayi sosai yace "na sani wlh love,nasan kan daidai kike tsabar sonki da son kasancewa inda kike yasan ya haka,amma zan dage naga nabaki hadin kai insha Allah bazan sake ba kiyi hkr kinji love d'ina."
Kai na d'aga masa yace "no magana nakeson kiyi mani"
Cikin shagwaba nace "to banayi hkrin ba"
Danneni yayi ya sakarmun duka nauhin sa saida nayi dariya sosai ya d'agani,na tayasa ya shirya cikin kayansa masu bala'in yin kyau na bacci masu taushin gaske,
Ina tsokanarsa nace "su hubby yau ango kake"
Harara ya sakarmun yana kaimun rankwashi yace"zan kamaki yarinya"
Na fesa masa turare nakama hannunsa zamu wuce kasa,yajawoni muka rungume juna muna sauke numfashi,marata yake shafawa cike da salo yace mun cikin sassauta murya.
"Wai love yaushe ne bbyna zai sauka ne shiru har hanxun"
Murmushi nai cike da jin shauk'insa nace "Allah kadai yasani yaya muyi addua ya bamu masu albarka koma yaushene"
Kissing dina yayi sosai sannan yace "hakane kinyi magana mai kyau,I will miss u love"
Muna rik'e da hannun juna na rakosa yayi dinner sannan nayi masa sallama na koma d'akina,inajin wani abu ga makoshina mai wahalar wucewa na runtse idona wani zafi nakeji sosai yana taso mani,tabbas inhar sauran ranaku babu abunda yashiga tsakaninsu banajin yau zata kare ba aiba.
Wannan dalilin yasa nasha kukana na godema Allah,nai sallata nai karatuna sannan na samu salama banyi bacci ba sai biyun dare.

*****

Tun ahanya yaga canji yanayi,k'amshi ta ko ina duk da ta saba hakan duk ranar da ata anshesa,saidai na yau na musammanne,tsaki yaja yace "na d'an lokacine kafin ta sake juyewa"
Sallama yayi yashiga bayan ta ansa masa,tana zaune kan sallaya tana karatun qur'ani muhin nata bacci kan d'an gadonsa.
Kallo d'aya yayi mata ya kauda kanshi,tatashi ta cire hijab d'in cikin d'ard'ar tace"sannu da xuwa dadyn muhin"
Atakaice yace "yawwa"
Tv dake cikin d'akin ya kunna yana kallon news ita kuma tatashi ta dauke abun sallar takai inda yake,taxo ta wuce toilet tayi brush tafito on ready daman kayan baccine jikinta masu saukin nuna jiki,ta kuma saka kamshi jikinta sosai ta fita taje ta kawo masa wani lemu da take yawan had'a masa agida,na ayaba ne yana son sa amma gabanta fad'uwa yake karya koreta ya mata tsawa.
Ta durkusa tana cewa "dadyn muhin gashi"
Kallonta yayi ita da lemun ya watsar sannan yace"duk munafurce munafurcenki dai bai wuce kice hakkinki kike nema ko,to gani naxo nabaki inkin shirya saiki mun magana"
Wannan kalami ba karamin toya ranta yayi ba,ta ja gefe tasaka kanta cikin guiwa tana rera kuka mai cin xuciya,tundaga xuciyarta kukan yake fitowa bata tab'a kawo fassarar da zai mata ba kenan,tabbas tana kewarsa tanan bangaren amma yafiyarsa da sakin fuskarsa dadawo da mu'amalarsa gareta sune tafi buk'ata,kozata so su kasance tare ba acikin wannan mood din nasa ba,tana kokarin ganin ta gyara amma baya bata dama kullin sai gwasalewa ba yabawa,kuskurene tayi kuma tayi nadama meyasa bazai fahimci haka ba.
Bai zaci zaiji wani abu akan kukanta ba amma sai yaji babu dadi,dixgin da yayi mata bai kyauta ba,kukan yana damunsa ya juya inda take yace "dukanki nai,ni kiyi mun shiru karki tayar mun da yaro yana bacci."
D'if yaji tayi shirun sai shashsheka da take tayi har lokacin kanta na cikin guiwarta,tausayinta yaji jin kuma tayi shiru daga magana d'aya rufe idonsa yayi yana son yadaina jin haushinta,ahankali ya kashe tv yace cikin murya mai sanyi.
"Taso ki xo nan wajen"
Tana ta son hawayen su tsaya tataso kamar kwai ya fashe mata aciki,idonta kasa bata kallesa ba ta tsaya gefensa tana ajiyar xuciya.
Hannunsa ya mik'o mata bata fahimci me yake so ba,saita kasa yin komi har saida yace "kixo nan nace"
Gabanta ya fad'i ta zaci wani abunne kuma,ta sake matsawa kusadashi yaja hannunta ya zaunar gefensa yace"bani lemun nasha"
Cikin sauri ta d'auko masa ta bashi ta kasa kallonsa,jikinta sai rawa yake.
Saida yasha sannan yace "inajinki,ba kince kinson na saurareki ba to yau kunnena bude yake bismillah"
Ta fara murzar hannunta tarasa ma ta ina zata kama,sai hawaye suka fara silalowa muryarta da kuka tace "ya......yaya kayafe mun."
Ta fashe da kuka kamar ranta zai fita,sosai take yinsa tana jan numfashi.
Hannunta ya rik'o ya matsa ahankali sannan yajawota jikinsa ta kwantar da kanta kan kafad'arsa hannunsa yana rungume da ita ta gefensa.
Yace "stop crying,kiyi mun magana ba tare da kuka ba inba haka ba zan barmaki d'akinki"
Tayi shiru tana jan iska tace "yaya ban kyauta maka ba,nayi maka badaidai ba hakika na aikata abunda zakai mun fiye da haka,na kasa fahimtarka na kasa gane muhimmancinka tare dani,na d'aga maka hankali na gaya maka maganganu marasa dadi duk kajure kana tare dani,don Allah yaya ka yafe mun wlh nayi dana sani nayi nadama bazan sake ba nai alkawarin hakan,don Allah kadaina wannan fushin dani yana cutar da xuciyata."
Wata irin ajiyar xuciya ya sauke yace "dagaske yana cutar da xuciyarki?"
D'aga kanta tayi tana sake boye fuskarta jikinsa."
Yace "nuna man nagani to"
Murmushi ya kubce mata  tana boyewa.
"Ki yankewa kanki hukunci Asmau,inhar irin haka ta sake faruwa mezan maki"
"Bazan iya cewa gashi ba,amman kayi mun duk abunda kake ganin shine daidai"
Jinjina kai yayi yace "fine,komi ya wuce abu na gaba kuma shine kiyi kokari ki zauna lafiya da nadeeya,domin bazan boye maki ba ta dalilinta ne har kika samu kaina ayau d'inan,don da inajin har mukoma gida bazaki taba gane kaina ba,don kin b'ata raina fiye da kima,so inhar kina son farin cikinmu nidake ya dore kizauna lafiya da ita,don nadeeya itace abokiyar zaman da kowace mace takeson in mijinta zai kara aure ya kawo mai kalar halinta,don daman abunda suke gudu mace tashigo gidanki ta kwace maki miji,ta lalata mu'amalarku ta kuma koreki kinaji kina gani,to da nadeya tayi niyya tsaf zatai haka nikuma na biye mata,saboda ina samun komi gareta wanda narasa wajenki.kisani yar uwatace wadda mukema so mai yawa,sannan naxo na aureta ina sonta sosai kamar yadda nake sonki.inkin kiyaye mu zauna lafiya cike da farin ciki inkuma kinkoma ruwa to saina ce maki allah ya had'a kowa da rabonsa"
Rik'o hannunsa tayi tana murzawa cikin jin sabuwar kaunarsa da tsananin kewarsa tace"naji wlh naji yaya anyi ya wuce baza'a kara ba kuma nayarda albarkacin nadeya naci,nakuma gode mata kaima nagode daka saurareni ka bani wata damar,insha Allah bazaku sake samun matsala daga gareni ba."
Ajiyar xuciya yayi yace "thank you,what next"
Shiru tayi tace "bakomi yaya"
Yace "au ba komi kuma,shikenan to d'agamun jiki natafi inda zan samu komin"
Tai saurin riko masa hannu tana yin narai narai da fuska tacs cike da kunya.
"Toni ai bance katafi ba ko"
Murmushi yayi yana a tsayen yace "oky to ina jinki saime kuma"
Kamar zata yi me dan jin nauyinsa tace"uhn......uhm....mu kwanta"
Ta fad'a tana juyar da kanta yayi dariya yana mik'a mata hannunsa yace "come to me" tataso ahankali saida jinta tayi yadauketa ya aza bisa gadon,ya kashe masu fitila.
Wannan dare ankashe arna sama da dubu,duk bashin da akaci saida aka biyashi,yaga rikici kala da kala bai dauka asmaun sa bace don bai tab'a samunta irin haka ba,kullin shitake bari da aiki yau kam ba sauki sai wajen Allah,daya niyyar kyaleta zata burkice masa haka sukaita abu guda har hud'un asuba sannan fa jikinta ya mutu musurus,basu samu sallar asuba ba sai dasafe.

Aranar daka kalli fuskar daya kasan suna cikin farin ciki,nayi iyakar kokarina na nuna babukomi don bilhakki da gaskiya wani nannauyan abu ya danne kirjina,yini nai ina kwance danaji motsin yayan zai shigo saina shige toilet kusan sau uku inayin haka,har ya fahimci menake ciki sai ya mamayeni ya boye bayan labule yaje yarufe k'ofar yadda zanji alamun anfita nikuma sai in fito.
Aikuwa ina fitowar ya cafkeni jikinsa yana yawo dani cikin d'akin,ban san nasaka masa kuka ba,bai hanani ba saida nai mai isata naji kirjina ya rage nauyi ya dago fuskata.
"Haba love,karki sace mun guiwa ya zaki kokarin tadama kanki hankali nima ki tada mani,karki bari hakan ya saka ki cikin wani halin."
Na kwantar da kaina ina ajiyar xuciya nace "I'm sorry,abunne yana da rad'ad'i yaya"
Cikin lallashi yace "hkr akeyi,dakinji saikiyi addua"
"Inayi yaya sosai ma har keeping kaina busy nake don kar abun ya daman,amman All in bend"
Shafa kaina yayi tayi yana lallashina tare da hura man iska tsawon lokaci sai gashi nai bacci jikinsa,wanda ban samu nayi cikin jin dadi ba.
Saiyaji kamar da masassara jikinta,ya kwantar da ita yafita.
Yace ma asmau taxo ta rakasa yaje ya siyowa deeya magani,yaji kamar bata lfy.
Cikin damuwa tace "nima naga hakan daxu da ta sakko Allah ya sawake"
Tare sukaje suka dawo sannan natashi kaina na bala'in ciwo,ga masassarar ta sake rufeni na gansu sun shigo,murmushi nai masu tace "ashe bakida lafiya daxun baki gaya mun ba"
Ina murmushi nace "wlh ban tabbatar ba sai yanxu"
Magani da ruwa yayan ya mik'omun nasha,ko minty biyar banba sai amai,dana gama sai wani tun abun yana da sauki har ya girmama,hankulansu ya tashi sosai karma ace yaya kamar zai haukace yafita ya fiddo mota.asmau ta taimaka mani muka fito,ganin ma tafiyar daker nakeyinta ya iso ya daukan ya bude motar yasakani aciki,muduka muka tai asibiti cikin yardar Allah aka samun drip da allurai sannan likitan yaba yaya result d'in awon jinin da akaimun............cikin mahaukacin murna ya rungume likitan yana masa godiya,har da tukuici yaxo yana Gayama asmau itama tashiga murna tana cewa"nayi zargin haka wlh tun kafin mutaho,ashe ashe dai gaskiyane Allah ya tabbatar da alkairi ya kawosa ko ya kawota lafiya."
Bansan wainar da suke toyawa ba saida muka dawo gida daddare yake gaya mun,nima nayi farin ciki sosai zai haifi d'an kaina jinin yaya su ammer zasuga jikansu ashe.

Tundaga nan zaman lafiya ya wanxu acikin gidan nan,yaya kamar bai taba haihuwa ba sosai yake bani kulawa,haka asama'u har mamakinta nake yadda take kula dani,kamar ba kishiryarta ba muna tare koda yaushe inba yaya ne yashigoba zata bamu waje,inbashine yace ta zauna ba bata zama.
Ahankali na ringa samun sauki saboda yana girma,labari har yaje Nigeria ai yaya shegen surutune dashi har su Ammer sun sani da su inna,awannan lokacinne nasamu na daidaita inna da yarta,bayan dogon dagiya danasha nayi mata rantsuwar aita gyara lafiya lau muke zaune.
Akwana atashi muka fara shirin komawa gida don su yaya sun kammala aikinsu cike da nasarori kala daban daban,anba yaya sabuwar kujerarsa mai babban muk'ami muna komawa gida muka samu anty da sabuwar bbynta rufaida,rufy kyakkyawar yarinya duk tafisu sharifa kyau.

Agidana sabo na sauka saboda komi ansaka mun gidan kamar ba anan kaduna ba,ya hadu matuka gaya don nai mamakin kyansa ashe haka yaya ya narkar da kudi wajen ginin,sai lokacin na lura ashe ginin mace biyune natsaresa da tambaya rannan.
Yanata aikinsa kan computer don yanxu company da Abba ya basa yana kawo kud'i over ma kuwa,so busy yake koda yaushe shiyasa yace dana haihu yarona yayi kwari zaibar company d'in hannuna,yaji da aikin office shikuma abun yamun dadi sosai yace ita kuma asmau zai maidata makaranta inta gama ya samar mata aikinyi,koda nai masa batun karo karatuna yace inyi hkr bazai iya barina ba,don baya son abunda zai ringa sawa yana nesa dani,gara dai na hkr da aikin company d'in sufita tare su dawo tare.

Yana ta aikinsa yana shan lemu wajen wani bagala wajen shan iskane yana dadin zama sosai naja kujerata na zauna ina turo baki nace.
"Agogo sarkin aiki,weekend d'inma bazaka huta ba wlh nidai banaso"
Kamar zan kuka na fad'a ajiye aikin yayi yana maida hankalinsa kaina ya kurbi lemunsa yana kallona,yayi murmushi "kinga yadda kika koma wata lukuta dake,wlh yanxun idan na daukeki har bayana ciwo yake"
Na yamutsa fuska zan kuka nace "aidai bani nayiwa kaina cikin ba ehe,"
Dariya yayi yace "sorry na sani ni naimaki shikenan ko"
Nai dariya nace "Allah bakajin kunya yaya kake fadar haka"
"Kunyar wa zanji?badai keba ai kunyar marar kunya asara,ko kin mance abunda kikemun wai intuna maki"
Nace "menakeyi badai sharri zakai mun ba"
Yana dariya yace "aa gaskiyace,jiya waye yake cewa wayyoly Allahna hubby karka bari yimun da karfi,ashhhh dadi yaya dadadi wlh..........dukansa nake cicin karfina shikuma yana dariya yana kokarin kare kansa,saida nagaji yajawoni yadora bisa cinya yana ajiyar xuciya.
"Hubby banaso aidai bana cikin hayyacina shiyasa ko"
Ya had'e fuskokin mu yana shinshina mun bakina kamar zai cinyesa yace"ni so nakema ink'ara inzaki bani dama"
Nafashe da kukan karya nace "aa nibanaso wuya akesha"
Yace "kuma ga dadi ba"
Dukansa nai yana dariya yace "to wasa nake shikenan,sauka in idasa aikin nan na kosa nayi maki handing over da aikin nan,wlh bana samun hutu"
Cikin tausayawa nace "sorry hubby ai end of this month ne na haihu,da na gama wanka zan dauki nanny dazata na kulaman da bbyn kaga saina ansa kaima ka samu sauk'i."
"Aa love sai yarona ko yarinyana yayi wata uku xuwa hudu sannan,"
Nace "Allah ya kaimu lfya wai hubby wancen part d'in wazaka saka"
Cikin tsokana yace "sabuwar amaryata mana"
Zaro ido nai ina kallonsa idona har ya kawo ruwa nace "kishiya zakaimun yaya?"
Dariya yake "yace eh mana,abokaina fa akwai mai hud'u kinga gara na cika uku zaifi"
Rushe masa da kuka nai bilhakki da gaskiya,ganin da gaske nake yasa yace "kidaina kukan wasa nake wlh,mom muhin nakeson ta dawo nan tunda naga dagaske ta gyara halinta,ko wancen karon daman ku biyun nagina mashi,to halinta yasa nace tsohon gidan inna zata zauna."
Dungure masa kai nayi mace "Amma yaya bakaji wlh"
Ya dage mun girarsa guda "kinfini rashin ji ai"

*******

Allah sarki rayuwar duniya komi yayi farko zayyi karshe,cikin lokaci kankane farin ciki ya baibaye family d'in mu anyi bikin sumy nima kuma na haifi kyakkyawan yarona mai sunan Abban su yaya ana kiransa da *muhseen* tuni na anshe company din yaya ina juya masa shi yadda ya dace,kuma yanajin dadin yadda nake tafiyar dakomk cikin tsari.
Asmau ta koma makaranta ta zab'e bangaren education xuwa shekara hudu ta kammala digree dinta,sannan yaranta hudu cif muhin ne babba sai aslamiyya ana ce mata aslam.sai mufida da jalila.
Nikuma ukku na haifa nafarkon muhseen sai twins dina mace da namiji,ammer da Abbana ana kiran macen *heedaya* namijin kuma *hisham* rayuwarmu gwanin dadi da ban sha'awa,kowa yaxo gidanmu saiyayi sha'awarmu yarana dana asmau babu nuna banbanci,sun daukan uwa kamar yadda suka dauki mamansu,haka ma su muhseen sun dauki asmau mahaifiya kamar yadda suka daukan.
Babu wani tashin hankali,yaya yana iyakar kokarinsa wajen kulawarsa akanmu,don kudi yake samu ta ko ina xuwa lokacin ya bude mun wani katafaren boutique wanda yake kawo kudi sosai,ya budema Asmau babban store hakika yaya tsaye yake agidansa.shiyasa albarka ta ko ina xuwar masa take.
Nikam alhamdulillah komi daidai nakejinsa kuma kullin soyayyar juna nida yaya gaba take ba baya ba,yaran mu uku sun isheni rayuwa shiyasa da Allah ya daukemun haihuwar ban damu ba,asmau ma bata sake haihuwar ba sai muka cigana da renon wadanda Allah yabamu..............zan iyacewa daganan *Alhamdulillah rayuwar nadeeya tana tafiya successful*

_Alhamdulillahi tammat godiya ta tabbata gaAllah buwayi gagara misali,hakika banida abunda zancewa ubangiji face godiya daya bani ikon kammala wannan doguwar tafiyar lfy cike da koshin lfy,ina rokon kuraran danayi allah yayafe mun,kuma kuma ina neman afuwa inhar na b'atawa wani._

_Sannan ina fatan darussan da ke cikin wannan labarin zaije ga duk inda banyi tsammani ba,Allah yasa abubuwan damuke aikatawa wanda sukaii shige da kalar wadannan mudauki hanyar gyarawa,sai nace maku sai mun hade cikin sabon labarina mai suna *SO DA SHAQUWA* akan sassaukan farashi dari uku so saikun niyoni acikinsa zaku gane abunda nake nufi ngd ngd masoyana na nesa dana kusa duk ina gaidaku Allah ya hada fuskokinmu da alkairi_
